================================================================================
FILE: .github/workflows/release.yml
================================================================================
name: Build Windows EXE Release

on:
  push:
    tags:
      - '*'
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  build-windows:
    runs-on: windows-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout repo
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          python -m pip install --upgrade pip
          pip install -r requirements.txt pyinstaller

      - name: Build EXE with PyInstaller
        run: |
          pyinstaller --noconfirm --onefile --windowed --name tbr --hidden-import waitress --add-data "web/templates;web/templates" --add-data "web/static;web/static" main.py

      - name: Generate SHA-256 Checksum
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          $hash = (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 dist/tbr.exe).Hash.ToLower()
          "$hash  tbr.exe" | Out-File -Encoding ascii -NoNewline dist/tbr.exe.sha256

      - name: Upload EXE and SHA256 to GitHub Release
        if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/')
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        with:
          files: |
            dist/tbr.exe
            dist/tbr.exe.sha256
          generate_release_notes: true

FILE: Dockerfile
================================================================================
FROM python:3.12-slim

WORKDIR /app

COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

COPY . .

ENV TBR_DATA_DIR=/data \
    TBR_BT_BACKUP=/bt_backup \
    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1

EXPOSE 5000

CMD ["python", "main.py", "--headless", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "5000"]

> [Читать на русском](README_RU.md)

# ⚡ tbr PRO — qBittorrent Swiss-Army Knife

> Core Version: **v3.0.1+** | Android Companion: **tbr-widget v1.3.0**  
> Integrated **EN / RU** localization toggle and custom themes right in the header!

# 🚀 Features & Arsenal

1. **Atomic .fastresume Stats Merger with Torrent Diff & Smart Match:**
   Ratio nerd's wet dream. Transfer `uploaded` & `seeding_time` metadata between torrents without corrupting bencode. Automatically shuts down and revives qBittorrent, inspects payload files via **Torrent Diff** (% volume match), auto-suggests matches via **Smart Match** (ignoring roman/arabic sequel numbering), and safely cleans up the source torrent with directory overlap safety (`content_path` inspection). Target goals auto-transfer seamlessly!

2. **Honest Like-for-Like (LFL) Analytics, Health Score & 365-Day Heatmap:**
   Zero phantom spikes from newly added torrents. "Today vs Yesterday" and "This Week vs Last" are calculated down to the exact second. Includes a GitHub-style 365-day upload heatmap, "Last Seeder" alert radar, swarm value metric (Health Score), and weighted historical ETA forecaster powered by 4 independent SQLite databases (WAL mode).

3. **Hardware & ISP Guard: Native Windows Anti-Sleep, Watchdog & ISP Detector:**
   Windows native sleep prevention via Win32 API (`SetThreadExecutionState`) keeps your rig awake when upload/download exceeds your threshold. qB Watchdog restarts unresponsive clients (up to 2 attempts with a 30s pause), Auto-Recheck tags dead drives with `io-error`, and the traffic inspector alerts you if your ISP is throttling your bandwidth.

4. **Disaster Recovery Manifest & Panic Killswitch:**
   Emergency red button for unexpected visitors or panic moments: instantly halts all torrents, locks global transfer limits to 1 B/s, freezes the autopilot for 1 hour, and optionally terminates the client process. Automated weekly Disaster Recovery manifests export your entire library, trackers, and magnet links to compact JSON.

5. **Orphan File Scanner:**
   Scans download roots for leftover files and abandoned folders that no longer belong to active torrents. Built with Path Traversal protection and double-confirmation safety (never deletes anything to recycle bin automatically).

6. **Smart Timers: Smart Freeze, Snooze & Expiration:**
   - **Smart Freeze:** Pauses only active torrents, memorizes them, and resumes exclusively what was running without unpausing long-forgotten torrents.
   - **Snooze Timer:** Background DB-backed sleep countdown (30m / 1h / 2h / custom) via speed throttling or freezing.
   - **Expiration Timer:** Self-destruct torrents based on age or upload volume (disk deletion is strictly opt-in).

7. **Zero-Trust Security & Private Vault:**
   Hashed passwords (`pbkdf2:sha256`) with auto-upgrade, standalone API tokens (`tbr_api_...`), single-use invite tokens (`/invite/<token>`), brute-force protection, and strict RBAC (Admin vs Viewer). The **Private Vault** filters out sensitive tags on the backend: torrents are either purged from guest views or anonymized in global sums without leaking titles.

8. **Next-Gen Automation & DND Quiet Hours (Telegram & Discord):**
   Fine-grained autopilot rules (+tags, -tags, pause by ratio, seed duration, or average speed window W). DND Mode ("Quiet Hours") buffers non-critical alerts into a morning digest while letting critical drive/client alerts punch through immediately.

9. **Android Home Screen Widget (`tbr-widget v1.3.0`) + PWA + Command Palette (Ctrl+K):**
   Native Android home screen companion rendered entirely on Canvas (RGB_565, low memory & battery friendly). One-tap chart toggling (Speed / Days / Top-N / All), offline cache, in-app APK auto-updating from GitHub Releases, and fast pairing via QR scanner. Web UI installs as an address-bar-free PWA, with a full-blown `Ctrl+K` command palette.

10. **Seeder Hall of Fame & Canvas Share Card:**
    10 permanent achievements ("Ratio God", "Chief Surgeon", "Night Shift"), funny real-world upload equivalents (modern games, 4K movies, OS ISOs), and an HTML5 Canvas Share Card generator with a "Hide torrent names" privacy toggle.

# 🛠️ Architecture

- **Backend:** Python 3, multi-threaded **Waitress** WSGI server (threads=8) with graceful Werkzeug fallback, modular Blueprint architecture (every file strictly < 300 lines).
- **Databases:** 4 isolated SQLite WAL-mode databases with `busy_timeout=30000` (upload history, profiles/invites, automation rules, app state).
- **Backup & Recovery:** Hot atomic backup via `sqlite3.backup()` into `.zip` (with secret-stripping options) and pre-boot restore.
- **Android Companion:** Kotlin, Android SDK 26–35, WorkManager, Jetpack Security Crypto, ZXing QR Scanner.

---

# 💻 Quick Start

### Option 1: Standalone `.exe` (Windows)
1. Grab the latest `tbr.exe` from [Releases](https://github.com/kamushek0o/tbr/releases).
2. Run it. The tray icon will appear, and the web UI will open in your default browser.
3. Updates are delivered seamlessly: checks SHA-256 checksums, downloads patches, and restarts automatically.

### Option 2: Docker / Headless (Linux / TrueNAS / Unraid)
```bash
docker build -t tbr-pro .
docker run -d \
  --name tbr-pro \
  -p 5000:5000 \
  -v /path/to/data:/data \
  -v /path/to/qBittorrent/BT_backup:/bt_backup \
  -e TBR_WEB_PASSWORD=your_super_password \
  tbr-pro
```
*Or headless via CLI:* `python main.py --headless --host 0.0.0.0 --port 5000`

### Option 3: From Source
```bash
git clone https://github.com/kamushek0o/tbr.git
cd tbr
pip install -r requirements.txt
python main.py
```
*(To build your own standalone `.exe`, execute `build.bat`)*

### 📱 Android Home Screen Widget
Download the pre-built `.apk` from the companion repo [tbr-widget Releases](https://github.com/kamushek0o/tbr-widget/releases). In the web interface, open `⚙️ Settings` -> `📱 QR Code`, scan it with your phone, and the widget will auto-configure without manual typing.

> [Read in English](README.md)

# ⚡ tbr PRO — швейцарский нож для qBittorrent

> Версия: **v3.0.1+** | Android-компаньон: **tbr-widget v1.3.0**  
> Встроенный переключатель языка **EN / RU** и тем оформления прямо в шапке!

# 🚀 Что умеет этот комбайн?

1. **Атомарный подменщик .fastresume с Torrent Diff и Smart Match:**
   Если вы любите красивую статистику и не хотите терять терабайты отдачи при перехешировании — переносите метаданные `uploaded` и `seeding_time` без битья bencode. Программа сама глушит и поднимает клиент, сверяет состав раздач пофайлово (% совпадения), находит пары через Smart Match (с защитой от римских/арабских сиквелов) и безопасно подчищает исходник с проверкой путей `content_path`. Планки целей (Goals) переносятся на лету!

2. **Честная аналитика, Like-for-Like (LFL) и 365-дневный Heatmap:**
   Никаких фантомных всплесков от добавления торрентов. Сравнение «Сегодня vs Вчера» и «Эта неделя vs Прошлая» считается строго секунда в секунду. Годовая тепловая карта активности в стиле GitHub, радар «Единственный сид», расчет индекса ценности раздачи (Health Score) и взвешенный калькулятор прогноза финиша на базе 4 SQLite-баз данных (WAL).

3. **Железная надёжность: Windows «Анти-сон», Watchdog и Детектор троттлинга:**
   Нативный перехватчик сна Windows через Win32 API (`SetThreadExecutionState`) держит систему бодрой, пока идет отдача выше порога. Сторожевой таймер Watchdog мягко перезапустит упавший qB (до 2 попыток с паузой 30с), Auto-Recheck повесит тег `io-error` при битом секторе диска, а анализатор графика сам распознает полку скорости и намекнет, что провайдер режет канал.

4. **Аварийный манифест (Disaster Recovery) и Panic Killswitch:**
   Красная кнопка на случай внезапного визита гостей или перегрузки: в один клик гасит всю отдачу, урезает глобальный лимит до 1 B/s, замораживает автопилот на 1 час и опционально убивает процесс клиента. А еженедельный экспорт манифеста упакует все раздачи, трекеры и magnet-ссылки в компактный JSON (хранятся 2 свежие копии).

5. **Сканер «сирот» на диске (Orphan Scanner):**
   Находит забытые гигабайты и папки в каталогах загрузок, которые давно удалены из клиента, но продолжают жрать место на SSD/HDD. Полная защита от Path Traversal, удаление строго вручную с двойным подтверждением.

6. **Умные таймеры: Smart Freeze, Snooze и Expiration:**
   - **Умная заморозка:** ставит на паузу только активные раздачи, запоминает их и возвращает в строй, не трогая старые паузы.
   - **Таймер сна (Snooze):** автономный таймер в базе (на 30м / 1ч / 2ч или вручную) с ограничением скорости или паузой.
   - **Срок жизни (Expiration):** самоуничтожение торрента по таймеру или достижению объёма отдачи (удаление файлов с диска по умолчанию выключено во избежание слёз).

7. **Параноидальная безопасность и Private Vault:**
   Раздельные пароли (`pbkdf2:sha256`) и API-токены (`tbr_api_...`), одноразовые ссылки-инвайты (`/invite/<token>`), защита от брутфорса и строгий RBAC (Admin / Viewer). Фирменный **Private Vault** на уровне бэкенда скрывает интимные теги от гостей (роли Viewer): раздачи либо полностью вырезаются из списков, либо обезличенно суммируются в общую статистику без утечки названий.

8. **Умные боты и «Тихий час» (Telegram & Discord):**
   Гибкие правила автопилота (+теги, -теги, пауза по ratio, дням или средней скорости в окне W). Режим DND («Тихий час») копит ночные уведомления в очередь и выкатывает аккуратный утренний дайджест, пропуская только критические тревоги. Регулярные дайджесты сами сравнят отдачу с прошлым периодом и дошлют отчет после простоя ПК.

9. **Android-виджет (`tbr-widget v1.3.0`) + PWA + Палитра команд (Ctrl+K):**
   Нативный виджет для рабочего стола Android на чистом Canvas (RGB_565, не жрёт память и батарею). Мгновенное переключение графиков в один тап (Скорость / Дни / Топ-N / Все сразу), оффлайн-кэш, автообновление APK прямо из GitHub Releases и быстрый вход по QR-коду. Веб-интерфейс работает как полноценное PWA-приложение, а по нажатию `Ctrl+K` доступна палитра команд в стиле VS Code.

10. **Зал славы сидера и локальный Share Card:**
    10 бессрочных достижений («Бог Ratio», «Главный хирург», «Ночная смена»), юмористические эквиваленты вашей отдачи (в современных играх, 4K фильмах и ISO-образах) и генератор красивой карточки статистики (Share Card PNG) на HTML5 Canvas с возможностью замазать названия раздач для безопасного хвастовства в чатах.

# 🛠️ Что под капотом?

- **Бэкенд:** Python 3, многопоточный сервер **Waitress** (threads=8) с мягким фоллбэком на Werkzeug, модульная архитектура на Blueprint'ах (все файлы строго до 300 строк).
- **Базы данных:** 4 независимые SQLite базы данных в режиме **WAL** с `busy_timeout=30000` (история отдачи, профили и инвайты, автопилот, состояние системы).
- **Надёжность:** горячий бэкап всех БД через `sqlite3.backup()` в `.zip` (с опциями скрытия секретов) и отложенное накатывание до старта движка.
- **Мобильный компаньон:** Kotlin, Android SDK 26–35, WorkManager, Jetpack Security Crypto, ZXing QR Scanner.

---

# 💻 Как запустить?

### Вариант 1: Готовый `.exe` (Windows)
1. Скачайте свежий `tbr.exe` из [Releases](https://github.com/kamushek0o/tbr/releases).
2. Запустите. В трее появится значок с молнией, а в браузере откроется панель управления.
3. Обновления прилетают по воздуху: программа сама проверит SHA-256, скачает патч и перезапустится через батник.

### Вариант 2: Headless-сервер / Docker (Linux / TrueNAS / Unraid)
```bash
docker build -t tbr-pro .
docker run -d \
  --name tbr-pro \
  -p 5000:5000 \
  -v /path/to/data:/data \
  -v /path/to/qBittorrent/BT_backup:/bt_backup \
  -e TBR_WEB_PASSWORD=your_super_password \
  tbr-pro
```
*Или напрямую через консоль:* `python main.py --headless --host 0.0.0.0 --port 5000`

### Вариант 3: Сборка из исходников
```bash
git clone https://github.com/kamushek0o/tbr.git
cd tbr
pip install -r requirements.txt
python main.py
```
*(Для самостоятельной компиляции в `.exe` запустите `build.bat`)*

### 📱 Виджет на Android
Забирайте готовый `.apk` из соседнего репозитория [tbr-widget Releases](https://github.com/kamushek0o/tbr-widget/releases). Откройте в веб-морде `⚙️ Настройки` -> `📱 QR-код`, наведите камеру телефона, и виджет подхватит хост и Viewer-токен без ручной возни.

================================================================================
FILE: build.bat
================================================================================
@echo off
chcp 65001 >nul
title Сборка tbr PRO (Self-Healing PyInstaller)

set "ROOT=%~dp0"
if "%ROOT:~-1%"=="\" set "ROOT=%ROOT:~0,-1%"

set "ENTRY=%ROOT%\main.py"
set "DATA_TPL=%ROOT%\web\templates;web\templates"
set "DATA_STC=%ROOT%\web\static;web\static"

echo ==================================================
echo            СБОРКА tbr PRO (PyInstaller)
echo ==================================================
echo.
echo [1] Только ONEFILE  (-^> dist\onefile\tbr.exe)
echo [2] Только ONEDIR   (-^> dist\onedir\tbr\tbr.exe)
echo [3] ОБА ВАРИАНТА    (-^> разложит по разным папкам)
echo.
set /p mode="Выберите режим [1, 2 или 3]: "

if "%mode%"=="1" goto prep
if "%mode%"=="2" goto prep
if "%mode%"=="3" goto prep

echo.
echo ❌ Неверный выбор! Введите 1, 2 или 3.
pause
exit /b 1

:prep
echo.
echo [🔧] Проверка среды и снятие блокировок...

taskkill /F /IM tbr.exe >nul 2>&1
timeout /t 1 /nobreak >nul

python --version >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo ❌ Python не найден в системе! Установи Python 3.10+ с галочкой Add to PATH.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

python -c "import PyInstaller, flask, pystray, PIL, waitress" >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [📦] Доустанавливаю библиотеки из requirements.txt и PyInstaller...
    python -m pip install --upgrade pip
    if exist "%ROOT%\requirements.txt" (
        python -m pip install -r "%ROOT%\requirements.txt" pyinstaller
    ) else (
        python -m pip install flask pystray pillow waitress pyinstaller
    )
    if errorlevel 1 (
        echo ❌ Не удалось установить зависимости!
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
)

if "%mode%"=="2" goto build_onedir
goto build_onefile

:build_onefile
echo.
echo [1/2] Очистка и сборка ONEFILE в dist\onefile\...
if exist "%ROOT%\dist\onefile" rd /s /q "%ROOT%\dist\onefile" >nul 2>&1
if exist "%ROOT%\dist\onefile" (
    echo ❌ Папка dist\onefile заблокирована! Закрой её в Проводнике Windows и повтори.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)
if exist "%ROOT%\build\onefile" rd /s /q "%ROOT%\build\onefile" >nul 2>&1
mkdir "%ROOT%\build\onefile"

python -m PyInstaller --noconfirm --clean --onefile --windowed --name "tbr" ^
    --hidden-import "waitress" ^
    --distpath "%ROOT%\dist\onefile" ^
    --workpath "%ROOT%\build\onefile" ^
    --specpath "%ROOT%\build\onefile" ^
    --add-data "%DATA_TPL%" ^
    --add-data "%DATA_STC%" "%ENTRY%"
if errorlevel 1 goto build_error

if "%mode%"=="3" goto build_onedir
goto done

:build_onedir
echo.
echo [2/2] Очистка и сборка ONEDIR в dist\onedir\...
if exist "%ROOT%\dist\onedir" rd /s /q "%ROOT%\dist\onedir" >nul 2>&1
if exist "%ROOT%\dist\onedir" (
    echo ❌ Папка dist\onedir заблокирована! Закрой её в Проводнике Windows и повтори.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)
if exist "%ROOT%\build\onedir" rd /s /q "%ROOT%\build\onedir" >nul 2>&1
mkdir "%ROOT%\build\onedir"

python -m PyInstaller --noconfirm --clean --onedir --windowed --name "tbr" ^
    --hidden-import "waitress" ^
    --distpath "%ROOT%\dist\onedir" ^
    --workpath "%ROOT%\build\onedir" ^
    --specpath "%ROOT%\build\onedir" ^
    --add-data "%DATA_TPL%" ^
    --add-data "%DATA_STC%" "%ENTRY%"
if errorlevel 1 goto build_error
goto done

:done
echo.
echo ==================================================
echo ✅ Сборка успешно завершена!
if not "%mode%"=="2" echo    OneFile: dist\onefile\tbr.exe
if not "%mode%"=="1" echo    OneDir:  dist\onedir\tbr\tbr.exe
echo ==================================================
pause
exit /b 0

:build_error
echo.
echo ❌ Произошла ошибка при компиляции!
pause
exit /b 1

    {'code': 'veteran_100d', 'icon': '🎖️', 'title': 'Ветеран раздачи', 'title_en': '100-Day Veteran', 'desc': 'Держать раздачу в активе 100+ дней', 'desc_en': 'Keep a torrent active for 100+ days'},
    {'code': 'keeper_5', 'icon': '🛡️', 'title': 'Хранитель', 'title_en': 'The Keeper', 'desc': 'Быть единственным сидом на 5+ раздачах', 'desc_en': 'Be the only seeder on 5+ torrents'},
    {'code': 'night_shift_500g', 'icon': '🌙', 'title': 'Ночная смена', 'title_en': 'Night Shift', 'desc': 'Отдать 150+ GiB за одну ночь (00:00–08:00)', 'desc_en': 'Upload 150+ GiB in one night (00:00–08:00)'},
    {'code': 'ratio_god', 'icon': '🔥', 'title': 'Бог Ratio', 'title_en': 'Ratio God', 'desc': 'Раздача >= 100 GiB с Ratio >= 10.0x', 'desc_en': 'Torrent >= 100 GiB with Ratio >= 10.0x'},
    {'code': 'collector_100', 'icon': '📦', 'title': 'Коллекционер', 'title_en': 'Hoarder', 'desc': 'Держать в клиенте 100+ раздач одновременно', 'desc_en': 'Seed 100+ torrents simultaneously'},
    {'code': 'marathon_1tb', 'icon': '⚡', 'title': 'Суточный марафон', 'title_en': 'Daily Marathon', 'desc': 'Отдать 500+ GiB за одни сутки', 'desc_en': 'Upload 500+ GiB in a single day'},
    {'code': 'gigabit_marathon_1tb', 'icon': '🚀', 'title': 'Гигабитный марафон', 'title_en': 'Gigabit Marathon', 'desc': 'Бонус: отдать 1+ TiB за одни сутки', 'desc_en': 'Bonus: upload 1+ TiB in a single day', 'hidden': True},
    {'code': 'surgeon', 'icon': '🩺', 'title': 'Главный хирург', 'title_en': 'Chief Surgeon', 'desc': 'Провести хотя бы один мердж .fastresume', 'desc_en': 'Complete at least one .fastresume merge'},
]

_LAST_EVAL_TS = 0.0


def _get_unlocked_map():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("SELECT code, unlocked_at, meta_json FROM achievements").fetchall()
                return {r['code']: {'unlocked_at': r['unlocked_at'], 'meta_json': r['meta_json']} for r in rows}
        except Exception:
            return {}


def _unlock(code, meta=None):
    now = time.time()
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO achievements (code, unlocked_at, meta_json) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                    (code, now, json.dumps(meta or {}, ensure_ascii=False))
                )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log(f"unlock achievement {code}", e)


def _check_night_and_day_records():
    rows = summary_tracker.db.get_snapshots_since(time.time() - 60 * 86400)
    night_by_date, day_by_date, prev = {}, {}, None
    for r in rows:
        if prev is not None:
            d = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev['total_uploaded']))
            dt = float(r['timestamp']) - float(prev['timestamp'])
            if dt >= 60 and d > 0 and (d / dt) < (1024 ** 3):
                dt_obj = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(r['timestamp'])
                ds = dt_obj.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
                day_by_date[ds] = day_by_date.get(ds, 0) + d
                if 0 <= dt_obj.hour < 8:
                    night_by_date[ds] = night_by_date.get(ds, 0) + d
        prev = r
    max_night = max(night_by_date.values(), default=0)
    max_day = max(day_by_date.values(), default=0)
    return max_night >= (150 * 1024 ** 3), max_day >= (500 * 1024 ** 3), max_day >= (1024 ** 4)


def evaluate_achievements(force=False):
    global _LAST_EVAL_TS
    now = time.time()
    if not force and (now - _LAST_EVAL_TS) < 3600:
        return
    _LAST_EVAL_TS = now

    unlocked = _get_unlocked_map()
    if len(unlocked) == len(ACHIEVEMENTS_CATALOG):
        return

    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in live.items()}

    tot_up, max_act, last_seed_cnt, has_ratio_god = 0, 0, 0, False
    for s in summaries:
        hl = (s.get('hash_id') or '').lower()
        lv = live_lower.get(hl, {})
        up = int(lv.get('uploaded', s.get('uploaded', 0)) or 0)
        sz = int(lv.get('total_size', s.get('size', 1)) or 1)
        act = int(s.get('active_seconds', 0) or 0)
        tot_up += up
        if act > max_act:
            max_act = act
        if sz >= (100 * 1024 ** 3) and (up / sz) >= 10.0:
            has_ratio_god = True
        if lv and compute_health_metrics(lv).get('is_last_seed'):
            last_seed_cnt += 1

    checks = {
        'first_tb': tot_up >= (1024 ** 4),
        'ten_tb': tot_up >= (10 * 1024 ** 4),
        'fifty_tb': tot_up >= (50 * 1024 ** 4),
        'veteran_100d': max_act >= (100 * 86400),
        'keeper_5': last_seed_cnt >= 5,
        'ratio_god': has_ratio_god,
        'collector_100': len(summaries) >= 100,
    }

    if any(k not in unlocked for k in ('night_shift_500g', 'marathon_1tb', 'gigabit_marathon_1tb')):
        hit_night, hit_500g, hit_1tb = _check_night_and_day_records()
        checks['night_shift_500g'] = hit_night
        checks['marathon_1tb'] = hit_500g
        checks['gigabit_marathon_1tb'] = hit_1tb

    if 'surgeon' not in unlocked:
        checks['surgeon'] = len(app_state_db.get_audit_events(category='merger', limit=1)) > 0

    for code, ok in checks.items():
        if ok and code not in unlocked:
            _unlock(code)


def get_achievements_view():
    evaluate_achievements(force=False)
    unlocked = _get_unlocked_map()
    out = []
    for item in ACHIEVEMENTS_CATALOG:
        u = unlocked.get(item['code'])
        is_hidden_locked = bool(item.get('hidden')) and not u
        dt_str = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(u['unlocked_at']).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if u else None
        entry = dict(item)
        if is_hidden_locked:
            entry['icon'] = '🎁'
            entry['title'] = 'Секретная бонусная ачивка'
            entry['title_en'] = 'Hidden Bonus Achievement'
            entry['desc'] = 'Для обладателей гигабитных каналов'
            entry['desc_en'] = 'For gigabit seedbox owners'
        entry['unlocked'] = bool(u)
        entry['unlocked_at'] = dt_str
        out.append(entry)
    return {
        'unlocked_count': sum(1 for x in out if x['unlocked']),
        'total_count': len(out),
        'items': out,
    }

================================================================================
FILE: core/analytics_insights.py

# core/analytics_insights.py
import time
import datetime
import threading
import urllib.parse
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.qb_api import get_torrent_peers, get_transfer_info
from core.scheduler import MIN_DELTA_SEC
from core.widget_service import build_detailed_speed_series
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db

_CLIENTS_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_CLIENTS_CACHE = {'ts': 0.0, 'data': None}
_CLIENTS_TTL = 900.0

_INSIGHTS_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_INSIGHTS_CACHE = {}
_INSIGHTS_TTL = 300.0
_ISP_TIERS_MBPS = (10, 20, 25, 30, 50, 75, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300, 500, 1000)


def _ensure_records_table():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS records_state (
                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id = 1),
                        peak_speed_bps REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                        peak_speed_date TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '—',
                        peak_snapshot_id INTEGER,
                        updated_at REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
                    )
                """)
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("records_state init", e)


_ensure_records_table()


def invalidate_insights_cache():
    with _INSIGHTS_LOCK:
        _INSIGHTS_CACHE.clear()


def get_cached_insights(cache_key):
    now = time.time()
    with _INSIGHTS_LOCK:
        entry = _INSIGHTS_CACHE.get(cache_key)
        if entry and (now - entry['ts']) < _INSIGHTS_TTL:
            return dict(entry['data'])
    return None


def set_cached_insights(cache_key, data):
    with _INSIGHTS_LOCK:
        _INSIGHTS_CACHE[cache_key] = {'ts': time.time(), 'data': dict(data)}


def fetch_torrent_peers_safe(ip, port, t_hash):
    if not port or not t_hash:
        return []
    raw = get_torrent_peers(ip, port, t_hash)
    peers = raw.get('peers') or {}
    if not isinstance(peers, dict):
        return []
    out = []
    for p in peers.values():
        if not isinstance(p, dict):
            continue
        up = int(p.get('up_speed', 0) or 0)
        dl = int(p.get('dl_speed', 0) or 0)
        prog = round(float(p.get('progress', 0.0) or 0.0) * 100.0, 1)
        out.append({
            'client': str(p.get('client') or 'Unknown').strip() or 'Unknown',
            'progress': prog, 'up_speed': up, 'up_speed_fmt': f"{format_file_size(up)}/s",
            'dl_speed': dl, 'dl_speed_fmt': f"{format_file_size(dl)}/s",
            'flags': str(p.get('flags') or ''), 'country_code': str(p.get('country_code') or '').upper()[:4]
        })
    out.sort(key=lambda x: x['up_speed'], reverse=True)
    return out


def _classify_peer_client(raw_client):
    low = str(raw_client or '').lower()
    for key, label in (
        ('qbittorrent', 'qBittorrent'), ('transmission', 'Transmission'), ('utorrent', 'uTorrent'),
        ('µtorrent', 'uTorrent'), ('deluge', 'Deluge'), ('biglybt', 'BiglyBT'),
        ('aria2', 'Aria2'), ('tixati', 'Tixati'), ('vuze', 'Vuze'), ('azureus', 'Vuze'),
        ('bittorrent', 'BitTorrent'), ('libtorrent', 'libtorrent')
    ):
        if key in low:
            return label
    return 'Прочие'


def get_peer_clients_breakdown(ip, port, live, force=False):
    now = time.time()
    with _CLIENTS_LOCK:
        if not force and _CLIENTS_CACHE['data'] is not None and (now - _CLIENTS_CACHE['ts']) < _CLIENTS_TTL:
            return dict(_CLIENTS_CACHE['data'])
    if not port or not live:
        return {'labels': [], 'values': [], 'total_peers': 0, 'cached_at': int(now)}

    active_hashes = [
        h for h, inf in sorted(
            live.items(),
            key=lambda kv: int(kv[1].get('num_leechs', 0) or 0) + int(kv[1].get('num_seeds', 0) or 0),
            reverse=True
        )
        if int(inf.get('num_leechs', 0) or 0) + int(inf.get('num_seeds', 0) or 0) > 0
    ][:40]

    counts, total_peers = {}, 0
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
        for fut in as_completed([pool.submit(fetch_torrent_peers_safe, ip, port, h) for h in active_hashes]):
            try:
                for p in fut.result():
                    fam = _classify_peer_client(p['client'])
                    counts[fam] = counts.get(fam, 0) + 1
                    total_peers += 1
            except Exception:
                pass

    sorted_items = sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
    res = {'labels': [k for k, _ in sorted_items], 'values': [v for _, v in sorted_items], 'total_peers': total_peers, 'cached_at': int(now)}
    with _CLIENTS_LOCK:
        _CLIENTS_CACHE['ts'] = now
        _CLIENTS_CACHE['data'] = dict(res)
    return res


def get_tracker_analytics(db, summaries, live=None, period='7d', allowed_hashes=None):
    per = period if period in ('7d', '30d', 'all') else '7d'
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in (live or {}).items()}
    allowed = {h.lower() for h in allowed_hashes} if allowed_hashes is not None else None
    domain_by_hash, all_up_by_hash = {}, {}

    for s in summaries:
        hl = (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower()
        if not hl or (allowed is not None and hl not in allowed):
            continue
        lv = live_lower.get(hl, {})
        tr_url = str(lv.get('tracker') or '').strip() or (str(s['trackers'][0] or '').strip() if s.get('trackers') else '')
        host = urllib.parse.urlparse(tr_url).netloc.lower().split(':')[0] if tr_url else ''
        domain_by_hash[hl] = host or 'Без трекера / DHT'
        all_up_by_hash[hl] = int(lv.get('uploaded', s.get('uploaded', 0)) or 0)

    vol_map = all_up_by_hash if per == 'all' else db.get_torrents_positive_deltas(time.time() - (30 if per == '30d' else 7) * 86400, allowed_hashes=domain_by_hash.keys())
    by_domain = {}
    for hl, dom in domain_by_hash.items():
        val = int(vol_map.get(hl, 0) or 0)
        if val > 0:
            by_domain[dom] = by_domain.get(dom, 0) + val

    sorted_dom = sorted(by_domain.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
    top = sorted_dom[:9] + ([('Другие', sum(v for _, v in sorted_dom[9:]))] if len(sorted_dom) > 9 else [])
    return {'period': per, 'labels': [k for k, _ in top], 'values': [round(v / (1024 ** 3), 2) for _, v in top]}


def get_heatmap_365(db):
    since_ts = time.time() - (365 * 86400)
    day_bytes = {}
    with db._lock:
        try:
            with db._get_connection() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("""
                    WITH diffs AS (
                        SELECT substr(datetime, 1, 10) AS day,
                               MAX(0, total_uploaded - LAG(total_uploaded, 1, total_uploaded) OVER (ORDER BY timestamp ASC)) AS delta
                        FROM snapshots
                        WHERE COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 AND timestamp >= ? AND length(datetime) >= 10
                    )
                    SELECT day, SUM(delta) AS up_bytes FROM diffs GROUP BY day ORDER BY day ASC
                """, (since_ts,)).fetchall()
                for r in rows:
                    day_bytes[r['day']] = int(r['up_bytes'] or 0)
        except Exception:
            pass

    today = datetime.date.today()
    cells, max_val = [], max(day_bytes.values(), default=0)
    for offset in range(364, -1, -1):
        d = today - datetime.timedelta(days=offset)
        ds = d.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
        b = day_bytes.get(ds, 0)
        lvl = 0 if (b <= 0 or max_val <= 0) else (4 if (b / max_val) >= 0.75 else (3 if (b / max_val) >= 0.45 else (2 if (b / max_val) >= 0.15 else 1)))
        cells.append({'date': ds, 'weekday': d.weekday(), 'bytes': b, 'gb': round(b / (1024 ** 3), 2), 'fmt': format_file_size(b), 'level': lvl})
    return cells


def _sum_range_deltas(rows, start_ts, end_ts, max_physical_bps=None):
    total, prev = 0, None
    for r in rows:
        ts = float(r['timestamp'])
        if prev is not None and start_ts <= ts <= end_ts:
            dt = ts - float(prev['timestamp'])
            d = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev['total_uploaded']))
            if dt >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and d > 0:
                if max_physical_bps is None or (d / dt) <= max_physical_bps:
                    total += d
        prev = r
    return total


def _recompute_and_save_peak(db):
    max_mbps = max(10, cfg.getint('Max_Channel_Mbps', 1000))
    max_physical_bps = ((max_mbps * 1_000_000) / 8.0) * 1.05
    all_rows = db.get_snapshots_since(0)
    peak_bps, peak_date, peak_snap_id, prev_r = 0.0, '—', None, None

    for r in all_rows:
        if prev_r is not None:
            dt = float(r['timestamp']) - float(prev_r['timestamp'])
            d = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev_r['total_uploaded']))
            if dt >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and d > 0:
                bps = d / dt
                if bps <= max_physical_bps and bps > peak_bps:
                    peak_bps = bps
                    peak_snap_id = r.get('id')
                    dt_raw = str(r.get('datetime') or '').replace('|', '').strip()
                    peak_date = dt_raw[:16] if dt_raw else '—'
        prev_r = r

    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute(
                    """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO records_state
                       (id, peak_speed_bps, peak_speed_date, peak_snapshot_id, updated_at)
                       VALUES (1, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                    (peak_bps, peak_date, peak_snap_id, time.time())
                )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("save records_state", e)
    return peak_bps, peak_date, peak_snap_id


def get_records_and_comparisons(db, heatmap_cells=None, force_recalc=False):
    now_dt = datetime.datetime.now()
    now_ts = now_dt.timestamp()
    today_start = now_dt.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
    yest_start, yest_same_end = today_start - 86400, now_ts - 86400
    week_start = (now_dt - datetime.timedelta(days=now_dt.weekday())).replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
    prev_week_start, prev_week_same_end = week_start - (7 * 86400), now_ts - (7 * 86400)

    max_mbps = max(10, cfg.getint('Max_Channel_Mbps', 1000))
    max_physical_bps = ((max_mbps * 1_000_000) / 8.0) * 1.05

    _, min_ts = db.get_snapshot_range_info()
    rows_15d = db.get_snapshots_since(prev_week_start - 3600)
    today_b = _sum_range_deltas(rows_15d, today_start, now_ts, max_physical_bps)
    yest_b = _sum_range_deltas(rows_15d, yest_start, yest_same_end, max_physical_bps)
    this_w_b = _sum_range_deltas(rows_15d, week_start, now_ts, max_physical_bps)
    prev_w_b = _sum_range_deltas(rows_15d, prev_week_start, prev_week_same_end, max_physical_bps)

    day_pct = round(((today_b - yest_b) / yest_b) * 100.0, 1) if (min_ts and min_ts <= yest_start + 3600 and yest_b > 0) else None
    week_pct = round(((this_w_b - prev_w_b) / prev_w_b) * 100.0, 1) if (min_ts and min_ts <= prev_week_start + 3600 and prev_w_b > 0) else None

    if force_recalc:
        peak_bps, peak_date, peak_snap_id = _recompute_and_save_peak(db)
    else:
        saved = None
        with app_state_db._lock:
            try:
                with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    saved = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM records_state WHERE id = 1").fetchone()
            except Exception:
                pass
        if saved:
            peak_bps = float(saved['peak_speed_bps'] or 0.0)
            peak_date = str(saved['peak_speed_date'] or '—')
            peak_snap_id = saved['peak_snapshot_id']
        else:
            peak_bps, peak_date, peak_snap_id = _recompute_and_save_peak(db)

    cells = heatmap_cells if heatmap_cells is not None else get_heatmap_365(db)
    best_cell = max(cells, key=lambda c: c['bytes'], default=None)

    return {
        'today_fmt': format_file_size(today_b),
        'yesterday_fmt': format_file_size(yest_b) if (min_ts and min_ts <= yest_start + 3600) else '—',
        'day_pct': day_pct,
        'this_week_fmt': format_file_size(this_w_b),
        'prev_week_fmt': format_file_size(prev_w_b) if (min_ts and min_ts <= prev_week_start + 3600) else '—',
        'week_pct': week_pct,
        'peak_speed_fmt': f"{format_file_size(peak_bps)}/s" if peak_bps > 0 else '—',
        'peak_speed_date': peak_date,
        'peak_snapshot_id': peak_snap_id,
        'best_day_fmt': best_cell['fmt'] if (best_cell and best_cell['bytes'] > 0) else '—',
        'best_day_date': best_cell['date'] if (best_cell and best_cell['bytes'] > 0) else '—',
    }


def detect_isp_throttling(db, ip, port):
    if port:
        tr = get_transfer_info(ip, port)
        if int(tr.get('up_rate_limit') or 0) > 0:
            return {'throttled': False, 'reason': 'qb_limit_set'}
    pts = build_detailed_speed_series(db, hours=48)
    active = sorted([p['speed_mbs'] for p in pts if p['speed_mbs'] >= 1.0], reverse=True)
    if len(active) < 8:
        return {'throttled': False}
    top_slice = active[:6]
    hi, lo = top_slice[0], top_slice[-1]
    if hi < 1.0 or ((hi - lo) / hi) > 0.08:
        return {'throttled': False}
    avg_mbs = sum(top_slice) / len(top_slice)
    avg_mbps = (avg_mbs * 1024 * 1024 * 8) / 1_000_000.0
    for tier in _ISP_TIERS_MBPS:
        if abs(avg_mbps - tier) / tier <= 0.12:
            return {'throttled': True, 'tier_mbps': tier, 'plateau_mbs': round(avg_mbs, 2)}
    return {'throttled': False}

================================================================================
FILE: core/app_state_db.py
================================================================================
# core/app_state_db.py
import os
import json
import time
import sqlite3
import threading
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, write_error_log


class AppStateDB:
    def __init__(self):
        self.db_file = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "app_state.db")
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._init_db()

    def _get_conn(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_file, timeout=30.0)
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 30000;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;")
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        return conn

    def _init_db(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.executescript("""
                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_log (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL,
                            category TEXT NOT NULL,
                            actor TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',
                            action TEXT NOT NULL,
                            details TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
                        );
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_cat_ts ON audit_log(category, created_at DESC);

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS smart_freeze (
                            hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            frozen_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS snooze_timers (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            resume_at REAL NOT NULL,
                            mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pause',
                            active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS expiration_timers (
                            hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            expires_at REAL,
                            target_uploaded INTEGER,
                            delete_files INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dnd_queue (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            title TEXT NOT NULL,
                            lines_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS torrent_targets (
                            hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            target_ratio REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            target_bytes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            achieved_at REAL,
                            updated_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS private_vault_tags (
                            tag TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS achievements (
                            code TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            unlocked_at REAL NOT NULL,
                            meta_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS watchdog_state (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id = 1),
                            consecutive_restarts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            down_since REAL,
                            last_restart_at REAL,
                            halted INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            recheck_attempts_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS merger_history (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            source_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
                            target_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
                            mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'add',
                            source_uploaded INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            target_uploaded INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            final_uploaded INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_merger_pair ON merger_history(source_hash, target_hash);

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS torrent_lineage (
                            torrent_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            torrent_uid TEXT NOT NULL,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_lineage_uid ON torrent_lineage(torrent_uid);
                    """)
                    conn.execute(
                        """INSERT OR IGNORE INTO watchdog_state
                           (id, consecutive_restarts, down_since, last_restart_at, halted, recheck_attempts_json)
                           VALUES (1, 0, NULL, NULL, 0, '{}')"""
                    )
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Инициализация app_state.db", e)

    def add_audit_event(self, category, action, details="", actor="system", max_rows=1000):
        cat = str(category or "system").strip().lower()
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute(
                        "INSERT INTO audit_log (created_at, category, actor, action, details) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                        (time.time(), cat, str(actor or "system")[:64], str(action)[:160], str(details or "")[:1000])
                    )
                    conn.execute(
                        """DELETE FROM audit_log WHERE id NOT IN (
                               SELECT id FROM audit_log ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?
                           )""",
                        (max(100, int(max_rows)),)
                    )
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка add_audit_event", e)

    def get_audit_events(self, category="", limit=100, offset=0):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    if category:
                        cur.execute(
                            "SELECT * FROM audit_log WHERE category = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?",
                            (category.strip().lower(), int(limit), int(offset))
                        )
                    else:
                        cur.execute(
                            "SELECT * FROM audit_log ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?",
                            (int(limit), int(offset))
                        )
                    return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
            except Exception:
                return []

    def get_watchdog_state(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM watchdog_state WHERE id = 1").fetchone()
                    if row:
                        return dict(row)
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_watchdog_state", e)
            return {
                "id": 1, "consecutive_restarts": 0, "down_since": None,
                "last_restart_at": None, "halted": 0, "recheck_attempts_json": "{}"
            }

    def update_watchdog_state(self, **fields):
        allowed = {"consecutive_restarts", "down_since", "last_restart_at", "halted", "recheck_attempts_json"}
        updates = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if not updates:
            return
        with self._lock:
            try:
                set_clause = ", ".join(f"{k} = ?" for k in updates.keys())
                params = list(updates.values())
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute(f"UPDATE watchdog_state SET {set_clause} WHERE id = 1", params)
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка update_watchdog_state", e)

    def get_recheck_attempts(self):
        st = self.get_watchdog_state()
        try:
            data = json.loads(st.get("recheck_attempts_json") or "{}")
            return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
        except Exception:
            return {}

    def save_recheck_attempts(self, attempts_map):
        clean = {str(k).lower(): float(v) for k, v in (attempts_map or {}).items()}
        self.update_watchdog_state(recheck_attempts_json=json.dumps(clean))

    def add_merger_record(self, src_hash, dst_hash, mode, src_up, dst_up, final_up):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute(
                        """INSERT INTO merger_history
                           (source_hash, target_hash, mode, source_uploaded, target_uploaded, final_uploaded, created_at)
                           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                        (src_hash.lower(), dst_hash.lower(), str(mode), int(src_up), int(dst_up), int(final_up), time.time())
                    )
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("add_merger_record", e)

    def get_merger_history(self, src_hash, dst_hash):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    rows = conn.execute(
                        """SELECT * FROM merger_history
                           WHERE source_hash = ? AND target_hash = ?
                           ORDER BY id DESC""",
                        (src_hash.lower(), dst_hash.lower())
                    ).fetchall()
                    return [dict(r) for r in rows]
            except Exception:
                return []

    def get_merges_for_targets(self, hashes):
        clean = [str(h).strip().lower() for h in (hashes or []) if h]
        if not clean:
            return []
        placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in clean)
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    rows = conn.execute(
                        f"SELECT * FROM merger_history WHERE target_hash IN ({placeholders}) ORDER BY created_at ASC",
                        clean
                    ).fetchall()
                    return [dict(r) for r in rows]
            except Exception:
                return []

    def link_torrent_lineage(self, src_hash, dst_hash):
        sh = str(src_hash or "").strip().lower()
        dh = str(dst_hash or "").strip().lower()
        if not sh or not dh or sh == dh:
            return None
        now = time.time()
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    r_src = conn.execute("SELECT torrent_uid FROM torrent_lineage WHERE torrent_hash = ?", (sh,)).fetchone()
                    r_dst = conn.execute("SELECT torrent_uid FROM torrent_lineage WHERE torrent_hash = ?", (dh,)).fetchone()
                    uid = r_src["torrent_uid"] if r_src else sh
                    if r_dst and r_dst["torrent_uid"] != uid:
                        conn.execute("UPDATE torrent_lineage SET torrent_uid = ? WHERE torrent_uid = ?", (uid, r_dst["torrent_uid"]))
                    conn.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO torrent_lineage (torrent_hash, torrent_uid, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (sh, uid, now))
                    conn.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO torrent_lineage (torrent_hash, torrent_uid, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (dh, uid, now))
                    conn.commit()
                    return uid
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("link_torrent_lineage", e)
                return None

    def get_lineage_hashes(self, t_hash):
        hl = str(t_hash or "").strip().lower()
        if not hl:
            return []
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    row = conn.execute("SELECT torrent_uid FROM torrent_lineage WHERE torrent_hash = ?", (hl,)).fetchone()
                    if not row:
                        return [hl]
                    rows = conn.execute("SELECT torrent_hash FROM torrent_lineage WHERE torrent_uid = ?", (row["torrent_uid"],)).fetchall()
                    hashes = [r["torrent_hash"] for r in rows]
                    return hashes if hashes else [hl]
            except Exception:
                return [hl]


app_state_db = AppStateDB()

# core/automation.py
import os
import time
import sqlite3
import threading
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, format_file_size, write_log, write_error_log
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, check_qb_api_alive, get_live_torrent_info,
    send_qb_command, add_torrent_tags, remove_torrent_tags
)
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.notifiers import notify_all, test_notifications
from core.system_alerts import system_alerts
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.keep_awake import keep_awake
from core.reliability import run_auto_recheck, run_watchdog
from core.disaster_recovery import maybe_run_weekly_manifest
from core.smart_timers import tick_snooze, tick_expirations
from core.smart_tagging import run_smart_tagging
from core.dnd_digest import flush_dnd_if_ready, maybe_send_digest
from core.torrent_targets import evaluate_targets
from core.achievements import evaluate_achievements
from core.autopilot_extras import tick_autopilot_extras
from core.hdd_smart_queue import tick_recheck_queue
from core.lineage_notes_events import record_torrent_event

VALID_TIME_MODES = ('seeding_time', 'time_active')
VALID_SPEED_OPS = ('', '>=', '<')


def format_duration_ru(seconds):
    sec = max(0, int(seconds or 0))
    if sec >= 86400:
        return f"{sec // 86400} д {(sec % 86400) // 3600} ч"
    if sec >= 3600:
        return f"{sec // 3600} ч"
    return f"{sec // 60} мин"


class AutomationEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.db_file = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "automation.db")
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._running = False
        self._thread = None
        self._init_db()

    def _get_conn(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_file, timeout=30.0)
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 30000;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;")
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        return conn

    def _init_db(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.executescript("""
                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rules (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            name TEXT NOT NULL, enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
                            time_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'seeding_time',
                            min_ratio REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, min_seed_days REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            min_uploaded_bytes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            speed_op TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', speed_bytes REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            speed_window_min INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 60,
                            action_pause INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, action_tag TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                            action_remove_tag TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', notify INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
                        );
                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notified_events (
                            rule_id INTEGER NOT NULL, hash_id TEXT NOT NULL, triggered_at REAL NOT NULL,
                            PRIMARY KEY (rule_id, hash_id), FOREIGN KEY (rule_id) REFERENCES rules(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
                        );
                    """)
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("PRAGMA table_info(rules)")
                    cols = {r['name'] for r in cur.fetchall()}
                    for col_name, col_def in [
                        ("time_mode", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'seeding_time'"),
                        ("min_uploaded_bytes", "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"),
                        ("speed_op", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''"),
                        ("speed_bytes", "REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"),
                        ("speed_window_min", "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 60"),
                        ("action_remove_tag", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''")
                    ]:
                        if col_name not in cols:
                            cur.execute(f"ALTER TABLE rules ADD COLUMN {col_name} {col_def}")
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Инициализация automation.db", e)

    def get_rules(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    return [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM rules ORDER BY id DESC").fetchall()]
            except Exception:
                return []

    def add_rule(self, name, min_ratio, min_seed_days, action_pause, action_tag, notify,
                 time_mode='seeding_time', min_uploaded_bytes=0, speed_op='', speed_bytes=0.0,
                 speed_window_min=60, action_remove_tag=''):
        t_mode = time_mode if time_mode in VALID_TIME_MODES else 'seeding_time'
        s_op = speed_op if speed_op in VALID_SPEED_OPS else ''
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute(
                        """INSERT INTO rules (name, enabled, time_mode, min_ratio, min_seed_days, min_uploaded_bytes,
                               speed_op, speed_bytes, speed_window_min, action_pause, action_tag, action_remove_tag, notify)
                           VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                        (name, t_mode, float(min_ratio), float(min_seed_days), int(min_uploaded_bytes),
                         s_op, float(speed_bytes), max(1, int(speed_window_min)),
                         1 if action_pause else 0, action_tag.strip(), action_remove_tag.strip(), 1 if notify else 0)
                    )
                    conn.commit()
                    return cur.lastrowid
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка add_rule", e)
                return None

    def update_rule(self, rule_id, name, min_ratio, min_seed_days, action_pause, action_tag, notify,
                    time_mode='seeding_time', min_uploaded_bytes=0, speed_op='', speed_bytes=0.0,
                    speed_window_min=60, action_remove_tag=''):
        t_mode = time_mode if time_mode in VALID_TIME_MODES else 'seeding_time'
        s_op = speed_op if speed_op in VALID_SPEED_OPS else ''
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute(
                        """UPDATE rules SET name = ?, time_mode = ?, min_ratio = ?, min_seed_days = ?, min_uploaded_bytes = ?,
                               speed_op = ?, speed_bytes = ?, speed_window_min = ?,
                               action_pause = ?, action_tag = ?, action_remove_tag = ?, notify = ? WHERE id = ?""",
                        (name, t_mode, float(min_ratio), float(min_seed_days), int(min_uploaded_bytes),
                         s_op, float(speed_bytes), max(1, int(speed_window_min)),
                         1 if action_pause else 0, action_tag.strip(), action_remove_tag.strip(), 1 if notify else 0, int(rule_id))
                    )
                    updated = cur.rowcount > 0
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM notified_events WHERE rule_id = ?", (int(rule_id),))
                    conn.commit()
                    return updated
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка update_rule", e)
                return False

    def toggle_rule(self, rule_id):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("UPDATE rules SET enabled = 1 - enabled WHERE id = ?", (int(rule_id),))
                    conn.commit()
                    return True
            except Exception:
                return False

    def delete_rule(self, rule_id):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("DELETE FROM rules WHERE id = ?", (int(rule_id),))
                    conn.commit()
                    return True
            except Exception:
                return False

    def _is_triggered(self, rule_id, t_hash):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    return conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM notified_events WHERE rule_id = ? AND hash_id = ?", (rule_id, t_hash)).fetchone() is not None
            except Exception:
                return False

    def _mark_triggered(self, rule_id, t_hash):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO notified_events (rule_id, hash_id, triggered_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (rule_id, t_hash, time.time()))
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _evaluate_rule_match(self, r, t_hash, info, now_ts, ignore_history=False):
        t_mode = r.get('time_mode') if r.get('time_mode') in VALID_TIME_MODES else 'seeding_time'
        min_r, min_d = float(r.get('min_ratio', 0) or 0), float(r.get('min_seed_days', 0) or 0)
        min_up = int(r.get('min_uploaded_bytes', 0) or 0)
        s_op, s_bytes = r.get('speed_op', '') or '', float(r.get('speed_bytes', 0) or 0)
        w_sec = max(60, int(r.get('speed_window_min', 60) or 60) * 60)
        use_speed = (s_op in ('>=', '<') and s_bytes > 0)
        if min_r <= 0 and min_d <= 0 and min_up <= 0 and not use_speed:
            return None

        ratio, uploaded = float(info.get('ratio', 0) or 0), int(info.get('uploaded', 0) or 0)
        time_sec = float(info.get(t_mode, 0) or 0)
        if (min_r > 0 and ratio < min_r) or (min_d > 0 and (time_sec / 86400.0) < min_d) or (min_up > 0 and uploaded < min_up):
            return None

        v_avg = None
        if use_speed:
            first, last, cnt = summary_tracker.db.get_torrent_window_bounds(t_hash, now_ts - w_sec, now_ts)
            if cnt < 2 or not first or not last or (last[0] - first[0]) < (0.5 * w_sec):
                return None
            v_avg = max(0.0, (last[1] - first[1]) / (last[0] - first[0]))
            if (s_op == '>=' and v_avg < s_bytes) or (s_op == '<' and v_avg >= s_bytes):
                return None

        if not ignore_history and self._is_triggered(r['id'], t_hash):
            return None

        cur_tags = {tg.strip().lower() for tg in str(info.get('tags', '') or '').split(',') if tg.strip()}
        add_tag, rem_tag = str(r.get('action_tag', '') or '').strip(), str(r.get('action_remove_tag', '') or '').strip()
        want_pause = bool(r.get('action_pause'))
        planned = []
        if want_pause:
            planned.append("⏸️ Пауза")
        if add_tag and add_tag.lower() not in cur_tags:
            planned.append(f"🏷️ +Тег: {add_tag}")
        if rem_tag and rem_tag.lower() in cur_tags:
            planned.append(f"✂️ -Тег: {rem_tag}")

        return {
            'rule_id': r['id'], 'rule_name': r['name'], 'hash': t_hash, 'name': info.get('name', t_hash),
            'ratio': ratio, 'uploaded': uploaded, 'uploaded_fmt': format_file_size(uploaded),
            'time_mode': t_mode, 'time_sec': time_sec, 'dur_fmt': format_duration_ru(time_sec),
            'v_avg': v_avg, 'want_pause': want_pause,
            'add_tag': add_tag if (add_tag and add_tag.lower() not in cur_tags) else '',
            'rem_tag': rem_tag if (rem_tag and rem_tag.lower() in cur_tags) else '',
            'planned': planned, 'already_triggered': self._is_triggered(r['id'], t_hash)
        }

    def dry_run(self, rule_id=None):
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        if not check_qb_api_alive(ip, port):
            return {'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен', 'matches': []}
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port)
        rules = self.get_rules()
        rules = [r for r in rules if r['id'] == int(rule_id)] if rule_id is not None else [r for r in rules if r.get('enabled')]
        now_ts, matches = time.time(), []
        for r in rules:
            for t_hash, info in live.items():
                m = self._evaluate_rule_match(r, t_hash, info, now_ts, ignore_history=True)
                if m:
                    matches.append(m)
        return {'success': True, 'matches': matches, 'rules_checked': len(rules), 'torrents_scanned': len(live)}

    def test_notifications(self):
        return test_notifications()

    def run_system_tick(self):
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        qb_up = check_qb_api_alive(ip, port) if port else False
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if qb_up else {}
        keep_awake.evaluate(live)
        system_alerts.check(qb_up, live=live)
        run_watchdog(qb_up)
        maybe_run_weekly_manifest()
        tick_snooze()
        flush_dnd_if_ready(notify_all)
        maybe_send_digest(notify_all)
        evaluate_achievements(force=False)
        if qb_up:
            try:
                from core.network_tools import check_network_interface_guard
                check_network_interface_guard()
            except Exception:
                pass
            evaluate_targets(live)
            tick_autopilot_extras(ip, port, live)
            tick_recheck_queue(ip, port, live)
        if qb_up and time.time() >= cfg.getint('Autopilot_Frozen_Until', 0):
            tick_expirations(ip, port, live)
            run_smart_tagging(ip, port, live, get_all_torrents(use_cache=True))
        return ip, port, live

    def run_cycle(self):
        ip, port, live = self.run_system_tick()
        if not live or time.time() < cfg.getint('Autopilot_Frozen_Until', 0):
            return 0
        now_ts = time.time()
        run_auto_recheck(ip, port, live)
        rules = [r for r in self.get_rules() if r.get('enabled')]
        if not rules:
            return 0
        triggered_count = 0
        for r in rules:
            for t_hash, info in live.items():
                m = self._evaluate_rule_match(r, t_hash, info, now_ts, ignore_history=False)
                if not m:
                    continue
                actions_done = []
                if m['want_pause'] and send_qb_command(ip, port, 'stop', t_hash):
                    actions_done.append("⏸️ Поставлено на паузу")
                if m['add_tag'] and add_torrent_tags(ip, port, t_hash, m['add_tag']):
                    actions_done.append(f"🏷️ +Тег: {m['add_tag']}")
                if m['rem_tag'] and remove_torrent_tags(ip, port, t_hash, m['rem_tag']):
                    actions_done.append(f"✂️ -Тег: {m['rem_tag']}")
                if not m['want_pause'] and (r.get('action_tag') or r.get('action_remove_tag')) and not actions_done:
                    self._mark_triggered(r['id'], t_hash)
                    continue
                self._mark_triggered(r['id'], t_hash)
                triggered_count += 1
                time_lbl = "Сид" if m['time_mode'] == 'seeding_time' else "Актив"
                write_log('automation', f"Правило '{r['name']}' -> '{m['name']}' (Ratio: {m['ratio']:.2f}, {time_lbl}: {m['dur_fmt']})")
                app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', f"Правило: {r['name']}", m['name'])
                record_torrent_event(t_hash, 'autopilot', f"Правило '{r['name']}': {', '.join(actions_done) or 'сработало'}")
                if r.get('notify'):
                    stat_line = f"Отдано: {m['uploaded_fmt']} | Ratio: {m['ratio']:.2f}x | {time_lbl}: {m['dur_fmt']}"
                    if m['v_avg'] is not None:
                        stat_line += f" | Ср. скор.: {format_file_size(m['v_avg'])}/s"
                    notify_all(f"🤖 Сработало правило: {r['name']}", [f"Раздача: {m['name']}", stat_line] + (["Действия: " + ", ".join(actions_done)] if actions_done else []))
        return triggered_count

    def start(self):
        if self._running:
            return
        self._running = True

        def loop():
            time.sleep(8)
            last_auto_ts = 0.0
            while self._running:
                now = time.time()
                try:
                    auto_due = cfg.getboolean('Auto_Enabled', False) and (now - last_auto_ts) >= max(1, cfg.getint('Auto_Interval_Min', 5)) * 60
                    if auto_due:
                        self.run_cycle()
                        last_auto_ts = time.time()
                    else:
                        self.run_system_tick()
                except Exception as e:
                    write_error_log("AutomationEngine loop", e)
                for _ in range(30):
                    if not self._running:
                        return
                    time.sleep(1)

        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=loop, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()


automation_engine = AutomationEngine()

FILE: core/autopilot_extras.py

# core/autopilot_extras.py
import json
import time
import datetime
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.notifiers import notify_all
from core.disk_monitor import get_monitored_disk_info
from core.qb_api import _post_qb_api, set_global_speed_limits

_REANNOUNCE_COOLDOWN = {}  # { hash_lower: ts }


def _ensure_tables():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.executescript("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS download_snipers (
                        hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                        idle_hours REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 4.0,
                        last_progress REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                        last_progress_at REAL NOT NULL,
                        notified_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
                    );
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auto_router_rules (
                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                        priority INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 10,
                        match_field TEXT NOT NULL,
                        pattern TEXT NOT NULL,
                        target_category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                        target_path TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
                    );
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auto_router_seen (
                        hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                        routed_at REAL NOT NULL
                    );
                """)
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("autopilot_extras init", e)


_ensure_tables()


def toggle_sniper(t_hash, idle_hours=4.0):
    hl = str(t_hash or '').strip().lower()
    if not hl:
        return False, False
    with app_state_db._lock:
        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
            ex = conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM download_snipers WHERE hash_id = ?", (hl,)).fetchone()
            if ex:
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM download_snipers WHERE hash_id = ?", (hl,))
                conn.commit()
                return True, False
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO download_snipers (hash_id, idle_hours, last_progress, last_progress_at, notified_state) VALUES (?, ?, 0, ?, '')",
                (hl, max(0.5, float(idle_hours)), time.time())
            )
            conn.commit()
            return True, True


def list_snipers():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
            return [r['hash_id'] for r in conn.execute("SELECT hash_id FROM download_snipers").fetchall()]


def _tick_snipers(live):
    now = time.time()
    with app_state_db._lock:
        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
            rows = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM download_snipers").fetchall()]
    if not rows:
        return

    live_map = {h.lower(): info for h, info in live.items()}
    free_b = int(get_monitored_disk_info(live).get('free_bytes') or 0)

    for r in rows:
        hl = r['hash_id']
        info = live_map.get(hl)
        if not info:
            continue
        prog = float(info.get('progress', 0.0) or 0.0)
        nm = str(info.get('name') or hl)
        if prog >= 0.9999:
            notify_all("🎯 Закачка завершена!", [f"Раздача: {nm}"], critical=True)
            with app_state_db._lock:
                with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("DELETE FROM download_snipers WHERE hash_id = ?", (hl,))
                    conn.commit()
            continue

        if prog > float(r['last_progress'] or 0) + 0.001:
            with app_state_db._lock:
                with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("UPDATE download_snipers SET last_progress = ?, last_progress_at = ?, notified_state = '' WHERE hash_id = ?", (prog, now, hl))
                    conn.commit()
            continue

        left_b = int(info.get('amount_left', 0) or 0)
        if free_b > 0 and left_b > free_b and r['notified_state'] != 'no_space':
            notify_all("⚠️ Недостаточно места для финиша закачки", [f"Раздача: {nm}", f"Нужно: {format_file_size(left_b)}, свободно: {format_file_size(free_b)}"], critical=True)
            with app_state_db._lock:
                with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("UPDATE download_snipers SET notified_state = 'no_space' WHERE hash_id = ?", (hl,))
                    conn.commit()
        elif (now - float(r['last_progress_at'])) >= float(r['idle_hours']) * 3600 and r['notified_state'] != 'stalled':
            notify_all("⏳ Закачка зависла без прогресса", [f"Раздача: {nm}", f"Прогресс: {prog*100:.1f}% (простой > {r['idle_hours']}ч)"])
            with app_state_db._lock:
                with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("UPDATE download_snipers SET notified_state = 'stalled' WHERE hash_id = ?", (hl,))
                    conn.commit()


def get_conductor_config():
    try:
        blocks = json.loads(cfg.get('Conductor_Blocks_JSON', '[]') or '[]')
    except Exception:
        blocks = []
    return {
        'enabled': cfg.getboolean('Conductor_Enabled', False),
        'blocks': blocks if isinstance(blocks, list) else [],
        'override_until': cfg.getint('Conductor_Override_Until', 0)
    }


def _hour_in_block(hr, start_h, end_h):
    if start_h == end_h:
        return True
    if start_h < end_h:
        return start_h <= hr < end_h
    return hr >= start_h or hr < end_h


def _tick_conductor(ip, port):
    c = get_conductor_config()
    now = time.time()
    last_applied = cfg.getint('Conductor_Last_Applied_Up', -1)

    if not c['enabled'] or not c['blocks'] or now < c['override_until']:
        if last_applied > 0:
            set_global_speed_limits(ip, port, 0, 0)
            cfg.set('Conductor_Last_Applied_Up', '0')
            cfg.save()
        return

    hr = datetime.datetime.now().hour
    matched = None
    for b in c['blocks']:
        if not isinstance(b, dict):
            continue
        try:
            sh = int(b.get('start_h', 0)) % 24
            eh = int(b.get('end_h', 24))
            eh = 24 if eh == 24 else (eh % 24)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            continue
        if _hour_in_block(hr, sh, eh):
            matched = b
            break

    if not matched:
        if last_applied > 0:
            set_global_speed_limits(ip, port, 0, 0)
            cfg.set('Conductor_Last_Applied_Up', '0')
            cfg.save()
        return

    try:
        pct = max(0, min(100, int(matched.get('pct_limit', 100))))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        pct = 100
    max_mbps = max(10, cfg.getint('Max_Channel_Mbps', 1000))
    up_bps = 0 if pct >= 100 else max(1024, int(((max_mbps * 1_000_000) / 8.0) * (pct / 100.0)))
    if last_applied != up_bps:
        set_global_speed_limits(ip, port, 0, up_bps)
        cfg.set('Conductor_Last_Applied_Up', str(up_bps))
        cfg.save()


def _tick_auto_router(ip, port, live):
    with app_state_db._lock:
        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
            rules = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM auto_router_rules ORDER BY priority ASC, id ASC").fetchall()]
            if not rules:
                return
            seen = {r['hash_id'] for r in conn.execute("SELECT hash_id FROM auto_router_seen").fetchall()}

    now = time.time()
    new_seen = []
    for raw_h, info in live.items():
        hl = raw_h.lower()
        if hl in seen:
            continue
        new_seen.append((hl, now))
        tr = str(info.get('tracker') or '').lower()
        sz_gib = float(info.get('total_size', 0) or 0) / (1024 ** 3)
        nm = str(info.get('name') or '').lower()

        for r in rules:
            fld = r['match_field']
            pat = str(r['pattern'] or '').lower().strip()
            if not pat:
                continue
            hit = False
            if fld == 'tracker':
                hit = pat in tr
            elif fld == 'ext':
                hit = pat in nm
            elif fld == 'min_gib':
                try:
                    hit = sz_gib >= float(pat)
                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                    hit = False
            if hit:
                if r['target_category']:
                    _post_qb_api(ip, port, 'torrents/setCategory', {'hashes': raw_h, 'category': r['target_category']})
                if r['target_path']:
                    _post_qb_api(ip, port, 'torrents/setLocation', {'hashes': raw_h, 'location': r['target_path']})
                break

    if new_seen:
        with app_state_db._lock:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.executemany("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO auto_router_seen (hash_id, routed_at) VALUES (?, ?)", new_seen)
                conn.commit()


def _tick_smart_queue_and_reannounce(ip, port, live):
    now = time.time()
    if cfg.getboolean('Smart_Queue_Enabled', False):
        small_fast = [h for h, inf in live.items() if float(inf.get('progress', 0) or 0) < 0.99 and 0 < int(inf.get('total_size', 0) or 0) <= (2 * 1024 ** 3) and int(inf.get('num_seeds', 0) or 0) >= 3 and int(inf.get('priority', 0) or 0) > 1]
        if small_fast:
            _post_qb_api(ip, port, 'torrents/topPrio', {'hashes': '|'.join(small_fast[:5])})

    if cfg.getboolean('Smart_Reannounce_Enabled', False):
        for raw_h, inf in live.items():
            hl = raw_h.lower()
            st = str(inf.get('state', '')).lower()
            if st not in ('stalledup', 'stalleddl', 'uploading', 'downloading'):
                continue
            if int(inf.get('num_leechs', 0) or 0) == 0 and int(inf.get('num_seeds', 0) or 0) == 0 and int(inf.get('time_active', 0) or 0) >= 600:
                if (now - _REANNOUNCE_COOLDOWN.get(hl, 0)) >= 1800:
                    _REANNOUNCE_COOLDOWN[hl] = now
                    _post_qb_api(ip, port, 'torrents/reannounce', {'hashes': raw_h})
                    time.sleep(0.2)


def tick_autopilot_extras(ip, port, live):
    if not port or not live:
        return
    try:
        _tick_snipers(live)
        _tick_conductor(ip, port)
        _tick_auto_router(ip, port, live)
        _tick_smart_queue_and_reannounce(ip, port, live)
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("tick_autopilot_extras", e)
