# core/autostart.py
import os
import sys


def get_exe_or_script_command():
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        return f'"{sys.executable}"'
    else:
        main_script = os.path.abspath(sys.argv[0])
        return f'"{sys.executable}" "{main_script}"'


def set_autostart(enable=True):
    if os.name != 'nt':
        return False, "Автозагрузка через реестр доступна только на Windows"
    try:
        import winreg
        key = winreg.OpenKey(
            winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run",
            0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE | winreg.KEY_QUERY_VALUE
        )
        app_name = "tbr_swiss_knife"
        if enable:
            cmd = get_exe_or_script_command()
            winreg.SetValueEx(key, app_name, 0, winreg.REG_SZ, cmd)
        else:
            try:
                winreg.DeleteValue(key, app_name)
            except FileNotFoundError:
                pass
        winreg.CloseKey(key)
        return True, "Автозагрузка включена" if enable else "Автозагрузка отключена"
    except Exception as e:
        return False, str(e)


def is_autostart_enabled():
    if os.name != 'nt':
        return False
    try:
        import winreg
        key = winreg.OpenKey(
            winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run",
            0, winreg.KEY_READ
        )
        try:
            val, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, "tbr_swiss_knife")
            winreg.CloseKey(key)
            return bool(val)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            winreg.CloseKey(key)
            return False
    except Exception:
        return False

# core/backup_manager.py
import os
import io
import shutil
import sqlite3
import zipfile
import tempfile
import configparser
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, LOG_FOLDER
from utils.config import CONFIG_FILE

DB_TARGETS = [
    ('profiles.db', os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, 'profiles.db')),
    ('automation.db', os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, 'automation.db')),
    ('app_state.db', os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, 'app_state.db')),
    ('file_index.db', os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, 'file_index.db')),
    ('summary_history.db', os.path.join(LOG_FOLDER, 'auto_hourly', 'summary_history.db')),
]
PENDING_DIR = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, '_restore_pending')
SECRET_KEYS = ('Web_Password', 'WebUI_Password', 'TG_Bot_Token', 'Discord_Webhook')


def apply_pending_restore_if_any():
    """Вызывается при старте main.py ДО открытия любых БД."""
    if not os.path.isdir(PENDING_DIR):
        return
    try:
        for arc_name, dest_path in DB_TARGETS:
            src = os.path.join(PENDING_DIR, arc_name)
            if os.path.isfile(src):
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dest_path), exist_ok=True)
                for ext in ('-wal', '-shm'):
                    if os.path.exists(dest_path + ext):
                        try:
                            os.remove(dest_path + ext)
                        except OSError:
                            pass
                shutil.move(src, dest_path)
        cfg_src = os.path.join(PENDING_DIR, 'config.ini')
        if os.path.isfile(cfg_src):
            shutil.move(cfg_src, CONFIG_FILE)
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(PENDING_DIR, ignore_errors=True)


def create_backup_zip(exclude_history=False, strip_secrets=False):
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
            for arc_name, src_path in DB_TARGETS:
                if exclude_history and arc_name == 'summary_history.db':
                    continue
                if not os.path.isfile(src_path):
                    continue
                dst_tmp = os.path.join(tmpdir, arc_name)
                src_conn = sqlite3.connect(src_path, timeout=30.0)
                dst_conn = sqlite3.connect(dst_tmp)
                try:
                    src_conn.backup(dst_conn)
                finally:
                    dst_conn.close()
                    src_conn.close()
                zf.write(dst_tmp, arcname=arc_name)

            if os.path.isfile(CONFIG_FILE):
                if strip_secrets:
                    cp = configparser.ConfigParser()
                    cp.read(CONFIG_FILE, encoding='utf-8')
                    if 'Settings' in cp:
                        for k in SECRET_KEYS:
                            if k in cp['Settings']:
                                cp['Settings'][k] = ''
                    s_buf = io.StringIO()
                    cp.write(s_buf)
                    zf.writestr('config.ini', s_buf.getvalue().encode('utf-8'))
                else:
                    zf.write(CONFIG_FILE, arcname='config.ini')

    return buf.getvalue()


def stage_restore_zip(file_bytes):
    allowed = {name for name, _ in DB_TARGETS} | {'config.ini'}
    os.makedirs(PENDING_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    extracted = []
    try:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(file_bytes), 'r') as zf:
            for info in zf.infolist():
                base = os.path.basename(info.filename)
                if not base or base not in allowed or info.file_size > 512 * 1024 * 1024:
                    continue
                data = zf.read(info.filename)
                with open(os.path.join(PENDING_DIR, base), 'wb') as out:
                    out.write(data)
                extracted.append(base)
        if not extracted:
            shutil.rmtree(PENDING_DIR, ignore_errors=True)
            return False, "В архиве не найдено ни одного файла tbr PRO (.db / config.ini)"
        return True, f"Подготовлено к восстановлению ({', '.join(extracted)}). Перезапустите tbr PRO для применения."
    except zipfile.BadZipFile:
        return False, "Некорректный ZIP-архив"
    except Exception as e:
        return False, str(e)

# core/batch_relocate.py
import os
import time
import shutil
import threading
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, set_torrent_location
from core.disk_monitor import _get_mount_key

_WORKER_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_WORKER_RUNNING = False


def _ensure_table():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS batch_relocate_queue (
                        hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                        name TEXT NOT NULL,
                        old_path TEXT NOT NULL,
                        new_path TEXT NOT NULL,
                        size_bytes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                        status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
                        created_at REAL NOT NULL
                    )
                """)
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("batch_relocate_queue init", e)


_ensure_table()


def _norm_sep(p):
    return os.path.normpath(str(p or '').strip())


def preview_batch_relocate(old_prefix, new_prefix):
    op = _norm_sep(old_prefix)
    np = _norm_sep(new_prefix)
    if not op or not np or op == np:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'Укажите разные непустые шаблоны пути', 'items': []}

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    if not live:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен', 'items': []}

    items = []
    need_bytes = 0
    op_cmp = os.path.normcase(op)

    for h, info in live.items():
        sp = _norm_sep(info.get('save_path', ''))
        if not sp:
            continue
        sp_cmp = os.path.normcase(sp)
        if sp_cmp == op_cmp or sp_cmp.startswith(op_cmp + os.sep):
            suffix = sp[len(op):].lstrip('\\/')
            dst = os.path.join(np, suffix) if suffix else np
            sz = int(info.get('total_size', 0) or 0)
            if _get_mount_key(sp) != _get_mount_key(np if os.path.exists(np) else os.path.dirname(np)):
                need_bytes += sz
            items.append({'hash': h, 'name': info.get('name', h), 'old_path': sp, 'new_path': dst, 'size': sz, 'size_fmt': format_file_size(sz)})

    free_bytes = None
    probe = np if os.path.exists(np) else os.path.dirname(np)
    if probe and os.path.exists(probe):
        try:
            free_bytes = shutil.disk_usage(probe).free
        except Exception:
            pass

    space_ok = True if (free_bytes is None or need_bytes == 0) else (free_bytes > need_bytes + (512 * 1024 * 1024))
    return {
        'success': True, 'items': items, 'count': len(items),
        'need_fmt': format_file_size(need_bytes),
        'free_fmt': format_file_size(free_bytes) if free_bytes is not None else '—',
        'space_ok': space_ok
    }


def start_batch_relocate(old_prefix, new_prefix, actor='admin'):
    prev = preview_batch_relocate(old_prefix, new_prefix)
    if not prev.get('success'):
        return prev
    if not prev.get('space_ok'):
        return {'success': False, 'error': f"Недостаточно места на целевом диске! Нужно {prev['need_fmt']}, свободно {prev['free_fmt']}"}
    items = prev.get('items') or []
    if not items:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'Нет раздач под указанный шаблон пути'}

    now = time.time()
    with app_state_db._lock:
        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM batch_relocate_queue WHERE status IN ('done', 'error')")
            conn.executemany(
                """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO batch_relocate_queue
                   (hash_id, name, old_path, new_path, size_bytes, status, created_at)
                   VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending', ?)""",
                [(it['hash'], it['name'], it['old_path'], it['new_path'], it['size'], now) for it in items]
            )
            conn.commit()

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Пакетный переезд запущен', f'{old_prefix} -> {new_prefix} ({len(items)} шт.)', actor=actor)
    _spawn_relocate_worker()
    return {'success': True, 'queued': len(items)}


def get_relocate_status():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM batch_relocate_queue ORDER BY created_at ASC").fetchall()]
                return {
                    'running': _WORKER_RUNNING,
                    'total': len(rows),
                    'pending': sum(1 for r in rows if r['status'] == 'pending'),
                    'moving': sum(1 for r in rows if r['status'] == 'moving'),
                    'done': sum(1 for r in rows if r['status'] == 'done'),
                    'items': rows[:50]
                }
        except Exception:
            return {'running': False, 'total': 0, 'pending': 0, 'moving': 0, 'done': 0, 'items': []}


def _spawn_relocate_worker():
    global _WORKER_RUNNING
    with _WORKER_LOCK:
        if _WORKER_RUNNING:
            return
        _WORKER_RUNNING = True

    def worker():
        global _WORKER_RUNNING
        try:
            while True:
                with app_state_db._lock:
                    with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                        batch = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute(
                            "SELECT * FROM batch_relocate_queue WHERE status = 'pending' LIMIT 4"
                        ).fetchall()]
                if not batch:
                    break

                ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
                if not port:
                    time.sleep(5)
                    continue

                by_dst = {}
                for b in batch:
                    by_dst.setdefault(b['new_path'], []).append(b['hash_id'])
                    with app_state_db._lock:
                        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                            conn.execute("UPDATE batch_relocate_queue SET status = 'moving' WHERE hash_id = ?", (b['hash_id'],))
                            conn.commit()

                for dst, hs in by_dst.items():
                    os.makedirs(dst, exist_ok=True)
                    set_torrent_location(ip, port, '|'.join(hs), dst)

                moving_hashes = {b['hash_id'].lower() for b in batch}
                deadline = time.monotonic() + 300.0
                while time.monotonic() < deadline:
                    time.sleep(2.0)
                    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port)
                    still_moving = any(
                        str(live.get(h, {}).get('state', '')).lower() == 'moving'
                        for h in live if h.lower() in moving_hashes
                    )
                    if not still_moving:
                        break

                with app_state_db._lock:
                    with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                        for b in batch:
                            conn.execute("UPDATE batch_relocate_queue SET status = 'done' WHERE hash_id = ?", (b['hash_id'],))
                        conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("batch_relocate worker", e)
        finally:
            with _WORKER_LOCK:
                _WORKER_RUNNING = False

    threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True).start()


def resume_pending_relocate_if_any():
    st = get_relocate_status()
    if st.get('pending', 0) > 0 or st.get('moving', 0) > 0:
        with app_state_db._lock:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("UPDATE batch_relocate_queue SET status = 'pending' WHERE status = 'moving'")
                conn.commit()
        _spawn_relocate_worker()

# core/config_vault.py
import os
import shutil
import difflib
import datetime
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.qb_process import is_qb_running

VAULT_DIR = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "qb_config_vault")
os.makedirs(VAULT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
TARGET_FILES = ("qBittorrent.ini", "qBittorrent.conf", "categories.json", "watched_folders.json")


def detect_qb_config_dir():
    custom = cfg.get("QB_Config_Dir", "").strip()
    if custom and os.path.isdir(custom):
        return custom
    if os.name == "nt":
        cands = [
            os.path.join(os.environ.get("APPDATA", ""), "qBittorrent"),
            os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "AppData", "Roaming", "qBittorrent"),
            os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "AppData", "Local", "qBittorrent"),
        ]
    else:
        cands = [
            "/config/qBittorrent",
            "/config",
            os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), ".config", "qBittorrent"),
        ]
    for c in cands:
        if c and os.path.isdir(c):
            return c
    return ""


def _atomic_copy(src, dst):
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
    tmp = dst + ".tmp"
    shutil.copy2(src, tmp)
    with open(tmp, "rb") as f:
        os.fsync(f.fileno())
    os.replace(tmp, dst)


def create_config_backup(reason="manual", max_keep=10):
    src_dir = detect_qb_config_dir()
    if not src_dir or not os.path.isdir(src_dir):
        return False, "Папка конфигов qBittorrent не найдена. Укажите её вручную."

    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    snap_name = f"cfg_{ts}_{reason}"
    snap_dir = os.path.join(VAULT_DIR, snap_name)
    os.makedirs(snap_dir, exist_ok=True)

    copied = []
    for fname in TARGET_FILES:
        src_p = os.path.join(src_dir, fname)
        if os.path.isfile(src_p):
            _atomic_copy(src_p, os.path.join(snap_dir, fname))
            copied.append(fname)

    rss_src = os.path.join(src_dir, "rss")
    if os.path.isdir(rss_src):
        shutil.copytree(rss_src, os.path.join(snap_dir, "rss"), dirs_exist_ok=True)
        copied.append("rss/")

    if not copied:
        shutil.rmtree(snap_dir, ignore_errors=True)
        return False, f"В {src_dir} не найдено целевых конфигов qBittorrent."

    try:
        snaps = sorted(
            [os.path.join(VAULT_DIR, d) for d in os.listdir(VAULT_DIR) if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(VAULT_DIR, d))],
            key=lambda p: os.path.getmtime(p), reverse=True
        )
        for old_d in snaps[max_keep:]:
            shutil.rmtree(old_d, ignore_errors=True)
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("rotate config_vault", e)

    return True, {"name": snap_name, "files": copied, "src_dir": src_dir}


def list_config_backups():
    out = []
    try:
        for d in os.listdir(VAULT_DIR):
            dp = os.path.join(VAULT_DIR, d)
            if not os.path.isdir(dp):
                continue
            files = []
            sz = 0
            for ent in os.scandir(dp):
                if ent.is_file():
                    files.append(ent.name)
                    sz += ent.stat().st_size
                elif ent.is_dir():
                    files.append(ent.name + "/")
            out.append({
                "name": d,
                "files": sorted(files),
                "size_fmt": format_file_size(sz),
                "mtime": int(os.path.getmtime(dp)),
                "date": datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(os.path.getmtime(dp)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
            })
        out.sort(key=lambda x: x["mtime"], reverse=True)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return out


def _safe_snap_dir(name):
    clean = os.path.basename(str(name or "").replace("\\", "/"))
    full = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(VAULT_DIR, clean))
    if os.path.commonpath([os.path.abspath(VAULT_DIR), full]) == os.path.abspath(VAULT_DIR) and os.path.isdir(full):
        return full
    return None


def diff_config_backups(snap_a, snap_b, filename):
    da, db = _safe_snap_dir(snap_a), _safe_snap_dir(snap_b)
    clean_f = os.path.basename(str(filename or ""))
    if not da or not db or clean_f not in TARGET_FILES:
        return False, "Некорректные параметры для сравнения."

    pa, pb = os.path.join(da, clean_f), os.path.join(db, clean_f)
    la = open(pa, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").readlines() if os.path.isfile(pa) else []
    lb = open(pb, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").readlines() if os.path.isfile(pb) else []
    diff_txt = "".join(difflib.unified_diff(la, lb, fromfile=snap_a, tofile=snap_b, lineterm="\n"))
    return True, diff_txt or "Различий в файле не найдено."


def restore_config_backup(snap_name):
    if is_qb_running():
        return False, "🛑 Восстановление заблокировано: сначала остановите qBittorrent (иначе клиент перезапишет конфиг из памяти при выходе)."
    snap_dir = _safe_snap_dir(snap_name)
    dst_dir = detect_qb_config_dir()
    if not snap_dir or not dst_dir or not os.path.isdir(dst_dir):
        return False, "Снимок или папка конфигов qBittorrent не найдены."

    restored = []
    for fname in TARGET_FILES:
        src_p = os.path.join(snap_dir, fname)
        if os.path.isfile(src_p):
            _atomic_copy(src_p, os.path.join(dst_dir, fname))
            restored.append(fname)
    rss_src = os.path.join(snap_dir, "rss")
    if os.path.isdir(rss_src):
        shutil.copytree(rss_src, os.path.join(dst_dir, "rss"), dirs_exist_ok=True)
        restored.append("rss/")
    return True, f"✅ Восстановлено в {dst_dir}: {', '.join(restored)}"

================================================================================

# core/deep_analytics.py
import time
import datetime
import statistics
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size
from core.scheduler import MIN_DELTA_SEC
from core.disk_monitor import get_monitored_disk_info


def get_rhythm_7x24(db):
    """#14 Ритм канала 7x24: медиана часовых скоростей за последние 45 дней."""
    since_ts = time.time() - (45 * 86400)
    rows = db.get_snapshots_since(since_ts)
    hour_buckets = {}
    prev = None

    for r in rows:
        if prev is not None:
            t1, t2 = float(prev['timestamp']), float(r['timestamp'])
            dt_sec = t2 - t1
            delta = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev['total_uploaded']))
            if dt_sec >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and delta > 0:
                cur_ts = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(t1).replace(minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
                while cur_ts < t2:
                    b_end = cur_ts + 3600.0
                    overlap = min(t2, b_end) - max(t1, cur_ts)
                    if overlap > 0:
                        dt_obj = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(cur_ts)
                        k = (dt_obj.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'), dt_obj.weekday(), dt_obj.hour)
                        b = hour_buckets.setdefault(k, {'bytes': 0.0, 'sec': 0.0})
                        b['bytes'] += delta * (overlap / dt_sec)
                        b['sec'] += overlap
                    cur_ts = b_end
        prev = r

    samples = {(wd, hr): [] for wd in range(7) for hr in range(24)}
    for (_, wd, hr), b in hour_buckets.items():
        if b['sec'] >= 60:
            samples[(wd, hr)].append((b['bytes'] / b['sec']) / (1024 * 1024))

    matrix = []
    max_med = 0.0
    for wd in range(7):
        row = []
        for hr in range(24):
            vals = samples[(wd, hr)]
            med = round(statistics.median(vals), 2) if vals else 0.0
            if med > max_med:
                max_med = med
            row.append(med)
        matrix.append(row)
    return {'matrix': matrix, 'max_mbs': round(max_med, 2)}


def get_day_hourly_breakdown(db, date_str):
    """#14 Почасовая разбивка выбранного дня при клике в хитмапе."""
    try:
        d = datetime.datetime.strptime(str(date_str).strip(), '%Y-%m-%d')
    except ValueError:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректная дата'}

    start_ts = d.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
    end_ts = d.replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59, microsecond=999999).timestamp()
    rows = db.get_snapshots_since(start_ts - 3600)
    hours = {h: 0.0 for h in range(24)}
    points_in_day = 0
    prev = None

    for r in rows:
        ts = float(r['timestamp'])
        if ts > end_ts:
            break
        if start_ts <= ts <= end_ts:
            points_in_day += 1
        if prev is not None:
            t1, t2 = float(prev['timestamp']), ts
            dt_sec = t2 - t1
            delta = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev['total_uploaded']))
            if dt_sec >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and delta > 0:
                cur_ts = max(start_ts, datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(t1).replace(minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp())
                while cur_ts < min(t2, end_ts):
                    b_end = cur_ts + 3600.0
                    overlap = min(t2, b_end, end_ts) - max(t1, cur_ts, start_ts)
                    if overlap > 0:
                        hr = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(cur_ts).hour
                        hours[hr] += delta * (overlap / dt_sec)
                    cur_ts = b_end
        prev = r

    has_detail = points_in_day >= 3
    return {
        'success': True,
        'date': date_str,
        'has_detail': has_detail,
        'hours': [{'hour': f"{h:02d}:00", 'bytes': int(hours[h]), 'fmt': format_file_size(hours[h])} for h in range(24)]
    }


def get_storage_runway(live, summaries):
    """#18 Взлётная полоса диска: остаток после очереди + прогноз дней по темпу 30д."""
    disk = get_monitored_disk_info(live)
    free_b = int(disk.get('free_bytes') or 0)
    queue_left = sum(int(v.get('amount_left', 0) or 0) for v in (live or {}).values() if int(v.get('amount_left', 0) or 0) > 0)
    post_queue_free = max(0, free_b - queue_left)

    now = time.time()
    cutoff_30d = now - (30 * 86400)
    added_30d_bytes = 0
    for s in summaries:
        cts = int(s.get('created_ts') or 0)
        if cts >= cutoff_30d:
            added_30d_bytes += int(s.get('size', 0) or 0)

    daily_growth = added_30d_bytes / 30.0
    reserve_gib = max(0, cfg.getint('Disk_Reserve_GiB', 20))
    usable_after_reserve = max(0, post_queue_free - (reserve_gib * 1024 ** 3))
    days_left = int(usable_after_reserve / daily_growth) if daily_growth > (1024 ** 2) else None

    return {
        'disk_path': disk.get('path', '—'),
        'free_fmt': format_file_size(free_b),
        'queue_left_fmt': format_file_size(queue_left),
        'post_queue_fmt': format_file_size(post_queue_free),
        'daily_growth_fmt': f"{format_file_size(daily_growth)}/день",
        'days_left': days_left,
        'reserve_gib': reserve_gib,
        'guard_triggered': post_queue_free < (reserve_gib * 1024 ** 3)
    }


def _calc_period_metrics(db, start_ts, end_ts):
    rows = db.get_snapshots_since(start_ts - 3600)
    total_b, speeds, peak_hour_map = 0, [], {h: 0.0 for h in range(24)}
    active_counts = []
    prev = None
    for r in rows:
        ts = float(r['timestamp'])
        if ts > end_ts:
            break
        if prev is not None and start_ts <= ts <= end_ts:
            dt = ts - float(prev['timestamp'])
            d = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev['total_uploaded']))
            if dt >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and d >= 0:
                total_b += d
                speeds.append((d / dt) / (1024 * 1024))
                hr = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(ts).hour
                peak_hour_map[hr] += d
                active_counts.append(int(r.get('count', 0) or 0))
        prev = r

    avg_mbs = round(sum(speeds) / len(speeds), 2) if speeds else 0.0
    peak_hr = max(peak_hour_map.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])[0] if any(peak_hour_map.values()) else None
    avg_cnt = int(round(sum(active_counts) / len(active_counts))) if active_counts else 0
    return {'bytes': total_b, 'fmt': format_file_size(total_b), 'avg_mbs': avg_mbs, 'peak_hour': f"{peak_hr:02d}:00" if peak_hr is not None else '—', 'avg_count': avg_cnt}


def compare_periods_ab(db, start_a_str, end_a_str, start_b_str, end_b_str, same_n_days=True):
    """#33 Машина времени: сравнение двух диапазонов дат."""
    try:
        da1 = datetime.datetime.strptime(str(start_a_str).strip(), '%Y-%m-%d')
        da2 = datetime.datetime.strptime(str(end_a_str).strip(), '%Y-%m-%d').replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59)
        db1 = datetime.datetime.strptime(str(start_b_str).strip(), '%Y-%m-%d')
        db2 = datetime.datetime.strptime(str(end_b_str).strip(), '%Y-%m-%d').replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59)
    except ValueError:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'Укажите даты в формате YYYY-MM-DD'}

    if da2 < da1 or db2 < db1:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'Дата окончания не может быть раньше даты начала'}

    if same_n_days:
        days_span = max(0, min((da2 - da1).days, (db2 - db1).days))
        da2 = (da1 + datetime.timedelta(days=days_span)).replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59)
        db2 = (db1 + datetime.timedelta(days=days_span)).replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59)

    m_a = _calc_period_metrics(db, da1.timestamp(), da2.timestamp())
    m_b = _calc_period_metrics(db, db1.timestamp(), db2.timestamp())
    diff_pct = round(((m_b['bytes'] - m_a['bytes']) / m_a['bytes']) * 100.0, 1) if m_a['bytes'] > 0 else None
    return {'success': True, 'period_a': m_a, 'period_b': m_b, 'diff_pct': diff_pct}


def get_monthly_quota_status(db):
    """#42 Счётчик месячной квоты строго по положительным приращениям из summary_history.db."""
    now_dt = datetime.datetime.now()
    month_start = now_dt.replace(day=1, hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
    rows = db.get_snapshots_since(month_start - 3600)
    used_b, prev = 0, None
    for r in rows:
        ts = float(r['timestamp'])
        if prev is not None and ts >= month_start:
            dt = ts - float(prev['timestamp'])
            d = int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev['total_uploaded'])
            if dt >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and d > 0:
                used_b += d
        prev = r

    quota_tib = max(0.0, float(cfg.get('Monthly_Quota_TiB', '0') or 0))
    quota_b = int(quota_tib * (1024 ** 4))
    pct = round(min(100.0, (used_b / quota_b) * 100.0), 1) if quota_b > 0 else 0.0
    elapsed_days = max(0.1, (now_dt.timestamp() - month_start) / 86400.0)
    proj_b = int((used_b / elapsed_days) * 30.0)
    return {
        'used_bytes': used_b, 'used_fmt': format_file_size(used_b),
        'quota_tib': quota_tib, 'quota_fmt': format_file_size(quota_b) if quota_b > 0 else 'Не задана',
        'percent': pct, 'projected_fmt': format_file_size(proj_b)
    }

# core/disaster_recovery.py
import os
import json
import time
import datetime
import urllib.parse
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, format_file_size, write_log, write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.notifiers import notify_all
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, send_qb_command,
    set_global_speed_limits, shutdown_qb_webui, close_qbittorrent
)

MANIFESTS_DIR = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "manifests")
os.makedirs(MANIFESTS_DIR, exist_ok=True)


def execute_panic_killswitch(kill_qb=False, actor="admin"):
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    details = []

    if port:
        if send_qb_command(ip, port, "stop", "all"):
            details.append("Все раздачи поставлены на паузу")
        if set_global_speed_limits(ip, port, 1, 1):
            details.append("Глобальный лимит скорости зажат до 1 B/s")

    frozen_until = int(time.time() + 3600)
    cfg.set("Autopilot_Frozen_Until", str(frozen_until))
    cfg.save()
    details.append("Автопилот заморожен на 1 час")

    if kill_qb:
        if port:
            shutdown_qb_webui(ip, port)
        if os.name == "nt":
            close_qbittorrent()
        details.append("Отправлена команда завершения qBittorrent")

    summary = "; ".join(details)
    write_log("app", f"PANIC KILLSWITCH ({actor}): {summary}")
    app_state_db.add_audit_event("system", "Panic Killswitch", summary, actor=actor)
    notify_all("🚨 PANIC KILLSWITCH АКТИВИРОВАН", details, critical=True)

    return {"success": True, "details": details, "frozen_until": frozen_until}


def _build_magnet(t_hash, name, trackers):
    params = [("xt", f"urn:btih:{t_hash}"), ("dn", name)]
    for tr in trackers:
        if tr:
            params.append(("tr", tr))
    return "magnet:?" + "&".join(f"{k}={urllib.parse.quote(str(v), safe=':')}" for k, v in params)


def _prune_old_manifests(max_keep=2):
    try:
        files = [
            os.path.join(MANIFESTS_DIR, f)
            for f in os.listdir(MANIFESTS_DIR)
            if f.startswith("tbr_manifest_") and f.endswith(".json")
        ]
        files.sort(key=lambda p: os.path.getmtime(p), reverse=True)
        for old_path in files[max_keep:]:
            try:
                os.remove(old_path)
            except OSError:
                pass
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Ротация аварийных манифестов", e)


def create_disaster_manifest(actor="admin"):
    from core.version import APP_VERSION
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    parsed = get_all_torrents(use_cache=True)

    items = []
    seen = set()
    for h, st in parsed.items():
        seen.add(h.lower())
        lv = live.get(h) or live.get(h.lower()) or {}
        name = str(lv.get("name") or st.get("name") or h)
        size = int(lv.get("total_size") or st.get("size") or 0)
        trackers = list(st.get("trackers") or [])
        tr_live = str(lv.get("tracker") or "").strip()
        if tr_live and tr_live not in trackers:
            trackers.insert(0, tr_live)
        items.append({
            "name": name,
            "hash": h.lower(),
            "size": size,
            "size_fmt": format_file_size(size),
            "trackers": trackers,
            "magnet": _build_magnet(h.lower(), name, trackers),
        })

    for h, lv in live.items():
        hl = h.lower()
        if hl in seen:
            continue
        name = str(lv.get("name") or hl)
        size = int(lv.get("total_size") or 0)
        tr_live = str(lv.get("tracker") or "").strip()
        trackers = [tr_live] if tr_live else []
        items.append({
            "name": name,
            "hash": hl,
            "size": size,
            "size_fmt": format_file_size(size),
            "trackers": trackers,
            "magnet": _build_magnet(hl, name, trackers),
        })

    items.sort(key=lambda x: x["name"].lower())
    ts_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    fname = f"tbr_manifest_{ts_str}.json"
    fpath = os.path.join(MANIFESTS_DIR, fname)

    payload = {
        "generated_at": datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        "app_version": APP_VERSION,
        "count": len(items),
        "torrents": items,
    }
    with open(fpath, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(payload, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    _prune_old_manifests(max_keep=2)
    cfg.set("Manifest_Last_Run", str(int(time.time())))
    cfg.save()
    app_state_db.add_audit_event("system", "Аварийный манифест", f"Создан {fname} ({len(items)} раздач)", actor=actor)
    return {"filename": fname, "count": len(items), "size": os.path.getsize(fpath)}


def list_manifests():
    res = []
    try:
        for f in os.listdir(MANIFESTS_DIR):
            if f.startswith("tbr_manifest_") and f.endswith(".json"):
                p = os.path.join(MANIFESTS_DIR, f)
                res.append({
                    "filename": f,
                    "size_fmt": format_file_size(os.path.getsize(p)),
                    "mtime": int(os.path.getmtime(p)),
                    "date": datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(os.path.getmtime(p)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                })
        res.sort(key=lambda x: x["mtime"], reverse=True)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return res[:2]


def get_manifest_path(filename):
    clean = os.path.basename(str(filename or "").replace("\\", "/"))
    if not clean or not clean.startswith("tbr_manifest_") or not clean.endswith(".json"):
        return None
    base = os.path.abspath(MANIFESTS_DIR)
    full = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(base, clean))
    if os.path.commonpath([base, full]) == base and os.path.isfile(full):
        return full
    return None


def maybe_run_weekly_manifest():
    if not cfg.getboolean("Manifest_Weekly", False):
        return
    last_ts = cfg.getint("Manifest_Last_Run", 0)
    if (time.time() - last_ts) >= 7 * 86400:
        try:
            create_disaster_manifest(actor="scheduler")
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("Еженедельный манифест", e)

# core/disk_monitor.py
import os
import shutil
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import BT_BACKUP_PATH, APP_DATA_DIR, format_file_size
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents


def _get_mount_key(path):
    ap = os.path.abspath(str(path).strip())
    if os.name == 'nt':
        drive, _ = os.path.splitdrive(ap)
        if drive:
            return drive.upper() + os.sep
    try:
        st = os.stat(ap)
        return f"dev:{st.st_dev}"
    except Exception:
        return ap


def _find_mount_root(path):
    ap = os.path.abspath(str(path).strip())
    if os.name == 'nt':
        drive, _ = os.path.splitdrive(ap)
        return (drive.upper() + os.sep) if drive else ap
    cur = ap
    while cur != os.path.dirname(cur):
        parent = os.path.dirname(cur)
        if os.path.ismount(cur) or not os.path.exists(parent):
            return cur
        try:
            if os.stat(cur).st_dev != os.stat(parent).st_dev:
                return cur
        except Exception:
            break
        cur = parent
    return cur


def list_active_disks(live=None):
    """Собирает уникальные диски/точки монтирования по save_path всех раздач."""
    mounts = {}

    def register_path(raw_p, weight=1):
        if not raw_p or not str(raw_p).strip():
            return
        p = str(raw_p).strip()
        if not os.path.exists(p):
            return
        key = _get_mount_key(p)
        if key not in mounts:
            root = _find_mount_root(p)
            try:
                usage = shutil.disk_usage(p)
                mounts[key] = {
                    'key': key,
                    'path': root,
                    'probe_path': p,
                    'torrents_count': 0,
                    'free_bytes': usage.free,
                    'total_bytes': usage.total,
                    'free_fmt': format_file_size(usage.free),
                    'total_fmt': format_file_size(usage.total),
                }
            except Exception:
                return
        mounts[key]['torrents_count'] += weight

    if live:
        for info in live.values():
            register_path(info.get('save_path'), weight=1)
    else:
        for st in get_all_torrents(use_cache=True).values():
            register_path(st.get('save_path'), weight=1)

    if not mounts:
        for fallback in (BT_BACKUP_PATH, APP_DATA_DIR):
            if fallback and os.path.exists(fallback):
                register_path(fallback, weight=0)
                break

    res = sorted(mounts.values(), key=lambda x: (x['torrents_count'], x['total_bytes']), reverse=True)
    return res


def get_monitored_disk_info(live=None):
    """Возвращает выбранный в настройках диск или диск с наибольшим числом раздач."""
    disks = list_active_disks(live)
    if not disks:
        return {'path': '—', 'free_bytes': 0, 'total_bytes': 0, 'free_fmt': '—', 'torrents_count': 0}

    preferred = cfg.get('Monitored_Disk_Path', '').strip()
    if preferred:
        pref_key = _get_mount_key(preferred)
        for d in disks:
            if d['key'] == pref_key or d['path'] == preferred:
                return d
        if os.path.exists(preferred):
            try:
                usage = shutil.disk_usage(preferred)
                return {
                    'path': preferred,
                    'free_bytes': usage.free,
                    'total_bytes': usage.total,
                    'free_fmt': format_file_size(usage.free),
                    'torrents_count': 0
                }
            except Exception:
                pass

    return disks[0]

================================================================================

# core/dnd_digest.py
import json
import time
import datetime
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents
from core.scheduler import MIN_DELTA_SEC
from core.disk_monitor import get_monitored_disk_info
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info


def _parse_hhmm(val, default_min):
    try:
        parts = str(val or "").strip().split(":")
        h, m = int(parts[0]), int(parts[1])
        if 0 <= h <= 23 and 0 <= m <= 59:
            return h * 60 + m
    except Exception:
        pass
    return default_min


def get_dnd_settings():
    raw_days = str(cfg.get("DND_Days", "0,1,2,3,4,5,6"))
    days = sorted({int(x) for x in raw_days.split(",") if x.strip().isdigit() and 0 <= int(x) <= 6})
    return {
        "enabled": cfg.getboolean("DND_Enabled", False),
        "start": cfg.get("DND_Start", "23:00") or "23:00",
        "end": cfg.get("DND_End", "08:00") or "08:00",
        "days": days,
    }


def is_dnd_active_now(now_dt=None):
    st = get_dnd_settings()
    if not st["enabled"] or not st["days"]:
        return False

    dt = now_dt or datetime.datetime.now()
    cur_min = dt.hour * 60 + dt.minute
    start_min = _parse_hhmm(st["start"], 23 * 60)
    end_min = _parse_hhmm(st["end"], 8 * 60)
    wd = dt.weekday()

    if start_min == end_min:
        return False
    if start_min < end_min:
        return (wd in st["days"]) and (start_min <= cur_min < end_min)
    if cur_min >= start_min:
        return wd in st["days"]
    if cur_min < end_min:
        return ((wd - 1) % 7) in st["days"]
    return False


def queue_dnd_message(title, lines):
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO dnd_queue (title, lines_json, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                    (str(title), json.dumps(list(lines or []), ensure_ascii=False), time.time())
                )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("queue_dnd_message", e)


def flush_dnd_if_ready(send_fn):
    if is_dnd_active_now():
        return 0
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM dnd_queue ORDER BY id ASC").fetchall()
                if not rows:
                    return 0
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM dnd_queue")
                conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            return 0

    lines = []
    for r in rows[:25]:
        ts_str = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(r["created_at"]).strftime("%H:%M")
        try:
            sub = json.loads(r["lines_json"] or "[]")
            preview = sub[0] if sub else ""
        except Exception:
            preview = ""
        lines.append(f"[{ts_str}] {r['title']}" + (f" — {preview}" if preview else ""))
    if len(rows) > 25:
        lines.append(f"...и ещё событий: {len(rows) - 25}")

    send_fn(f"🌙 Итоги «тихого часа» ({len(rows)} событий)", lines, critical=True)
    return len(rows)


def _sum_positive_delta(since_ts, until_ts):
    rows = summary_tracker.db.get_snapshots_since(since_ts)
    total, prev = 0, None
    for r in rows:
        ts = float(r["timestamp"])
        if ts > until_ts:
            break
        if prev is not None:
            dt = ts - float(prev["timestamp"])
            d = max(0, int(r["total_uploaded"]) - int(prev["total_uploaded"]))
            if dt >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and d > 0:
                total += d
        prev = r
    return total


def get_digest_settings():
    return {
        "mode": cfg.get("Digest_Mode", "off"),
        "hour": max(0, min(23, cfg.getint("Digest_Hour", 21))),
        "weekday": max(0, min(6, cfg.getint("Digest_Weekday", 6))),
    }


def _last_scheduled_ts(now_dt, mode, target_hour, target_wd):
    cand = now_dt.replace(hour=target_hour, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    if mode == "daily":
        return (cand - datetime.timedelta(days=1)).timestamp() if cand > now_dt else cand.timestamp()
    days_back = (now_dt.weekday() - target_wd) % 7
    cand = (now_dt - datetime.timedelta(days=days_back)).replace(hour=target_hour, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    if cand > now_dt:
        cand -= datetime.timedelta(days=7)
    return cand.timestamp()


def maybe_send_digest(send_fn):
    st = get_digest_settings()
    mode = st["mode"]
    if mode not in ("daily", "weekly"):
        return False

    now_dt = datetime.datetime.now()
    now_ts = now_dt.timestamp()
    sched_ts = _last_scheduled_ts(now_dt, mode, st["hour"], st["weekday"])
    last_sent = float(cfg.getint("Digest_Last_Sent_Ts", 0) or 0)

    if last_sent == 0:
        cfg.set("Digest_Last_Sent_Ts", str(int(now_ts)))
        cfg.save()
        return False
    if last_sent >= sched_ts:
        return False

    period_sec = 7 * 86400 if mode == "weekly" else 86400
    delayed_sec = now_ts - sched_ts
    since_ts = max(last_sent, now_ts - (30 * 86400)) if delayed_sec > 3600 else (now_ts - period_sec)
    span_sec = max(3600.0, now_ts - since_ts)

    cur_vol = _sum_positive_delta(since_ts, now_ts)
    prev_vol = _sum_positive_delta(since_ts - span_sec, since_ts)
    if prev_vol > 0:
        diff_pct = ((cur_vol - prev_vol) / prev_vol) * 100.0
        pct_str = f" ({'+' if diff_pct >= 0 else ''}{diff_pct:.1f}% к прошл. периоду)"
    else:
        pct_str = ""

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    disk_info = get_monitored_disk_info(live)

    torrents = get_all_torrents(use_cache=True)
    deltas = summary_tracker.db.get_torrents_positive_deltas(since_ts)
    top3 = sorted(deltas.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:3]

    span_hours = int(round(span_sec / 3600.0))
    lbl = "Недельный дайджест" if mode == "weekly" else "Дневной дайджест"
    lines = [
        f"⏱️ Интервал: {span_hours} ч" + (" (дослан после простоя)" if delayed_sec > 3600 else ""),
        f"📤 Отдано: {format_file_size(cur_vol)}{pct_str}",
        f"💾 Свободно ({disk_info['path']}): {disk_info['free_fmt']}",
    ]
    if top3:
        lines.append("🏆 Топ-3 раздачи:")
        for idx, (h, val) in enumerate(top3, 1):
            nm = (torrents.get(h) or {}).get("name") or h[:10]
            lines.append(f"{idx}. {nm[:36]} — {format_file_size(val)}")

    cfg.set("Digest_Last_Sent_Ts", str(int(now_ts)))
    cfg.save()
    send_fn(f"📊 tbr PRO — {lbl}", lines, critical=False)
    app_state_db.add_audit_event("automation", lbl, f"Отдано {format_file_size(cur_vol)}")
    return True

# core/env_doctor.py
import os
import re
import time
import sqlite3
import platform
import email.utils
import urllib.request
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, BT_BACKUP_PATH, LOG_FOLDER
from core.version import APP_VERSION
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, check_qb_api_alive, get_qb_version, is_qb_running
from core.disk_monitor import list_active_disks
from core.config_vault import detect_qb_config_dir

_DB_LIST = [
    ("summary_history.db", os.path.join(LOG_FOLDER, "auto_hourly", "summary_history.db")),
    ("profiles.db", os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "profiles.db")),
    ("automation.db", os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "automation.db")),
    ("app_state.db", os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "app_state.db")),
    ("file_index.db", os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "file_index.db")),
]


def _check_time_skew():
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request("https://api.github.com", method="HEAD", headers={"User-Agent": f"tbr-pro/{APP_VERSION}"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=3.0) as resp:
            hdr = resp.headers.get("Date")
            if hdr:
                remote_ts = email.utils.mktime_tz(email.utils.parsedate_tz(hdr))
                diff = abs(time.time() - remote_ts)
                return {"ok": diff < 120, "skew_sec": round(diff, 1)}
    except Exception:
        pass
    return {"ok": None, "skew_sec": None}


def run_environment_doctor():
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    api_ok = check_qb_api_alive(ip, port) if port else False
    qb_ver = get_qb_version(ip, port, timeout=2.0) if api_ok else None

    bt_exists = os.path.isdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH)
    bt_rw = False
    if bt_exists:
        test_f = os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, ".tbr_rw_test.tmp")
        try:
            with open(test_f, "w") as f:
                f.write("ok")
            os.remove(test_f)
            bt_rw = True
        except Exception:
            bt_rw = False

    dbs = []
    for name, path in _DB_LIST:
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            dbs.append({"name": name, "exists": False, "quick_check": "missing", "wal": False})
            continue
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=5.0)
            qc = conn.execute("PRAGMA quick_check;").fetchone()[0]
            jm = conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode;").fetchone()[0]
            conn.close()
            dbs.append({"name": name, "exists": True, "quick_check": str(qc), "wal": str(jm).lower() == "wal"})
        except Exception as e:
            dbs.append({"name": name, "exists": True, "quick_check": f"error: {e}", "wal": False})

    return {
        "app_version": APP_VERSION,
        "os": f"{platform.system()} {platform.release()}",
        "python": platform.python_version(),
        "qb": {
            "running": is_qb_running(),
            "ip": ip, "port": port,
            "api_ok": api_ok,
            "version": qb_ver or ("OK" if api_ok else "не отвечает"),
            "config_dir": detect_qb_config_dir() or "не найдена"
        },
        "bt_backup": {"path": BT_BACKUP_PATH, "exists": bt_exists, "rw": bt_rw},
        "databases": dbs,
        "disks": list_active_disks(),
        "notifiers": {
            "telegram_configured": bool(cfg.get("TG_Bot_Token", "").strip() and cfg.get("TG_Chat_ID", "").strip()),
            "discord_configured": bool(cfg.get("Discord_Webhook", "").strip())
        },
        "time_skew": _check_time_skew()
    }


def sanitize_text_for_github(raw_text):
    s = str(raw_text or "")
    s = re.sub(r'tbr_api_[A-Za-z0-9_\-]+', 'tbr_api_<REDACTED>', s)
    s = re.sub(r'inv_[A-Za-z0-9_\-]+', 'inv_<REDACTED>', s)
    s = re.sub(r'bot\d+:[A-Za-z0-9_\-]+', 'bot<REDACTED>', s)
    s = re.sub(r'https://discord(?:app)?\.com/api/webhooks/[^\s\)]+', 'https://discord.com/api/webhooks/<REDACTED>', s)
    s = re.sub(r'passkey=[A-Za-z0-9]+', 'passkey=<REDACTED>', s, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    s = re.sub(r'C:\\Users\\[^\\\s]+', r'C:\\Users\\<USER>', s, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    s = re.sub(r'/home/[^/\s]+', '/home/<USER>', s)
    s = re.sub(r'\b(?!127\.0\.0\.1\b)(?!10\.)(?!192\.168\.)(?!100\.)\d{1,3}(?:\.\d{1,3}){3}\b', '<EXTERNAL_IP>', s)
    return s


def generate_github_issue_report(diag=None):
    d = diag or run_environment_doctor()
    db_lines = "\n".join(f"  - `{x['name']}`: quick_check=`{x['quick_check']}`, WAL=`{x['wal']}`" for x in d["databases"])
    disk_lines = "\n".join(f"  - `{dk['path']}`: {dk['free_fmt']} free / {dk['total_fmt']} ({dk['torrents_count']} torrents)" for dk in d["disks"])
    skew = d["time_skew"]
    skew_txt = f"{skew['skew_sec']}s" if skew["skew_sec"] is not None else "n/a"

    md = (
        f"### Environment Report — tbr PRO v{d['app_version']}\n"
        f"- **OS / Python:** `{d['os']}` / Python `{d['python']}`\n"
        f"- **qBittorrent:** running=`{d['qb']['running']}`, API=`{d['qb']['api_ok']}` (`{d['qb']['version']}`)\n"
        f"- **BT_backup:** exists=`{d['bt_backup']['exists']}`, rw=`{d['bt_backup']['rw']}`\n"
        f"- **Time Skew (vs GitHub UTC):** `{skew_txt}`\n"
        f"- **Notifiers:** TG=`{d['notifiers']['telegram_configured']}`, Discord=`{d['notifiers']['discord_configured']}`\n"
        f"- **Databases:**\n{db_lines}\n"
        f"- **Disks:**\n{disk_lines}\n"
    )
    return sanitize_text_for_github(md)


def probe_onboarding_defaults():
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    diag = run_environment_doctor()
    return {
        "completed": cfg.getboolean("Onboarding_Completed", False),
        "detected_ip": ip,
        "detected_port": port,
        "qb_api_ok": diag["qb"]["api_ok"],
        "qb_version": diag["qb"]["version"],
        "bt_backup_ok": diag["bt_backup"]["exists"] and diag["bt_backup"]["rw"],
        "disks": diag["disks"],
        "max_channel_mbps": cfg.getint("Max_Channel_Mbps", 1000)
    }

# core/fastresume_merger.py
import os
import time
import shutil
import datetime
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import BT_BACKUP_PATH, format_file_size, write_error_log, write_log
from utils.bencode import decode_bencode, encode_bencode
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, delete_torrents,
    is_qb_running, close_qbittorrent, launch_qbittorrent, get_qb_executable_path
)
from core.torrent_parser import force_clear_cache
from core.torrent_targets import handle_targets_on_merge
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.soft_trash import create_fastresume_snapshot
from core.lineage_notes_events import record_torrent_event

VALID_DELETE_MODES = ('none', 'safe', 'force')
VALID_MERGE_MODES = ('add', 'replace')


def _find_fastresume_file(t_hash):
    if not os.path.exists(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        return None
    target = t_hash.lower() + '.fastresume'
    for f in os.listdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        if f.lower() == target:
            return os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, f)
    return None


def _get_bencode_val(data, key):
    if key in data:
        return data[key]
    return data.get(key.encode('ascii'))


def _norm_path(p):
    if not p or not str(p).strip():
        return ''
    return os.path.normcase(os.path.normpath(os.path.abspath(str(p).strip())))


def paths_overlap(path_a, path_b):
    na = _norm_path(path_a)
    nb = _norm_path(path_b)
    if not na or not nb or na == nb:
        return True
    try:
        common = os.path.commonpath([na, nb])
        return common == na or common == nb
    except ValueError:
        return False


def _wait_for_target_live(ip, port, new_hash, timeout_sec=12.0):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_sec
    nh = new_hash.lower()
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port)
        if live:
            for k, v in live.items():
                if k.lower() == nh:
                    return live, v
        time.sleep(1.2)
    return {}, None


def _evaluate_delete_plan(old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode, expected_up, live_before):
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live_after, new_live = _wait_for_target_live(ip, port, new_hash, timeout_sec=12.0) if port else ({}, None)
    oh = old_hash.lower()

    if new_live is not None:
        new_up = int(new_live.get('uploaded', 0) or 0)
        if expected_up > 0 and new_up < expected_up:
            return None, f"⚠️ WebUI: статистика получателя не подтверждена ({new_up} < {expected_up}). Удаление заблокировано."

        old_live = next((v for k, v in live_after.items() if k.lower() == oh), None) or \
                   next((v for k, v in live_before.items() if k.lower() == oh), {})
        src_path = str(old_live.get('content_path') or old_live.get('save_path') or '')
        dst_path = str(new_live.get('content_path') or new_live.get('save_path') or '')

        if delete_mode == 'safe':
            overlap = paths_overlap(src_path, dst_path)
            delete_files = not overlap
            downgraded = overlap
        else:
            delete_files = True
            downgraded = False

        return {
            'method': 'webui', 'old_hash': old_hash, 'new_hash': new_hash,
            'old_name': old_live.get('name', old_hash), 'delete_mode': delete_mode,
            'delete_files': delete_files, 'downgraded': downgraded,
            'src_path': src_path, 'dst_path': dst_path
        }, None

    new_file = _find_fastresume_file(new_hash)
    if new_file and os.path.exists(new_file):
        with open(new_file, 'rb') as f:
            disk_data = decode_bencode(f.read()) or {}
        disk_up = int(_get_bencode_val(disk_data, 'total_uploaded') or 0)
        if disk_up >= expected_up:
            return {
                'method': 'disk_only', 'old_hash': old_hash, 'new_hash': new_hash,
                'old_name': old_hash, 'delete_mode': delete_mode,
                'delete_files': False, 'downgraded': True, 'src_path': '', 'dst_path': ''
            }, None

    return None, "⚠️ WebUI недоступен и диск-проверка не пройдена — удаление заблокировано."


def run_fastresume_merge(old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode='none', merge_mode='add', force_duplicate=False):
    d_mode = delete_mode if delete_mode in VALID_DELETE_MODES else 'none'
    m_mode = merge_mode if merge_mode in VALID_MERGE_MODES else 'add'
    log = []

    if m_mode == 'add' and not force_duplicate:
        prev = app_state_db.get_merger_history(old_hash, new_hash)
        if prev:
            dt_str = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(prev[0]['created_at']).strftime('%d.%m.%Y %H:%M')
            return False, log, None, 409, {
                'is_duplicate': True, 'merged_at': dt_str,
                'warning': f"Эти раздачи уже объединялись {dt_str}. Точно сложить ещё раз?"
            }

    old_file = _find_fastresume_file(old_hash)
    new_file = _find_fastresume_file(new_hash)
    if not old_file or not os.path.exists(old_file):
        return False, [f'❌ Файл {old_hash}.fastresume не найден!'], None, 404, None
    if not new_file or not os.path.exists(new_file):
        return False, [f'❌ Файл {new_hash}.fastresume не найден!'], None, 404, None

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live_before = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if (d_mode != 'none' and port) else {}
    exe_path = get_qb_executable_path()
    was_running = is_qb_running()
    expected_up = 0

    try:
        if was_running:
            log.append("🛑 Завершаем qBittorrent (сброс дискового кэша)...")
            if not close_qbittorrent(max_wait_sec=30.0):
                raise RuntimeError("Не удалось завершить qBittorrent за 30с. Убедитесь, что WebUI доступен.")
            time.sleep(1.0)

        create_fastresume_snapshot([old_hash, new_hash], reason='merger')

        with open(old_file, 'rb') as f:
            old_data = decode_bencode(f.read())
        with open(new_file, 'rb') as f:
            new_data = decode_bencode(f.read())

        if not isinstance(old_data, dict) or not isinstance(new_data, dict):
            raise ValueError("Повреждённый формат .fastresume (ожидался bencode-словарь).")

        old_up = int(_get_bencode_val(old_data, 'total_uploaded') or 0)
        new_up = int(_get_bencode_val(new_data, 'total_uploaded') or 0)
        old_seed = int(_get_bencode_val(old_data, 'seeding_time') or 0)
        new_seed = int(_get_bencode_val(new_data, 'seeding_time') or 0)
        old_act = int(_get_bencode_val(old_data, 'active_time') or 0)
        new_act = int(_get_bencode_val(new_data, 'active_time') or 0)

        final_up = (old_up + new_up) if m_mode == 'add' else old_up
        final_seed = (old_seed + new_seed) if m_mode == 'add' else old_seed
        final_act = (old_act + new_act) if m_mode == 'add' else old_act
        expected_up = final_up

        def set_val(d, key, val):
            kb = key.encode('ascii')
            if kb in d or any(isinstance(k, bytes) for k in d.keys()):
                d[kb] = val
                d.pop(key, None)
            else:
                d[key] = val
                d.pop(kb, None)

        set_val(new_data, 'total_uploaded', final_up)
        set_val(new_data, 'seeding_time', final_seed)
        set_val(new_data, 'active_time', final_act)

        old_add = int(_get_bencode_val(old_data, 'added_time') or 0)
        new_add = int(_get_bencode_val(new_data, 'added_time') or 0)
        valid_adds = [x for x in (old_add, new_add) if x > 0]
        if valid_adds:
            set_val(new_data, 'added_time', min(valid_adds))

        if m_mode == 'add':
            log.append(f"➕ Режим СЛОЖЕНИЯ: {format_file_size(old_up)} + {format_file_size(new_up)} = {format_file_size(final_up)}")
            log.append(f"⏱️ Сидирование: {old_seed // 3600}ч + {new_seed // 3600}ч = {final_seed // 3600}ч")
        else:
            log.append(f"🔄 Режим ЗАМЕНЫ: перенесено {format_file_size(final_up)}")

        encoded_bytes = encode_bencode(new_data)
        validated = decode_bencode(encoded_bytes)
        if not isinstance(validated, dict) or len(validated) != len(new_data):
            raise ValueError("Контрольная проверка целостности bencode не пройдена!")

        bak = new_file + '.bak'
        if not os.path.exists(bak):
            shutil.copy2(new_file, bak)
            log.append(f"📦 Бэкап создан: {os.path.basename(bak)}")

        tmp_file = new_file + '.tmp'
        try:
            with open(tmp_file, 'wb') as f:
                f.write(encoded_bytes)
                f.flush()
                os.fsync(f.fileno())
            os.replace(tmp_file, new_file)
        finally:
            if os.path.exists(tmp_file):
                try:
                    os.remove(tmp_file)
                except OSError:
                    pass

        force_clear_cache()
        app_state_db.add_merger_record(old_hash, new_hash, m_mode, old_up, new_up, final_up)
        uid = app_state_db.link_torrent_lineage(old_hash, new_hash)
        if uid:
            log.append(f"🔗 Личная история поколений связана (UID: {uid[:8]}...)")
        record_torrent_event(new_hash, 'merge', f"Мердж ({m_mode}): {format_file_size(old_up)} + {format_file_size(new_up)} → {format_file_size(final_up)}")
        if handle_targets_on_merge(old_hash, new_hash, final_uploaded=final_up):
            log.append("🎯 Целевая планка (Goal) синхронизирована с новым объёмом.")

        log.append("🎉 Метаданные атомарно записаны в .fastresume!")
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("run_fastresume_merge", e)
        log.append(f"❌ Ошибка: {e}")
        if was_running and not is_qb_running():
            launch_qbittorrent(exe_path)
        return False, log, None, 500, None

    if was_running and not is_qb_running():
        log.append("🚀 Перезапуск qBittorrent...")
        launch_qbittorrent(exe_path)

    confirm_info = None
    if d_mode != 'none':
        log.append("🔎 Проверка подхвата клиентом и путей файлов...")
        confirm_info, warn = _evaluate_delete_plan(old_hash, new_hash, d_mode, expected_up, live_before)
        if warn:
            log.append(warn)
        elif confirm_info:
            if confirm_info['method'] == 'disk_only':
                log.append("ℹ️ WebUI недоступен: удаление файлов заблокировано (только .fastresume/.torrent).")
            elif confirm_info['downgraded']:
                log.append("🛡️ Пути пересекаются — удаление понижено до deleteFiles=false.")
            else:
                log.append("⚡ Готов к безопасному удалению исходника.")

    calc_info = {'source_up': old_up, 'target_up': new_up, 'final_up': final_up, 'calc_fmt': f"{format_file_size(old_up)} + {format_file_size(new_up)} = {format_file_size(final_up)}"}
    return True, log, confirm_info, 200, calc_info


def execute_source_deletion(old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode):
    mode = delete_mode if delete_mode in ('safe', 'force') else 'safe'
    old_file = _find_fastresume_file(old_hash)
    new_file = _find_fastresume_file(new_hash)
    if not new_file or not os.path.exists(new_file):
        return False, ["❌ Получатель не найден на диске, удаление отменено."]

    with open(new_file, 'rb') as f:
        new_disk = decode_bencode(f.read()) or {}
    expected_up = int(_get_bencode_val(new_disk, 'total_uploaded') or 0)

    plan, err = _evaluate_delete_plan(old_hash, new_hash, mode, expected_up, {})
    if err or not plan:
        return False, [err or "❌ Не удалось подтвердить состояние получателя."]

    if plan['method'] == 'webui':
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        ok = delete_torrents(ip, port, old_hash, delete_files=plan['delete_files'])
        force_clear_cache()
        if ok:
            act = "с файлами на диске (deleteFiles=true)" if plan['delete_files'] else "без удаления файлов (deleteFiles=false)"
            write_log('merger', f"Удалён исходник {old_hash} ({act})")
            return True, [f"🗑️ Исходник удалён через WebUI {act}."]
        return False, ["❌ WebUI отклонил команду удаления."]

    removed = []
    if old_file and os.path.exists(old_file):
        os.remove(old_file)
        removed.append(os.path.basename(old_file))
    for ext in ('.torrent', '.fastresume.bak'):
        cand = os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, old_hash.lower() + ext)
        if os.path.exists(cand):
            os.remove(cand)
            removed.append(os.path.basename(cand))
    force_clear_cache()
    return True, [f"🗑️ Удалены метаданные исходника: {', '.join(removed) or 'ничего'}."]

================================================================================
FILE: core/file_fts_index.py

# core/file_fts_index.py
import os
import sqlite3
import threading
import urllib.parse
from utils.bencode import decode_bencode
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, BT_BACKUP_PATH, format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.qb_api import get_torrent_files

_FTS_DB = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "file_index.db")
_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_HAS_FTS5 = True


def _get_conn():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(_FTS_DB, timeout=30.0)
    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
    conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 30000;")
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn


def _init_fts():
    global _HAS_FTS5
    with _LOCK:
        try:
            with _get_conn() as conn:
                try:
                    conn.execute("CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS files_fts USING fts5(hash_id UNINDEXED, torrent_name UNINDEXED, file_path, file_size UNINDEXED)")
                    _HAS_FTS5 = True
                except sqlite3.OperationalError:
                    _HAS_FTS5 = False
                    conn.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS files_plain (hash_id TEXT, torrent_name TEXT, file_path TEXT, file_size INTEGER)")
                    conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_fp_path ON files_plain(file_path)")
                conn.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS indexed_hashes (hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, mtime REAL NOT NULL)")
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("init_fts", e)


_init_fts()


def _extract_files_from_torrent(fpath):
    with open(fpath, 'rb') as f:
        data = decode_bencode(f.read()) or {}
    info = data.get('info') or data.get(b'info') or {}
    if not isinstance(info, dict):
        return '', []
    raw_nm = info.get('name.utf-8') or info.get('name') or info.get(b'name') or ''
    t_name = raw_nm.decode('utf-8', errors='replace') if isinstance(raw_nm, bytes) else str(raw_nm)

    if 'length' in info or b'length' in info:
        sz = int(info.get('length') or info.get(b'length') or 0)
        return t_name, [(t_name, sz)]

    files = []
    for entry in (info.get('files') or info.get(b'files') or []):
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            continue
        sz = int(entry.get('length') or entry.get(b'length') or 0)
        parts = entry.get('path.utf-8') or entry.get('path') or entry.get(b'path') or []
        rel = "/".join(p.decode('utf-8', errors='replace') if isinstance(p, bytes) else str(p) for p in parts)
        files.append((rel, sz))

    # Лимит для гигантских паков (>5000 файлов) — индексируем топ-500 крупнейших
    if len(files) > 5000:
        files.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
        files = files[:500]
    return t_name, files


def reindex_torrent_files(force=False):
    """#45 Индексация файлов локальных .torrent из BT_backup без нагрузки на API qB."""
    if not os.path.isdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'BT_backup не найден'}

    with _LOCK:
        with _get_conn() as conn:
            known = {} if force else {r['hash_id']: r['mtime'] for r in conn.execute("SELECT hash_id, mtime FROM indexed_hashes").fetchall()}
            if force:
                tbl = "files_fts" if _HAS_FTS5 else "files_plain"
                conn.execute(f"DELETE FROM {tbl}")
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM indexed_hashes")
                conn.commit()

    indexed_cnt = 0
    for ent in os.scandir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        if not ent.is_file() or not ent.name.lower().endswith('.torrent'):
            continue
        hl = ent.name[:-8].lower()
        mtime = ent.stat().st_mtime
        if hl in known and abs(known[hl] - mtime) < 1.0:
            continue
        try:
            t_name, files = _extract_files_from_torrent(ent.path)
            if not files:
                continue
            with _LOCK:
                with _get_conn() as conn:
                    tbl = "files_fts" if _HAS_FTS5 else "files_plain"
                    conn.execute(f"DELETE FROM {tbl} WHERE hash_id = ?", (hl,))
                    conn.executemany(
                        f"INSERT INTO {tbl} (hash_id, torrent_name, file_path, file_size) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                        [(hl, t_name, fp, sz) for fp, sz in files]
                    )
                    conn.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO indexed_hashes (hash_id, mtime) VALUES (?, ?)", (hl, mtime))
                    conn.commit()
            indexed_cnt += 1
        except Exception:
            continue

    return {'success': True, 'indexed': indexed_cnt, 'fts5': _HAS_FTS5}


def search_files_fts(query, limit=25, hidden_hashes=None):
    q = str(query or '').strip()
    if len(q) < 2:
        return []
    hidden = {h.lower() for h in (hidden_hashes or set())}
    out = []
    with _LOCK:
        try:
            with _get_conn() as conn:
                if _HAS_FTS5:
                    tokens = [f'"{tok.replace(chr(34), "")}"*' for tok in q.split() if tok.strip()]
                    if not tokens:
                        return []
                    rows = conn.execute("SELECT hash_id, torrent_name, file_path, file_size FROM files_fts WHERE files_fts MATCH ? LIMIT ?", (" ".join(tokens), limit * 3)).fetchall()
                else:
                    rows = conn.execute("SELECT hash_id, torrent_name, file_path, file_size FROM files_plain WHERE file_path LIKE ? LIMIT ?", (f"%{q}%", limit * 3)).fetchall()
                for r in rows:
                    if r['hash_id'] in hidden:
                        continue
                    out.append({
                        'hash': r['hash_id'],
                        'torrent_name': r['torrent_name'],
                        'file_path': r['file_path'],
                        'size_fmt': format_file_size(int(r['file_size'] or 0))
                    })
                    if len(out) >= limit:
                        break
        except Exception:
            pass
    return out


def build_treemap_data(summaries, live, group_by='tags', hidden_hashes=None):
    """#22 Карта тяжеловесов (Treemap) по тегам, трекерам или торрентам."""
    hidden = {h.lower() for h in (hidden_hashes or set())}
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in (live or {}).items()}
    buckets = {}

    for s in summaries:
        hl = (s.get('hash_id') or '').lower()
        if not hl or hl in hidden:
            continue
        lv = live_lower.get(hl, {})
        sz = int(lv.get('total_size', s.get('size', 0)) or 0)
        if sz <= 0:
            continue

        if group_by == 'torrents':
            key = str(lv.get('name') or s.get('name') or hl)[:40]
        elif group_by == 'trackers':
            tr = str(lv.get('tracker') or (s['trackers'][0] if s.get('trackers') else ''))
            key = urllib.parse.urlparse(tr).netloc.lower().split(':')[0] if tr else 'DHT / Без трекера'
        else:
            raw_tags = str(lv.get('tags') or '').strip()
            key = raw_tags.split(',')[0].strip() if raw_tags else 'Без тега'

        buckets[key] = buckets.get(key, 0) + sz

    sorted_b = sorted(buckets.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:18]
    return [{'label': k, 'bytes': v, 'gb': round(v / (1024 ** 3), 2), 'fmt': format_file_size(v)} for k, v in sorted_b]


def get_torrent_files_paginated(ip, port, t_hash, page=1, per_page=100):
    files = get_torrent_files(ip, port, t_hash) if port else []
    total = len(files)
    p = max(1, int(page))
    pp = max(10, min(200, int(per_page)))
    slice_items = files[(p - 1) * pp: p * pp]
    out = []
    for idx, f in enumerate(slice_items, start=(p - 1) * pp):
        out.append({
            'id': int(f.get('index', idx)),
            'name': str(f.get('name', '')),
            'size_fmt': format_file_size(f.get('size', 0)),
            'progress': round(float(f.get('progress', 0.0) or 0.0) * 100.0, 1),
            'priority': int(f.get('priority', 1) or 0)
        })
    return {'files': out, 'total': total, 'page': p, 'total_pages': max(1, (total + pp - 1) // pp)}

================================================================================
