# core/forecaster.py
import os
import time
import csv
import datetime
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, parse_bytes, parse_speed, sanitize_csv_cell, write_error_log


def fmt_ratio_h(val):
    if val is None or val <= 0:
        return "0.000 /ч"
    if val < 0.001:
        return f"{val:.4f} /ч"
    if val < 1.0:
        return f"{val:.3f} /ч"
    return f"{val:.2f} /ч"


def fmt_speed(bps):
    if bps is None or bps <= 0:
        return "0 B/s"
    return f"{format_file_size(bps)}/s"


def calculate_smart_forecast(db, target_type, target_hash, target_str, speed_str, summaries):
    try:
        is_seedbox = (target_type == 'all' or not target_hash)
        t_hash_lower = (target_hash or '').lower()

        if is_seedbox:
            current_up = sum(s.get('uploaded', 0) for s in summaries)
            total_sz = sum(s.get('size', 0) for s in summaries)
            name = "🌐 Весь сидбокс (суммарно)"
            target_bytes = parse_bytes(target_str, torrent_size=total_sz)
        else:
            t_item = next(
                (s for s in summaries if (str(s.get('hash_id', '')).lower() == t_hash_lower or
                                         str(s.get('hash', '')).lower() == t_hash_lower)),
                None
            )
            if not t_item:
                return {'success': False, 'error': "Выбранная раздача не найдена!"}
            current_up = t_item.get('uploaded', 0)
            total_sz = t_item.get('size', 0)
            name = t_item.get('name', target_hash)
            target_bytes = parse_bytes(target_str, torrent_size=total_sz)

        remaining_bytes = max(0, target_bytes - current_up)
        if remaining_bytes == 0:
            return {
                'success': True, 'achieved': True, 'name': name,
                'current_up_fmt': format_file_size(current_up),
                'target_fmt': format_file_size(target_bytes),
                'message': "Цель уже достигнута!"
            }

        is_manual = bool(speed_str and speed_str.strip().lower() not in ('авто', 'auto', ''))
        manual_speed = 0
        if is_manual:
            manual_speed = parse_speed(speed_str)
            if manual_speed <= 0:
                return {'success': False, 'error': "Скорость должна быть больше 0!"}

        raw_w1 = max(0, cfg.getint('Weight_W1', 70))
        raw_w2 = max(0, cfg.getint('Weight_W2', 30))
        raw_w3 = max(0, cfg.getint('Weight_W3', 0))
        total_w = raw_w1 + raw_w2 + raw_w3

        if not is_manual and total_w <= 0:
            return {'success': False, 'error': "Все веса расчёта равны 0! Задайте их в ⚙️ Настройки."}

        nw1 = raw_w1 / total_w if total_w > 0 else 0.0
        nw2 = raw_w2 / total_w if total_w > 0 else 0.0
        nw3 = raw_w3 / total_w if total_w > 0 else 0.0

        req_days = 21 if raw_w3 > 0 else (14 if raw_w2 > 0 else 7)
        now_ts = time.time()

        cnt, min_ts = db.get_snapshot_range_info()
        days_available = (now_ts - min_ts) / 86400.0 if (cnt and min_ts) else 0.0

        if not is_manual and (cnt < 2 or days_available < (req_days - 0.15)):
            return {
                'success': False, 'need_manual': True,
                'days_available': round(days_available, 1),
                'req_days': req_days,
                'error': f"Недостаточно данных (накоплено {days_available:.1f} из {req_days} дн.). Укажите скорость вручную."
            }

        forecast_24h_rows = []
        projected_24h_volume = 0

        for i in range(24):
            h_start = now_ts + (i * 3600)
            h_end = h_start + 3600
            s_dt = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(h_start)
            e_dt = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(h_end)

            if is_manual:
                vol_hour = int(manual_speed * 3600)
                speed_hour = manual_speed
                basis_text = "Ручной ввод скорости"
            else:
                d1 = db.get_hist_delta(h_start - (168 * 3600), h_end - (168 * 3600), is_seedbox, t_hash_lower) if nw1 > 0 else 0
                d2 = db.get_hist_delta(h_start - (336 * 3600), h_end - (336 * 3600), is_seedbox, t_hash_lower) if nw2 > 0 else 0
                d3 = db.get_hist_delta(h_start - (504 * 3600), h_end - (504 * 3600), is_seedbox, t_hash_lower) if nw3 > 0 else 0
                vol_hour = int((nw1 * d1) + (nw2 * d2) + (nw3 * d3))
                speed_hour = vol_hour / 3600.0
                parts = []
                if nw1 > 0: parts.append(f"1нед: {d1/(1024**2):.1f}МБ ({nw1*100:.0f}%)")
                if nw2 > 0: parts.append(f"2нед: {d2/(1024**2):.1f}МБ ({nw2*100:.0f}%)")
                if nw3 > 0: parts.append(f"3нед: {d3/(1024**2):.1f}МБ ({nw3*100:.0f}%)")
                basis_text = " + ".join(parts)

            projected_24h_volume += vol_hour
            forecast_24h_rows.append({
                'date': s_dt.strftime('%d.%m'),
                'hour': f"{s_dt.strftime('%H:00')} – {e_dt.strftime('%H:00')}",
                'volume_bytes': vol_hour,
                'volume_mb': f"{vol_hour / (1024 * 1024):.2f} МБ",
                'speed_fmt': fmt_speed(speed_hour),
                'basis': basis_text
            })

        max_seconds = 3650 * 86400
        eta_dt = None
        eta_str = ""

        if is_manual:
            seconds_needed = int(remaining_bytes / manual_speed) if manual_speed > 0 else max_seconds + 1
            if seconds_needed <= max_seconds:
                eta_dt = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(now_ts + seconds_needed)
            else:
                eta_str = f"Более 10 лет (~{seconds_needed // (365*86400)} лет)"
        else:
            simulated_ts = now_ts
            simulated_rem = remaining_bytes
            idx = 0
            while simulated_rem > 0 and (simulated_ts - now_ts) < (365 * 86400):
                hour_vol = forecast_24h_rows[idx % 24]['volume_bytes']
                if hour_vol <= 0:
                    hour_vol = max(1, int(projected_24h_volume / 24)) if projected_24h_volume > 0 else 1024
                if simulated_rem <= hour_vol:
                    simulated_ts += int((simulated_rem / hour_vol) * 3600)
                    simulated_rem = 0
                    break
                simulated_rem -= hour_vol
                simulated_ts += 3600
                idx += 1
            if simulated_rem == 0:
                eta_dt = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(simulated_ts)
            else:
                eta_str = "Более 1 года при текущем темпе"

        if eta_dt:
            eta_str = eta_dt.strftime('%A, %d.%m.%Y в %H:%M')
            ru_days = {'Monday': 'Понедельник', 'Tuesday': 'Вторник', 'Wednesday': 'Среда',
                       'Thursday': 'Четверг', 'Friday': 'Пятница', 'Saturday': 'Суббота', 'Sunday': 'Воскресенье'}
            for eng, ru in ru_days.items():
                eta_str = eta_str.replace(eng, ru)

        weights_desc = f"{nw1*100:.0f}% / {nw2*100:.0f}% / {nw3*100:.0f}% ({req_days} дн.)"

        return {
            'success': True, 'achieved': False, 'name': name,
            'target_type': target_type, 'is_seedbox': is_seedbox, 'is_manual': is_manual,
            'weights_desc': weights_desc,
            'current_up_fmt': format_file_size(current_up),
            'target_fmt': format_file_size(target_bytes),
            'remaining_fmt': format_file_size(remaining_bytes),
            'forecast_24h_vol_fmt': format_file_size(projected_24h_volume),
            'ratio_current': round(current_up / total_sz, 2) if total_sz > 0 else 0.0,
            'ratio_after_24h': round((current_up + projected_24h_volume) / total_sz, 2) if total_sz > 0 else 0.0,
            'eta_str': eta_str,
            'forecast_rows': forecast_24h_rows
        }
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("SmartCalc Error", e)
        return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}


def export_forecast_csv(manual_folder, forecast_data):
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
    raw_name = str(forecast_data.get('name', 'forecast'))
    clean_name = "".join(c for c in raw_name if c.isalnum() or c in (' ', '_', '-')).strip()[:30] or 'forecast'
    filename = f"forecast_24h_{clean_name}_{ts}.csv"
    filepath = os.path.join(manual_folder, filename)

    lines = [
        f"# === ПОЧАСОВОЙ ПРОГНОЗ ОТДАЧИ НА 24 ЧАСА ===",
        f"# Объект: {sanitize_csv_cell(forecast_data.get('name', ''))}",
        f"# Дата расчета: {datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}",
        f"# Цель: {forecast_data.get('target_fmt', '')} | Осталось: {forecast_data.get('remaining_fmt', '')}",
        f"# Ожидаемая отдача за следующие 24ч: {forecast_data.get('forecast_24h_vol_fmt', '')}",
        f"# Ожидаемое время достижения цели: {forecast_data.get('eta_str', '')}",
        f"# =========================================="
    ]

    try:
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8-sig', newline='') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines) + '\n')
            w = csv.writer(f, delimiter=';')
            w.writerow(['Дата', 'Интервал (час)', 'Ожидаемый объём (МБ)', 'Ожидаемая скорость', 'Обоснование расчёта'])
            for r in forecast_data.get('forecast_rows', [])[:100]:
                w.writerow([
                    sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('date', '')),
                    sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('hour', '')),
                    sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('volume_mb', '')),
                    sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('speed_fmt', '')),
                    sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('basis', ''))
                ])
        return filepath, filename
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Ошибка экспорта прогноза в CSV", e)
        return None, None

================================================================================
FILE: core/hdd_smart_queue.py

# core/hdd_smart_queue.py
import os
import json
import time
import shutil
import subprocess
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.qb_api import recheck_torrents
from core.disk_monitor import _get_mount_key

_WIN_FLAGS = getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0)
_CHECKING_STATES = {'checkingup', 'checkingdl', 'checkingresumedata'}


def _ensure_table():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recheck_queue (
                        hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                        drive_key TEXT NOT NULL,
                        name TEXT NOT NULL,
                        added_at REAL NOT NULL
                    )
                """)
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("recheck_queue init", e)


_ensure_table()


def enqueue_recheck(t_hash, save_path='', name=''):
    hl = str(t_hash or '').strip().lower()
    if not hl:
        return False
    dkey = _get_mount_key(save_path) if save_path and os.path.exists(save_path) else 'default'
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO recheck_queue (hash_id, drive_key, name, added_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                    (hl, dkey, str(name or hl)[:160], time.time())
                )
                conn.commit()
                return True
        except Exception:
            return False


def list_recheck_queue():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                return [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM recheck_queue ORDER BY added_at ASC").fetchall()]
        except Exception:
            return []


def tick_recheck_queue(ip, port, live):
    """Гарантирует не более 1 активного речека на физический диск."""
    if not port or not live:
        return 0
    queue = list_recheck_queue()
    if not queue:
        return 0

    busy_drives = set()
    live_map = {}
    for raw_h, info in live.items():
        live_map[raw_h.lower()] = (raw_h, info)
        if str(info.get('state', '')).lower() in _CHECKING_STATES:
            sp = str(info.get('save_path') or '')
            busy_drives.add(_get_mount_key(sp) if sp and os.path.exists(sp) else 'default')

    started = 0
    for item in queue:
        hl, dkey = item['hash_id'], item['drive_key']
        if hl not in live_map:
            with app_state_db._lock:
                with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("DELETE FROM recheck_queue WHERE hash_id = ?", (hl,))
                    conn.commit()
            continue
        if dkey in busy_drives:
            continue
        raw_h, _ = live_map[hl]
        if recheck_torrents(ip, port, raw_h):
            busy_drives.add(dkey)
            started += 1
            with app_state_db._lock:
                with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("DELETE FROM recheck_queue WHERE hash_id = ?", (hl,))
                    conn.commit()
    return started


def get_smart_disks_status():
    """Опциональный SMART-монитор через smartctl с безопасным фоллбэком."""
    exe = shutil.which('smartctl')
    if not exe:
        return {'available': False, 'reason': 'Утилита smartctl (smartmontools) не установлена в системе', 'disks': []}

    try:
        scan = subprocess.run([exe, '--scan', '-j'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=4.0, creationflags=_WIN_FLAGS)
        data = json.loads(scan.stdout or '{}')
        devs = [d.get('name') for d in (data.get('devices') or []) if d.get('name')]
    except Exception as e:
        return {'available': False, 'reason': f'Нет доступа к SMART (нужны права Администратора/root): {e}', 'disks': []}

    disks = []
    for dev in devs[:10]:
        try:
            r = subprocess.run([exe, '-A', '-H', '-j', dev], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=4.0, creationflags=_WIN_FLAGS)
            info = json.loads(r.stdout or '{}')
            passed = (info.get('smart_status') or {}).get('passed')
            temp = (info.get('temperature') or {}).get('current')
            model = info.get('model_name') or dev
            disks.append({'device': dev, 'model': model, 'passed': passed, 'temp_c': temp})
        except Exception:
            continue

    if not disks:
        return {'available': False, 'reason': 'Не удалось прочитать SMART-атрибуты (требуются повышенные права)', 'disks': []}
    return {'available': True, 'disks': disks}

# core/health_score.py
from core.torrent_targets import compute_target_progress


def compute_health_metrics(live_item):
    if not live_item:
        return {
            'health_score': -1.0,
            'health_label': 'нет данных',
            'num_complete': -1,
            'num_incomplete': -1,
            'is_last_seed': False,
        }

    num_complete = int(live_item.get('num_complete', -1) if live_item.get('num_complete') is not None else -1)
    num_incomplete = int(live_item.get('num_incomplete', -1) if live_item.get('num_incomplete') is not None else -1)
    progress = float(live_item.get('progress', 0.0) or 0.0)

    if num_complete < 0 or num_incomplete < 0:
        return {
            'health_score': -1.0,
            'health_label': 'нет данных',
            'num_complete': num_complete,
            'num_incomplete': num_incomplete,
            'is_last_seed': False,
        }

    if num_complete == 0 and num_incomplete > 0:
        score = round(num_incomplete * 100.0 + 50.0, 1)
    else:
        score = round((num_incomplete * 100.0) / (num_complete + 1), 1)

    is_last_seed = (progress >= 0.9999 and num_complete == 0)
    return {
        'health_score': score,
        'health_label': f"{score:.1f}",
        'num_complete': num_complete,
        'num_incomplete': num_incomplete,
        'is_last_seed': is_last_seed,
    }


def enrich_live_metrics(item, live_item, target_row=None):
    hm = compute_health_metrics(live_item)
    item.update(hm)
    item['upspeed'] = int((live_item or {}).get('upspeed', 0) or 0)
    item['dlspeed'] = int((live_item or {}).get('dlspeed', 0) or 0)
    item['progress'] = float((live_item or {}).get('progress', 0.0) or 0.0)
    item['target'] = compute_target_progress(
        target_row,
        int(item.get('uploaded', 0) or 0),
        float(item.get('ratio', 0.0) or 0.0)
    )
    return item

# core/history_db.py
import os
import time
import sqlite3
import datetime
import threading
from utils.helpers import LOG_FOLDER, write_log, write_error_log
from core.history_migrations import migrate_json_if_needed, migrate_old_csvs_if_needed

AUTO_FOLDER = os.path.join(LOG_FOLDER, "auto_hourly")
MANUAL_FOLDER = os.path.join(LOG_FOLDER, "manual_exports")

os.makedirs(AUTO_FOLDER, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(MANUAL_FOLDER, exist_ok=True)


class HistoryDB:
    def __init__(self, auto_folder=AUTO_FOLDER):
        self.auto_folder = auto_folder
        self.db_file = os.path.join(self.auto_folder, "summary_history.db")
        self.old_json_file = os.path.join(self.auto_folder, "summary_history.json")
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

        self._init_db()
        with self._lock:
            migrate_json_if_needed(self._get_connection, self.old_json_file)
            migrate_old_csvs_if_needed(self._get_connection, self.auto_folder)

    def _get_connection(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_file, timeout=30.0)
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 30000;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;")
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        return conn

    def _init_db(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    conn.executescript("""
                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS snapshots (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            timestamp REAL NOT NULL,
                            datetime TEXT NOT NULL,
                            total_uploaded INTEGER NOT NULL,
                            total_size INTEGER NOT NULL,
                            count INTEGER NOT NULL,
                            ignore_in_stats INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
                        );
                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS torrent_snapshots (
                            snapshot_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                            hash_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                            uploaded INTEGER NOT NULL,
                            FOREIGN KEY(snapshot_id) REFERENCES snapshots(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
                        );
                    """)
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("PRAGMA table_info(snapshots)")
                    cols = {r['name'] for r in cur.fetchall()}
                    if 'ignore_in_stats' not in cols:
                        cur.execute("ALTER TABLE snapshots ADD COLUMN ignore_in_stats INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")

                    conn.executescript("""
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_snapshots_ts ON snapshots(timestamp);
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_snapshots_ts_up ON snapshots(timestamp, id, total_uploaded);
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tor_snap ON torrent_snapshots(snapshot_id, hash_id);
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tor_hash ON torrent_snapshots(hash_id);
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tor_hash_snap ON torrent_snapshots(hash_id, snapshot_id, uploaded);
                    """)
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Инициализация SQLite summary_history.db", e)

    def set_snapshot_ignored(self, snap_id, ignored=True):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.execute(
                        "UPDATE snapshots SET ignore_in_stats = ? WHERE id = ?",
                        (1 if ignored else 0, int(snap_id))
                    )
                    conn.commit()
                    return cur.rowcount > 0
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("set_snapshot_ignored", e)
                return False

    def get_last_snapshot_ts(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    row = conn.execute("SELECT MAX(timestamp) FROM snapshots WHERE COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0").fetchone()
                    return row[0] if row and row[0] is not None else None
            except Exception:
                return None

    def record_snapshot(self, summaries, min_delta_sec=60):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                now = time.time()
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT MAX(timestamp) FROM snapshots")
                    row = cur.fetchone()
                    last_ts = row[0] if row and row[0] is not None else None
                    if last_ts is not None and (now - last_ts) < min_delta_sec:
                        return False

                    dt_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
                    tot_up = sum(s.get('uploaded', 0) for s in summaries)
                    tot_sz = sum(s.get('size', 0) for s in summaries)
                    cur.execute(
                        "INSERT INTO snapshots (timestamp, datetime, total_uploaded, total_size, count, ignore_in_stats) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)",
                        (now, dt_str, tot_up, tot_sz, len(summaries))
                    )
                    snap_id = cur.lastrowid

                    tor_rows = [
                        (snap_id, (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower(), int(s.get('uploaded', 0)))
                        for s in summaries if (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash'))
                    ]
                    if tor_rows:
                        cur.executemany("INSERT INTO torrent_snapshots (snapshot_id, hash_id, uploaded) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", tor_rows)
                    conn.commit()
                    return True
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка записи record_snapshot в SQLite", e)
                return False

    def downsample_old_snapshots(self):
        now = time.time()
        cutoff_hourly = now - (31 * 86400)
        cutoff_daily = now - (365 * 86400)

        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT id, timestamp, datetime FROM snapshots WHERE timestamp < ? ORDER BY timestamp ASC", (cutoff_hourly,))
                    rows = cur.fetchall()
                    if not rows:
                        return 0

                    buckets = {}
                    for r in rows:
                        ts = r['timestamp']
                        dt_str = r['datetime'] or datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(ts).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
                        key = ('day', dt_str[:10]) if ts < cutoff_daily else ('hour', dt_str[:13])
                        buckets.setdefault(key, []).append((ts, r['id']))

                    delete_ids = []
                    for items in buckets.values():
                        if len(items) > 1:
                            items.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
                            delete_ids.extend(item[1] for item in items[:-1])

                    if delete_ids:
                        for i in range(0, len(delete_ids), 500):
                            chunk = delete_ids[i:i + 500]
                            placeholders = ','.join('?' for _ in chunk)
                            cur.execute(f"DELETE FROM snapshots WHERE id IN ({placeholders})", chunk)
                        conn.commit()
                        write_log("summary", f"Даунсэмплинг SQLite: сжато старых снимков: {len(delete_ids)}")
                    return len(delete_ids)
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка downsample_old_snapshots", e)
                return 0

    def get_stats_snapshots(self, now_ts):
        latest_snap = prev_snap = snap_1h = snap_24h = None
        snap_1h_torrents, snap_24h_torrents = {}, {}

        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT id, timestamp, total_uploaded FROM snapshots WHERE COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 2")
                    last_rows = cur.fetchall()
                    if last_rows:
                        latest_snap = dict(last_rows[0])
                        if len(last_rows) > 1:
                            prev_snap = dict(last_rows[1])

                    cur.execute("SELECT id, timestamp, total_uploaded FROM snapshots WHERE COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 AND timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY ABS((? - timestamp) - 3600) ASC LIMIT 1", (now_ts - 7200, now_ts - 1800, now_ts))
                    r_1h = cur.fetchone()
                    if r_1h:
                        snap_1h = dict(r_1h)
                        cur.execute("SELECT hash_id, uploaded FROM torrent_snapshots WHERE snapshot_id = ?", (snap_1h['id'],))
                        snap_1h_torrents = {row['hash_id']: row['uploaded'] for row in cur.fetchall()}

                    cur.execute("SELECT id, timestamp, total_uploaded FROM snapshots WHERE COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 AND timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY ABS((? - timestamp) - 86400) ASC LIMIT 1", (now_ts - 100000, now_ts - 72000, now_ts))
                    r_24h = cur.fetchone()
                    if r_24h:
                        snap_24h = dict(r_24h)
                        cur.execute("SELECT hash_id, uploaded FROM torrent_snapshots WHERE snapshot_id = ?", (snap_24h['id'],))
                        snap_24h_torrents = {row['hash_id']: row['uploaded'] for row in cur.fetchall()}
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_stats_snapshots из SQLite", e)

        return latest_snap, prev_snap, snap_1h, snap_1h_torrents, snap_24h, snap_24h_torrents

    def get_snapshots_since(self, since_ts):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT id, timestamp, datetime, total_uploaded, count FROM snapshots WHERE COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 AND timestamp >= ? ORDER BY timestamp ASC", (since_ts,))
                    return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_snapshots_since из SQLite", e)
                return []

    def get_torrents_positive_deltas(self, since_ts, allowed_hashes=None):
        totals = {}
        allowed = {h.lower() for h in allowed_hashes} if allowed_hashes is not None else None
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("""
                        SELECT ts.hash_id, s.timestamp, ts.uploaded
                        FROM torrent_snapshots ts
                        JOIN snapshots s ON s.id = ts.snapshot_id
                        WHERE COALESCE(s.ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 AND s.timestamp >= ?
                        ORDER BY ts.hash_id ASC, s.timestamp ASC
                    """, (since_ts,))
                    cur_hash = prev_up = prev_ts = None
                    for row in cur:
                        h = row['hash_id']
                        if allowed is not None and h not in allowed:
                            continue
                        up, ts = int(row['uploaded'] or 0), float(row['timestamp'] or 0)
                        if h != cur_hash:
                            cur_hash, prev_up, prev_ts = h, up, ts
                            continue
                        d, dt = up - prev_up, max(1.0, ts - prev_ts)
                        if d > 0:
                            bps = d / dt
                            if not ((bps > 1073741824) or (prev_up < 104857600 and d > 5368709120 and bps > 314572800)):
                                totals[h] = totals.get(h, 0) + d
                        prev_up, prev_ts = up, ts
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_torrents_positive_deltas", e)
        return totals

    def get_snapshot_range_info(self):
        with self._lock:
            with self._get_connection() as conn:
                return conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*), MIN(timestamp) FROM snapshots WHERE COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0").fetchone()

    def get_hist_delta(self, t_start, t_end, is_seedbox, t_hash_lower=''):
        with self._lock:
            with self._get_connection() as conn:
                cur = conn.cursor()
                cur.execute("SELECT id, total_uploaded FROM snapshots WHERE COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 ORDER BY ABS(timestamp - ?) ASC LIMIT 1", (t_start,))
                r1 = cur.fetchone()
                cur.execute("SELECT id, total_uploaded FROM snapshots WHERE COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 ORDER BY ABS(timestamp - ?) ASC LIMIT 1", (t_end,))
                r2 = cur.fetchone()
                if not r1 or not r2:
                    return 0
                if is_seedbox:
                    return max(0, r2['total_uploaded'] - r1['total_uploaded'])
                u1_row = cur.execute("SELECT uploaded FROM torrent_snapshots WHERE snapshot_id = ? AND hash_id = ? LIMIT 1", (r1['id'], t_hash_lower)).fetchone()
                u2_row = cur.execute("SELECT uploaded FROM torrent_snapshots WHERE snapshot_id = ? AND hash_id = ? LIMIT 1", (r2['id'], t_hash_lower)).fetchone()
                return max(0, (u2_row['uploaded'] if u2_row else 0) - (u1_row['uploaded'] if u1_row else 0))

    def get_torrent_history_rows(self, t_hash, limit_hours=36):
        t_hash_lower = (t_hash or '').lower()
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    if t_hash == 'all':
                        cur.execute("SELECT datetime, timestamp, total_uploaded AS uploaded FROM snapshots WHERE COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?", (limit_hours,))
                    else:
                        cur.execute("SELECT s.datetime, s.timestamp, ts.uploaded FROM snapshots s JOIN torrent_snapshots ts ON s.id = ts.snapshot_id WHERE COALESCE(s.ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 AND ts.hash_id = ? ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT ?", (t_hash_lower, limit_hours))
                    return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_torrent_history_rows из SQLite", e)
                return []

    def get_torrent_window_bounds(self, t_hash, since_ts, until_ts):
        t_hash_lower = (t_hash or '').lower()
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    rows = conn.execute("SELECT s.timestamp, ts.uploaded FROM snapshots s JOIN torrent_snapshots ts ON s.id = ts.snapshot_id WHERE COALESCE(s.ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 AND ts.hash_id = ? AND s.timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY s.timestamp ASC", (t_hash_lower, since_ts, until_ts)).fetchall()
                    if not rows:
                        return None, None, 0
                    return (rows[0]['timestamp'], rows[0]['uploaded']), (rows[-1]['timestamp'], rows[-1]['uploaded']), len(rows)
            except Exception:
                return None, None, 0

    def get_available_snapshot_days(self, limit=90):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    rows = conn.execute("SELECT DISTINCT substr(datetime, 1, 10) AS day FROM snapshots WHERE length(datetime) >= 10 ORDER BY day DESC LIMIT ?", (int(limit),)).fetchall()
                    return [r['day'] for r in rows if r['day']]
            except Exception:
                return []

    def get_snapshots_by_day_paginated(self, start_ts, end_ts, page=1, per_page=15):
        page, per_page = max(1, int(page)), max(1, min(100, int(per_page)))
        offset = (page - 1) * per_page
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    total = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM snapshots WHERE timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ?", (start_ts, end_ts)).fetchone()[0]
                    rows = conn.execute("SELECT id, timestamp, datetime, total_uploaded, total_size, count, COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) AS ignore_in_stats FROM snapshots WHERE timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?", (start_ts, end_ts, per_page, offset)).fetchall()
                    return {'items': [dict(r) for r in rows], 'total': total, 'page': page, 'per_page': per_page, 'total_pages': max(1, (total + per_page - 1) // per_page)}
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_snapshots_by_day_paginated", e)
                return {'items': [], 'total': 0, 'page': 1, 'per_page': per_page, 'total_pages': 1}

    def get_snapshots_by_day_all(self, start_ts, end_ts):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    rows = conn.execute("SELECT datetime, total_uploaded, total_size, count FROM snapshots WHERE COALESCE(ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 AND timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY timestamp ASC", (start_ts, end_ts)).fetchall()
                    return [dict(r) for r in rows]
            except Exception:
                return []

================================================================================
FILE: core/history_migrations.py
================================================================================
# core/history_migrations.py
import os
import re
import csv
import json
import datetime
from utils.helpers import LOG_FOLDER, write_log, write_error_log

_DATE_COMMENT_RE = re.compile(r'^#\s*Дата выгрузки:\s*(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\s+\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2})')
_FNAME_DATE_RE = re.compile(r'summary_hourly_(\d{8})_(\d{4})\.csv$', re.IGNORECASE)


def migrate_json_if_needed(get_conn_fn, old_json_file):
    if not os.path.exists(old_json_file):
        return
    try:
        with get_conn_fn() as conn:
            cur = conn.cursor()
            cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM snapshots")
            if cur.fetchone()[0] > 0:
                return

            write_log("summary", "Обнаружен старый summary_history.json. Начинаем миграцию в SQLite...")
            with open(old_json_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                data = json.load(f)

            if isinstance(data, list):
                for s in data:
                    ts = s.get('timestamp', 0)
                    dt = s.get('datetime', '')
                    tot_up = s.get('total_uploaded', 0)
                    tot_sz = s.get('total_size', 0)
                    count = s.get('count', 0)
                    torrents = s.get('torrents', {})

                    cur.execute(
                        "INSERT INTO snapshots (timestamp, datetime, total_uploaded, total_size, count) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                        (ts, dt, tot_up, tot_sz, count)
                    )
                    snap_id = cur.lastrowid
                    if torrents and isinstance(torrents, dict):
                        rows = [(snap_id, str(h).lower(), int(up)) for h, up in torrents.items()]
                        cur.executemany(
                            "INSERT INTO torrent_snapshots (snapshot_id, hash_id, uploaded) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                            rows
                        )

            conn.commit()
            write_log("summary", "Миграция JSON в SQLite успешно завершена.")

        bak_file = old_json_file + ".migrated"
        if not os.path.exists(bak_file):
            os.rename(old_json_file, bak_file)
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Ошибка миграции summary_history.json в SQLite", e)


def migrate_old_csvs_if_needed(get_conn_fn, auto_folder):
    candidates = []
    for folder in (LOG_FOLDER, auto_folder):
        if not os.path.exists(folder):
            continue
        for fname in os.listdir(folder):
            low = fname.lower()
            if low.startswith('summary_hourly_') and low.endswith('.csv'):
                candidates.append(os.path.join(folder, fname))

    if not candidates:
        return

    imported = 0
    try:
        with get_conn_fn() as conn:
            cur = conn.cursor()
            for path in sorted(candidates):
                try:
                    with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8-sig', errors='ignore') as f:
                        lines = f.readlines()

                    snap_dt_str = None
                    data_lines = []
                    for line in lines:
                        if line.startswith('#'):
                            m = _DATE_COMMENT_RE.match(line.strip())
                            if m:
                                snap_dt_str = m.group(1)
                        elif line.strip():
                            data_lines.append(line)

                    if not data_lines:
                        continue

                    if snap_dt_str:
                        snap_ts = datetime.datetime.strptime(snap_dt_str, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S').timestamp()
                    else:
                        mf = _FNAME_DATE_RE.search(os.path.basename(path))
                        if mf:
                            dt_obj = datetime.datetime.strptime(mf.group(1) + mf.group(2), '%Y%m%d%H%M')
                            snap_ts = dt_obj.timestamp()
                            snap_dt_str = dt_obj.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
                        else:
                            snap_ts = os.path.getmtime(path)
                            snap_dt_str = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(snap_ts).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')

                    cur.execute("SELECT 1 FROM snapshots WHERE ABS(timestamp - ?) < 180 LIMIT 1", (snap_ts,))
                    if cur.fetchone():
                        continue

                    reader = csv.DictReader(data_lines, delimiter=';')
                    tor_rows = []
                    tot_up = 0
                    tot_sz = 0
                    for row in reader:
                        h_id = (row.get('Хэш') or row.get('hash_id') or '').strip().lstrip("'").lower()
                        if not h_id:
                            continue
                        try:
                            up = int(float(row.get('Отдано (байт)') or row.get('uploaded') or 0))
                            sz = int(float(row.get('Размер (байт)') or row.get('size') or 0))
                        except (ValueError, TypeError):
                            continue
                        tot_up += up
                        tot_sz += sz
                        tor_rows.append((h_id, up))

                    if not tor_rows:
                        continue

                    cur.execute(
                        "INSERT INTO snapshots (timestamp, datetime, total_uploaded, total_size, count) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                        (snap_ts, snap_dt_str, tot_up, tot_sz, len(tor_rows))
                    )
                    snap_id = cur.lastrowid
                    cur.executemany(
                        "INSERT INTO torrent_snapshots (snapshot_id, hash_id, uploaded) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                        [(snap_id, h, u) for h, u in tor_rows]
                    )
                    imported += 1
                except Exception as e:
                    write_error_log(f"Импорт старого CSV {os.path.basename(path)}", e)

            if imported > 0:
                conn.commit()
                write_log("summary", f"Импортировано старых почасовых CSV-снимков в SQLite: {imported}")
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Ошибка _migrate_old_csvs_if_needed", e)

================================================================================
FILE: core/hygiene_disk.py

# core/hygiene_disk.py
import os
import time
import datetime
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import BT_BACKUP_PATH, format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, get_torrent_files
from core.soft_trash import move_to_trash
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db

_JUNK_EXTS = {'.!qb', '.tmp', '.bak', '.nfo', '.txt', '.url', '.lnk'}
_ARCHIVE_EXTS = {'.zip', '.rar', '.7z', '.tar', '.gz'}
_SUB_EXTS = {'.srt', '.ass', '.ssa', '.vtt', '.sub'}


def scan_bt_backup_garbage():
    """Санитар BT_backup: безпарные, 0-байтные и призраки старше 24ч, отсутствующие в API qB."""
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    if not live:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'Для безопасной чистки BT_backup необходим запущенный WebUI qBittorrent.', 'items': []}
    if not os.path.isdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'Папка BT_backup не найдена', 'items': []}

    live_hashes = {h.lower() for h in live.keys()}
    now = time.time()
    min_age = 86400.0

    by_hash = {}
    for ent in os.scandir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        if not ent.is_file():
            continue
        low = ent.name.lower()
        if low.endswith('.fastresume'):
            h, kind = low[:-11], 'fastresume'
        elif low.endswith('.torrent'):
            h, kind = low[:-8], 'torrent'
        else:
            continue
        st = ent.stat()
        by_hash.setdefault(h, {})[kind] = {'path': ent.path, 'name': ent.name, 'size': st.st_size, 'mtime': st.st_mtime}

    items = []
    for h, pair in by_hash.items():
        for kind, info in pair.items():
            if (now - info['mtime']) < min_age:
                continue
            reason = None
            if info['size'] == 0:
                reason = 'Битый файл (0 байт)'
            elif h not in live_hashes:
                reason = 'Призрак (нет в qBittorrent)'
            elif len(pair) == 1:
                reason = f"Без пары (нет .{'torrent' if kind == 'fastresume' else 'fastresume'})"
            if reason:
                items.append({
                    'path': info['path'], 'name': info['name'], 'hash': h,
                    'reason': reason, 'size': info['size'], 'size_fmt': format_file_size(info['size']),
                    'mtime': info['mtime'],
                    'date': datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(info['mtime']).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')
                })

    items.sort(key=lambda x: x['mtime'])
    return {'success': True, 'items': items, 'total_size_fmt': format_file_size(sum(x['size'] for x in items))}


def _classify_inner_file(fname, progress):
    low = fname.lower()
    if low.endswith('.!qb'):
        return 'junk' if progress >= 0.9999 else 'active_part'
    if low.endswith('.parts'):
        return 'active_part'
    _, ext = os.path.splitext(low)
    if ext in _JUNK_EXTS:
        return 'junk'
    if ext in _ARCHIVE_EXTS:
        return 'archive'
    if ext in _SUB_EXTS:
        return 'subtitle'
    return 'other'


def scan_inner_content_junk(limit_torrents=80):
    """Пылесос мусора внутри content_path живых раздач с защитой кросс-сида в общей папке."""
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    if not live:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен', 'items': []}

    dir_groups = {}
    scanned = 0
    for t_hash, info in live.items():
        cp = str(info.get('content_path') or '').strip()
        if not cp or not os.path.isdir(cp):
            continue
        sp = str(info.get('save_path') or '').strip()
        if sp and os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(cp)) == os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(sp)):
            continue
        scanned += 1
        if scanned > limit_torrents:
            break

        files = get_torrent_files(ip, port, t_hash)
        if not files:
            continue

        cp_abs = os.path.abspath(cp)
        cp_key = os.path.normcase(cp_abs)
        grp = dir_groups.setdefault(cp_key, {'cp_abs': cp_abs, 'names': [], 'expected': set(), 'min_prog': 1.0})
        grp['names'].append(info.get('name', t_hash))
        grp['min_prog'] = min(grp['min_prog'], float(info.get('progress', 0.0) or 0.0))

        for f in files:
            rel = str(f.get('name', '')).replace('\\', '/').strip('/')
            parts = rel.split('/', 1)
            rel_inner = parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else parts[0]
            grp['expected'].add(os.path.normcase(rel_inner.replace('/', os.sep)))

    found = []
    seen_paths = set()
    for grp in dir_groups.values():
        cp_abs = grp['cp_abs']
        expected = grp['expected']
        prog = grp['min_prog']
        t_label = grp['names'][0] if len(grp['names']) == 1 else f"{grp['names'][0]} (+{len(grp['names']) - 1})"
        try:
            for root, dirs, fnames in os.walk(cp_abs):
                dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d.lower() != '.unwanted']
                for fn in fnames:
                    full_p = os.path.join(root, fn)
                    norm_fp = os.path.normcase(full_p)
                    if norm_fp in seen_paths:
                        continue
                    rel_disk = os.path.relpath(full_p, cp_abs)
                    rel_check = rel_disk[:-4] if (prog < 0.9999 and rel_disk.lower().endswith('.!qb')) else rel_disk
                    if os.path.normcase(rel_check) in expected or os.path.normcase(rel_disk) in expected:
                        continue
                    cat = _classify_inner_file(fn, prog)
                    if cat == 'active_part':
                        continue
                    seen_paths.add(norm_fp)
                    try:
                        sz = os.path.getsize(full_p)
                    except OSError:
                        sz = 0
                    found.append({
                        'path': full_p, 'name': fn, 'rel_path': rel_disk,
                        'torrent_name': t_label, 'category': cat,
                        'size': sz, 'size_fmt': format_file_size(sz)
                    })
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log(f"scan_inner_content_junk {cp_abs}", e)

    found.sort(key=lambda x: x['size'], reverse=True)
    return {'success': True, 'items': found[:500], 'scanned': scanned}


def trash_paths_list(paths, actor='admin', reason='hygiene'):
    moved, errors = [], []
    for p in (paths or []):
        ok, res = move_to_trash(p, name=os.path.basename(str(p)), actor=actor)
        if ok:
            moved.append(p)
        else:
            errors.append(f"{os.path.basename(str(p))}: {res}")
    if moved:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', f'Гигиена ({reason}) -> _tbr_trash', f'Объектов: {len(moved)}', actor=actor)
    return len(errors) == 0, moved, errors

================================================================================
FILE: core/hygiene_trackers.py

# core/hygiene_trackers.py
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, get_torrent_trackers, add_torrent_tags
from core.health_score import compute_health_metrics

_FATAL_KEYWORDS = ('unregistered', 'not registered', 'deleted', 'banned', 'not found', 'не зарегистрирован', 'удален', 'удалён', 'запрещен', 'запрещён')
_PROTECTED_TAGS = {'keep', 'vip', 'protected', 'archive'}


def _ensure_quarantine_table():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS quarantine_tracker (
                        hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                        first_error_at REAL NOT NULL,
                        error_msg TEXT NOT NULL,
                        tagged_at REAL
                    )
                """)
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("quarantine_tracker init", e)


_ensure_quarantine_table()


def _is_fatal_msg(msg):
    low = str(msg or '').lower()
    return any(k in low for k in _FATAL_KEYWORDS)


def scan_quarantine(hours_threshold=72, apply_tags=False):
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    if not live:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен', 'items': []}

    now = time.time()
    thresh_sec = max(1, int(hours_threshold)) * 3600

    def check_tor(t_hash, info):
        trackers = get_torrent_trackers(ip, port, t_hash)
        real = [tr for tr in trackers if not str(tr.get('url', '')).startswith('**[') and tr.get('status', 0) != 0]
        if not real:
            return t_hash.lower(), None
        all_fatal = all(tr.get('status') == 4 and _is_fatal_msg(tr.get('msg')) for tr in real)
        if not all_fatal:
            return t_hash.lower(), None
        return t_hash.lower(), str(real[0].get('msg') or 'Fatal tracker error').strip()

    fatal_map = {}
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=10) as pool:
        for fut in as_completed([pool.submit(check_tor, h, inf) for h, inf in live.items()]):
            try:
                hl, msg = fut.result()
                if msg:
                    fatal_map[hl] = msg
            except Exception:
                pass

    with app_state_db._lock:
        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
            existing = {r['hash_id']: dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM quarantine_tracker").fetchall()}
            for hl in list(existing.keys()):
                if hl not in fatal_map:
                    conn.execute("DELETE FROM quarantine_tracker WHERE hash_id = ?", (hl,))
            for hl, msg in fatal_map.items():
                if hl not in existing:
                    conn.execute("INSERT INTO quarantine_tracker (hash_id, first_error_at, error_msg, tagged_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, NULL)", (hl, now, msg))
            conn.commit()
            rows = {r['hash_id']: dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM quarantine_tracker").fetchall()}

    live_lower = {k.lower(): (k, v) for k, v in live.items()}
    out = []
    for hl, r in rows.items():
        if hl not in live_lower:
            continue
        raw_h, info = live_lower[hl]
        elapsed_h = round((now - float(r['first_error_at'])) / 3600.0, 1)
        ready = (now - float(r['first_error_at'])) >= thresh_sec
        cur_tags = {tg.strip().lower() for tg in str(info.get('tags', '') or '').split(',') if tg.strip()}
        if ready and apply_tags and 'quarantine' not in cur_tags:
            if add_torrent_tags(ip, port, raw_h, 'quarantine'):
                cur_tags.add('quarantine')
        out.append({
            'hash': raw_h, 'name': info.get('name', raw_h),
            'size': int(info.get('total_size', 0) or 0),
            'size_fmt': format_file_size(info.get('total_size', 0)),
            'content_path': info.get('content_path') or info.get('save_path') or '',
            'error_msg': r['error_msg'], 'elapsed_hours': elapsed_h,
            'ready': ready, 'in_quarantine': 'quarantine' in cur_tags
        })

    out.sort(key=lambda x: x['elapsed_hours'], reverse=True)
    return {'success': True, 'items': out, 'threshold_hours': hours_threshold}


def scan_stalled_incompletes(stalled_days=0):
    """#37 Кладбище недокачек: незавершённые раздачи (на паузе, зависшие, без сидов или avail < 1.0)."""
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    if not live:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен', 'items': []}

    now = time.time()
    min_idle = max(0, int(stalled_days or 0)) * 86400
    items = []

    for h, info in live.items():
        progress = float(info.get('progress', 0.0) or 0.0)
        amt_left = int(info.get('amount_left', 0) or 0)
        state = str(info.get('state', '')).lower()

        # Полностью скачанные и отдающиеся раздачи отбрасываем
        if progress >= 0.9999 and amt_left == 0:
            continue
        if state in ('uploading', 'seeding', 'stalledup', 'forcedup', 'pausedup', 'stoppedup'):
            continue

        raw_avail = info.get('availability')
        avail = float(raw_avail) if raw_avail is not None else -1.0
        num_seeds = int(info.get('num_seeds', 0) or 0)
        num_complete = int(info.get('num_complete', 0) or 0)
        dlspeed = int(info.get('dlspeed', 0) or 0)

        # Любая недокачка, которая сейчас не качает на полной скорости
        is_stalled = (
            (0.0 <= avail < 1.0) or
            avail < 0 or
            (num_seeds == 0 and num_complete <= 0) or
            state in ('stalleddl', 'pauseddl', 'stoppeddl', 'queueddl', 'metadl') or
            dlspeed == 0
        )
        if not is_stalled:
            continue

        last_act = int(info.get('last_activity') or 0)
        added_on = int(info.get('added_on') or now)
        ref_ts = last_act if last_act > 0 else added_on
        idle_sec = max(0, now - ref_ts)

        if min_idle > 0 and idle_sec < min_idle:
            continue

        sz = int(info.get('total_size', 0) or 0)
        downloaded = int(info.get('downloaded', 0) or (sz - amt_left if sz > amt_left else 0))
        items.append({
            'hash': h,
            'name': info.get('name', h),
            'progress_pct': round(progress * 100.0, 1),
            'availability': round(avail, 2) if avail >= 0 else '—',
            'state': state,
            'idle_days': round(idle_sec / 86400.0, 1),
            'size': sz,
            'size_fmt': format_file_size(sz),
            'downloaded_fmt': format_file_size(downloaded),
            'content_path': info.get('content_path') or info.get('save_path') or ''
        })

    items.sort(key=lambda x: x['size'], reverse=True)
    return {'success': True, 'items': items}


def scan_dead_weight(idle_days=30, min_seeds=10):
    """#4 Рейтинг тунеядцев: защита Последнего сида, свежих и защищённых тегов."""
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    if not live:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен', 'items': []}

    now = time.time()
    min_idle_sec = max(1, int(idle_days)) * 86400
    fresh_guard_sec = 14 * 86400
    items = []

    for h, info in live.items():
        if float(info.get('progress', 0.0) or 0.0) < 0.9999:
            continue
        hm = compute_health_metrics(info)
        if hm.get('is_last_seed') or hm.get('num_complete', 0) < int(min_seeds):
            continue
        tags = {tg.strip().lower() for tg in str(info.get('tags', '') or '').split(',') if tg.strip()}
        if tags & _PROTECTED_TAGS:
            continue
        added = int(info.get('added_on') or now)
        if (now - added) < fresh_guard_sec:
            continue
        last_act = int(info.get('last_activity') or added)
        idle_sec = max(0, now - last_act)
        if idle_sec < min_idle_sec:
            continue

        sz = int(info.get('total_size', 0) or 0)
        up = int(info.get('uploaded', 0) or 0)
        items.append({
            'hash': h, 'name': info.get('name', h),
            'size': sz, 'size_fmt': format_file_size(sz),
            'uploaded_fmt': format_file_size(up),
            'ratio': round(float(info.get('ratio', 0.0) or 0.0), 2),
            'seeds': hm.get('num_complete', 0),
            'idle_days': round(idle_sec / 86400.0, 1),
            'content_path': info.get('content_path') or info.get('save_path') or ''
        })

    items.sort(key=lambda x: (x['size'], x['idle_days']), reverse=True)
    return {'success': True, 'items': items}

================================================================================
FILE: core/keep_awake.py
================================================================================
# core/keep_awake.py
import os
import threading
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import write_log, write_error_log

ES_CONTINUOUS = 0x80000000
ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED = 0x00000001
ES_DISPLAY_REQUIRED = 0x00000002

_ACTIVE_DL_STATES = ("downloading", "forceddl", "metadl", "allocating", "stalleddl")


class KeepAwakeController:
    def __init__(self):
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._active = False
        self._display_on = False
        self._want_keep = False
        self._want_display = False
        self._stop_event = threading.Event()
        self._wake_event = threading.Event()
        self._thread = None

    @staticmethod
    def is_supported():
        return os.name == "nt"

    def _set_win_state(self, keep, with_display):
        if not self.is_supported():
            return
        try:
            import ctypes
            flags = ES_CONTINUOUS
            if keep:
                flags |= ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED
                if with_display:
                    flags |= ES_DISPLAY_REQUIRED
            ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetThreadExecutionState(flags)
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("KeepAwake SetThreadExecutionState", e)

    def _ensure_thread(self):
        if not self.is_supported():
            return
        if self._thread and self._thread.is_alive():
            return

        def worker():
            while not self._stop_event.is_set():
                with self._lock:
                    w_keep = self._want_keep
                    w_disp = self._want_display
                if w_keep != self._active or (w_keep and w_disp != self._display_on):
                    self._set_win_state(w_keep, w_disp)
                    self._active = w_keep
                    self._display_on = w_disp if w_keep else False
                    write_log("app", f"Keep-Awake: {'ВКЛ' if w_keep else 'ВЫКЛ'} (display={w_disp})")
                elif w_keep:
                    # Поддерживаем актуальность состояния пока идёт активность
                    self._set_win_state(True, w_disp)
                self._wake_event.wait(timeout=30.0)
                self._wake_event.clear()

            if self._active:
                self._set_win_state(False, False)
                self._active = False
                self._display_on = False

        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()

    def evaluate(self, live_torrents):
        if not self.is_supported():
            return False

        enabled = cfg.getboolean("KeepAwake_Enabled", False)
        if not enabled:
            self.release()
            return False

        min_kb = max(0, cfg.getint("KeepAwake_Min_KB", 500))
        with_display = cfg.getboolean("KeepAwake_Display", False)

        has_dl = False
        total_up_bps = 0
        for info in (live_torrents or {}).values():
            st = str(info.get("state", "")).lower()
            amt_left = int(info.get("amount_left", 0) or 0)
            dl_spd = int(info.get("dlspeed", 0) or 0)
            if (st in _ACTIVE_DL_STATES and amt_left > 0) or dl_spd > 0:
                has_dl = True
            total_up_bps += int(info.get("upspeed", 0) or 0)

        should_keep = has_dl or (total_up_bps >= (min_kb * 1024))
        with self._lock:
            self._want_keep = should_keep
            self._want_display = with_display
        self._ensure_thread()
        self._wake_event.set()
        return should_keep

    def release(self):
        with self._lock:
            changed = self._want_keep
            self._want_keep = False
            self._want_display = False
        if changed and self._thread and self._thread.is_alive():
            self._wake_event.set()

    def stop(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._want_keep = False
            self._want_display = False
        self._stop_event.set()
        self._wake_event.set()
        if self._thread and self._thread.is_alive():
            self._thread.join(timeout=1.5)


keep_awake = KeepAwakeController()

# core/lineage_notes_events.py
import time
import datetime
import threading
from collections import deque
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from utils.helpers import write_error_log

_TICKER_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_TICKER_BUF = deque(maxlen=80)
_EVENT_DEBOUNCE = {}  # (uid, event_type) -> ts


def _ensure_tables():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.executescript("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS torrent_events (
                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                        uid TEXT NOT NULL,
                        event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                        title TEXT NOT NULL,
                        created_at REAL NOT NULL
                    );
                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tor_ev_uid ON torrent_events(uid, created_at DESC);

                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS torrent_notes (
                        uid TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                        note TEXT NOT NULL,
                        last_name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                        updated_at REAL NOT NULL
                    );
                """)
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("lineage_notes_events init", e)


_ensure_tables()


def resolve_uid(t_hash):
    hl = str(t_hash or '').strip().lower()
    if not hl:
        return ''
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                row = conn.execute("SELECT torrent_uid FROM torrent_lineage WHERE torrent_hash = ?", (hl,)).fetchone()
                return row['torrent_uid'] if row else hl
        except Exception:
            return hl


def push_activity(category, text, t_hash=''):
    """#44 Живой пульс событий (кольцевой буфер на 80 записей)."""
    with _TICKER_LOCK:
        _TICKER_BUF.appendleft({
            'ts': int(time.time()),
            'time': datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'),
            'category': str(category),
            'text': str(text)[:180],
            'hash': str(t_hash or '').lower()
        })


def get_activity_feed(hidden_hashes=None):
    hidden = {h.lower() for h in (hidden_hashes or set())}
    with _TICKER_LOCK:
        return [ev for ev in _TICKER_BUF if not ev['hash'] or ev['hash'] not in hidden]


def record_torrent_event(t_hash, event_type, title):
    """#15 Биография раздачи по uid (с дебаунсом 60с от миганий сети)."""
    uid = resolve_uid(t_hash)
    if not uid:
        return
    now = time.time()
    key = (uid, str(event_type))
    if (now - _EVENT_DEBOUNCE.get(key, 0)) < 60.0:
        return
    _EVENT_DEBOUNCE[key] = now

    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO torrent_events (uid, event_type, title, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                    (uid, str(event_type)[:32], str(title)[:240], now)
                )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            pass
    push_activity(event_type, title, t_hash=t_hash)


def get_torrent_timeline(t_hash):
    uid = resolve_uid(t_hash)
    if not uid:
        return []
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute(
                    "SELECT event_type, title, created_at FROM torrent_events WHERE uid = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 100",
                    (uid,)
                ).fetchall()
                out = []
                for r in rows:
                    d = dict(r)
                    d['date'] = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(d['created_at']).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')
                    out.append(d)
                return out
        except Exception:
            return []


def get_torrent_note(t_hash):
    uid = resolve_uid(t_hash)
    if not uid:
        return ''
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                row = conn.execute("SELECT note FROM torrent_notes WHERE uid = ?", (uid,)).fetchone()
                return row['note'] if row else ''
        except Exception:
            return ''


def set_torrent_note(t_hash, note_text, last_name=''):
    uid = resolve_uid(t_hash)
    if not uid:
        return False
    clean = str(note_text or '').strip()[:2000]
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                if not clean:
                    conn.execute("DELETE FROM torrent_notes WHERE uid = ?", (uid,))
                else:
                    conn.execute(
                        "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO torrent_notes (uid, note, last_name, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                        (uid, clean, str(last_name or '')[:160], time.time())
                    )
                conn.commit()
                return True
        except Exception:
            return False


def list_all_notes(hidden_uids=None):
    hidden = {u.lower() for u in (hidden_uids or set())}
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM torrent_notes ORDER BY updated_at DESC").fetchall()
                return [dict(r) for r in rows if r['uid'] not in hidden]
        except Exception:
            return []

# core/mediainfo_lite.py
import os
import json
import struct
import shutil
import subprocess
from utils.helpers import format_file_size

_WIN_FLAGS = getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0)
_VIDEO_EXTS = {'.mkv', '.mp4', '.avi', '.mov', '.m4v', '.ts', '.wmv', '.webm'}


def _find_video_files(content_path, limit=10):
    if not content_path or not os.path.exists(content_path):
        return []
    if os.path.isfile(content_path):
        return [content_path] if os.path.splitext(content_path)[1].lower() in _VIDEO_EXTS else []
    found = []
    for root, _, files in os.walk(content_path):
        for fn in files:
            if os.path.splitext(fn)[1].lower() in _VIDEO_EXTS:
                fp = os.path.join(root, fn)
                try:
                    found.append((os.path.getsize(fp), fp))
                except OSError:
                    pass
    found.sort(reverse=True)
    return [p for _, p in found[:limit]]


def _probe_with_ffprobe(ffprobe_exe, fpath):
    try:
        cmd = [ffprobe_exe, '-v', 'quiet', '-print_format', 'json', '-show_format', '-show_streams', fpath]
        r = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=4.0, creationflags=_WIN_FLAGS)
        data = json.loads(r.stdout or '{}')
        fmt = data.get('format') or {}
        streams = data.get('streams') or []
        v_streams = [s for s in streams if s.get('codec_type') == 'video']
        a_streams = [s for s in streams if s.get('codec_type') == 'audio']
        s_streams = [s for s in streams if s.get('codec_type') == 'subtitle']

        v_str = '—'
        if v_streams:
            vs = v_streams[0]
            v_str = f"{str(vs.get('codec_name', '')).upper()} {vs.get('width', '?')}x{vs.get('height', '?')}"
        a_list = [f"{str(a.get('codec_name', '')).upper()} ({(a.get('tags') or {}).get('language', 'und')})" for a in a_streams[:4]]
        sub_list = [(s.get('tags') or {}).get('language', 'und') for s in s_streams[:6]]

        dur = float(fmt.get('duration') or 0)
        br = int(fmt.get('bit_rate') or 0)
        return {
            'engine': 'ffprobe',
            'file': os.path.basename(fpath),
            'size_fmt': format_file_size(os.path.getsize(fpath)),
            'duration_fmt': f"{int(dur // 60)}м {int(dur % 60)}с" if dur > 0 else '—',
            'bitrate_fmt': f"{round(br / 1_000_000, 1)} Mbps" if br > 0 else '—',
            'video': v_str,
            'audio': ", ".join(a_list) or '—',
            'subtitles': ", ".join(sub_list) or 'нет'
        }
    except Exception:
        return None


def _probe_fallback_headers(fpath):
    """Базовый pure-Python парсер заголовков MKV/MP4 без внешних зависимостей."""
    ext = os.path.splitext(fpath)[1].lower()
    container = ext.lstrip('.').upper()
    dur_fmt = '—'
    try:
        with open(fpath, 'rb') as f:
            head = f.read(65536)
        if ext == '.mkv' and head.startswith(b'\x1a\x45\xdf\xa3'):
            container = 'Matroska (EBML)'
        elif ext in ('.mp4', '.m4v', '.mov') and b'ftyp' in head[:32]:
            container = 'ISO Base Media (MP4)'
            idx = head.find(b'mvhd')
            if idx != -1 and idx + 24 < len(head):
                ver = head[idx + 4]
                if ver == 0:
                    timescale, duration = struct.unpack('>II', head[idx + 16:idx + 24])
                    if timescale > 0:
                        sec = duration / timescale
                        dur_fmt = f"{int(sec // 60)}м {int(sec % 60)}с"
    except Exception:
        pass

    return {
        'engine': 'header-fallback',
        'file': os.path.basename(fpath),
        'size_fmt': format_file_size(os.path.getsize(fpath)),
        'duration_fmt': dur_fmt,
        'bitrate_fmt': 'нужен ffprobe',
        'video': f'{container}',
        'audio': 'нужен ffprobe',
        'subtitles': '—'
    }


def inspect_torrent_media(content_path):
    videos = _find_video_files(content_path, limit=8)
    if not videos:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'Видеофайлы на диске не найдены', 'items': []}

    ffprobe = shutil.which('ffprobe')
    items = []
    for vp in videos:
        res = _probe_with_ffprobe(ffprobe, vp) if ffprobe else None
        if not res:
            res = _probe_fallback_headers(vp)
        items.append(res)
    return {'success': True, 'has_ffprobe': bool(ffprobe), 'items': items}

# core/network_tools.py
import os
import re
import time
import socket
import urllib.parse
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import write_log
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, _get_qb_json, get_live_torrent_info,
    get_transfer_info, is_qb_running, close_qbittorrent, launch_qbittorrent
)
from core.tracker_inspector import inspect_tracker_errors
from core.analytics_insights import detect_isp_throttling
from core.env_doctor import sanitize_text_for_github
from core.disaster_recovery import execute_panic_killswitch
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.notifiers import notify_all


def diagnose_bottleneck(db):
    """«Почему всё тормозит?» — диагностика узких горлышек в реальном времени."""
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return {'success': False, 'issues': ['qBittorrent WebUI недоступен']}

    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port)
    tr = get_transfer_info(ip, port)
    prefs = _get_qb_json(ip, port, "app/preferences", timeout=2.0) or {}
    issues = []

    checking = sum(1 for v in live.values() if 'checking' in str(v.get('state', '')).lower())
    if checking > 0:
        issues.append(f"💽 Идёт проверка хеша ({checking} раздач) — диск может быть перегружен")

    if tr.get('use_alt_speed_limits'):
        issues.append("🐢 Включён альтернативный лимит скорости («Черепаха») в qBittorrent")
    elif int(tr.get('up_rate_limit') or 0) > 0:
        issues.append(f"🚦 Задан глобальный лимит отдачи в qB: {int(tr['up_rate_limit']) // 1024} KiB/s")

    if int(prefs.get('max_active_uploads') or 999) <= 3:
        issues.append(f"🎰 Мало активных слотов отдачи в настройках qB (max_active_uploads={prefs.get('max_active_uploads')})")

    err_map = inspect_tracker_errors(ip, port, live, force=False)
    timeout_cnt = sum(1 for e in err_map.values() if e.get('category') == 'timeout')
    if timeout_cnt >= 3:
        issues.append(f"🌐 Массовые таймауты трекеров ({timeout_cnt} раздач)")

    th = detect_isp_throttling(db, ip, port)
    if th.get('throttled'):
        issues.append(f"🚧 Обнаружена полка скорости ~{th['tier_mbps']} Мбит/с (возможен шейпинг провайдера)")

    if not issues:
        issues.append("📉 Узких мест в клиенте и сети не выявлено — вероятно, дефицит спроса со стороны пиров")

    return {'success': True, 'issues': issues, 'active_torrents': len(live)}


def ping_trackers_dns_tcp(max_hosts=25):
    """Пингер трекеров: только DNS + TCP handshake (без HTTP GET к announce URL)."""
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    hosts = {}
    for info in live.values():
        tr = str(info.get('tracker') or '').strip()
        if not tr or tr.startswith('**['):
            continue
        p = urllib.parse.urlparse(tr)
        if not p.hostname or p.scheme not in ('http', 'https', 'udp'):
            continue
        def_port = 443 if p.scheme == 'https' else (80 if p.scheme == 'http' else 6969)
        hosts[p.hostname.lower()] = (p.hostname.lower(), p.port or def_port, p.scheme)

    targets = list(hosts.values())[:max_hosts]

    def probe(host, t_port, scheme):
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        try:
            addrs = socket.getaddrinfo(host, t_port, type=socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            dns_ms = round((time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000.0, 1)
            if scheme == 'udp':
                return {'host': host, 'port': t_port, 'scheme': 'udp', 'dns_ok': True, 'tcp_ok': None, 'latency_ms': dns_ms, 'error': ''}
            t1 = time.monotonic()
            with socket.create_connection((addrs[0][4][0], t_port), timeout=3.0):
                tcp_ms = round((time.monotonic() - t1) * 1000.0, 1)
            return {'host': host, 'port': t_port, 'scheme': scheme, 'dns_ok': True, 'tcp_ok': True, 'latency_ms': tcp_ms, 'error': ''}
        except Exception as e:
            return {'host': host, 'port': t_port, 'scheme': scheme, 'dns_ok': False, 'tcp_ok': False, 'latency_ms': None, 'error': str(e)[:80]}

    results = []
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
        for fut in as_completed([pool.submit(probe, h, p, s) for h, p, s in targets]):
            results.append(fut.result())
    results.sort(key=lambda x: (x['latency_ms'] is None, x['latency_ms'] or 99999))
    return {'success': True, 'items': results}


def check_network_interface_guard():
    """Страж сетевого интерфейса qBittorrent."""
    enabled = cfg.getboolean('Interface_Guard_Enabled', False)
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    prefs = _get_qb_json(ip, port, "app/preferences", timeout=2.0) if port else None
    if not isinstance(prefs, dict):
        return {'enabled': enabled, 'ok': True, 'reason': 'qb_down', 'current': '', 'current_name': ''}

    cur_iface = str(prefs.get('current_network_interface') or '').strip()
    cur_name = str(prefs.get('current_network_interface_name') or cur_iface).strip()
    expected = cfg.get('Expected_Net_Interface', '').strip()
    if not enabled or not expected:
        return {'enabled': enabled, 'ok': True, 'current': cur_iface, 'current_name': cur_name}

    if cur_iface != expected and cur_name != expected:
        msg = f"Сетевой интерфейс qB изменился: '{expected}' → '{cur_name or cur_iface or 'Any'}'!"
        last_alert = cfg.getint('Interface_Last_Alert_Ts', 0)
        if (time.time() - last_alert) >= 300:
            cfg.set('Interface_Last_Alert_Ts', str(int(time.time())))
            cfg.save()
            app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Страж интерфейса: несовпадение', msg)
            notify_all("🛡️ Страж интерфейса qBittorrent", [msg], critical=True)
            if cfg.getboolean('Interface_Guard_Panic', False):
                execute_panic_killswitch(kill_qb=False, actor='interface_guard')
        return {'enabled': True, 'ok': False, 'expected': expected, 'current': cur_iface, 'current_name': cur_name, 'message': msg}
    return {'enabled': True, 'ok': True, 'expected': expected, 'current': cur_iface, 'current_name': cur_name}


def _find_qb_log_path():
    if os.name == 'nt':
        cands = [
            os.path.join(os.environ.get('LOCALAPPDATA', ''), 'qBittorrent', 'logs', 'qbittorrent.log'),
            os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), 'AppData', 'Local', 'qBittorrent', 'logs', 'qbittorrent.log'),
        ]
    else:
        cands = [
            '/config/qBittorrent/logs/qbittorrent.log',
            os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), '.local', 'share', 'qBittorrent', 'logs', 'qbittorrent.log'),
        ]
    return next((p for p in cands if p and os.path.isfile(p)), '')


def read_sanitized_qb_log(lines_count=80):
    """Безопасное чтение последних N строк qbittorrent.log с маскировкой IP и пасскеев."""
    path = _find_qb_log_path()
    if not path:
        return False, "Файл qbittorrent.log не найден"
    try:
        with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='replace') as f:
            lines = f.readlines()[-max(10, min(200, int(lines_count))):]
        text = sanitize_text_for_github("".join(lines))
        text = re.sub(r'\b\d{1,3}(?:\.\d{1,3}){3}\b', '<IP>', text)
        return True, text
    except Exception as e:
        return False, str(e)


def reanimate_qb(force_kill=False):
    """Удалённый реаниматор qB: мягкий или быстрый перезапуск."""
    if os.name != 'nt':
        return False, "Локальное управление процессом доступно только на Windows"
    write_log('system', f"Реаниматор qBittorrent (force={force_kill})")
    if is_qb_running():
        wait_sec = 2.0 if force_kill else 20.0
        closed = close_qbittorrent(max_wait_sec=wait_sec)
        if not closed and not force_kill:
            return False, "qBittorrent не завершился мягко за 20с. Используйте принудительный перезапуск."
    time.sleep(1.5)
    ok = launch_qbittorrent()
    app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Реаниматор qB', f'restarted (ok={ok}, force={force_kill})', actor='admin')
    return ok, "qBittorrent перезапущен" if ok else "Не удалось запустить qBittorrent"
