# core/sessions.py
import time
import secrets
import threading
import ipaddress
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from utils.helpers import write_error_log


class SessionAndRateManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self._rate_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._login_fails = {}   # { ip: [ts, ...] }
        self._token_fails = {}   # { ip: [ts, ...] }
        self._init_table()

    def _init_table(self):
        with profiles_db._lock:
            try:
                with profiles_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.executescript("""
                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_sessions (
                            sid TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            profile_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                            ip TEXT NOT NULL,
                            user_agent TEXT NOT NULL,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL,
                            last_seen REAL NOT NULL,
                            revoked INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            FOREIGN KEY(profile_id) REFERENCES profiles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
                        );
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sessions_prof ON user_sessions(profile_id, revoked);
                    """)
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Инициализация user_sessions", e)

    @staticmethod
    def is_whitelisted_ip(ip_str):
        s = str(ip_str or '').strip().lower()
        if s in ('', '127.0.0.1', '::1', 'localhost'):
            return True
        try:
            obj = ipaddress.ip_address(s)
            return obj.is_loopback or obj.is_private
        except ValueError:
            return False

    def check_rate_limit(self, ip, bucket='login'):
        if self.is_whitelisted_ip(ip):
            return True
        now = time.time()
        window = 600 if bucket == 'login' else 300
        max_att = 5 if bucket == 'login' else 15
        store = self._login_fails if bucket == 'login' else self._token_fails
        with self._rate_lock:
            hist = [t for t in store.get(ip, []) if (now - t) < window]
            store[ip] = hist
            return len(hist) < max_att

    def record_failure(self, ip, bucket='login'):
        if self.is_whitelisted_ip(ip):
            return
        now = time.time()
        window = 600 if bucket == 'login' else 300
        store = self._login_fails if bucket == 'login' else self._token_fails
        with self._rate_lock:
            hist = [t for t in store.get(ip, []) if (now - t) < window]
            hist.append(now)
            store[ip] = hist

    def clear_failures(self, ip, bucket='login'):
        store = self._login_fails if bucket == 'login' else self._token_fails
        with self._rate_lock:
            store.pop(ip, None)

    def create_session(self, profile_id, ip, user_agent):
        sid = secrets.token_urlsafe(24)
        now = time.time()
        ua = str(user_agent or 'Unknown')[:220]
        with profiles_db._lock:
            try:
                with profiles_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute(
                        """INSERT INTO user_sessions (sid, profile_id, ip, user_agent, created_at, last_seen, revoked)
                           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)""",
                        (sid, int(profile_id), str(ip)[:64], ua, now, now)
                    )
                    conn.commit()
                    return sid
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("create_session", e)
                return sid

    def validate_and_touch(self, sid):
        if not sid:
            return False
        now = time.time()
        with profiles_db._lock:
            try:
                with profiles_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    row = conn.execute("SELECT revoked, last_seen FROM user_sessions WHERE sid = ?", (sid,)).fetchone()
                    if not row or row['revoked']:
                        return False
                    if (now - float(row['last_seen'] or 0)) >= 30.0:
                        conn.execute("UPDATE user_sessions SET last_seen = ? WHERE sid = ?", (now, sid))
                        conn.commit()
                    return True
            except Exception:
                return True

    def revoke_session(self, sid):
        if not sid:
            return False
        with profiles_db._lock:
            try:
                with profiles_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    cur = conn.execute("UPDATE user_sessions SET revoked = 1 WHERE sid = ?", (sid,))
                    conn.commit()
                    return cur.rowcount > 0
            except Exception:
                return False

    def revoke_other_sessions(self, current_sid, profile_id=None):
        with profiles_db._lock:
            try:
                with profiles_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    if profile_id is not None:
                        cur = conn.execute(
                            "UPDATE user_sessions SET revoked = 1 WHERE profile_id = ? AND sid != ? AND revoked = 0",
                            (int(profile_id), str(current_sid or ''))
                        )
                    else:
                        cur = conn.execute(
                            "UPDATE user_sessions SET revoked = 1 WHERE sid != ? AND revoked = 0",
                            (str(current_sid or ''),)
                        )
                    conn.commit()
                    return cur.rowcount
            except Exception:
                return 0

    def list_active_sessions(self, current_sid='', profile_id=None):
        cutoff = time.time() - (30 * 86400)
        with profiles_db._lock:
            try:
                with profiles_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    if profile_id is not None:
                        rows = conn.execute(
                            """SELECT s.*, p.name AS profile_name, p.login AS profile_login, p.role AS profile_role
                               FROM user_sessions s JOIN profiles p ON p.id = s.profile_id
                               WHERE s.revoked = 0 AND s.last_seen >= ? AND s.profile_id = ?
                               ORDER BY s.last_seen DESC""",
                            (cutoff, int(profile_id))
                        ).fetchall()
                    else:
                        rows = conn.execute(
                            """SELECT s.*, p.name AS profile_name, p.login AS profile_login, p.role AS profile_role
                               FROM user_sessions s JOIN profiles p ON p.id = s.profile_id
                               WHERE s.revoked = 0 AND s.last_seen >= ?
                               ORDER BY s.last_seen DESC""",
                            (cutoff,)
                        ).fetchall()
                    out = []
                    for r in rows:
                        d = dict(r)
                        d['is_current'] = (d['sid'] == current_sid)
                        out.append(d)
                    return out
            except Exception:
                return []


sessions_mgr = SessionAndRateManager()

# core/single_instance.py
import os
import time
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR


class SingleInstanceLock:
    def __init__(self, name="tbr_swiss_knife_single_instance"):
        self.name = name
        self._handle = None
        self._fd = None
        self._lock_path = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "tbr.lock")

    def acquire(self, wait_sec=1.5):
        deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(wait_sec))
        if os.name == 'nt':
            return self._acquire_windows(deadline)
        return self._acquire_posix(deadline)

    def _acquire_windows(self, deadline):
        import ctypes
        from ctypes import wintypes

        kernel32 = ctypes.WinDLL('kernel32', use_last_error=True)
        CreateMutexW = kernel32.CreateMutexW
        CreateMutexW.argtypes = [wintypes.LPVOID, wintypes.BOOL, wintypes.LPCWSTR]
        CreateMutexW.restype = wintypes.HANDLE
        CloseHandle = kernel32.CloseHandle
        ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS = 183

        mutex_name = f"Local\\{self.name}"
        while True:
            ctypes.set_last_error(0)
            handle = CreateMutexW(None, False, mutex_name)
            err = ctypes.get_last_error()
            if handle and err != ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS:
                self._handle = handle
                return True
            if handle:
                CloseHandle(handle)
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                return False
            time.sleep(0.15)

    def _acquire_posix(self, deadline):
        import fcntl
        os.makedirs(APP_DATA_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        fd = os.open(self._lock_path, os.O_CREAT | os.O_RDWR, 0o600)
        while True:
            try:
                fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
                self._fd = fd
                return True
            except BlockingIOError:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    os.close(fd)
                    return False
                time.sleep(0.15)
            except Exception:
                os.close(fd)
                return True

    def release(self):
        if os.name == 'nt' and self._handle:
            try:
                import ctypes
                ctypes.WinDLL('kernel32', use_last_error=True).CloseHandle(self._handle)
            except Exception:
                pass
            self._handle = None
        elif self._fd is not None:
            try:
                import fcntl
                fcntl.flock(self._fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
                os.close(self._fd)
            except Exception:
                pass
            self._fd = None

# core/smart_tagging.py
import time
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.qb_api import add_torrent_tags
from utils.helpers import write_log, write_error_log

VALID_MATCH_FIELDS = ('tracker', 'save_path')


def _ensure_table():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tagging_rules (
                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                        name TEXT NOT NULL,
                        enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
                        match_field TEXT NOT NULL,
                        pattern TEXT NOT NULL,
                        target_tag TEXT NOT NULL,
                        created_at REAL NOT NULL
                    )
                """)
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("tagging_rules init", e)


_ensure_table()


def list_tagging_rules():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM tagging_rules ORDER BY id DESC").fetchall()
                return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        except Exception:
            return []


def add_tagging_rule(name, match_field, pattern, target_tag):
    fld = match_field if match_field in VALID_MATCH_FIELDS else 'tracker'
    pat = str(pattern or '').strip()[:120]
    tag = str(target_tag or '').strip()[:32]
    nm = str(name or '').strip()[:64] or f"{fld}: {pat} -> {tag}"
    if not pat or not tag:
        return None
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                cur = conn.cursor()
                cur.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO tagging_rules (name, enabled, match_field, pattern, target_tag, created_at) VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                    (nm, fld, pat, tag, time.time())
                )
                conn.commit()
                return cur.lastrowid
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("add_tagging_rule", e)
            return None


def toggle_tagging_rule(rule_id):
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("UPDATE tagging_rules SET enabled = 1 - enabled WHERE id = ?", (int(rule_id),))
                conn.commit()
                return True
        except Exception:
            return False


def delete_tagging_rule(rule_id):
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM tagging_rules WHERE id = ?", (int(rule_id),))
                conn.commit()
                return True
        except Exception:
            return False


def run_smart_tagging(ip, port, live, parsed_torrents=None):
    rules = [r for r in list_tagging_rules() if r.get('enabled')]
    if not rules or not ip or not port or not live:
        return 0

    parsed = parsed_torrents or {}
    applied = 0
    for raw_hash, info in live.items():
        cur_tags = {tg.strip().lower() for tg in str(info.get('tags', '') or '').split(',') if tg.strip()}
        save_path = str(info.get('save_path') or '').lower()
        tr_list = [str(info.get('tracker') or '')]
        st_item = parsed.get(raw_hash.lower()) or {}
        tr_list.extend(str(x) for x in (st_item.get('trackers') or []))
        trackers_blob = " ".join(tr_list).lower()

        for r in rules:
            tgt = str(r.get('target_tag') or '').strip()
            pat = str(r.get('pattern') or '').strip().lower()
            if not tgt or not pat or tgt.lower() in cur_tags:
                continue

            matched = (pat in trackers_blob) if r.get('match_field') == 'tracker' else (pat in save_path)
            if matched and add_torrent_tags(ip, port, raw_hash, tgt):
                cur_tags.add(tgt.lower())
                applied += 1
                nm = str(info.get('name') or raw_hash[:8])
                write_log('automation', f"Smart Tagging: '{nm}' -> +{tgt} (по правилу '{r['name']}')")
                app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', f'Авто-тег +{tgt}', nm)

    return applied

# core/smart_timers.py
import time
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, write_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.notifiers import notify_all
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, send_qb_command,
    delete_torrents, get_transfer_info, set_global_speed_limits
)

_INACTIVE_STATES = {'pausedup', 'pauseddl', 'stoppedup', 'stoppeddl', 'error', 'missingfiles'}


def get_freeze_status():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("SELECT hash_id, frozen_at FROM smart_freeze").fetchall()
                return {'frozen': len(rows) > 0, 'count': len(rows), 'hashes': [r['hash_id'] for r in rows]}
        except Exception:
            return {'frozen': False, 'count': 0, 'hashes': []}


def freeze_active_torrents(actor='admin'):
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    if not live:
        return False, "qBittorrent недоступен", 0

    active_hashes = [
        h for h, info in live.items()
        if str(info.get('state', '')).lower() not in _INACTIVE_STATES
    ]
    if not active_hashes:
        return False, "Нет активных раздач для заморозки", 0

    if not send_qb_command(ip, port, 'stop', '|'.join(active_hashes)):
        return False, "Не удалось поставить раздачи на паузу", 0

    now = time.time()
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.executemany(
                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO smart_freeze (hash_id, frozen_at) VALUES (?, ?)",
                    [(h.lower(), now) for h in active_hashes]
                )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            pass

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', 'Умная заморозка', f'Заморожено раздач: {len(active_hashes)}', actor=actor)
    return True, f"Заморожено активных раздач: {len(active_hashes)}", len(active_hashes)


def unfreeze_torrents(actor='admin'):
    st = get_freeze_status()
    saved_hashes = st.get('hashes') or []
    if not saved_hashes:
        return False, "Список заморозки пуст", 0

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    live_map = {h.lower(): h for h in live.keys()}

    to_start = [live_map[h.lower()] for h in saved_hashes if h.lower() in live_map]
    if to_start and port:
        send_qb_command(ip, port, 'start', '|'.join(to_start))

    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM smart_freeze")
                conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            pass

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', 'Разморозка', f'Возобновлено раздач: {len(to_start)}', actor=actor)
    return True, f"Возобновлено раздач: {len(to_start)} (удалённые пропущены)", len(to_start)


def get_snooze_status():
    now = time.time()
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                row = conn.execute(
                    "SELECT id, resume_at, mode, created_at FROM snooze_timers WHERE active = 1 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
                ).fetchone()
                if row and row['resume_at'] > now:
                    return {
                        'active': True,
                        'id': row['id'],
                        'resume_at': int(row['resume_at']),
                        'remaining_sec': int(row['resume_at'] - now),
                        'mode': row['mode']
                    }
        except Exception:
            pass
    return {'active': False, 'resume_at': 0, 'remaining_sec': 0, 'mode': 'pause'}


def start_snooze(minutes, mode='pause', actor='admin'):
    mins = max(1, min(1440, int(minutes or 30)))
    m = 'limit' if mode == 'limit' else 'pause'
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return False, "qBittorrent недоступен"

    if m == 'pause':
        ok, msg, _ = freeze_active_torrents(actor=actor)
        if not ok and "Нет активных" not in msg:
            return False, msg
    else:
        tr = get_transfer_info(ip, port)
        cfg.set('Snooze_Saved_DL', str(int(tr.get('dl_rate_limit', 0) or 0)))
        cfg.set('Snooze_Saved_UP', str(int(tr.get('up_rate_limit', 0) or 0)))
        cfg.save()
        set_global_speed_limits(ip, port, 1, 1)

    now = time.time()
    resume_at = now + (mins * 60)
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("UPDATE snooze_timers SET active = 0 WHERE active = 1")
                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO snooze_timers (resume_at, mode, active, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, 1, ?)",
                    (resume_at, m, now)
                )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            pass

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', 'Таймер сна (Snooze)', f'{mins} мин, режим={m}', actor=actor)
    return True, f"Таймер сна включён на {mins} мин"


def cancel_snooze(actor='admin', auto_triggered=False):
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                row = conn.execute("SELECT id, mode FROM snooze_timers WHERE active = 1 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
                conn.execute("UPDATE snooze_timers SET active = 0 WHERE active = 1")
                conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            row = None

    if not row:
        return False, "Активный таймер не найден"

    mode = row['mode']
    if mode == 'pause':
        unfreeze_torrents(actor=actor)
    else:
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        if port:
            dl = cfg.getint('Snooze_Saved_DL', 0)
            up = cfg.getint('Snooze_Saved_UP', 0)
            set_global_speed_limits(ip, port, dl, up)

    title = "⏰ Таймер сна завершён" if auto_triggered else "⏰ Таймер сна отменён"
    app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', title, f'mode={mode}', actor=actor)
    if auto_triggered:
        notify_all(title, ["Ограничения сняты, раздачи возвращены в работу."])
    return True, title


def tick_snooze():
    now = time.time()
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                row = conn.execute(
                    "SELECT id FROM snooze_timers WHERE active = 1 AND resume_at <= ? LIMIT 1", (now,)
                ).fetchone()
        except Exception:
            row = None
    if row:
        cancel_snooze(actor='scheduler', auto_triggered=True)


def list_expirations():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM expiration_timers ORDER BY created_at DESC").fetchall()
                return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        except Exception:
            return []


def set_expiration(t_hash, hours=0.0, target_up_bytes=0, delete_files=False, actor='admin'):
    hl = str(t_hash or '').strip().lower()
    hrs = max(0.0, float(hours or 0))
    tgt = max(0, int(target_up_bytes or 0))
    if not hl or (hrs <= 0 and tgt <= 0):
        return False, "Укажите срок (часы/дни) или порог отдачи"

    now = time.time()
    expires_at = (now + hrs * 3600.0) if hrs > 0 else None
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute(
                    """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO expiration_timers
                       (hash_id, expires_at, target_uploaded, delete_files, created_at)
                       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                    (hl, expires_at, tgt if tgt > 0 else None, 1 if delete_files else 0, now)
                )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            return False, str(e)

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', 'Таймер удаления задан', f'{hl[:8]} (delete_files={bool(delete_files)})', actor=actor)
    return True, "Таймер самоуничтожения установлен"


def remove_expiration(t_hash):
    hl = str(t_hash or '').strip().lower()
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM expiration_timers WHERE hash_id = ?", (hl,))
                conn.commit()
                return True
        except Exception:
            return False


def tick_expirations(ip, port, live):
    if not port or not live:
        return 0
    rows = list_expirations()
    if not rows:
        return 0

    now = time.time()
    live_map = {h.lower(): (h, info) for h, info in live.items()}
    triggered = 0

    for r in rows:
        hl = r['hash_id'].lower()
        if hl not in live_map:
            remove_expiration(hl)
            continue
        raw_hash, info = live_map[hl]
        up = int(info.get('uploaded', 0) or 0)
        exp_ts = r.get('expires_at')
        tgt_up = r.get('target_uploaded')
        hit_time = bool(exp_ts and now >= float(exp_ts))
        hit_up = bool(tgt_up and int(tgt_up) > 0 and up >= int(tgt_up))

        if hit_time or hit_up:
            del_files = bool(r.get('delete_files'))
            if delete_torrents(ip, port, raw_hash, delete_files=del_files):
                remove_expiration(hl)
                triggered += 1
                nm = str(info.get('name') or hl)
                act = "с файлами на диске" if del_files else "только из клиента"
                write_log('automation', f"Expiration Timer: удалён '{nm}' ({act})")
                app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', 'Срок жизни истёк', f"'{nm}' удалён ({act})")
                notify_all("⏳ Срок жизни раздачи истёк", [f"Раздача: {nm}", f"Отдано: {format_file_size(up)}", f"Удалена {act}."])
    return triggered

================================================================================
FILE: core/soft_trash.py

# core/soft_trash.py
import os
import json
import time
import shutil
import secrets
import datetime
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, BT_BACKUP_PATH, format_file_size, write_error_log, write_log
from core.disk_monitor import _find_mount_root, list_active_disks

SNAPSHOTS_DIR = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "fastresume_snapshots")
os.makedirs(SNAPSHOTS_DIR, exist_ok=True)


def _trash_root_for_path(target_path):
    """Создаёт _tbr_trash в корне того же тома/раздела для мгновенного os.replace."""
    mount = _find_mount_root(target_path)
    cand = os.path.join(mount, "_tbr_trash")
    try:
        os.makedirs(cand, exist_ok=True)
        return cand
    except PermissionError:
        # Фоллбэк на том же томе, если корень системного диска закрыт UAC
        same_drive_app = os.path.splitdrive(os.path.abspath(APP_DATA_DIR))[0].upper()
        target_drive = os.path.splitdrive(os.path.abspath(target_path))[0].upper()
        if os.name == "nt" and same_drive_app == target_drive:
            alt = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "_tbr_trash")
        else:
            alt = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(target_path)), "_tbr_trash")
        os.makedirs(alt, exist_ok=True)
        return alt


def _entry_size(path):
    if os.path.isfile(path) or os.path.islink(path):
        try:
            return os.path.getsize(path)
        except OSError:
            return 0
    total = 0
    for root, _, files in os.walk(path, followlinks=False):
        for fn in files:
            try:
                total += os.path.getsize(os.path.join(root, fn))
            except OSError:
                pass
    return total


def _atomic_write_json(fpath, data):
    tmp = fpath + ".tmp"
    with open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        f.flush()
        os.fsync(f.fileno())
    os.replace(tmp, fpath)


def move_to_trash(target_path, name="", t_hash="", ttl_days=None, actor="admin"):
    abs_p = os.path.abspath(str(target_path or "").strip())
    if not os.path.exists(abs_p) and not os.path.islink(abs_p):
        return False, "Путь не найден"

    ttl = int(ttl_days if ttl_days is not None else cfg.getint("Trash_TTL_Days", 7))
    trash_root = _trash_root_for_path(abs_p)
    item_id = f"{int(time.time())}_{secrets.token_hex(4)}"
    item_dir = os.path.join(trash_root, item_id)
    os.makedirs(item_dir, exist_ok=True)

    sz = _entry_size(abs_p)
    base_name = os.path.basename(abs_p)
    payload_dst = os.path.join(item_dir, "payload_" + base_name)

    try:
        os.replace(abs_p, payload_dst)
    except OSError:
        shutil.move(abs_p, payload_dst)

    has_tor = False
    if t_hash and os.path.isdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        tor_cand = os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, t_hash.lower() + ".torrent")
        if os.path.isfile(tor_cand):
            shutil.copy2(tor_cand, os.path.join(item_dir, "backup.torrent"))
            has_tor = True

    now = time.time()
    meta = {
        "id": item_id,
        "name": name or base_name,
        "hash": str(t_hash or "").lower(),
        "original_path": abs_p,
        "payload_path": payload_dst,
        "trash_root": trash_root,
        "size": sz,
        "size_fmt": format_file_size(sz),
        "has_torrent": has_tor,
        "deleted_at": now,
        "expires_at": now + (max(1, ttl) * 86400),
        "actor": actor
    }
    _atomic_write_json(os.path.join(item_dir, "meta.json"), meta)
    write_log("system", f"Soft-Delete -> _tbr_trash: '{meta['name']}' ({meta['size_fmt']})")
    return True, meta


def _discover_trash_roots():
    roots = set()
    for d in list_active_disks():
        cand = os.path.join(d["path"], "_tbr_trash")
        if os.path.isdir(cand):
            roots.add(cand)
    app_tr = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "_tbr_trash")
    if os.path.isdir(app_tr):
        roots.add(app_tr)
    return sorted(roots)


def list_trash_items():
    items = []
    for tr_root in _discover_trash_roots():
        try:
            for ent in os.scandir(tr_root):
                if not ent.is_dir():
                    continue
                meta_p = os.path.join(ent.path, "meta.json")
                if not os.path.isfile(meta_p):
                    continue
                try:
                    with open(meta_p, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                        m = json.load(f)
                    m["item_dir"] = ent.path
                    m["deleted_fmt"] = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(m.get("deleted_at", 0)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
                    m["expires_fmt"] = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(m.get("expires_at", 0)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
                    items.append(m)
                except Exception:
                    continue
        except Exception:
            continue
    items.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("deleted_at", 0), reverse=True)
    return items


def restore_trash_item(item_id):
    target = next((x for x in list_trash_items() if x.get("id") == item_id), None)
    if not target:
        return False, "Запись в корзине не найдена"
    orig = target["original_path"]
    payload = target["payload_path"]
    if os.path.exists(orig):
        return False, f"Целевой путь уже существует: {orig}"
    if not os.path.exists(payload):
        return False, "Содержимое корзины отсутствует на диске"

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(orig), exist_ok=True)
    try:
        os.replace(payload, orig)
    except OSError:
        shutil.move(payload, orig)

    tor_bak = os.path.join(target["item_dir"], "backup.torrent")
    if os.path.isfile(tor_bak) and target.get("hash") and os.path.isdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        dst_tor = os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, target["hash"] + ".torrent")
        if not os.path.exists(dst_tor):
            shutil.copy2(tor_bak, dst_tor)

    shutil.rmtree(target["item_dir"], ignore_errors=True)
    return True, f"Восстановлено в {orig}"


def purge_trash_item(item_id):
    target = next((x for x in list_trash_items() if x.get("id") == item_id), None)
    if not target:
        return False, "Запись не найдена"
    shutil.rmtree(target["item_dir"], ignore_errors=True)
    return True, f"Удалено безвозвратно: {target.get('name')}"


def cleanup_expired_trash():
    now = time.time()
    removed = 0
    for item in list_trash_items():
        if float(item.get("expires_at") or 0) <= now:
            shutil.rmtree(item["item_dir"], ignore_errors=True)
            removed += 1
    return removed


def create_fastresume_snapshot(hashes, reason="pre_op", max_keep=10):
    if not hashes or not os.path.isdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        return None
    ts_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    snap_dir = os.path.join(SNAPSHOTS_DIR, f"{ts_str}_{reason}")
    os.makedirs(snap_dir, exist_ok=True)
    copied = 0
    for h in hashes:
        hl = str(h or "").strip().lower()
        for ext in (".fastresume", ".torrent"):
            src = os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, hl + ext)
            if os.path.isfile(src):
                shutil.copy2(src, os.path.join(snap_dir, hl + ext))
                copied += 1
    if copied == 0:
        shutil.rmtree(snap_dir, ignore_errors=True)
        return None

    try:
        snaps = sorted(
            [os.path.join(SNAPSHOTS_DIR, d) for d in os.listdir(SNAPSHOTS_DIR) if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(SNAPSHOTS_DIR, d))],
            key=lambda p: os.path.getmtime(p), reverse=True
        )
        for old_d in snaps[max_keep:]:
            shutil.rmtree(old_d, ignore_errors=True)
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("rotate fastresume_snapshots", e)
    return snap_dir

================================================================================
FILE: core/summary_tracker.py

# core/summary_tracker.py
import os
import time
import csv
import datetime
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import LOG_FOLDER, format_file_size, sanitize_csv_cell, write_error_log
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info
from core.history_db import HistoryDB, AUTO_FOLDER, MANUAL_FOLDER
from core.forecaster import fmt_ratio_h, fmt_speed, calculate_smart_forecast, export_forecast_csv
from core.scheduler import SnapshotScheduler, MIN_DELTA_SEC
from core.widget_service import build_detailed_speed_series, build_hourly_aggregated_speed_series, compute_top_n_torrents
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db


class SummaryTracker:
    def __init__(self, log_folder=LOG_FOLDER):
        self.log_folder = log_folder
        self.auto_folder = AUTO_FOLDER
        self.manual_folder = MANUAL_FOLDER
        self.db = HistoryDB(auto_folder=self.auto_folder)
        self._get_summaries_fn = None
        self.scheduler = SnapshotScheduler(
            db=self.db,
            on_record_fn=self.record_snapshot
        )

    def record_snapshot(self, summaries, min_delta_sec=MIN_DELTA_SEC):
        return self.db.record_snapshot(summaries, min_delta_sec=min_delta_sec)

    def notify_interval_changed(self):
        self.scheduler.notify_interval_changed()

    def flush_on_exit(self):
        if self._get_summaries_fn:
            self.scheduler.flush_on_exit(self._get_summaries_fn)

    def get_stats(self, summaries):
        now_ts = time.time()
        total_uploaded = sum(s.get('uploaded', 0) for s in summaries)
        total_size = sum(s.get('size', 0) for s in summaries)

        latest_snap, prev_snap, snap_1h, snap_1h_torrents, snap_24h, snap_24h_torrents = self.db.get_stats_snapshots(now_ts)
        dt_1h = max(1, now_ts - snap_1h['timestamp']) if snap_1h else 3600
        dt_24h = max(1, now_ts - snap_24h['timestamp']) if snap_24h else 86400

        global_hour_volume, global_hour_note = 0, "ожидание замера"
        if latest_snap:
            elapsed_sec = now_ts - latest_snap.get('timestamp', now_ts)
            if 300 <= elapsed_sec <= 7200:
                global_hour_volume = int((max(0, total_uploaded - latest_snap.get('total_uploaded', total_uploaded)) / elapsed_sec) * 3600)
                global_hour_note = f"прошло {int(elapsed_sec // 60)} мин с техлога"
            elif elapsed_sec < 300 and prev_snap:
                dt_prev = max(1, latest_snap['timestamp'] - prev_snap['timestamp'])
                global_hour_volume = int((max(0, latest_snap.get('total_uploaded', 0) - prev_snap.get('total_uploaded', 0)) / dt_prev) * 3600)
                global_hour_note = "прошлый полный час (<5м нового)"
            elif snap_1h:
                global_hour_volume = max(0, total_uploaded - snap_1h.get('total_uploaded', total_uploaded))
                global_hour_note = "по снимку ~1ч назад"

        if snap_24h:
            global_day_volume = max(0, total_uploaded - snap_24h.get('total_uploaded', total_uploaded))
        else:
            active_times = [s.get('active_seconds', 0) for s in summaries if s.get('active_seconds', 0) > 0]
            max_act = max(active_times) if active_times else 0
            global_day_volume = int((total_uploaded / max_act) * 86400) if max_act > 0 else 0

        if global_hour_volume == 0 and global_day_volume > 0:
            global_hour_volume = int(global_day_volume / 24)
            global_hour_note = "расчётное от суток"

        global_hour_speed_bps = global_hour_volume / 3600.0 if global_hour_volume > 0 else 0.0
        global_day_speed_bps = global_day_volume / 86400.0 if global_day_volume > 0 else 0.0
        max_act_global = max((s.get('active_seconds', 1) for s in summaries), default=1)

        enhanced_items = []
        for s in summaries:
            item = dict(s)
            h_id = (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower()
            up, act, sz = s.get('uploaded', 0), s.get('active_seconds', 0), s.get('size', 0)
            item['ratio'] = round(up / sz, 2) if sz > 0 else 0.0
            avg_all = (up / act) if act > 0 else 0
            item['speed_all_time'], item['speed_all_time_fmt'] = avg_all, fmt_speed(avg_all)

            delta_1h = max(0, up - snap_1h_torrents[h_id]) if h_id in snap_1h_torrents else 0
            sp_1h = (delta_1h / dt_1h) if h_id in snap_1h_torrents else 0
            item['speed_1h'], item['speed_1h_fmt'] = sp_1h, fmt_speed(sp_1h)

            delta_24h = max(0, up - snap_24h_torrents[h_id]) if h_id in snap_24h_torrents else 0
            sp_24h = (delta_24h / dt_24h) if h_id in snap_24h_torrents else 0
            item['speed_24h'], item['speed_24h_fmt'], item['delta_24h'] = sp_24h, fmt_speed(sp_24h), delta_24h

            vol_1h = delta_1h if delta_1h > 0 else int(avg_all * 3600)
            vol_24h = delta_24h if delta_24h > 0 else int(avg_all * 86400)
            item['vol_hour'], item['vol_hour_fmt'] = vol_1h, f"{vol_1h / (1024 * 1024):.2f} МБ/ч"
            item['vol_day'], item['vol_day_fmt'] = vol_24h, f"{vol_24h / (1024 * 1024):.2f} МБ/день"

            r_h_all = (avg_all * 3600.0 / sz) if sz > 0 else 0.0
            item['ratio_h_all'], item['ratio_h_all_fmt'] = r_h_all, fmt_ratio_h(r_h_all)
            item['ratio_h_hour'] = (sp_1h * 3600.0 / sz) if sz > 0 else 0.0
            item['ratio_h_hour_fmt'] = fmt_ratio_h(item['ratio_h_hour'])
            item['ratio_h_day'] = (sp_24h * 3600.0 / sz) if sz > 0 else 0.0
            item['ratio_h_day_fmt'] = fmt_ratio_h(item['ratio_h_day'])
            enhanced_items.append(item)

        return {
            'total_torrents': len(summaries), 'total_size': total_size,
            'total_size_fmt': format_file_size(total_size), 'total_uploaded': total_uploaded,
            'total_uploaded_fmt': format_file_size(total_uploaded), 'global_hour_volume': global_hour_volume,
            'global_hour_volume_fmt': f"{format_file_size(global_hour_volume)} / час",
            'global_hour_speed_fmt': fmt_speed(global_hour_speed_bps), 'global_hour_note': global_hour_note,
            'global_day_volume': global_day_volume, 'global_day_volume_fmt': f"{format_file_size(global_day_volume)} / день",
            'global_day_speed_fmt': fmt_speed(global_day_speed_bps),
            'global_ratio_h_all_fmt': fmt_ratio_h((total_uploaded / max(1, max_act_global) * 3600.0 / total_size) if total_size > 0 else 0.0),
            'global_ratio_h_day_fmt': fmt_ratio_h((global_day_volume / 24.0 / total_size) if total_size > 0 else 0.0),
            'global_ratio_h_hour_fmt': fmt_ratio_h((global_hour_volume / total_size) if total_size > 0 else 0.0),
            'items': enhanced_items
        }

    def get_charts_data(self, summaries, hours=24, top_n=5, top_period='1d'):
        now_ts = time.time()
        win_hours = hours if hours in (6, 12, 24, 72, 168) else 24
        top_limit = max(1, min(25, int(top_n or 5)))
        per = top_period if top_period in ('1d', '7d', '30d', 'all') else '1d'

        rows = self.db.get_snapshots_since(now_ts - (14 * 86400))
        daily_map, prev_row = {}, None
        for r in rows:
            if prev_row is not None:
                t1, t2 = prev_row['timestamp'], r['timestamp']
                dt_sec = t2 - t1
                delta = max(0, r['total_uploaded'] - prev_row['total_uploaded'])
                if dt_sec >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and delta > 0:
                    cur_ts = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(t1).replace(minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
                    while cur_ts < t2:
                        b_end = cur_ts + 3600.0
                        overlap = min(t2, b_end) - max(t1, cur_ts)
                        if overlap > 0:
                            dk = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(cur_ts).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
                            daily_map[dk] = daily_map.get(dk, 0.0) + delta * (overlap / dt_sec)
                        cur_ts = b_end
            prev_row = r

        sorted_days = sorted(daily_map.keys())[-14:]
        speed_series = build_detailed_speed_series(self.db, hours=win_hours) if win_hours <= 24 else build_hourly_aggregated_speed_series(self.db, hours=win_hours)

        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
        top_items, others = compute_top_n_torrents(self.db, summaries, live=live, top_n=top_limit, period=per, with_others=True)

        return {
            'daily': {'labels': [f"{d[8:10]}.{d[5:7]}" for d in sorted_days], 'values': [round(daily_map[d] / (1024 ** 3), 3) for d in sorted_days]},
            'hourly': {'hours': win_hours, 'labels': [p['label'] for p in speed_series], 'values': [p['speed_mbs'] for p in speed_series], 'meta': speed_series},
            'detailed': speed_series,
            'top5': {'n': top_limit, 'period': per, 'labels': [x['short_name'] for x in top_items], 'full_labels': [x['full_name'] for x in top_items], 'values': [x['gb'] for x in top_items], 'others': others}
        }

    def get_torrent_chart_series(self, t_hash, period='24h'):
        hours = {'24h': 24, '7d': 168, '30d': 720}.get(period, 24)
        since_ts = time.time() - (hours * 3600)
        hashes = app_state_db.get_lineage_hashes(t_hash)
        if not hashes:
            return []

        merges_by_target = {}
        for m in app_state_db.get_merges_for_targets(hashes):
            merges_by_target.setdefault(m['target_hash'], []).append(m)

        placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in hashes)
        with self.db._lock:
            try:
                with self.db._get_connection() as conn:
                    rows = conn.execute(f"""
                        SELECT ts.hash_id, s.timestamp, s.datetime, ts.uploaded
                        FROM snapshots s
                        JOIN torrent_snapshots ts ON s.id = ts.snapshot_id
                        WHERE COALESCE(s.ignore_in_stats, 0) = 0 AND ts.hash_id IN ({placeholders}) AND s.timestamp >= ?
                        ORDER BY ts.hash_id ASC, s.timestamp ASC
                    """, (*hashes, since_ts)).fetchall()
            except Exception:
                rows = []

        max_bps = (max(10, cfg.getint('Max_Channel_Mbps', 1000)) * 1024 * 1024) / 8.0
        buckets, consumed_merges, by_hash = {}, set(), {}
        for r in rows:
            by_hash.setdefault(r['hash_id'], []).append(r)

        for hl, h_rows in by_hash.items():
            t_merges = merges_by_target.get(hl, [])
            prev = None
            for r in h_rows:
                if prev is not None:
                    t1, t2 = float(prev['timestamp']), float(r['timestamp'])
                    dt = t2 - t1
                    if dt >= MIN_DELTA_SEC:
                        delta = max(0, int(r['uploaded']) - int(prev['uploaded']))
                        is_merge = False
                        for m in t_merges:
                            if m['id'] not in consumed_merges and (t1 - 5.0) <= float(m['created_at']) <= (t2 + 5.0):
                                injected = max(0, int(m['final_uploaded']) - int(m['target_uploaded']))
                                delta = max(0, delta - injected)
                                is_merge = True
                                consumed_merges.add(m['id'])

                        bps = delta / dt
                        if bps > max_bps:
                            delta, bps = 0, 0.0

                        b_key = int(round(t2 / 60.0) * 60)
                        b = buckets.setdefault(b_key, {'delta': 0, 'bps': 0.0, 'dt_str': str(r['datetime'] or ''), 'is_merge': False})
                        b['delta'] += delta
                        b['bps'] += bps
                        if is_merge:
                            b['is_merge'] = True
                prev = r

        points = []
        for b_key in sorted(buckets.keys()):
            b = buckets[b_key]
            dt_raw = b['dt_str']
            lbl = dt_raw[11:16] if hours <= 24 else dt_raw[5:16]
            points.append({
                'label': lbl,
                'speed_mbs': round(b['bps'] / (1024 * 1024), 3),
                'delta_fmt': format_file_size(b['delta']),
                'is_merge': b['is_merge']
            })
        return points

    def calculate_smart_forecast(self, target_type, target_hash, target_str, speed_str, summaries):
        return calculate_smart_forecast(self.db, target_type, target_hash, target_str, speed_str, summaries)

    def export_forecast_csv(self, forecast_data):
        return export_forecast_csv(self.manual_folder, forecast_data)

    def save_manual_csv(self, items):
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
        filepath = os.path.join(self.manual_folder, f"summary_manual_{ts}.csv")
        try:
            with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8-sig', newline='') as f:
                f.write(f"# === РУЧНАЯ ВЫГРУЗКА СВОДКИ ({datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}) ===\n# Раздач: {len(items)}\n")
                w = csv.writer(f, delimiter=';')
                w.writerow(['Имя', 'Хэш', 'Размер (байт)', 'Размер (ГБ)', 'Отдано (байт)', 'Отдано (ГБ)', 'Ratio', 'Ср. скорость (всё время)', 'Скорость за 1ч', 'Скорость за 24ч', 'МБ/ч', 'МБ/день', 'Ratio/ч (всего)', 'Ratio/ч (1ч)', 'Ratio/ч (24ч)', 'Дата создания'])
                for s in items:
                    w.writerow([
                        sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('name', '')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('hash_id', '')),
                        s.get('size', 0), f"{s.get('size', 0) / (1024 ** 3):.2f}",
                        s.get('uploaded', 0), f"{s.get('uploaded', 0) / (1024 ** 3):.2f}", f"{s.get('ratio', 0.0):.2f}",
                        sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_all_time_fmt', '0 B/s')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_1h_fmt', '0 B/s')),
                        sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_24h_fmt', '0 B/s')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('vol_hour_fmt', '0.00 МБ/ч')),
                        sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('vol_day_fmt', '0.00 МБ/день')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_all_fmt', '0.000 /ч')),
                        sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_hour_fmt', '0.000 /ч')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_day_fmt', '0.000 /ч')),
                        sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('created', ''))
                    ])
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("Ошибка ручного сохранения CSV", e)
        return filepath

    def get_torrent_hourly_history(self, t_hash, limit_hours=36):
        rows = self.db.get_torrent_history_rows(t_hash, limit_hours=limit_hours)
        rows.reverse()
        result, prev_snap = [], None
        for snap in rows:
            up, ts = snap.get('uploaded'), float(snap.get('timestamp', 0) or 0)
            if up is not None:
                if prev_snap is not None:
                    dt_sec = max(1.0, ts - float(prev_snap.get('timestamp', ts) or ts))
                    delta = max(0, up - prev_snap.get('uploaded', up))
                    bps, interval_min = delta / dt_sec, int(round(dt_sec / 60.0))
                else:
                    delta, bps, interval_min = 0, 0.0, 0
                result.append({'datetime': snap.get('datetime', ''), 'timestamp': int(ts), 'interval_min': interval_min, 'uploaded': up, 'uploaded_fmt': format_file_size(up), 'delta_bytes': delta, 'delta_mb': f"{delta / (1024*1024):.2f} МБ", 'speed_fmt': fmt_speed(bps)})
                prev_snap = snap
        result.reverse()
        return result

    def start_auto_export(self, get_summaries_fn):
        self._get_summaries_fn = get_summaries_fn
        self.scheduler.start(get_summaries_fn)


summary_tracker = SummaryTracker()

# core/swarm_analytics.py
import re
import urllib.parse
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from core.qb_api import _get_qb_json, get_torrent_peers

_TITLE_STRIP_RE = re.compile(
    r'\[[^\]]*\]|\([^\)]*\)|\b(?:repack|rip|bdrip|web-dl|webdl|hdrip|bluray|1080p|720p|2160p|480p|4k|uhd|x264|x265|h264|h265|hevc|av1|rus|eng|multi|sub|dub|fitgirl|dodi|xatab|v?\d+(?:\.\d+)+)\b',
    re.IGNORECASE
)


def get_tracker_survivability(summaries, live, hidden_hashes=None):
    """#19 Рейтинг живучести трекеров и GB-ROI (мультитрекерные выделяются отдельно)."""
    hidden = {h.lower() for h in (hidden_hashes or set())}
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in (live or {}).items()}
    groups = {}

    for s in summaries:
        hl = (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower()
        if not hl or hl in hidden:
            continue
        lv = live_lower.get(hl, {})
        tr_list = list(s.get('trackers') or [])
        tr_live = str(lv.get('tracker') or '').strip()
        if tr_live and tr_live not in tr_list:
            tr_list.insert(0, tr_live)

        hosts = {urllib.parse.urlparse(t).netloc.lower().split(':')[0] for t in tr_list if t and urllib.parse.urlparse(t).netloc}
        if len(hosts) > 1:
            key = '🔀 Мультитрекерные'
        elif len(hosts) == 1:
            key = next(iter(hosts))
        else:
            key = 'Без трекера / DHT'

        up = int(lv.get('uploaded', s.get('uploaded', 0)) or 0)
        sz = int(lv.get('total_size', s.get('size', 0)) or 0)
        st = str(lv.get('state', '')).lower()
        is_ok = st not in ('error', 'missingfiles')

        g = groups.setdefault(key, {'tracker': key, 'count': 0, 'ok_count': 0, 'size': 0, 'uploaded': 0})
        g['count'] += 1
        if is_ok:
            g['ok_count'] += 1
        g['size'] += sz
        g['uploaded'] += up

    out = []
    for g in groups.values():
        sz_gb = max(0.01, g['size'] / (1024 ** 3))
        up_gb = g['uploaded'] / (1024 ** 3)
        out.append({
            'tracker': g['tracker'],
            'count': g['count'],
            'uptime_pct': round((g['ok_count'] / max(1, g['count'])) * 100.0, 1),
            'gb_roi': round(up_gb / sz_gb, 2),
            'uploaded_gb': round(up_gb, 2)
        })
    out.sort(key=lambda x: x['uploaded_gb'], reverse=True)
    return out[:20]


def get_protocol_efficiency(ip, port, live, hidden_hashes=None):
    """#43 uTP против TCP и IPv4 против IPv6 (только топ-12 активных раздач прямо сейчас)."""
    if not port or not live:
        return {'utp': 0, 'tcp': 0, 'ipv4': 0, 'ipv6': 0, 'utp_speed': 0, 'tcp_speed': 0, 'sampled': 0}
    hidden = {h.lower() for h in (hidden_hashes or set())}
    active = sorted(
        [(h, v) for h, v in live.items() if h.lower() not in hidden and (int(v.get('upspeed', 0) or 0) + int(v.get('dlspeed', 0) or 0)) > 0],
        key=lambda kv: int(kv[1].get('upspeed', 0) or 0) + int(kv[1].get('dlspeed', 0) or 0),
        reverse=True
    )[:12]

    utp_cnt = tcp_cnt = v4_cnt = v6_cnt = utp_spd = tcp_spd = 0
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=6) as pool:
        futs = [pool.submit(get_torrent_peers, ip, port, h) for h, _ in active]
        for fut in as_completed(futs):
            try:
                peers = (fut.result() or {}).get('peers') or {}
                for p in peers.values():
                    if not isinstance(p, dict):
                        continue
                    flags = str(p.get('flags') or '')
                    ip_addr = str(p.get('ip') or '')
                    spd = int(p.get('up_speed', 0) or 0) + int(p.get('dl_speed', 0) or 0)
                    if 'u' in flags:
                        utp_cnt += 1
                        utp_spd += spd
                    else:
                        tcp_cnt += 1
                        tcp_spd += spd
                    if ':' in ip_addr:
                        v6_cnt += 1
                    elif ip_addr:
                        v4_cnt += 1
            except Exception:
                pass

    return {
        'utp': utp_cnt, 'tcp': tcp_cnt,
        'ipv4': v4_cnt, 'ipv6': v6_cnt,
        'utp_avg_kbs': round((utp_spd / max(1, utp_cnt)) / 1024.0, 1),
        'tcp_avg_kbs': round((tcp_spd / max(1, tcp_cnt)) / 1024.0, 1),
        'sampled': len(active)
    }


def get_qb_slot_recommendations(ip, port, live, max_channel_mbps=1000):
    """#50 Авто-тюнер слотов qB (только рекомендации с объяснением «почему»)."""
    if not port:
        return []
    prefs = _get_qb_json(ip, port, "app/preferences", timeout=2.0) or {}
    recs = []
    max_up_slots = int(prefs.get('max_active_uploads') or 0)
    global_connec = int(prefs.get('max_connec') or 0)
    up_slots_per_tor = int(prefs.get('max_uploads_per_torrent') or 0)

    if 0 < max_up_slots < 10 and len(live or {}) > 30:
        recs.append(f"🎰 Увеличьте max_active_uploads (сейчас {max_up_slots}) до 15–20: у вас {len(live)} раздач, мелкие сиды могут простаивать в очереди.")
    if max_channel_mbps <= 150 and global_connec > 500:
        recs.append(f"🔌 Снизьте max_connec (сейчас {global_connec}) до 250–300: для канала {max_channel_mbps} Мбит/с лишние соединения только грузят роутер.")
    if up_slots_per_tor > 10:
        recs.append(f"⚡ Умерьте слоты отдачи на торрент (сейчас {up_slots_per_tor}) до 4–8: дробление канала на десятки пиров снижает эффективность TCP.")
    if not recs:
        recs.append("✅ Текущие лимиты слотов и соединений qBittorrent сбалансированы под ваш канал.")
    return recs


def find_normalized_title_groups(items, hidden_hashes=None):
    """#39 Группировщик версий по нормализованному названию (информационный инструмент)."""
    hidden = {h.lower() for h in (hidden_hashes or set())}
    buckets = {}
    for it in items:
        h = str(it.get('hash') or it.get('hash_id') or '').lower()
        if h in hidden:
            continue
        nm = str(it.get('name') or '')
        norm = re.sub(r'[._\-]+', ' ', _TITLE_STRIP_RE.sub(' ', nm.lower()))
        norm = ' '.join(norm.split())
        if len(norm) < 4:
            continue
        buckets.setdefault(norm, []).append(it)

    groups = []
    for norm, grp in buckets.items():
        if len(grp) < 2:
            continue
        tot_sz = sum(int(x.get('size', 0) or 0) for x in grp)
        groups.append({
            'title': norm,
            'count': len(grp),
            'total_size': tot_sz,
            'items': [{'name': x.get('name', ''), 'size_fmt': x.get('size_fmt', ''), 'uploaded_fmt': x.get('uploaded_fmt', '')} for x in grp]
        })
    groups.sort(key=lambda x: x['total_size'], reverse=True)
    return groups[:30]

# core/system_alerts.py
import time
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, write_log
from core.notifiers import notify_all
from core.disk_monitor import get_monitored_disk_info


class SystemAlertsMonitor:
    def __init__(self):
        self.disk_state = 'OK'  # OK | ALERT
        self.qb_state = 'OK'    # OK | ALERT
        self._qb_down_since = None

    def check(self, qb_available, live=None):
        now = time.time()

        if cfg.getboolean('Alert_QB_Down', False):
            if not qb_available:
                if self._qb_down_since is None:
                    self._qb_down_since = now
                elif (now - self._qb_down_since) >= 150 and self.qb_state == 'OK':
                    self.qb_state = 'ALERT'
                    write_log('automation', "ALERT: WebUI qBittorrent недоступен > 2.5 мин")
                    notify_all("🚨 tbr PRO — qBittorrent недоступен", ["WebUI qBittorrent не отвечает уже более 2.5 минут."], critical=True)
            else:
                self._qb_down_since = None
                if self.qb_state == 'ALERT':
                    self.qb_state = 'OK'
                    write_log('automation', "RECOVERED: WebUI qBittorrent снова в сети")
                    notify_all("✅ tbr PRO — qBittorrent восстановлен", ["Связь с WebUI qBittorrent успешно восстановлена."], critical=True)

        min_gib = float(cfg.getint('Alert_Disk_Min_GiB', 0) or 0)
        if min_gib > 0:
            disk = get_monitored_disk_info(live)
            if disk.get('total_bytes', 0) > 0:
                free_gib = float(disk['free_bytes']) / (1024 ** 3)
                free_fmt = format_file_size(disk['free_bytes'])
                if free_gib < min_gib and self.disk_state == 'OK':
                    self.disk_state = 'ALERT'
                    write_log('automation', f"ALERT: мало места на {disk['path']} ({free_fmt} < {min_gib:.0f} GiB)")
                    notify_all("⚠️ tbr PRO — Мало места на диске!", [f"Диск: {disk['path']}", f"Свободно: {free_fmt} (порог: {min_gib:.0f} GiB)"], critical=True)
                elif free_gib >= (min_gib + 5.0) and self.disk_state == 'ALERT':
                    self.disk_state = 'OK'
                    write_log('automation', f"RECOVERED: место на {disk['path']} в норме ({free_fmt})")
                    notify_all("✅ tbr PRO — Место на диске в норме", [f"Диск: {disk['path']}", f"Свободно: {free_fmt}"], critical=True)


system_alerts = SystemAlertsMonitor()

# core/torrent_archiver.py
import os
import io
import gzip
import time
import zipfile
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, BT_BACKUP_PATH, format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info
from core.private_vault import get_hidden_hashes

ARCHIVE_DIR = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "torrent_archive")
os.makedirs(ARCHIVE_DIR, exist_ok=True)


def _ensure_table():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS torrent_archive_index (
                        hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                        uid TEXT NOT NULL,
                        name TEXT NOT NULL,
                        tags TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                        raw_size INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                        gz_size INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                        archived_at REAL NOT NULL
                    )
                """)
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("torrent_archive_index init", e)


_ensure_table()


def sync_torrent_archive():
    if not os.path.isdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'BT_backup не найден'}

    parsed = get_all_torrents(use_cache=True)
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in live.items()}

    with app_state_db._lock:
        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
            uid_map = {r['torrent_hash']: r['torrent_uid'] for r in conn.execute("SELECT torrent_hash, torrent_uid FROM torrent_lineage").fetchall()}

    added, now = 0, time.time()
    records = []
    for ent in os.scandir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        if not ent.is_file() or not ent.name.lower().endswith('.torrent'):
            continue
        hl = ent.name[:-8].lower()
        gz_path = os.path.join(ARCHIVE_DIR, hl + '.torrent.gz')
        lv = live_lower.get(hl, {})
        st = parsed.get(hl, {})
        name = str(lv.get('name') or st.get('name') or hl)
        tags = str(lv.get('tags') or '')
        uid = uid_map.get(hl, hl)

        if not os.path.isfile(gz_path):
            try:
                raw = open(ent.path, 'rb').read()
                tmp = gz_path + '.tmp'
                with gzip.open(tmp, 'wb', compresslevel=6) as gf:
                    gf.write(raw)
                os.replace(tmp, gz_path)
                added += 1
            except Exception:
                continue

        raw_sz = ent.stat().st_size
        gz_sz = os.path.getsize(gz_path) if os.path.isfile(gz_path) else 0
        records.append((hl, uid, name, tags, raw_sz, gz_sz, now))

    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.executemany(
                    """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO torrent_archive_index
                       (hash_id, uid, name, tags, raw_size, gz_size, archived_at)
                       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                    records
                )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("sync_torrent_archive db", e)

    return {'success': True, 'added': added, 'total': len(records)}


def list_archived_torrents(query='', tag='', role='admin'):
    q = str(query or '').strip().lower()
    tg = str(tag or '').strip().lower()
    hidden = set()
    if role != 'admin':
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
        hidden = get_hidden_hashes(live)

    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM torrent_archive_index ORDER BY archived_at DESC").fetchall()]
        except Exception:
            rows = []

    out = []
    for r in rows:
        if r['hash_id'] in hidden:
            continue
        if q and q not in r['name'].lower() and q not in r['hash_id'].lower() and q not in r['uid'].lower():
            continue
        if tg and tg not in {x.strip().lower() for x in r['tags'].split(',') if x.strip()}:
            continue
        r['gz_fmt'] = format_file_size(r['gz_size'])
        out.append(r)
    return out


def get_archived_torrent_bytes(t_hash, role='admin'):
    hl = str(t_hash or '').strip().lower()
    if role != 'admin':
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
        if hl in get_hidden_hashes(live):
            return None, None
    gz_path = os.path.join(ARCHIVE_DIR, hl + '.torrent.gz')
    if not os.path.isfile(gz_path):
        return None, None
    with gzip.open(gz_path, 'rb') as gf:
        return gf.read(), hl + '.torrent'


def export_archive_zip_by_tag(tag='', role='admin'):
    items = list_archived_torrents(tag=tag, role=role)
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        for it in items:
            raw, fname = get_archived_torrent_bytes(it['hash_id'], role=role)
            if raw and fname:
                zf.writestr(fname, raw)
    return buf.getvalue(), len(items)

# core/torrent_diff.py
import os
import re
import datetime
from difflib import SequenceMatcher
from utils.bencode import decode_bencode
from utils.helpers import BT_BACKUP_PATH, format_file_size
from core.qb_api import get_torrent_files

_SEASON_EP_RE = re.compile(
    r'(?:s(\d{1,2})(?:e(\d{1,3}))?|season\s*(\d{1,2})|сезон\s*(\d{1,2})|\b(\d{1,2})x(\d{1,3})\b)',
    re.IGNORECASE
)
_JUNK_TAGS_RE = re.compile(
    r'\[[^\]]*\]|\([^\)]*\)|\b(?:repack|rip|bdrip|web-dl|webdl|hdrip|bluray|1080p|720p|2160p|480p|4k|uhd|x264|x265|h264|h265|hevc|rus|eng|multi|portable|fitgirl|dodi|xatab|build\s*\d+|\d+(?:gb|mb|tb|gib|mib|tib|fps|hz|bit|ch))\b',
    re.IGNORECASE
)
_VERSION_EXPLICIT_RE = re.compile(r'\bv(?:er(?:sion)?)?[\s._-]*(\d+(?:\.\d+)*)\b', re.IGNORECASE)
_VERSION_NUMERIC_RE = re.compile(r'\b(\d+(?:\.\d+)+)\b')

_ROMAN_MAP = {
    'I': 1, 'II': 2, 'III': 3, 'IV': 4, 'V': 5, 'VI': 6, 'VII': 7, 'VIII': 8,
    'IX': 9, 'X': 10, 'XI': 11, 'XII': 12, 'XIII': 13, 'XIV': 14, 'XV': 15
}
_ROMAN_MULTI_RE = re.compile(r'(?:^|[\s._:\-])(XIII|XIV|XV|XII|XI|VIII|VII|VI|IV|IX|III|II)(?=$|[\s._:\-])', re.IGNORECASE)
_ROMAN_SINGLE_RE = re.compile(r'(?:^|[\s._:\-])(I|V|X)(?=$|[\s._:\-])')
_ROMAN_TRAIL_RE = re.compile(r'\s+(i|v|x)$', re.IGNORECASE)
_ARABIC_PART_RE = re.compile(r'(?:^|[\s._:\-])(\d{1,4})(?=$|[\s._:\-])')
_GENERIC_ROOT_NAMES = {
    'downloads', 'download', 'torrents', 'torrent', 'games', 'movies',
    'films', 'series', 'music', 'video', 'загрузки', 'игры', 'фильмы', 'сериалы', 'торренты'
}


def _norm_rel(path):
    return str(path or '').replace('\\', '/').strip('/')


def _strip_root(path):
    p = _norm_rel(path)
    return p.split('/', 1)[1] if '/' in p else p


def _read_files_from_torrent_file(t_hash):
    if not os.path.isdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        return []
    target = t_hash.lower() + '.torrent'
    fpath = next((os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, f) for f in os.listdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH) if f.lower() == target), None)
    if not fpath:
        return []
    try:
        with open(fpath, 'rb') as fp:
            data = decode_bencode(fp.read()) or {}
        info = data.get('info') or data.get(b'info') or {}
        if not isinstance(info, dict):
            return []
        raw_name = info.get('name.utf-8') or info.get('name') or info.get(b'name') or t_hash
        root_name = raw_name.decode('utf-8', errors='replace') if isinstance(raw_name, bytes) else str(raw_name)
        if 'length' in info or b'length' in info:
            return [{'path': _norm_rel(root_name), 'size': int(info.get('length') or info.get(b'length') or 0)}]
        out = []
        for entry in (info.get('files') or info.get(b'files') or []):
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                continue
            sz = int(entry.get('length') or entry.get(b'length') or 0)
            parts_raw = entry.get('path.utf-8') or entry.get('path') or entry.get(b'path') or []
            parts = [p.decode('utf-8', errors='replace') if isinstance(p, bytes) else str(p) for p in parts_raw]
            rel = '/'.join([root_name] + parts) if root_name else '/'.join(parts)
            out.append({'path': _norm_rel(rel), 'size': sz})
        return out
    except Exception:
        return []


def _get_files_map(ip, port, t_hash):
    raw = get_torrent_files(ip, port, t_hash) if port else []
    if raw:
        return [{'path': _norm_rel(f.get('name', '')), 'size': int(f.get('size', 0) or 0)} for f in raw if f.get('name')]
    return _read_files_from_torrent_file(t_hash)


def compute_torrent_diff(ip, port, old_hash, new_hash):
    old_list = _get_files_map(ip, port, old_hash)
    new_list = _get_files_map(ip, port, new_hash)
    if not old_list and not new_list:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'Не удалось получить списки файлов (проверьте WebUI или .torrent в BT_backup).'}

    old_exact = {f['path']: f['size'] for f in old_list}
    new_exact = {f['path']: f['size'] for f in new_list}
    use_stripped = False
    if old_exact and new_exact and not (set(old_exact.keys()) & set(new_exact.keys())):
        old_sub = {_strip_root(f['path']): f['size'] for f in old_list}
        new_sub = {_strip_root(f['path']): f['size'] for f in new_list}
        if set(old_sub.keys()) & set(new_sub.keys()):
            use_stripped = True
            old_map, new_map = old_sub, new_sub
        else:
            old_map, new_map = old_exact, new_exact
    else:
        old_map, new_map = old_exact, new_exact

    all_keys = sorted(set(old_map.keys()) | set(new_map.keys()))
    rows, exact_cnt, changed_cnt, removed_cnt, added_cnt, exact_bytes = [], 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
    old_total, new_total = sum(old_map.values()), sum(new_map.values())

    for k in all_keys:
        in_old, in_new = k in old_map, k in new_map
        if in_old and in_new:
            osize, nsize = old_map[k], new_map[k]
            if osize == nsize:
                status, exact_cnt, exact_bytes = 'exact', exact_cnt + 1, exact_bytes + osize
            else:
                status, changed_cnt = 'changed', changed_cnt + 1
        elif in_old:
            osize, nsize, status, removed_cnt = old_map[k], None, 'removed', removed_cnt + 1
        else:
            osize, nsize, status, added_cnt = None, new_map[k], 'added', added_cnt + 1
        if len(rows) < 2000:
            rows.append({
                'path': k, 'status': status, 'old_size': osize, 'new_size': nsize,
                'old_size_fmt': format_file_size(osize) if osize is not None else '—',
                'new_size_fmt': format_file_size(nsize) if nsize is not None else '—',
            })

    match_pct = round(min(100.0, (exact_bytes / max(1, old_total, new_total)) * 100.0), 1)
    return {
        'success': True, 'stripped_root': use_stripped, 'match_percent': match_pct,
        'old_total_fmt': format_file_size(old_total), 'new_total_fmt': format_file_size(new_total),
        'stats': {'exact': exact_cnt, 'changed': changed_cnt, 'removed': removed_cnt, 'added': added_cnt, 'total_rows': len(all_keys), 'truncated': len(all_keys) > 2000},
        'files': rows
    }


def _extract_season_sig(name):
    sigs = []
    for m in _SEASON_EP_RE.finditer(name or ''):
        s = m.group(1) or m.group(3) or m.group(4) or m.group(5) or ''
        e = m.group(2) or m.group(6) or ''
        sigs.append((s.lstrip('0') or '0', e.lstrip('0') if e else ''))
    return sigs


def _extract_sequel_numbers(name):
    base = re.sub(r'\.[a-z0-9]{2,4}$', '', str(name or ''))
    base = _JUNK_TAGS_RE.sub(' ', base)
    base = _VERSION_EXPLICIT_RE.sub(' ', base)
    base = _VERSION_NUMERIC_RE.sub(' ', base)
    nums = []
    for m in _ROMAN_MULTI_RE.finditer(base):
        val = _ROMAN_MAP.get(m.group(1).upper())
        if val:
            nums.append(val)
    for m in _ROMAN_SINGLE_RE.finditer(base):
        val = _ROMAN_MAP.get(m.group(1))
        if val:
            nums.append(val)
    if not nums:
        mt = _ROMAN_TRAIL_RE.search(base.strip())
        if mt:
            val = _ROMAN_MAP.get(mt.group(1).upper())
            if val:
                nums.append(val)
    for m in _ARABIC_PART_RE.finditer(base):
        nums.append(int(m.group(1)))
    return tuple(nums)


def _extract_version_tuple(name):
    raw = str(name or '')
    m = _VERSION_EXPLICIT_RE.search(raw)
    if m:
        try:
            return tuple(int(x) for x in m.group(1).split('.') if x.isdigit())
        except Exception:
            pass
    m2 = _VERSION_NUMERIC_RE.search(raw)
    if m2:
        try:
            parts = [int(x) for x in m2.group(1).split('.') if x.isdigit()]
            if len(parts) >= 2:
                return tuple(parts)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return None


def _compare_version_tuples(ver_a, ver_b):
    if not ver_a or not ver_b:
        return 0
    max_len = max(len(ver_a), len(ver_b))
    pad_a = ver_a + (0,) * (max_len - len(ver_a))
    pad_b = ver_b + (0,) * (max_len - len(ver_b))
    if pad_a < pad_b:
        return -1
    if pad_a > pad_b:
        return 1
    return 0


def _clean_title(name):
    s = re.sub(r'\.[a-z0-9]{2,4}$', '', str(name or '').lower())
    s = _JUNK_TAGS_RE.sub(' ', s)
    s = _VERSION_EXPLICIT_RE.sub(' ', s)
    s = _VERSION_NUMERIC_RE.sub(' ', s)
    s = re.sub(r'[._\-]+', ' ', s)
    return ' '.join(s.split())


def _extract_content_leaf(item):
    sp = str(item.get('save_path') or '').strip()
    cp = str(item.get('content_path') or '').strip()
    if not cp:
        return ''
    cp_norm = os.path.normcase(os.path.normpath(cp))
    sp_norm = os.path.normcase(os.path.normpath(sp)) if sp else ''
    if sp_norm and cp_norm == sp_norm:
        return ''
    leaf = os.path.basename(cp_norm).strip()
    if not leaf or leaf in _GENERIC_ROOT_NAMES:
        return ''
    return leaf


def find_smart_merge_candidates(items, max_results=20):
    if not items or len(items) < 2:
        return []

    prepared = []
    for it in items:
        nm = it.get('name', '')
        prepared.append({
            'item': it,
            'clean': _clean_title(nm),
            'version': _extract_version_tuple(nm),
            'seasons': _extract_season_sig(nm),
            'sequels': _extract_sequel_numbers(nm),
            'cp_leaf': _extract_content_leaf(it),
            'added_on': int(it.get('added_on') or it.get('created_ts') or 0)
        })

    candidates = []
    n = len(prepared)
    for i in range(n):
        a = prepared[i]
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            b = prepared[j]
            if (a['seasons'] and b['seasons'] and a['seasons'] != b['seasons']) or (bool(a['seasons']) != bool(b['seasons'])):
                continue
            if a['sequels'] != b['sequels']:
                continue

            same_content_dir = bool(a['cp_leaf'] and b['cp_leaf'] and a['cp_leaf'] == b['cp_leaf'])
            sim = SequenceMatcher(None, a['clean'], b['clean']).ratio() if (a['clean'] and b['clean']) else 0.0

            if not ((same_content_dir and sim >= 0.55) or sim >= 0.78):
                continue

            ver_cmp = _compare_version_tuples(a['version'], b['version'])
            if ver_cmp != 0:
                old_it, new_it = (a['item'], b['item']) if ver_cmp < 0 else (b['item'], a['item'])
                v_old = a['version'] if ver_cmp < 0 else b['version']
                v_new = b['version'] if ver_cmp < 0 else a['version']
                reason = f"Обновление версии: v{'.'.join(map(str, v_old))} → v{'.'.join(map(str, v_new))}"
            else:
                ta, tb = a['added_on'], b['added_on']
                if ta > 0 and tb > 0 and ta != tb:
                    old_it, new_it = (a['item'], b['item']) if ta < tb else (b['item'], a['item'])
                    d_old = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(min(ta, tb)).strftime('%d.%m.%Y')
                    d_new = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(max(ta, tb)).strftime('%d.%m.%Y')
                    reason = f"По дате добавления ({d_old} → {d_new})"
                else:
                    old_it, new_it = a['item'], b['item']
                    reason = f"Одинаковая папка контента: {a['cp_leaf']}" if same_content_dir else f"Совпадение названия {int(sim * 100)}%"

            candidates.append({
                'old_hash': old_it['hash'], 'new_hash': new_it['hash'],
                'old_name': old_it['name'], 'new_name': new_it['name'],
                'old_up_fmt': old_it.get('uploaded_fmt', '0 B'), 'new_up_fmt': new_it.get('uploaded_fmt', '0 B'),
                'old_up_bytes': int(old_it.get('uploaded', 0) or 0), 'new_up_bytes': int(new_it.get('uploaded', 0) or 0),
                'similarity': int(round(sim * 100)), 'same_dir': same_content_dir,
                'reason': reason, 'rank': (1.5 if ver_cmp != 0 else (1.0 if same_content_dir else 0.0)) + sim
            })

    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x['rank'], reverse=True)
    return candidates[:max_results]
