# core/torrent_parser.py
import os
import time
import shutil
import datetime
import threading
from utils.bencode import decode_bencode
from utils.helpers import BT_BACKUP_PATH, write_error_log

_LOCK = threading.Lock()
# { hash_id: {'sig': (mtime_ns, size), 'data': dict} }
_FILE_CACHE = {}


def force_clear_cache():
    with _LOCK:
        _FILE_CACHE.clear()


def _extract_str(val):
    if val is None:
        return ""
    if isinstance(val, bytes):
        try:
            return val.decode('utf-8')
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            try:
                return val.decode('cp1251')
            except Exception:
                return val.decode('latin1', errors='ignore')
    return str(val).strip()


def _get_field(data, *keys):
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return None
    for k in keys:
        if k in data and data[k] is not None:
            return data[k]
        if isinstance(k, str):
            kb = k.encode('ascii', errors='ignore')
            if kb in data and data[kb] is not None:
                return data[kb]
    return None


def parse_single_torrent(fastresume_path, tor_map=None):
    t_hash = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(fastresume_path))[0].lower()
    base_dir = os.path.dirname(fastresume_path)

    with open(fastresume_path, 'rb') as f:
        fr_data = decode_bencode(f.read())
    if not isinstance(fr_data, dict):
        raise ValueError(f"Битый или неполный fastresume: {t_hash}")

    tor_data = {}
    tor_path = (tor_map or {}).get(t_hash) or os.path.join(base_dir, t_hash + '.torrent')
    if os.path.exists(tor_path):
        try:
            with open(tor_path, 'rb') as f:
                tor_data = decode_bencode(f.read()) or {}
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log(f"decode torrent {t_hash}", e)

    info_dict = _get_field(tor_data, 'info') or {}

    name = ""
    tor_name = _extract_str(_get_field(info_dict, 'name.utf-8', 'name'))
    if tor_name and tor_name.lower() != t_hash:
        name = tor_name
    if not name:
        qbt_name = _extract_str(_get_field(fr_data, 'qBt-name'))
        if qbt_name and qbt_name.lower() != t_hash:
            name = qbt_name
    if not name:
        fr_name = _extract_str(_get_field(fr_data, 'name'))
        if fr_name and fr_name.lower() != t_hash:
            name = fr_name

    save_path = _extract_str(_get_field(fr_data, 'save_path', 'qBt-savePath'))
    if not name and save_path:
        base_name = os.path.basename(os.path.normpath(save_path))
        if base_name and len(base_name) > 2 and base_name.lower() != t_hash:
            name = base_name
    if not name:
        name = t_hash

    size = 0
    if 'length' in info_dict or b'length' in info_dict:
        size = int(_get_field(info_dict, 'length') or 0)
    elif 'files' in info_dict or b'files' in info_dict:
        files = _get_field(info_dict, 'files') or []
        size = sum(int(_get_field(f, 'length') or 0) for f in files)
    if size <= 0:
        size = int(_get_field(fr_data, 'total_size', 'total_downloaded') or 1)

    uploaded = int(_get_field(fr_data, 'total_uploaded') or 0)
    active_seconds = int(_get_field(fr_data, 'active_time', 'seeding_time') or 0)

    added_time = _get_field(fr_data, 'added_time', 'completed_time', 'creation date')
    if added_time and int(added_time) > 0:
        created_ts = int(added_time)
        created_str = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(created_ts).strftime('%d.%m.%Y %H:%M')
    else:
        try:
            mtime = int(os.path.getmtime(fastresume_path))
            created_ts = mtime
            created_str = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(mtime).strftime('%d.%m.%Y %H:%M')
        except Exception:
            created_ts = 0
            created_str = "Дата неизвестна"

    trackers = []
    raw_trackers = _get_field(fr_data, 'trackers')
    if raw_trackers and isinstance(raw_trackers, list):
        for tr in raw_trackers:
            if isinstance(tr, list) and tr:
                trackers.append(_extract_str(tr[0]))
            elif isinstance(tr, (str, bytes)):
                trackers.append(_extract_str(tr))

    return {
        'name': name,
        'hash_id': t_hash,
        'size': size,
        'uploaded': uploaded,
        'ratio': round(uploaded / size, 2) if size > 0 else 0.0,
        'active_seconds': active_seconds,
        'save_path': save_path,
        'trackers': trackers,
        'created': created_str,
        'created_ts': created_ts,
        'info_hash': t_hash,
        'files': []
    }


def get_all_torrents(use_cache=True):
    if not os.path.exists(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        return {}

    try:
        entries = list(os.scandir(BT_BACKUP_PATH))
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("scandir BT_backup", e)
        return {}

    tor_map = {}
    fr_entries = []
    for ent in entries:
        low = ent.name.lower()
        if low.endswith('.torrent'):
            tor_map[low[:-8]] = ent.path
        elif low.endswith('.fastresume'):
            fr_entries.append((low[:-11], ent))

    seen_hashes = set()
    result = {}

    with _LOCK:
        if not use_cache:
            _FILE_CACHE.clear()

        for t_hash, ent in fr_entries:
            seen_hashes.add(t_hash)
            try:
                st = ent.stat()
                sig = (st.st_mtime_ns, st.st_size)
            except OSError:
                continue

            cached = _FILE_CACHE.get(t_hash)
            if cached and cached['sig'] == sig:
                result[t_hash] = cached['data']
                continue

            try:
                item = parse_single_torrent(ent.path, tor_map=tor_map)
                _FILE_CACHE[t_hash] = {'sig': sig, 'data': item}
                result[t_hash] = item
            except Exception:
                # Файл в процессе перезаписи клиентом: сохраняем прошлое валидное значение или пропускаем тик
                if cached:
                    result[t_hash] = cached['data']

        # Авточистка «призраков» (файлы, исчезнувшие из BT_backup)
        ghosts = [h for h in _FILE_CACHE if h not in seen_hashes]
        for g in ghosts:
            del _FILE_CACHE[g]

    return result


def get_global_torrent_summaries():
    return list(get_all_torrents(use_cache=True).values())


def copy_torrent_to_watch(t_hash):
    if not os.path.exists(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        return False, "Папка BT_backup не найдена"
    tor_path = os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, t_hash.lower() + '.torrent')
    if not os.path.exists(tor_path):
        for candidate in os.listdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
            if candidate.lower() == (t_hash.lower() + '.torrent'):
                tor_path = os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, candidate)
                break
    if not os.path.exists(tor_path):
        return False, f"Файл {t_hash}.torrent не найден"

    watch_dir = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), 'Downloads')
    os.makedirs(watch_dir, exist_ok=True)
    try:
        dest = os.path.join(watch_dir, os.path.basename(tor_path))
        shutil.copy2(tor_path, dest)
        return True, f"Скопировано в {dest}"
    except Exception as e:
        return False, str(e)

# core/torrent_targets.py
import time
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.notifiers import notify_all


def list_targets():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM torrent_targets").fetchall()
                return {r['hash_id'].lower(): dict(r) for r in rows}
        except Exception:
            return {}


def set_target(t_hash, target_ratio=0.0, target_bytes=0, actor='admin'):
    hl = str(t_hash or '').strip().lower()
    tr = max(0.0, float(target_ratio or 0))
    tb = max(0, int(target_bytes or 0))
    if not hl or (tr <= 0 and tb <= 0):
        return False, "Укажите целевой Ratio или объём отдачи"

    now = time.time()
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute(
                    """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO torrent_targets
                       (hash_id, target_ratio, target_bytes, achieved_at, updated_at)
                       VALUES (?, ?, ?, NULL, ?)""",
                    (hl, tr, tb, now)
                )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("set_target", e)
            return False, str(e)

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Цель раздачи задана', f'{hl[:8]} ratio={tr} bytes={tb}', actor=actor)
    return True, "Цель сохранена"


def delete_target(t_hash):
    hl = str(t_hash or '').strip().lower()
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM torrent_targets WHERE hash_id = ?", (hl,))
                conn.commit()
                return True
        except Exception:
            return False


def handle_targets_on_merge(old_hash, new_hash, final_uploaded=0):
    """
    При мердже переносит цель на получателя (если у цели её не было).
    При сложении сразу проверяет достижение планки по новому объёму отдачи.
    """
    oh = str(old_hash or '').strip().lower()
    nh = str(new_hash or '').strip().lower()
    if not oh or not nh or oh == nh:
        return False

    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                src_row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM torrent_targets WHERE hash_id = ?", (oh,)).fetchone()
                dst_row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM torrent_targets WHERE hash_id = ?", (nh,)).fetchone()

                active_target = None
                if src_row and not dst_row:
                    conn.execute(
                        """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO torrent_targets
                           (hash_id, target_ratio, target_bytes, achieved_at, updated_at)
                           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                        (nh, src_row['target_ratio'], src_row['target_bytes'], src_row['achieved_at'], time.time())
                    )
                    active_target = src_row
                elif dst_row:
                    active_target = dst_row

                if active_target and final_uploaded > 0:
                    tgt_bytes = int(active_target['target_bytes'] or 0)
                    if tgt_bytes > 0 and final_uploaded >= tgt_bytes and not active_target['achieved_at']:
                        conn.execute("UPDATE torrent_targets SET achieved_at = ? WHERE hash_id = ?", (time.time(), nh))

                if src_row:
                    conn.execute("DELETE FROM torrent_targets WHERE hash_id = ?", (oh,))

                conn.commit()
                return bool(active_target)
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("handle_targets_on_merge", e)
            return False


def transfer_target_on_merge(old_hash, new_hash):
    return handle_targets_on_merge(old_hash, new_hash)


def compute_target_progress(target_row, uploaded, ratio):
    if not target_row:
        return None
    tr = float(target_row.get('target_ratio') or 0)
    tb = int(target_row.get('target_bytes') or 0)
    pcts = []
    if tr > 0:
        pcts.append(min(100.0, (float(ratio or 0) / tr) * 100.0))
    if tb > 0:
        pcts.append(min(100.0, (int(uploaded or 0) / tb) * 100.0))
    if not pcts:
        return None
    pct = round(max(pcts), 1)
    return {
        'target_ratio': tr,
        'target_bytes': tb,
        'target_bytes_fmt': format_file_size(tb) if tb > 0 else '',
        'percent': pct,
        'achieved': pct >= 100.0 or bool(target_row.get('achieved_at'))
    }


def evaluate_targets(live):
    targets = list_targets()
    if not targets or not live:
        return 0

    now = time.time()
    live_map = {h.lower(): info for h, info in live.items()}
    newly_achieved = 0

    for hl, row in targets.items():
        if row.get('achieved_at'):
            continue
        info = live_map.get(hl)
        if not info:
            continue
        up = int(info.get('uploaded', 0) or 0)
        ratio = float(info.get('ratio', 0) or 0)
        tr = float(row.get('target_ratio') or 0)
        tb = int(row.get('target_bytes') or 0)
        hit = (tr > 0 and ratio >= tr) or (tb > 0 and up >= tb)
        if not hit:
            continue

        with app_state_db._lock:
            try:
                with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("UPDATE torrent_targets SET achieved_at = ? WHERE hash_id = ?", (now, hl))
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception:
                continue

        newly_achieved += 1
        nm = str(info.get('name') or hl)
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', 'Цель раздачи достигнута', nm)
        notify_all("🎯 Цель раздачи достигнута!", [f"Раздача: {nm}", f"Отдано: {format_file_size(up)} | Ratio: {ratio:.2f}x"])

    return newly_achieved

FILE: core/tracker_inspector.py
================================================================================
# core/tracker_inspector.py
import time
import threading
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from core.qb_api import get_torrent_trackers

_CACHE_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_TRACKER_ERR_CACHE = {'ts': 0.0, 'map': {}}
_CACHE_TTL = 45.0


def _classify_error(msg):
    low = str(msg or '').lower()
    if any(k in low for k in ('not registered', 'unregistered', 'not found', 'не зарегистрирован', 'не найдена', 'torrent not registered')):
        return 'unregistered', 'Не зарегистрирована'
    if any(k in low for k in ('passkey', 'unauthorized', 'auth', 'forbidden', '401', '403', 'invalid key', 'ключ', 'авториз')):
        return 'auth', 'Ошибка авторизации / passkey'
    if any(k in low for k in ('timed out', 'timeout', 'unreachable', 'connection', 'refused', 'reset', 'таймаут', 'host not found')):
        return 'timeout', 'Превышен таймаут / сеть'
    return 'other', 'Прочая ошибка трекера'


def inspect_tracker_errors(ip, port, live, force=False):
    if not port or not live:
        return {}
    now = time.time()
    with _CACHE_LOCK:
        if not force and (now - _TRACKER_ERR_CACHE['ts']) < _CACHE_TTL:
            return dict(_TRACKER_ERR_CACHE['map'])

    err_map = {}

    def check_hash(t_hash, info):
        state = str(info.get('state', '')).lower()
        if state in ('pausedup', 'pauseddl', 'stoppedup', 'stoppeddl'):
            return None
        trackers = get_torrent_trackers(ip, port, t_hash)
        real = [tr for tr in trackers if not str(tr.get('url', '')).startswith('**[') and tr.get('status', 0) != 0]
        if not real:
            return None

        # Берём первую критическую ошибку (status == 4), не маскируя её успехом доп. трекеров
        for tr in real:
            if tr.get('status') == 4:
                raw_msg = str(tr.get('msg') or '').strip() or 'Трекер не отвечает'
                cat, cat_lbl = _classify_error(raw_msg)
                return {
                    'category': cat,
                    'category_label': cat_lbl,
                    'message': raw_msg
                }
        return None

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
        futs = {pool.submit(check_hash, h, inf): h.lower() for h, inf in live.items()}
        for fut in as_completed(futs):
            hl = futs[fut]
            try:
                res = fut.result()
                if res:
                    err_map[hl] = res
            except Exception:
                pass

    with _CACHE_LOCK:
        _TRACKER_ERR_CACHE['ts'] = now
        _TRACKER_ERR_CACHE['map'] = dict(err_map)
    return err_map

FILE: core/tracker_replacer.py

# core/tracker_replacer.py
import json
import time
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, get_torrent_trackers, edit_torrent_tracker


def _ensure_undo_table():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS undo_stack (
                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                        op_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                        title TEXT NOT NULL,
                        payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                        created_at REAL NOT NULL,
                        undone INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
                    )
                """)
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("undo_stack init", e)


_ensure_undo_table()


def push_undo_record(op_type, title, payload):
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO undo_stack (op_type, title, payload_json, created_at, undone) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 0)",
                    (str(op_type), str(title)[:160], json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False), time.time())
                )
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM undo_stack WHERE id NOT IN (SELECT id FROM undo_stack ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 10)")
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("push_undo_record", e)


def preview_tracker_replace(find_str, replace_str):
    find_s = str(find_str or '').strip()
    rep_s = str(replace_str or '').strip()
    if not find_s or not rep_s or find_s == rep_s:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'Укажите разные непустые строки поиска и замены', 'matches': []}

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    if not live:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен', 'matches': []}

    matches = []
    for h, info in live.items():
        for tr in get_torrent_trackers(ip, port, h):
            url = str(tr.get('url', '') or '')
            if not url or url.startswith('**[') or find_s not in url:
                continue
            new_url = url.replace(find_s, rep_s)
            matches.append({
                'hash': h,
                'name': info.get('name', h),
                'old_url': url,
                'new_url': new_url
            })
    return {'success': True, 'matches': matches, 'count': len(matches)}


def apply_tracker_replace(find_str, replace_str, actor='admin'):
    prev = preview_tracker_replace(find_str, replace_str)
    if not prev.get('success'):
        return prev
    matches = prev.get('matches') or []
    if not matches:
        return {'success': False, 'error': 'Совпадений в трекерах не найдено'}

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    updated = 0
    reverse_ops = []

    for m in matches:
        if edit_torrent_tracker(ip, port, m['hash'], m['old_url'], m['new_url']):
            updated += 1
            reverse_ops.append({'hash': m['hash'], 'orig_url': m['new_url'], 'new_url': m['old_url']})
        time.sleep(0.15)

    if reverse_ops:
        push_undo_record('tracker_replace', f"Замена трекеров ({updated} шт.)", reverse_ops)
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Смена трекеров/пасскеев', f'Обновлено URL: {updated}', actor=actor)

    return {'success': True, 'updated': updated, 'total': len(matches)}

# core/ui_defaults.py
import json
from copy import deepcopy

DEFAULT_UI_SETTINGS = {
    "version": 2,
    "theme": "dark",
    "custom_colors": {"bg_main": "#121214", "bg_panel": "#1a1a20", "text": "#ffffff", "accent": "#2ecc71"},

    "allow_google_fonts": False,
    "mobile_mode": True,
    "charts": {
        "daily": {"color": "#2ecc71", "color2": "#10b981", "gradient": True, "opacity": 0.75},
        "top5": {"color": "#ff9f1c", "color2": "#ff4d6d", "gradient": True, "opacity": 0.8},
        "hourly": {"color": "#00f5d4", "color2": "#9b5de5", "gradient": True, "opacity": 0.35},
        "hourly_hours": 24,
        "hourly_y_min": None,
        "top_n": 5,
        "top_period": "1d",
        "top_scale": "lin"
    },
    "peer_client_colors": {
        "qBittorrent": "#2ecc71",
        "Transmission": "#e74c3c",
        "uTorrent": "#27ae60",
        "Deluge": "#3498db",
        "BiglyBT": "#9b59b6",
        "Aria2": "#1abc9c",
        "Tixati": "#f39c12",
        "Vuze": "#00bcd4",
        "libtorrent": "#95a5a6",
        "Прочие": "#64748b"
    },
    "summary_columns": {
        "visible": ["name", "size", "uploaded", "ratio", "health_score", "speed_all_time", "speed_1h", "speed_24h", "vol_hour", "vol_day", "ratio_h_all", "created"],
        "order": ["name", "size", "uploaded", "ratio", "health_score", "speed_all_time", "speed_1h", "speed_24h", "vol_hour", "vol_day", "ratio_h_all", "created"],
        "widths": {}
    }
}


def merge_ui_defaults(raw_json):
    result = deepcopy(DEFAULT_UI_SETTINGS)
    if not raw_json:
        return result
    try:
        data = json.loads(raw_json) if isinstance(raw_json, str) else dict(raw_json)
    except Exception:
        return result

    def deep_merge(dst, src):
        for k, v in src.items():
            if k in dst and isinstance(dst[k], dict) and isinstance(v, dict):
                deep_merge(dst[k], v)
            else:
                dst[k] = v

    if isinstance(data, dict):
        deep_merge(result, data)
    result["version"] = DEFAULT_UI_SETTINGS["version"]
    return result

# core/undo_presets.py
import json
import time
from utils.config import cfg
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.automation import automation_engine
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, edit_torrent_tracker,
    add_torrent_tags, remove_torrent_tags, send_qb_command
)


def list_undo_stack():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute(
                    "SELECT id, op_type, title, created_at, undone FROM undo_stack ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 10"
                ).fetchall()
                return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        except Exception:
            return []


def execute_undo(undo_id):
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM undo_stack WHERE id = ?", (int(undo_id),)).fetchone()
                if not row:
                    return False, "Запись отката не найдена"
                if row['undone']:
                    return False, "Эта операция уже была отменена"
                rec = dict(row)
        except Exception as e:
            return False, str(e)

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return False, "qBittorrent недоступен для отката"

    payload = json.loads(rec['payload_json'] or '[]')
    op = rec['op_type']
    reverted = 0

    if op == 'tracker_replace':
        for it in payload:
            if edit_torrent_tracker(ip, port, it['hash'], it['orig_url'], it['new_url']):
                reverted += 1
            time.sleep(0.1)
    elif op == 'tags_bulk':
        act_fn = remove_torrent_tags if payload.get('action') == 'addTags' else add_torrent_tags
        if act_fn(ip, port, payload.get('hashes', ''), payload.get('tag', '')):
            reverted = 1
    elif op == 'pause_start_bulk':
        rev_act = 'start' if payload.get('action') == 'stop' else 'stop'
        if send_qb_command(ip, port, rev_act, payload.get('hashes', '')):
            reverted = 1

    with app_state_db._lock:
        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
            conn.execute("UPDATE undo_stack SET undone = 1 WHERE id = ?", (int(undo_id),))
            conn.commit()

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Откат операции (Undo)', f"{rec['title']} (reverted={reverted})", actor='admin')
    return True, f"Откат выполнен: {rec['title']}"


def export_presets_json():
    rules = automation_engine.get_rules()
    clean_rules = []
    for r in rules:
        d = dict(r)
        d.pop('id', None)
        clean_rules.append(d)

    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS saved_views (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, name TEXT NOT NULL, filters_json TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL)")
                views = [dict(v) for v in conn.execute("SELECT name, filters_json FROM saved_views ORDER BY id ASC").fetchall()]
        except Exception:
            views = []

    return {
        'format': 'tbr_pro_presets_v1',
        'exported_at': int(time.time()),
        'automation_rules': clean_rules,
        'saved_views': views
    }


def import_presets_json(data):
    if not isinstance(data, dict) or data.get('format') != 'tbr_pro_presets_v1':
        return False, "Неверный формат пресета (ожидался tbr_pro_presets_v1)"

    imported_rules = 0
    for r in (data.get('automation_rules') or []):
        if not isinstance(r, dict):
            continue
        rid = automation_engine.add_rule(
            name=str(r.get('name', 'Импорт'))[:64],
            min_ratio=float(r.get('min_ratio', 0) or 0),
            min_seed_days=float(r.get('min_seed_days', 0) or 0),
            action_pause=bool(r.get('action_pause', False)),
            action_tag=str(r.get('action_tag', '') or '')[:32],
            notify=bool(r.get('notify', True)),
            time_mode=str(r.get('time_mode', 'seeding_time')),
            min_uploaded_bytes=int(r.get('min_uploaded_bytes', 0) or 0),
            speed_op=str(r.get('speed_op', '') or ''),
            speed_bytes=float(r.get('speed_bytes', 0) or 0),
            speed_window_min=int(r.get('speed_window_min', 60) or 60),
            action_remove_tag=str(r.get('action_remove_tag', '') or '')[:32]
        )
        if rid:
            # Все импортируемые правила строго ВЫКЛЮЧЕНЫ (#29)
            automation_engine.toggle_rule(rid)
            imported_rules += 1

    imported_views = 0
    if data.get('saved_views'):
        with app_state_db._lock:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS saved_views (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, name TEXT NOT NULL, filters_json TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL)")
                for v in data['saved_views']:
                    if isinstance(v, dict) and v.get('name') and v.get('filters_json'):
                        conn.execute("INSERT INTO saved_views (name, filters_json, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (str(v['name'])[:40], str(v['filters_json']), time.time()))
                        imported_views += 1
                conn.commit()

    return True, f"Импортировано правил (выключены!): {imported_rules}, видов: {imported_views}. Рекомендуется прогнать Dry-Run."

FILE: core/updater.py

# core/updater.py
import os
import sys
import hmac
import json
import time
import hashlib
import tempfile
import threading
import subprocess
import urllib.request
from core.version import APP_VERSION, GITHUB_API_LATEST, GITHUB_RELEASES_URL, is_newer_version
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from utils.helpers import write_log, write_error_log

CACHE_TTL_SEC = 6 * 3600
_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_CACHE = {"ts": 0.0, "data": None}
_SHUTDOWN_HOOK = None


def register_shutdown_hook(fn):
    global _SHUTDOWN_HOOK
    _SHUTDOWN_HOOK = fn


def is_frozen_windows():
    return bool(getattr(sys, "frozen", False) and os.name == "nt")


def _http_get_bytes(url, timeout=15):
    req = urllib.request.Request(
        url,
        headers={
            "User-Agent": f"tbr-pro/{APP_VERSION}",
            "Accept": "application/vnd.github+json",
        },
    )
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
        return resp.read()


def check_for_updates(force=False):
    now = time.time()
    with _LOCK:
        if not force and _CACHE["data"] and (now - _CACHE["ts"]) < CACHE_TTL_SEC:
            return dict(_CACHE["data"])

    result = {
        "success": True,
        "current_version": APP_VERSION,
        "latest_version": APP_VERSION,
        "update_available": False,
        "can_auto_update": is_frozen_windows(),
        "release_name": "",
        "changelog": "",
        "html_url": GITHUB_RELEASES_URL,
        "exe_url": "",
        "sha256": "",
        "checked_at": int(now),
        "error": None,
    }

    try:
        raw = _http_get_bytes(GITHUB_API_LATEST, timeout=10)
        rel = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
        tag = str(rel.get("tag_name") or "").strip()
        latest_ver = tag.lstrip("vV") or APP_VERSION
        assets = rel.get("assets") or []

        exe_asset = None
        sha_asset = None
        for a in assets:
            nm = str(a.get("name") or "").lower()
            if nm.endswith(".exe") and not exe_asset:
                exe_asset = a
            elif nm.endswith(".sha256") and not sha_asset:
                sha_asset = a

        sha_val = ""
        if exe_asset:
            digest = str(exe_asset.get("digest") or "")
            if digest.lower().startswith("sha256:"):
                sha_val = digest.split(":", 1)[1].strip().lower()
        if not sha_val and sha_asset and sha_asset.get("browser_download_url"):
            try:
                sha_txt = _http_get_bytes(sha_asset["browser_download_url"], timeout=8).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
                token = sha_txt.strip().split()[0].lower()
                if len(token) == 64 and all(c in "0123456789abcdef" for c in token):
                    sha_val = token
            except Exception:
                pass

        result.update({
            "latest_version": latest_ver,
            "update_available": is_newer_version(latest_ver, APP_VERSION),
            "release_name": str(rel.get("name") or tag or latest_ver),
            "changelog": str(rel.get("body") or ""),
            "html_url": str(rel.get("html_url") or GITHUB_RELEASES_URL),
            "exe_url": str(exe_asset.get("browser_download_url") or "") if exe_asset else "",
            "sha256": sha_val,
        })
        with _LOCK:
            _CACHE["ts"] = now
            _CACHE["data"] = dict(result)
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Проверка обновлений GitHub", e)
        result["success"] = False
        result["error"] = str(e)

    return result


def _spawn_updater_bat(pid, current_exe, new_exe):
    bat_path = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "tbr_updater.bat")
    bak_exe = current_exe + ".bak"
    script = (
        "@echo off\r\n"
        "chcp 65001 >nul\r\n"
        f"set \"PID={int(pid)}\"\r\n"
        f"set \"CUR={current_exe}\"\r\n"
        f"set \"NEW={new_exe}\"\r\n"
        f"set \"BAK={bak_exe}\"\r\n"
        ":waitloop\r\n"
        "tasklist /FI \"PID eq %PID%\" 2>NUL | find /I \"%PID%\" >NUL\r\n"
        "if \"%ERRORLEVEL%\"==\"0\" (\r\n"
        "    timeout /t 1 /nobreak >NUL\r\n"
        "    goto waitloop\r\n"
        ")\r\n"
        "if exist \"%BAK%\" del /F /Q \"%BAK%\" >NUL 2>&1\r\n"
        "if exist \"%CUR%\" move /Y \"%CUR%\" \"%BAK%\" >NUL 2>&1\r\n"
        "move /Y \"%NEW%\" \"%CUR%\" >NUL 2>&1\r\n"
        "start \"\" \"%CUR%\"\r\n"
        "del /F /Q \"%~f0\" >NUL 2>&1\r\n"
    )
    with open(bat_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(script)

    flags = getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0) | getattr(subprocess, "DETACHED_PROCESS", 0)
    subprocess.Popen(["cmd.exe", "/c", bat_path], creationflags=flags, close_fds=True)


def download_and_apply_update(actor="admin"):
    if not is_frozen_windows():
        return False, "Автообновление доступно только в собранном tbr.exe под Windows."

    info = check_for_updates(force=True)
    if not info.get("success"):
        return False, f"Ошибка проверки релиза: {info.get('error')}"
    if not info.get("update_available"):
        return False, "Установлена актуальная версия."
    if not info.get("exe_url"):
        return False, "В релизе не найден бинарный файл .exe."
    if not info.get("sha256"):
        return False, "В релизе отсутствует SHA-256 контрольная сумма — обновление отменено."

    current_exe = os.path.abspath(sys.executable)
    exe_dir = os.path.dirname(current_exe)
    new_exe = os.path.join(exe_dir, "tbr_new.exe")

    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(info["exe_url"], headers={"User-Agent": f"tbr-pro/{APP_VERSION}"})
        hasher = hashlib.sha256()
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as resp, open(new_exe, "wb") as out:
            while True:
                chunk = resp.read(65536)
                if not chunk:
                    break
                out.write(chunk)
                hasher.update(chunk)

        actual_sha = hasher.hexdigest().lower()
        if not hmac.compare_digest(actual_sha, info["sha256"].lower()):
            try:
                os.remove(new_exe)
            except OSError:
                pass
            app_state_db.add_audit_event("system", "Ошибка обновления", f"SHA-256 mismatch for v{info['latest_version']}", actor=actor)
            return False, "Контрольная сумма SHA-256 не совпала! Файл удалён."

        _spawn_updater_bat(os.getpid(), current_exe, new_exe)
        write_log("app", f"Запущено автообновление до v{info['latest_version']}")
        app_state_db.add_audit_event("system", "Автообновление", f"Переход на v{info['latest_version']}", actor=actor)

        def delayed_exit():
            time.sleep(0.6)
            if _SHUTDOWN_HOOK:
                try:
                    _SHUTDOWN_HOOK()
                except Exception:
                    pass
            os._exit(0)

        threading.Thread(target=delayed_exit, daemon=True).start()
        return True, f"Обновление v{info['latest_version']} проверено и скачано. Перезапуск tbr PRO..."
    except Exception as e:
        if os.path.exists(new_exe):
            try:
                os.remove(new_exe)
            except OSError:
                pass
        write_error_log("Ошибка применения обновления", e)
        return False, f"Ошибка скачивания: {e}"

FILE: core/version.py

# core/version.py
import re

APP_VERSION = "4.0.0"
GITHUB_OWNER = "kamushek0o"
GITHUB_REPO = "tbr"
GITHUB_RELEASES_URL = f"https://github.com/{GITHUB_OWNER}/{GITHUB_REPO}/releases"
GITHUB_API_LATEST = f"https://api.github.com/repos/{GITHUB_OWNER}/{GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest"

_SEMVER_RE = re.compile(r'(\d+)\.(\d+)\.(\d+)')


def parse_semver(ver_str):
    """Строгое числовое разложение версии в кортеж (major, minor, patch)."""
    m = _SEMVER_RE.search(str(ver_str or "").strip().lstrip("vV"))
    if not m:
        return (0, 0, 0)
    return (int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2)), int(m.group(3)))


def is_newer_version(remote_ver, local_ver=APP_VERSION):
    """Возвращает True, если remote_ver строго новее local_ver."""
    return parse_semver(remote_ver) > parse_semver(local_ver)

# core/webhooks_bundler.py
import json
import time
import threading
import urllib.request
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db

_BUNDLE_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_BUNDLE_BUFFER = []
_BUNDLE_TIMER = None


def _ensure_webhook_table():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS webhook_deliveries (
                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                        event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                        url TEXT NOT NULL,
                        status_code INTEGER NOT NULL,
                        ok INTEGER NOT NULL,
                        error TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                        created_at REAL NOT NULL
                    )
                """)
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("webhook_deliveries init", e)


_ensure_webhook_table()


def emit_webhook(event_type, payload):
    """#41 Исходящие JSON POST вебхуки в отдельном потоке с жёстким таймаутом 4с."""
    raw_urls = cfg.get('Outbound_Webhooks', '').strip()
    if not raw_urls:
        return
    urls = [u.strip() for u in raw_urls.splitlines() if u.strip().startswith(('http://', 'https://'))]
    if not urls:
        return

    body_bytes = json.dumps({
        'event': str(event_type),
        'timestamp': int(time.time()),
        'data': payload or {}
    }, ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf-8')

    def send_one(url):
        code, ok, err = 0, False, ''
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                url, data=body_bytes, method='POST',
                headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'User-Agent': 'tbr-pro-webhook/3.2'}
            )
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=4.0) as resp:
                code = resp.getcode()
                ok = (200 <= code < 300)
        except Exception as e:
            err = str(e)[:200]
        with app_state_db._lock:
            try:
                with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute(
                        "INSERT INTO webhook_deliveries (event_type, url, status_code, ok, error, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                        (str(event_type), url[:250], code, 1 if ok else 0, err, time.time())
                    )
                    conn.execute("DELETE FROM webhook_deliveries WHERE id NOT IN (SELECT id FROM webhook_deliveries ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 30)")
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception:
                pass

    for u in urls[:5]:
        threading.Thread(target=send_one, args=(u,), daemon=True).start()


def get_webhook_deliveries(limit=20):
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM webhook_deliveries ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?", (int(limit),)).fetchall()
                return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        except Exception:
            return []


def queue_bundled_alert(title, lines, send_fn):
    """#49 Cascade Alert Bundler: группирует некритические алерты в окно 45 секунд."""
    global _BUNDLE_TIMER
    with _BUNDLE_LOCK:
        _BUNDLE_BUFFER.append((title, list(lines or [])))
        if _BUNDLE_TIMER is not None and _BUNDLE_TIMER.is_alive():
            return

        def flush():
            global _BUNDLE_TIMER
            with _BUNDLE_LOCK:
                batch = list(_BUNDLE_BUFFER)
                _BUNDLE_BUFFER.clear()
                _BUNDLE_TIMER = None
            if not batch:
                return
            if len(batch) == 1:
                send_fn(batch[0][0], batch[0][1])
                return
            merged_lines = []
            for t, lns in batch[:20]:
                preview = lns[0] if lns else ''
                merged_lines.append(f"• {t}" + (f": {preview}" if preview else ""))
            if len(batch) > 20:
                merged_lines.append(f"...и ещё событий: {len(batch) - 20}")
            send_fn(f"📦 Сводка событий ({len(batch)} шт. за 45с)", merged_lines)

        _BUNDLE_TIMER = threading.Timer(45.0, flush)
        _BUNDLE_TIMER.daemon = True
        _BUNDLE_TIMER.start()

# core/widget_service.py
import io
import time
import datetime
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, is_qb_running
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents
from core.scheduler import MIN_DELTA_SEC
from core.disk_monitor import get_monitored_disk_info
from core.private_vault import get_hidden_hashes, get_vault_settings

_ICON_CACHE = {}
PERIOD_DAYS = {'1d': 1, '7d': 7, '30d': 30}


def _smooth_anomalies(points):
    if len(points) >= 3:
        for i in range(1, len(points) - 1):
            p_prev, p_cur, p_next = points[i - 1]['speed_mbs'], points[i]['speed_mbs'], points[i + 1]['speed_mbs']
            avg_n = (p_prev + p_next) / 2.0
            if p_cur > 15.0 and p_cur > (avg_n * 5.0):
                points[i]['speed_mbs'] = round(avg_n, 3)
                points[i]['speed_bps'] = round(avg_n * 1024 * 1024, 2)
                points[i]['delta_bytes'] = int(points[i]['speed_bps'] * points[i]['dt_sec'])
                points[i]['delta_fmt'] = format_file_size(points[i]['delta_bytes'])
    return points


def build_detailed_speed_series(db, hours=24):
    since_ts = time.time() - (max(1, int(hours)) * 3600)
    rows = db.get_snapshots_since(since_ts)
    points, prev = [], None
    for r in rows:
        if prev is not None:
            t1, t2 = float(prev['timestamp']), float(r['timestamp'])
            dt_sec = t2 - t1
            if dt_sec >= MIN_DELTA_SEC:
                delta = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev['total_uploaded']))
                bps = delta / dt_sec
                if int(r.get('count', 0) or 0) > int(prev.get('count', 0) or 0) and bps > (200 * 1024 * 1024):
                    bps = points[-1]['speed_bps'] if points else 0.0
                dt_obj = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(t2)
                points.append({
                    'ts': int(t2),
                    'label': dt_obj.strftime('%H:%M') if hours <= 24 else dt_obj.strftime('%d.%m %H:%M'),
                    'speed_bps': round(bps, 2),
                    'speed_mbs': round(bps / (1024 * 1024), 3),
                    'delta_bytes': delta,
                    'delta_fmt': format_file_size(delta),
                    'dt_sec': int(dt_sec),
                    'interval_min': int(round(dt_sec / 60.0))
                })
        prev = r
    return _smooth_anomalies(points)


def build_hourly_aggregated_speed_series(db, hours=72):
    since_ts = time.time() - (max(24, int(hours)) * 3600)
    rows = db.get_snapshots_since(since_ts)
    buckets, prev = {}, None
    for r in rows:
        if prev is not None:
            t1, t2 = float(prev['timestamp']), float(r['timestamp'])
            dt_sec = t2 - t1
            delta = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev['total_uploaded']))
            if dt_sec >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and delta > 0:
                if int(r.get('count', 0) or 0) > int(prev.get('count', 0) or 0) and (delta / dt_sec) > (200 * 1024 * 1024):
                    delta = 0
                cur_ts = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(t1).replace(minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
                while cur_ts < t2:
                    b_end = cur_ts + 3600.0
                    overlap = min(t2, b_end) - max(t1, cur_ts)
                    if overlap > 0:
                        b = buckets.setdefault(int(cur_ts), {'bytes': 0.0, 'sec': 0.0})
                        b['bytes'] += delta * (overlap / dt_sec)
                        b['sec'] += overlap
                    cur_ts = b_end
        prev = r

    points = []
    for h_ts in sorted(buckets.keys()):
        b = buckets[h_ts]
        sec = max(1.0, b['sec'])
        bps = b['bytes'] / sec
        delta_i = int(b['bytes'])
        points.append({
            'ts': h_ts,
            'label': datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(h_ts).strftime('%d.%m %H:00'),
            'speed_bps': round(bps, 2),
            'speed_mbs': round(bps / (1024 * 1024), 3),
            'delta_bytes': delta_i,
            'delta_fmt': format_file_size(delta_i),
            'dt_sec': int(sec),
            'interval_min': int(round(sec / 60.0))
        })
    return _smooth_anomalies(points)


def compute_top_n_torrents(db, summaries, live=None, top_n=5, period='1d', with_others=False):
    n = max(1, min(25, int(top_n or 5)))
    per = period if period in ('1d', '7d', '30d', 'all') else '1d'

    active_map = {}
    if live:
        by_hash = {(s.get('hash_id') or '').lower(): s for s in summaries}
        for h_raw, info in live.items():
            h = h_raw.lower()
            st_name = (by_hash.get(h) or {}).get('name', '')
            nm = str(info.get('name') or st_name or h)
            active_map[h] = {'name': nm, 'uploaded': int(info.get('uploaded', 0) or 0)}
    else:
        for s in summaries:
            h = (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower()
            if h:
                active_map[h] = {'name': str(s.get('name') or h), 'uploaded': int(s.get('uploaded', 0) or 0)}

    if not active_map:
        return ([], {'gb': 0.0, 'count': 0}) if with_others else []

    if per == 'all':
        scored = [(h, v['name'], v['uploaded']) for h, v in active_map.items() if v['uploaded'] > 0]
    else:
        since_ts = time.time() - (PERIOD_DAYS[per] * 86400)
        deltas = db.get_torrents_positive_deltas(since_ts, allowed_hashes=active_map.keys())
        scored = [(h, active_map[h]['name'], deltas.get(h, 0)) for h in active_map if deltas.get(h, 0) > 0]

    scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)
    prec = 3 if per != 'all' else 2
    top_items = [
        {'hash': h, 'full_name': nm, 'short_name': (nm[:26] + '…') if len(nm) > 27 else nm, 'gb': round(val / (1024 ** 3), prec)}
        for h, nm, val in scored[:n]
    ]
    if not with_others:
        return top_items

    rest = scored[n:]
    rest_bytes = max(0, sum(x[2] for x in rest))
    others = {'gb': round(rest_bytes / (1024 ** 3), prec), 'count': len(rest)}
    return top_items, others


def get_widget_summary(db, ui_settings=None, top_period=None, top_scale=None, role='admin'):
    now_ts = time.time()
    torrents = list(get_all_torrents(use_cache=True).values())
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    qb_up = is_qb_running()
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if (qb_up and port) else {}

    hidden = get_hidden_hashes(live) if role != 'admin' else set()
    vault_mode = get_vault_settings().get('aggregate_mode', 'exclude') if hidden else 'anonymize'

    vis_live = {h: v for h, v in live.items() if h.lower() not in hidden} if hidden else live
    vis_torrents = [t for t in torrents if (t.get('hash_id') or '').lower() not in hidden] if hidden else torrents

    agg_live = vis_live if (hidden and vault_mode == 'exclude') else live
    agg_torrents = vis_torrents if (hidden and vault_mode == 'exclude') else torrents

    if agg_live:
        total_uploaded = sum(int(t.get('uploaded', 0) or 0) for t in agg_live.values())
        cur_bps = sum(int(t.get('upspeed', 0) or 0) for t in agg_live.values())
        active_cnt = sum(1 for t in agg_live.values() if str(t.get('state', '')).lower() not in ('pausedup', 'pauseddl', 'stoppedup', 'stoppeddl', 'error', 'missingfiles'))
        total_cnt = len(agg_live)
    else:
        total_uploaded = sum(int(t.get('uploaded', 0) or 0) for t in agg_torrents)
        active_cnt, total_cnt = len(agg_torrents), len(agg_torrents)
        s6 = build_detailed_speed_series(db, hours=6)
        cur_bps = s6[-1]['speed_bps'] if s6 else 0.0

    rows_24h = db.get_snapshots_since(now_ts - 86400)
    up_24h = max(0, total_uploaded - int(rows_24h[0]['total_uploaded'])) if rows_24h else 0
    midnight_ts = datetime.datetime.now().replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
    rows_today = db.get_snapshots_since(midnight_ts)
    up_today = max(0, total_uploaded - int(rows_today[0]['total_uploaded'])) if rows_today else 0

    ch_cfg = (ui_settings or {}).get('charts') or {}
    per = top_period or ch_cfg.get('top_period', '1d')
    scale = top_scale or ch_cfg.get('top_scale', 'lin')
    if per not in ('1d', '7d', '30d', 'all'):
        per = '1d'
    if scale not in ('lin', 'log10'):
        scale = 'lin'

    speed_pts = build_detailed_speed_series(db, hours=24)
    if len(speed_pts) > 48:
        speed_pts = speed_pts[::max(1, len(speed_pts) // 48)]

    rows_7d = db.get_snapshots_since(now_ts - 7 * 86400)
    daily_map, prev_r = {}, None
    for r in rows_7d:
        if prev_r is not None:
            dt = float(r['timestamp']) - float(prev_r['timestamp'])
            delta = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev_r['total_uploaded']))
            if dt >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and delta > 0:
                dk = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(r['timestamp']).strftime('%d.%m')
                daily_map[dk] = daily_map.get(dk, 0.0) + delta / (1024 ** 3)
        prev_r = r

    # В Топ-5 никогда не раскрываем названия скрытых раздач Private Vault
    top_items = compute_top_n_torrents(db, vis_torrents, live=vis_live, top_n=5, period=per)
    disk = get_monitored_disk_info(live)

    return {
        'qb_running': qb_up, 'total_torrents': total_cnt, 'active_torrents': active_cnt,
        'current_speed_bps': int(cur_bps), 'current_speed_fmt': f"{format_file_size(cur_bps)}/s",
        'uploaded_24h_bytes': up_24h, 'uploaded_24h_fmt': format_file_size(up_24h),
        'uploaded_today_bytes': up_today, 'uploaded_today_fmt': format_file_size(up_today),
        'total_uploaded_bytes': total_uploaded, 'total_uploaded_fmt': format_file_size(total_uploaded),
        'disk_free_bytes': disk.get('free_bytes', 0), 'disk_free_fmt': disk.get('free_fmt', '0 B'),
        'timestamp': int(now_ts),
        'charts': {
            'top_period': per, 'top_scale': scale,
            'speed_24h': [{'label': p['label'], 'speed_mbs': p['speed_mbs']} for p in speed_pts],
            'daily_7d': [{'label': k, 'gb': round(v, 2)} for k, v in list(daily_map.items())[-7:]],
            'top_5': [{'name': x['full_name'][:40], 'gb': x['gb']} for x in top_items]
        }
    }


def render_widget_chart_png(db, ui_settings, hours=24, width=560, height=200, chart_type='speed'):
    w, h = max(240, min(1200, int(width))), max(100, min(600, int(height)))
    img = Image.new('RGB', (w, h), (26, 26, 32))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    series = build_detailed_speed_series(db, hours=hours)
    if len(series) >= 2:
        t_min, t_span = series[0]['ts'], max(1, series[-1]['ts'] - series[0]['ts'])
        v_max = max(max(p['speed_mbs'] for p in series), 0.1)
        pts = [(14 + int(((p['ts'] - t_min) / t_span) * (w - 28)), h - 18 - int((p['speed_mbs'] / v_max) * (h - 36))) for p in series]
        draw.line(pts, fill=(52, 152, 219), width=2)
    else:
        draw.text((20, h // 2 - 6), "No data", fill=(149, 165, 166), font=ImageFont.load_default())
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    img.save(buf, format='PNG', optimize=True)
    return buf.getvalue()


def get_pwa_icon_png(size=192):
    sz = int(size)
    if sz in _ICON_CACHE:
        return _ICON_CACHE[sz]
    img = Image.new('RGBA', (sz, sz), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    draw.rounded_rectangle([0, 0, sz - 1, sz - 1], radius=max(8, sz // 5), fill=(26, 26, 32, 255))
    s = sz / 48.0
    draw.polygon([(26 * s, 6 * s), (12 * s, 26 * s), (24 * s, 26 * s), (20 * s, 42 * s), (36 * s, 22 * s), (24 * s, 22 * s)], fill=(46, 204, 113, 255))
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    img.save(buf, format='PNG')
    _ICON_CACHE[sz] = buf.getvalue()
    return _ICON_CACHE[sz]

# core/wrapped_service.py
import io
import csv
import json
import time
import datetime
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, sanitize_csv_cell
from core.scheduler import MIN_DELTA_SEC
from core.health_score import compute_health_metrics


def build_wrapped_summary(db, summaries, live, period='month', hidden_hashes=None):
    """#40 tbr Wrapped: итоги месяца/года со строгим исключением Private Vault."""
    hidden = {h.lower() for h in (hidden_hashes or set())}
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in (live or {}).items()}
    days = 365 if period == 'year' else 30
    since_ts = time.time() - (days * 86400)

    visible = [s for s in summaries if (s.get('hash_id') or '').lower() not in hidden]
    deltas = db.get_torrents_positive_deltas(since_ts, allowed_hashes=[(s.get('hash_id') or '').lower() for s in visible])
    period_up = sum(deltas.values())

    last_seed_saved = 0
    tag_vols = {}
    for s in visible:
        hl = (s.get('hash_id') or '').lower()
        lv = live_lower.get(hl, {})
        if lv and compute_health_metrics(lv).get('is_last_seed'):
            last_seed_saved += 1
        vol = deltas.get(hl, 0)
        for tg in str(lv.get('tags') or '').split(','):
            t_clean = tg.strip()
            if t_clean and vol > 0:
                tag_vols[t_clean] = tag_vols.get(t_clean, 0) + vol

    rows = db.get_snapshots_since(since_ts)
    day_totals, prev = {}, None
    for r in rows:
        if prev is not None:
            dt = float(r['timestamp']) - float(prev['timestamp'])
            d = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev['total_uploaded']))
            if dt >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and d > 0:
                dk = str(r.get('datetime') or '')[:10]
                if dk:
                    day_totals[dk] = day_totals.get(dk, 0) + d
        prev = r

    best_day = max(day_totals.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], default=('—', 0))
    top_tags = sorted(tag_vols.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:3]

    return {
        'period': period,
        'period_label': 'За год (365 дн.)' if period == 'year' else 'За месяц (30 дн.)',
        'total_uploaded_fmt': format_file_size(period_up),
        'visible_torrents': len(visible),
        'last_seed_saved': last_seed_saved,
        'best_day_date': best_day[0],
        'best_day_fmt': format_file_size(best_day[1]),
        'top_tags': [{'tag': k, 'fmt': format_file_size(v)} for k, v in top_tags]
    }


def export_full_inventory(summaries, live, lineage_map, notes_map, fmt='json', hidden_hashes=None):
    """#53 Полный экспорт инвентаря в JSON / CSV / Markdown с изоляцией Private Vault."""
    hidden = {h.lower() for h in (hidden_hashes or set())}
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in (live or {}).items()}
    rows = []

    for s in summaries:
        hl = (s.get('hash_id') or '').lower()
        if not hl or hl in hidden:
            continue
        lv = live_lower.get(hl, {})
        uid = lineage_map.get(hl, hl)
        up = int(lv.get('uploaded', s.get('uploaded', 0)) or 0)
        sz = int(lv.get('total_size', s.get('size', 0)) or 0)
        rows.append({
            'uid': uid,
            'hash': hl,
            'name': str(lv.get('name') or s.get('name') or hl),
            'size_bytes': sz,
            'size_fmt': format_file_size(sz),
            'uploaded_bytes': up,
            'uploaded_fmt': format_file_size(up),
            'ratio': round(up / sz, 2) if sz > 0 else 0.0,
            'tags': str(lv.get('tags') or ''),
            'tracker': str(lv.get('tracker') or (s['trackers'][0] if s.get('trackers') else '')),
            'note': notes_map.get(uid, '')
        })

    if fmt == 'json':
        return json.dumps({'exported_at': datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(), 'count': len(rows), 'items': rows}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode('utf-8'), 'application/json', 'tbr_inventory.json'

    if fmt == 'md':
        lines = [f"# tbr PRO Inventory ({len(rows)} torrents)", "", "| Name | UID | Size | Uploaded | Ratio | Tags | Note |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
        for r in rows:
            lines.append(f"| {r['name']} | `{r['uid'][:8]}` | {r['size_fmt']} | {r['uploaded_fmt']} | {r['ratio']}x | {r['tags']} | {r['note']} |")
        return "\n".join(lines).encode('utf-8'), 'text/markdown', 'tbr_inventory.md'

    buf = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(buf, delimiter=';')
    w.writerow(['UID', 'Hash', 'Name', 'Size', 'Uploaded', 'Ratio', 'Tags', 'Tracker', 'Note'])
    for r in rows:
        w.writerow([
            sanitize_csv_cell(r['uid']), sanitize_csv_cell(r['hash']), sanitize_csv_cell(r['name']),
            r['size_bytes'], r['uploaded_bytes'], r['ratio'],
            sanitize_csv_cell(r['tags']), sanitize_csv_cell(r['tracker']), sanitize_csv_cell(r['note'])
        ])
    return buf.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), 'text/csv', 'tbr_inventory.csv'

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
tbr PRO / tbr-widget — Project Context Collector
Скрипт собирает все текстовые файлы проекта с учётом .gitignore в единый файл для промпта.
"""

import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Бинарники и служебный мусор, который точно не надо слать в LLM
BINARY_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".exe", ".apk", ".aab", ".zip", ".tar", ".gz", ".7z",
    ".db", ".sqlite", ".sqlite3", ".wal", ".shm",
    ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".ico", ".webp", ".gif",
    ".keystore", ".jks", ".pyc", ".pyo", ".pyd",
    ".dll", ".so", ".dylib", ".bin", ".dat"
}

OUTPUT_FILE = "project_dump.txt"


def get_git_files() -> list[str]:
    """Получает список файлов проекта, исключая .gitignore."""
    try:
        cmd = ["git", "ls-files", "--cached", "--others", "--exclude-standard"]
        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
        return [line.strip() for line in result.stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[!] Ошибка вызова git: {e}")
        print("[*] Убедись, что Git установлен и ты запускаешь скрипт в корне репозитория.")
        return []


def is_text_file(filepath: Path) -> bool:
    """Проверяет расширение и пытается прочитать первый чанк файла."""
    if filepath.suffix.lower() in BINARY_EXTENSIONS:
        return False
    
    # Игнорируем сам файл дампа и сам этот скрипт
    if filepath.name in (OUTPUT_FILE, "dump_project.py"):
        return False

    try:
        with open(filepath, "tr", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.read(1024)
            return True
    except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
        return False


def main():
    root_dir = Path.cwd()
    print(f"[*] Сканирую репозиторий в: {root_dir}")

    files = get_git_files()
    if not files:
        print("[-] Список файлов пуст или проект не инициализирован как git-репо.")
        sys.exit(1)

    collected_count = 0
    skipped_count = 0

    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
        for rel_path in files:
            full_path = root_dir / rel_path
            
            if not full_path.is_file():
                continue

            if not is_text_file(full_path):
                skipped_count += 1
                continue

            try:
                content = full_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
                out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
                out.write(f"FILE: {rel_path.replace(os.sep, '/')}\n")
                out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
                out.write(content)
                if not content.endswith("\n"):
                    out.write("\n")
                out.write("\n")
                collected_count += 1
            except Exception as ex:
                print(f"[!] Пропущен {rel_path}: {ex}")
                skipped_count += 1

    size_mb = os.path.getsize(OUTPUT_FILE) / (1024 * 1024)
    print("\n" + "=" * 40)
    print(f"[+] Готово! Собрано файлов: {collected_count}")
    print(f"[-] Пропущено (бинарники/кэш): {skipped_count}")
    print(f"[>] Результат сохранён в: {OUTPUT_FILE} ({size_mb:.2f} MB)")
    print("=" * 40)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# gui/main_window.py
import os
import webbrowser
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import messagebox

from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import LOG_FOLDER
from core.version import APP_VERSION
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, is_qb_running, launch_qbittorrent, close_qbittorrent
from core.autostart import set_autostart, is_autostart_enabled
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.torrent_parser import get_global_torrent_summaries
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from gui.tray import SystemTrayController


class TorrentApp:
    def __init__(self, root, on_quit_callback=None):
        self.root = root
        self.on_quit_callback = on_quit_callback
        self.qb_ip, self.qb_port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        self.port = cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000)
        self._closing = False

        self.root.title(f"tbr PRO v{APP_VERSION} — Control Panel")
        self.root.geometry("480x555")
        self.root.minsize(440, 520)
        self.root.configure(bg="#121214")

        summary_tracker.start_auto_export(get_global_torrent_summaries)
        self.build_ui()

        self.tray = SystemTrayController(
            root=self.root,
            on_show_window=self.restore_from_tray,
            on_open_web=self.open_web,
            on_exit_app=self.quit_app
        )
        self.tray_active = self.tray.start()

        self.root.bind("<Unmap>", self._on_unmap)
        self.root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self.quit_app)

        if self.tray_active and cfg.getboolean('Start_Minimized', False):
            self.root.withdraw()

        self.update_status()

    def build_ui(self):
        f = tk.Frame(self.root, bg="#1a1a20", bd=1, relief="solid")
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=14, pady=14)

        tk.Label(f, text=f"⚡ tbr PRO v{APP_VERSION}", font=("Segoe UI", 13, "bold"), fg="#2ecc71", bg="#1a1a20").pack(
            pady=(14, 4))
        tk.Label(f, text=f"tbr PRO v{APP_VERSION} • Веб-сервер работает в фоне", font=("Segoe UI", 9), fg="#95a5a6", bg="#1a1a20").pack(
            pady=(0, 12))

        tk.Button(f, text="🌐 ОТКРЫТЬ ВЕБ-ИНТЕРФЕЙС", font=("Segoe UI", 11, "bold"), bd=0,
                  bg="#2ecc71", fg="#000000", cursor="hand2", command=self.open_web).pack(fill="x", padx=24, pady=4,
                                                                                          ipady=6)

        net_frame = tk.Frame(f, bg="#262630", bd=0)
        net_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=24, pady=12, ipady=6)

        tk.Label(net_frame, text="Хост и пароль веб-доступа (для 0.0.0.0 пароль обязателен):",
                 font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), fg="#95a5a6", bg="#262630").pack(anchor="w", padx=10, pady=(6, 2))

        row_net = tk.Frame(net_frame, bg="#262630")
        row_net.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=2)

        self.e_host = tk.Entry(row_net, width=12, font=("Segoe UI", 9), bg="#1a1a20", fg="#ffffff",
                               insertbackground="#ffffff", bd=0)
        self.e_host.insert(0, cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1'))
        self.e_host.pack(side="left", ipady=3, padx=(0, 6))

        self.e_pass = tk.Entry(row_net, show="*", font=("Segoe UI", 9), bg="#1a1a20", fg="#ffffff",
                               insertbackground="#ffffff", bd=0)
        self.e_pass.insert(0, cfg.get('Web_Password', ''))
        self.e_pass.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, ipady=3, padx=(0, 6))

        tk.Button(row_net, text="💾", font=("Segoe UI", 9, "bold"), bg="#2ecc71", fg="#000000", bd=0, padx=8,
                  cursor="hand2", command=self.save_net_settings).pack(side="right")

        self.lbl_qb = tk.Label(f, text="qBittorrent: проверка...", font=("Segoe UI", 9, "bold"), fg="#ffffff",
                               bg="#1a1a20")
        self.lbl_qb.pack(pady=(6, 4))

        qb_row = tk.Frame(f, bg="#1a1a20")
        qb_row.pack(fill="x", padx=24, pady=4)
        qb_row.columnconfigure((0, 1), weight=1)

        self.btn_qb_toggle = tk.Button(qb_row, text="Пуск / Стоп qB", font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), bd=0, bg="#262630",
                                       fg="#ffffff", cursor="hand2", command=self.toggle_qb)
        self.btn_qb_toggle.grid(row=0, column=0, padx=3, ipady=4, sticky="ew")

        tk.Button(qb_row, text="🔄 Перезапуск qB", font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), bd=0, bg="#262630", fg="#ffffff",
                  cursor="hand2", command=self.restart_qb).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=3, ipady=4, sticky="ew")

        bot_row = tk.Frame(f, bg="#1a1a20")
        bot_row.pack(fill="x", padx=24, pady=(12, 4))
        bot_row.columnconfigure((0, 1), weight=1)

        self.btn_auto = tk.Button(bot_row, text="Автозагрузка", font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), bd=0, bg="#262630",
                                  fg="#ffffff", cursor="hand2", command=self.toggle_autostart)
        self.btn_auto.grid(row=0, column=0, padx=3, ipady=4, sticky="ew")

        tk.Button(bot_row, text="📁 Папка логов", font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), bd=0, bg="#262630", fg="#ffffff",
                  cursor="hand2", command=lambda: os.startfile(LOG_FOLDER)).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=3, ipady=4,
                                                                                 sticky="ew")

        self.var_start_min = tk.BooleanVar(value=cfg.getboolean('Start_Minimized', False))
        tk.Checkbutton(
            f, text="Запускать свёрнутым в трей", variable=self.var_start_min,
            command=self.toggle_start_minimized, font=("Segoe UI", 9),
            bg="#1a1a20", fg="#95a5a6", selectcolor="#262630",
            activebackground="#1a1a20", activeforeground="#ffffff", bd=0
        ).pack(pady=(6, 4))

        tk.Button(f, text="🔑 Перевыпустить Admin-токен", font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), bd=0, bg="#262630",
                  fg="#f39c12", cursor="hand2", command=self.reset_admin_token).pack(fill="x", padx=24, pady=(4, 0),
                                                                                     ipady=5)

    def _on_unmap(self, event):
        if event.widget is self.root and self.tray_active and not self._closing:
            try:
                if self.root.state() == 'iconic':
                    self.root.withdraw()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def restore_from_tray(self):
        try:
            self.root.deiconify()
            self.root.state('normal')
            self.root.lift()
            self.root.focus_force()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def quit_app(self):
        if self._closing:
            return
        self._closing = True
        if self.tray:
            self.tray.stop()
        if self.on_quit_callback:
            try:
                self.on_quit_callback()
            except Exception:
                pass
        try:
            self.root.quit()
            self.root.destroy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def toggle_start_minimized(self):
        cfg.set('Start_Minimized', str(bool(self.var_start_min.get())))
        cfg.save()

    def reset_admin_token(self):
        new_tok = profiles_db.reset_admin_token_local()
        if new_tok:
            self.root.clipboard_clear()
            self.root.clipboard_append(new_tok)
            messagebox.showinfo("Admin-токен", f"Новый Admin-токен скопирован в буфер обмена:\n\n{new_tok}")

    def save_net_settings(self):
        host = self.e_host.get().strip() or '127.0.0.1'
        pwd = self.e_pass.get().strip()
        if host not in ('127.0.0.1', 'localhost') and not pwd:
            return messagebox.showwarning("Внимание", "Для хоста 0.0.0.0 обязательно впишите пароль!")
        cfg.set('Web_Host', host)
        cfg.set('Web_Password', pwd)
        cfg.save()
        messagebox.showinfo("Сохранено", "Настройки сохранены!\nПерезапустите tbr, чтобы применить новый хост.")

    def open_web(self):
        port = cfg.getint('Web_Port', self.port)
        webbrowser.open(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/")

    def update_status(self):
        if self._closing:
            return
        running = is_qb_running()
        if running:
            self.lbl_qb.config(text="qBittorrent: 🟢 Запущен", fg="#2ecc71")
            self.btn_qb_toggle.config(text="⏹ Остановить qB", bg="#e74c3c", fg="#ffffff")
        else:
            self.lbl_qb.config(text="qBittorrent: 🔴 Остановлен", fg="#e74c3c")
            self.btn_qb_toggle.config(text="▶ Запустить qB", bg="#2ecc71", fg="#000000")

        auto_on = is_autostart_enabled()
        self.btn_auto.config(text="Автозагрузка: ВКЛ" if auto_on else "Автозагрузка: ВЫКЛ",
                             fg="#2ecc71" if auto_on else "#95a5a6")
        self.root.after(3000, self.update_status)

    def toggle_qb(self):
        if is_qb_running():
            close_qbittorrent()
        else:
            launch_qbittorrent()
        self.root.after(1000, self.update_status)

    def restart_qb(self):
        close_qbittorrent()
        self.root.after(1500, launch_qbittorrent)
        self.root.after(2500, self.update_status)

    def toggle_autostart(self):
        curr = is_autostart_enabled()
        ok, msg = set_autostart(not curr)
        messagebox.showinfo("Автозагрузка", msg)
        self.update_status()

# gui/theme.py
def get_theme_config(is_dark_theme):
    themes = {
        True: {
            "bm": "#121214", "bp": "#1a1a20", "bi": "#2d2d38", "bt": "#1e1e24", "bo": "#0c0c0f",
            "ft": "#ffffff", "fl": "#95a5a6", "fo": "#2ecc71", "ib": "white", "tc": "#2a2a32",
            "cb": "#2ecc71", "rb": "#3498db", "tb": "#2a2a32", "cpb": "#34495e", "sh": "#34495e"
        },
        False: {
            "bm": "#f0f2f5", "bp": "#ffffff", "bi": "#e4e6eb", "bt": "#ffffff", "bo": "#f5f6f7",
            "ft": "#000000", "fl": "#4b4f56", "fo": "#000000", "ib": "black", "tc": "#e4e6eb",
            "cb": "#0078d4", "rb": "#e1dfdd", "tb": "#e1dfdd", "cpb": "#e1dfdd", "sh": "#c8c6c4"
        }
    }
    return themes[is_dark_theme]

# gui/tray.py
import os
import threading
from utils.helpers import write_error_log

try:
    import pystray
    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
    TRAY_LIBS_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    TRAY_LIBS_AVAILABLE = False


def has_display():
    if os.name == 'nt':
        return True
    return bool(os.environ.get('DISPLAY') or os.environ.get('WAYLAND_DISPLAY'))


def _create_tray_image():
    size = 64
    img = Image.new('RGBA', (size, size), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    draw.rounded_rectangle([2, 2, size - 2, size - 2], radius=14, fill=(26, 26, 32, 255), outline=(46, 204, 113, 255), width=3)
    # Молния в стиле логотипа tbr PRO
    bolt = [(35, 10), (17, 35), (31, 35), (27, 54), (47, 29), (33, 29)]
    draw.polygon(bolt, fill=(46, 204, 113, 255))
    return img


class SystemTrayController:
    def __init__(self, root, on_show_window, on_open_web, on_exit_app):
        self.root = root
        self.on_show_window = on_show_window
        self.on_open_web = on_open_web
        self.on_exit_app = on_exit_app
        self.icon = None
        self.is_active = False

    def start(self):
        if not TRAY_LIBS_AVAILABLE or not has_display():
            return False

        try:
            menu = pystray.Menu(
                pystray.MenuItem("Открыть окно", self._dispatch(self.on_show_window), default=True),
                pystray.MenuItem("Открыть веб-панель", self._dispatch(self.on_open_web)),
                pystray.Menu.SEPARATOR,
                pystray.MenuItem("Выход", self._dispatch(self.on_exit_app))
            )
            self.icon = pystray.Icon("tbr_pro", _create_tray_image(), "tbr PRO", menu)
            threading.Thread(target=self.icon.run, daemon=True).start()
            self.is_active = True
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("Ошибка запуска системного трея", e)
            self.is_active = False
            return False

    def _dispatch(self, callback):
        def handler(icon, item):
            try:
                self.root.after(0, callback)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return handler

    def stop(self):
        self.is_active = False
        if self.icon:
            try:
                self.icon.stop()
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.icon = None

# main.py
import sys
import os
import atexit
import signal
import argparse
import webbrowser
import threading

from core.backup_manager import apply_pending_restore_if_any
apply_pending_restore_if_any()

from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import write_log
from core.version import APP_VERSION
from core.single_instance import SingleInstanceLock
from core.keep_awake import keep_awake
from core.updater import register_shutdown_hook
from web.app import web_server
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker


def parse_arguments():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=f"tbr PRO v{APP_VERSION} (Швейцарский нож qBittorrent)")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=None, help="Порт веб-сервера")
    parser.add_argument("--host", type=str, default=None, help="Хост веб-сервера")
    parser.add_argument("--web-only", "--headless", dest="web_only", action="store_true",
                        help="Запустить только веб без окна (для серверов и Docker)")
    parser.add_argument("--no-web", action="store_true", help="Запустить только окно без веб-сервера")
    return parser.parse_args()


def get_browser_url(host, port):
    b_host = '127.0.0.1' if host in ('0.0.0.0', '*') else host
    return f"http://{b_host}:{port}"


def main():
    args = parse_arguments()

    instance_lock = SingleInstanceLock()
    if not instance_lock.acquire(wait_sec=1.5):
        msg = "tbr уже запущен (проверь системный трей)"
        if args.web_only:
            write_log("app", msg)
            print(f"⚠️ {msg}")
            sys.exit(0)
        try:
            import tkinter as tk
            from tkinter import messagebox
            tmp_root = tk.Tk()
            tmp_root.withdraw()
            messagebox.showinfo(f"tbr PRO v{APP_VERSION}", msg)
            tmp_root.destroy()
        except Exception:
            print(f"⚠️ {msg}")
        sys.exit(0)

    env_pwd = os.environ.get('TBR_WEB_PASSWORD', '').strip()
    if env_pwd:
        cfg.set('Web_Password', env_pwd)
    if args.port:
        cfg.set('Web_Port', args.port)
    if args.host:
        cfg.set('Web_Host', args.host)
    if args.port or args.host or env_pwd:
        cfg.save()

    web_port = cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000)
    web_host = cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1')

    def on_shutdown():
        keep_awake.stop()
        summary_tracker.flush_on_exit()
        web_server.stop()
        instance_lock.release()

    register_shutdown_hook(on_shutdown)
    atexit.register(on_shutdown)
    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):
        try:
            signal.signal(sig, lambda s, f: (on_shutdown(), sys.exit(0)))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not args.no_web:
        web_server.start(host=web_host, port=web_port)

    if args.web_only:
        url = get_browser_url(web_host, web_port)
        if os.name == 'nt':
            try:
                webbrowser.open(url)
            except Exception:
                pass
        print(f"✅ tbr PRO v{APP_VERSION} запущен на {url}. Нажмите Ctrl+C для выхода.")
        try:
            while True:
                threading.Event().wait(1)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            on_shutdown()
            sys.exit(0)

    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import messagebox
    try:
        from gui.main_window import TorrentApp
    except ImportError:
        TorrentApp = None

    if TorrentApp is None:
        messagebox.showerror("Ошибка запуска", "Файл интерфейса gui/main_window.py не найден!")
        on_shutdown()
        return

    if os.name == 'nt':
        try:
            from ctypes import windll
            windll.shcore.SetProcessDpiAwareness(1)
        except Exception:
            pass

    root = tk.Tk()
    TorrentApp(root, on_quit_callback=on_shutdown)
    root.mainloop()
    on_shutdown()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# utils/bencode.py

def decode_bencode(data):
    """Декодирует bencode-данные: валидные UTF-8 строки -> str, сырые бинарные байты -> bytes без искажения."""
    if isinstance(data, str):
        data = data.encode('latin1')

    def decode_first(idx):
        char = data[idx:idx + 1]
        if char == b'i':
            end = data.index(b'e', idx + 1)
            return int(data[idx + 1:end]), end + 1
        elif char == b'l':
            idx += 1
            res = []
            while data[idx:idx + 1] != b'e':
                val, idx = decode_first(idx)
                res.append(val)
            return res, idx + 1
        elif char == b'd':
            idx += 1
            res = {}
            while data[idx:idx + 1] != b'e':
                raw_key, idx = decode_first(idx)
                if isinstance(raw_key, bytes):
                    try:
                        key = raw_key.decode('utf-8')
                    except UnicodeDecodeError:
                        # Не-UTF8 ключи оставляем сырыми bytes во избежание порчи при обратном кодировании
                        key = raw_key
                else:
                    key = raw_key if isinstance(raw_key, str) else str(raw_key)

                val, idx = decode_first(idx)
                res[key] = val
            return res, idx + 1
        elif char.isdigit():
            colon = data.index(b':', idx)
            length = int(data[idx:colon])
            start = colon + 1
            end = start + length
            raw_bytes = data[start:end]
            try:
                val = raw_bytes.decode('utf-8')
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                val = raw_bytes
            return val, end
        raise ValueError(f"Неизвестный токен bencode на позиции {idx}: {char}")

    val, _ = decode_first(0)
    return val


def encode_bencode(val):
    """Безопасное кодирование обратно в bencode с корректной сортировкой ключей."""
    if isinstance(val, int):
        return b'i' + str(val).encode('ascii') + b'e'
    elif isinstance(val, (bytes, bytearray)):
        return str(len(val)).encode('ascii') + b':' + bytes(val)
    elif isinstance(val, str):
        b = val.encode('utf-8')
        return str(len(b)).encode('ascii') + b':' + b
    elif isinstance(val, (list, tuple)):
        return b'l' + b''.join(encode_bencode(x) for x in val) + b'e'
    elif isinstance(val, dict):
        def to_bytes(k):
            return bytes(k) if isinstance(k, (bytes, bytearray)) else str(k).encode('utf-8')

        sorted_keys = sorted(val.keys(), key=to_bytes)
        out = [b'd']
        for k in sorted_keys:
            out.append(encode_bencode(to_bytes(k)))
            out.append(encode_bencode(val[k]))
        out.append(b'e')
        return b''.join(out)
    raise TypeError(f"Невозможно закодировать тип в bencode: {type(val)}")
