# utils/config.py
import os
import sys
import shutil
import configparser
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR

if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
    OLD_BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
else:
    OLD_BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

CONFIG_FILE = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, 'config.ini')
OLD_CONFIG_FILE = os.path.join(OLD_BASE_DIR, 'config.ini')


class AppConfig:
    def __init__(self):
        self.config_file = CONFIG_FILE
        self.config = configparser.ConfigParser()
        self._migrate_old_config()
        self.load()

    def _migrate_old_config(self):
        if not os.path.exists(self.config_file) and os.path.exists(OLD_CONFIG_FILE):
            try:
                os.makedirs(APP_DATA_DIR, exist_ok=True)
                shutil.move(OLD_CONFIG_FILE, self.config_file)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    shutil.copy2(OLD_CONFIG_FILE, self.config_file)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    def load(self):
        if os.path.exists(self.config_file):
            try:
                self.config.read(self.config_file, encoding='utf-8')
            except Exception:
                pass
        if 'Settings' not in self.config:
            self.config['Settings'] = {}

        defaults = {
            'Theme': 'True',
            'Web_Port': '5000',
            'Web_Host': '127.0.0.1',
            'Web_Password': '',
            'AutoStartWeb': 'True',
            'AutoOpenBrowser': 'False',
            'WebUI_Address': '',
            'WebUI_Port': '',
            'WebUI_User': 'admin',
            'WebUI_Password': '',
            'Weight_W1': '70',
            'Weight_W2': '30',
            'Weight_W3': '0',
            'Snapshot_Interval_Min': '60',
            'Start_Minimized': 'False',
            'Auto_Enabled': 'False',
            'Auto_Interval_Min': '5',
            'TG_Bot_Token': '',
            'TG_Chat_ID': '',
            'Discord_Webhook': '',
            'Alert_Disk_Min_GiB': '0',
            'Alert_QB_Down': 'False',
            'KeepAwake_Enabled': 'False',
            'KeepAwake_Min_KB': '500',
            'KeepAwake_Display': 'False',
            'AutoRecheck_Enabled': 'False',
            'Watchdog_Enabled': 'False',
            'Watchdog_Timeout_Min': '3',
            'Watchdog_Exe_Path': '',
            'Manifest_Weekly': 'False',
            'Manifest_Last_Run': '0',
            'Autopilot_Frozen_Until': '0',
            'Vault_Aggregate_Mode': 'exclude',
            'DND_Enabled': 'False',
            'DND_Start': '23:00',
            'DND_End': '08:00',
            'DND_Days': '0,1,2,3,4,5,6',
            'Digest_Mode': 'off',
            'Digest_Hour': '21',
            'Digest_Weekday': '6',
            'Digest_Last_Sent_Ts': '0',
            'Snooze_Saved_DL': '0',
            'Snooze_Saved_UP': '0',
            'Max_Channel_Mbps': '1000'
        }
        changed = False
        for k, v in defaults.items():
            if k not in self.config['Settings']:
                self.config['Settings'][k] = v
                changed = True
        if changed or not os.path.exists(self.config_file):
            self.save()

    def save(self):
        try:
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.config_file), exist_ok=True)
            with open(self.config_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                self.config.write(f)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def get(self, key, fallback=''):
        return self.config.get('Settings', key, fallback=fallback)

    def getint(self, key, fallback=0):
        try:
            val = self.config.get('Settings', key, fallback='').strip()
            return int(val) if val else fallback
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return fallback

    def getboolean(self, key, fallback=False):
        try:
            return self.config.getboolean('Settings', key, fallback=fallback)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return fallback

    def set(self, key, value):
        if 'Settings' not in self.config:
            self.config['Settings'] = {}
        self.config['Settings'][key] = str(value)


cfg = AppConfig()

# utils/helpers.py
import os
import datetime
import traceback
import re


def _get_app_data_dir():
    env_dir = os.environ.get('TBR_DATA_DIR', '').strip()
    if env_dir:
        return env_dir
    if os.name == 'nt':
        local_app = os.environ.get('LOCALAPPDATA', os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), 'AppData', 'Local'))
        return os.path.join(local_app, 'tbr')
    return os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), '.config', 'tbr')


def _get_bt_backup_path():
    env_bt = os.environ.get('TBR_BT_BACKUP', '').strip()
    if env_bt:
        return env_bt
    if os.name == 'nt':
        return os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), 'AppData', 'Local', 'qBittorrent', 'BT_backup')
    return os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), '.local', 'share', 'qBittorrent', 'BT_backup')


APP_DATA_DIR = _get_app_data_dir()
BT_BACKUP_PATH = _get_bt_backup_path()
LOG_FOLDER = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, 'logs')

os.makedirs(APP_DATA_DIR, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(LOG_FOLDER, exist_ok=True)


def write_log(category, message):
    try:
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
        log_file = os.path.join(LOG_FOLDER, f"{category}.log")
        with open(log_file, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(f"[{ts}] {message}\n")
    except Exception:
        pass


def write_error_log(context, error):
    try:
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
        log_file = os.path.join(LOG_FOLDER, "error.log")
        with open(log_file, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(f"[{ts}] ОШИБКА в {context}: {error}\n")
            f.write(traceback.format_exc() + "\n")
    except Exception:
        pass


def sanitize_csv_cell(val):
    """Защита от CSV Formula Injection."""
    s = str(val) if val is not None else ''
    if s and s[0] in ('=', '+', '-', '@', '\t', '\r'):
        return "'" + s
    return s


def format_file_size(size_bytes):
    if size_bytes is None:
        return "0 B"
    try:
        size = float(size_bytes)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return "0 B"
    if size <= 0:
        return "0 B"
    if size < 1.0:
        return f"{size:.2f} B"
    for unit in ['B', 'KiB', 'MiB', 'GiB', 'TiB', 'PiB']:
        if size < 1024.0:
            return f"{size:.2f} {unit}"
        size /= 1024.0
    return f"{size:.2f} EiB"


def parse_bytes(text, torrent_size=0):
    if not text:
        raise ValueError("Цель не может быть пустой!")
    text = text.strip().lower().replace(',', '.')

    m_ratio = re.match(r'^([\d\.]+)\s*(?:x|х|ratio|р|ратио)?$', text)
    if m_ratio:
        ratio = float(m_ratio.group(1))
        if ratio <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Ratio должен быть больше 0!")
        return int(torrent_size * ratio)

    m_size = re.match(r'^([\d\.]+)\s*([a-zа-яё]+)?$', text)
    if not m_size:
        raise ValueError("Неверный формат цели! (Пример: 50 тиб, 100 GiB или 2.0x)")

    val = float(m_size.group(1))
    unit = (m_size.group(2) or 'b').lower()

    multipliers = {
        'b': 1, 'б': 1, 'байт': 1,
        'k': 1024, 'kb': 1024, 'kib': 1024, 'к': 1024, 'кб': 1024, 'киб': 1024,
        'm': 1024**2, 'mb': 1024**2, 'mib': 1024**2, 'м': 1024**2, 'мб': 1024**2, 'миб': 1024**2,
        'g': 1024**3, 'gb': 1024**3, 'gib': 1024**3, 'г': 1024**3, 'гб': 1024**3, 'гиб': 1024**3,
        't': 1024**4, 'tb': 1024**4, 'tib': 1024**4, 'т': 1024**4, 'тб': 1024**4, 'тиб': 1024**4,
        'p': 1024**5, 'pb': 1024**5, 'pib': 1024**5, 'п': 1024**5, 'пб': 1024**5, 'пиб': 1024**5
    }

    if unit not in multipliers:
        raise ValueError(f"Неизвестная единица измерения: {unit}")

    return int(val * multipliers[unit])


def parse_speed(text, default_bytes_sec=0):
    if not text or text.strip().lower() in ('авто', 'auto'):
        return default_bytes_sec
    text = text.strip().lower().replace(',', '.')

    m = re.match(r'^([\d\.]+)\s*([a-zа-яё/]+)?$', text)
    if not m:
        raise ValueError("Неверный формат скорости!")

    val = float(m.group(1))
    raw_unit = (m.group(2) or 'mib').replace('/s', '').replace('/с', '').replace('/сек', '').strip()

    multipliers = {
        'b': 1, 'б': 1,
        'k': 1024, 'kb': 1024, 'kib': 1024, 'к': 1024, 'кб': 1024, 'киб': 1024,
        'm': 1024**2, 'mb': 1024**2, 'mib': 1024**2, 'м': 1024**2, 'мб': 1024**2, 'миб': 1024**2,
        'g': 1024**3, 'gb': 1024**3, 'gib': 1024**3, 'г': 1024**3, 'гб': 1024**3, 'гиб': 1024**3
    }

    unit = raw_unit if raw_unit in multipliers else 'mib'
    return int(val * multipliers[unit])

def get_resource_path(relative_path):
    base_path = getattr(sys, '_MEIPASS', os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
    return os.path.join(base_path, relative_path)


def _get_or_create_secret_key():
    key_file = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, '.flask_secret')
    try:
        if os.path.isfile(key_file):
            data = open(key_file, 'rb').read()
            if len(data) >= 24:
                return data
        new_key = os.urandom(32)
        with open(key_file, 'wb') as f:
            f.write(new_key)
        return new_key
    except Exception:
        return os.urandom(24)

    __name__,
    template_folder=get_resource_path(os.path.join('web', 'templates')),
    static_folder=get_resource_path(os.path.join('web', 'static'))
)
app.secret_key = _get_or_create_secret_key()
app.config['BUILD_VER'] = str(int(time.time()))

class WebServerController:
    def __init__(self):
        self.server_instance = None
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self.current_host = '127.0.0.1'
        self.current_port = 5000

    def start(self, host=None, port=None):
        with self._lock:
            self._start_locked(host, port)

    def _start_locked(self, host=None, port=None):
        h = (host or cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1')).strip() or '127.0.0.1'
        p = port or cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000)

        is_exposed = h not in ('127.0.0.1', 'localhost')
        if is_exposed and not cfg.get('Web_Password', '').strip():
            print("⚠️ Внимание: выбран внешний хост, но пароль не задан! Безопасный откат на 127.0.0.1.")
            h = '127.0.0.1'
            is_exposed = False

        app.config['IS_EXPOSED'] = is_exposed
        self.current_host = h
        self.current_port = p

        summary_tracker.start_auto_export(get_global_torrent_summaries)
        automation_engine.start()
        resume_pending_relocate_if_any()

        try:
            if HAS_WAITRESS and create_server:
                self.server_instance = create_server(app, host=h, port=p, threads=8)
                engine_name = "Waitress"
            else:
                from werkzeug.serving import make_server
                self.server_instance = make_server(h, p, app, threaded=True)
                engine_name = "Werkzeug (fallback)"

            print(f"🚀 Сервер {engine_name} запущен на http://{h}:{p} (Авторизация: {'ВКЛ' if is_exposed else 'ВЫКЛ'})")

            def serve():
                try:
                    if self.server_instance:
                        if hasattr(self.server_instance, 'run'):
                            self.server_instance.run()
                        else:
                            self.server_instance.serve_forever()
                except Exception as e:
                    write_error_log("WebServer serve error", e)

            threading.Thread(target=serve, daemon=True).start()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("WebServerController Start Error", e)
            print(f"❌ Ошибка запуска веб-сервера на {h}:{p}: {e}")

    def stop(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self.server_instance:
                try:
                    if hasattr(self.server_instance, 'close'):
                        self.server_instance.close()
                    elif hasattr(self.server_instance, 'shutdown'):
                        self.server_instance.shutdown()
                except Exception:
                    pass
                self.server_instance = None

    def restart(self, new_host, new_port):
        def do_restart():
            time.sleep(0.5)
            self.stop()
            with self._lock:
                self._start_locked(new_host, new_port)

        threading.Thread(target=do_restart, daemon=True).start()


web_server = WebServerController()
app.config['WEB_SERVER'] = web_server

# web/errors.py
from flask import request, jsonify, render_template
from werkzeug.exceptions import HTTPException
from utils.helpers import write_error_log

_ERROR_COPY = {
    400: ("Кривой запрос", "То ли CSRF протух, то ли JSON помялся по дороге. Обнови страницу и попробуй ещё раз."),
    403: ("Доступ закрыт", "Сюда пускают только с погонами Admin (или твой Viewer-токен упёрся в шлагбаум)."),
    404: ("Страница ушла в сид", "Такого маршрута в tbr PRO нет — возможно, он ушёл в DHT и не вернулся."),
    429: ("Притормози, ковбой", "Слишком много запросов подряд. Дай серверу отдышаться пару минут."),
    500: ("Внутри что-то хрустнуло", "Сервер поймал внутреннюю ошибку. Подробности аккуратно записаны в error.log — ничего лишнего наружу не утекло.")
}


def _wants_json():
    if request.path.startswith('/api/'):
        return True
    if request.is_json or request.headers.get('X-Requested-With') == 'XMLHttpRequest':
        return True
    accept = request.headers.get('Accept', '')
    return 'application/json' in accept and 'text/html' not in accept


def _render_error(code):
    safe_code = code if code in _ERROR_COPY else 500
    title, msg = _ERROR_COPY[safe_code]
    if _wants_json():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': title, 'message': msg, 'code': safe_code}), safe_code
    return render_template('error.html', code=safe_code, title=title, message=msg), safe_code


def register_error_handlers(app):
    for code in (400, 403, 404, 429, 500):
        app.register_error_handler(code, lambda e, c=code: _render_error(c))

    @app.errorhandler(Exception)
    def handle_any_exception(e):
        if isinstance(e, HTTPException):
            return _render_error(e.code or 500)
        write_error_log("Flask HTTP 500", e)
        return _render_error(500)

def _parse_day_bounds(date_str):
    try:
        d = datetime.datetime.strptime(str(date_str).strip(), '%Y-%m-%d')
    except ValueError:
        d = datetime.datetime.now().replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    start_ts = d.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
    end_ts = d.replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59, microsecond=999999).timestamp()
    return d.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'), start_ts, end_ts


def _is_strict_localhost():
    if os.path.exists('/.dockerenv') or bool(os.environ.get('TBR_DATA_DIR', '').strip()):
        return False
    if request.headers.get('X-Forwarded-For') or request.headers.get('X-Real-IP'):
        return False
    return (request.remote_addr or '').strip() in ('127.0.0.1', '::1')


def _get_live_dict():
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    return get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}


@analytics_bp.route('/api/summary')
def api_summary():
    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()
    stats = summary_tracker.get_stats(summaries)
    live = _get_live_dict()
    targets = list_targets()
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in live.items()}

    for it in stats.get('items', []):
        hl = (it.get('hash_id') or it.get('hash') or '').lower()
        enrich_live_metrics(it, live_lower.get(hl), targets.get(hl))

    role = g.get('role') or 'viewer'
    if role != 'admin':
        stats = filter_summary_stats_for_role(stats, role, get_hidden_hashes(live))
    return jsonify(stats)


@analytics_bp.route('/api/analytics/history')
def api_analytics_history():
    t_hash = request.args.get('hash', 'all')
    if t_hash != 'all' and not HASH_RE.fullmatch(t_hash):
        return jsonify([])
    if t_hash != 'all' and g.get('role') != 'admin':
        if t_hash.lower() in get_hidden_hashes(_get_live_dict()):
            return jsonify([])
    limit = min(100, max(1, request.args.get('limit', 36, type=int)))
    return jsonify(summary_tracker.get_torrent_hourly_history(t_hash, limit_hours=limit))


@analytics_bp.route('/api/analytics/charts')
def api_analytics_charts():
    chart = request.args.get('chart', 'all')
    hours = request.args.get('hours', 24, type=int)
    top_n = max(1, min(25, request.args.get('top_n', 5, type=int)))
    top_period = request.args.get('top_period', '1d')
    win_hours = hours if hours in (6, 12, 24, 72, 168) else 24

    if chart == 'hourly':
        series = build_detailed_speed_series(summary_tracker.db, hours=win_hours) if win_hours <= 24 else build_hourly_aggregated_speed_series(summary_tracker.db, hours=win_hours)
        return jsonify({'hourly': {'hours': win_hours, 'labels': [p['label'] for p in series], 'values': [p['speed_mbs'] for p in series], 'meta': series}})

    if chart == 'daily':
        rows = summary_tracker.db.get_snapshots_since(time.time() - (14 * 86400))
        daily_map, prev_row = {}, None
        for r in rows:
            if prev_row is not None:
                t1, t2 = prev_row['timestamp'], r['timestamp']
                dt_sec, delta = t2 - t1, max(0, r['total_uploaded'] - prev_row['total_uploaded'])
                if dt_sec >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and delta > 0:
                    cur_ts = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(t1).replace(minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
                    while cur_ts < t2:
                        b_end = cur_ts + 3600.0
                        overlap = min(t2, b_end) - max(t1, cur_ts)
                        if overlap > 0:
                            dk = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(cur_ts).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
                            daily_map[dk] = daily_map.get(dk, 0.0) + delta * (overlap / dt_sec)
                        cur_ts = b_end
            prev_row = r
        s_days = sorted(daily_map.keys())[-14:]
        return jsonify({'daily': {'labels': [f"{d[8:10]}.{d[5:7]}" for d in s_days], 'values': [round(daily_map[d] / (1024 ** 3), 3) for d in s_days]}})

    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()
    live = _get_live_dict()
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        hidden = get_hidden_hashes(live)
        if hidden:
            summaries = [s for s in summaries if (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower() not in hidden]

    if chart == 'top5':
        per = top_period if top_period in ('1d', '7d', '30d', 'all') else '1d'
        top_items, others = compute_top_n_torrents(summary_tracker.db, summaries, live=live, top_n=top_n, period=per, with_others=True)
        return jsonify({'top5': {'n': top_n, 'period': per, 'labels': [x['short_name'] for x in top_items], 'full_labels': [x['full_name'] for x in top_items], 'values': [x['gb'] for x in top_items], 'others': others}})

    return jsonify(summary_tracker.get_charts_data(summaries, hours=hours, top_n=top_n, top_period=top_period))


@analytics_bp.route('/api/smart_calc', methods=['POST'])
def api_smart_calc():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неверный формат JSON'}), 400
    return jsonify(summary_tracker.calculate_smart_forecast(
        str(data.get('target_type', 'torrent')), str(data.get('hash', '')),
        str(data.get('target', ''))[:64], str(data.get('speed', ''))[:64], get_global_torrent_summaries()
    ))


@analytics_bp.route('/api/smart_calc/export', methods=['POST'])
def api_smart_calc_export():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    forecast_data = data.get('forecast_data')
    rows = forecast_data.get('forecast_rows') if isinstance(forecast_data, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or len(rows) > 100 or not all(isinstance(r, dict) for r in rows):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные строки прогноза'}), 400
    filepath, filename = summary_tracker.export_forecast_csv(forecast_data)
    if not filepath:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Ошибка создания файла'}), 500
    with open(filepath, 'rb') as f:
        file_bytes = f.read()
    return Response(file_bytes, mimetype="text/csv", headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename*=UTF-8''{urllib.parse.quote(filename)}"})


@analytics_bp.route('/api/export_csv', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_export_csv():
    role = g.get('role') or 'viewer'
    if request.method == 'POST':
        req_data = request.get_json(silent=True)
        items = req_data.get('items') if isinstance(req_data, dict) else None
        if not isinstance(items, list) or len(items) > 5000 or not all(isinstance(x, dict) for x in items):
            return jsonify({'error': 'Некорректный список или превышен лимит 5000 строк'}), 400
        summary_tracker.save_manual_csv(items)
    else:
        stats = summary_tracker.get_stats(get_global_torrent_summaries())
        if role != 'admin':
            stats = filter_summary_stats_for_role(stats, role, get_hidden_hashes(_get_live_dict()))
        items = stats.get('items', [])

    output = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(output, delimiter=';')
    writer.writerow(['Имя', 'Хэш', 'Размер (байт)', 'Размер (ГБ)', 'Отдано (байт)', 'Отдано (ГБ)', 'Ratio', 'Ср. скорость (всё время)', 'Скорость за 1ч', 'Скорость за 24ч', 'МБ/ч', 'МБ/день', 'Ratio/ч (всего)', 'Ratio/ч (1ч)', 'Ratio/ч (24ч)', 'Дата создания'])
    for s in items:
        sz = int(s.get('size', 0)) if isinstance(s.get('size'), (int, float)) else 0
        up = int(s.get('uploaded', 0)) if isinstance(s.get('uploaded'), (int, float)) else 0
        rt = float(s.get('ratio', 0.0)) if isinstance(s.get('ratio'), (int, float)) else 0.0
        writer.writerow([
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('name', '')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('hash_id', '')),
            sz, f"{sz / (1024 ** 3):.2f}", up, f"{up / (1024 ** 3):.2f}", f"{rt:.2f}",
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_all_time_fmt', '0 B/s')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_1h_fmt', '0 B/s')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_24h_fmt', '0 B/s')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('vol_hour_fmt', '0.00 МБ/ч')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('vol_day_fmt', '0.00 МБ/день')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_all_fmt', '0.000 /ч')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_hour_fmt', '0.000 /ч')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_day_fmt', '0.000 /ч')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('created', ''))
        ])
    filename = f"summary_manual_{datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.csv"
    return Response(output.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), mimetype="text/csv", headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename={filename}"})

@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/open_folder', methods=['POST'])
def api_logs_open_folder():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin' or not _is_strict_localhost() or os.name != 'nt':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Доступно только локально с localhost'}), 403
    try:
        os.startfile(LOG_FOLDER)
        return jsonify({'success': True})
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': str(e)}), 500

@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/day')
def api_logs_by_day():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    date_str, start_ts, end_ts = _parse_day_bounds(request.args.get('date', ''))
    res = summary_tracker.db.get_snapshots_by_day_paginated(start_ts, end_ts, page=request.args.get('page', 1, type=int), per_page=request.args.get('per_page', 15, type=int))
    for it in res['items']:
        it['uploaded_fmt'] = format_file_size(it.get('total_uploaded', 0))
        it['size_fmt'] = format_file_size(it.get('total_size', 0))
    res['date'] = date_str
    return jsonify(res)


@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/snapshot/ignore', methods=['POST'])
def api_logs_snapshot_ignore():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    snap_id = data.get('id')
    if not snap_id:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан id снимка'}), 400
    ignored = bool(data.get('ignored', True))
    ok = summary_tracker.db.set_snapshot_ignored(int(snap_id), ignored=ignored)
    if not ok:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Снимок не найден'}), 404
    invalidate_insights_cache()
    hm = get_heatmap_365(summary_tracker.db)
    records = get_records_and_comparisons(summary_tracker.db, heatmap_cells=hm, force_recalc=True)
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'records': records})


@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/day/download')
def api_logs_by_day_download():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return "Только для Admin", 403
    date_str, start_ts, end_ts = _parse_day_bounds(request.args.get('date', ''))
    rows = summary_tracker.db.get_snapshots_by_day_all(start_ts, end_ts)
    output = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(output, delimiter=';')
    writer.writerow(['Время снимка', 'Всего отдано (байт)', 'Всего отдано', 'Общий размер (байт)', 'Раздач'])
    for r in rows:
        writer.writerow([sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('datetime', '')), r.get('total_uploaded', 0), sanitize_csv_cell(format_file_size(r.get('total_uploaded', 0))), r.get('total_size', 0), r.get('count', 0)])
    return Response(output.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), mimetype="text/csv", headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename=snapshots_{date_str}.csv"})

# web/routes/automation_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify

from utils.config import cfg
from core.automation import automation_engine, VALID_TIME_MODES, VALID_SPEED_OPS
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db

automation_bp = Blueprint('automation_bp', __name__)


def _parse_rule_payload(data):
    name = str(data.get('name', '')).strip()[:64] or 'Правило'
    time_mode = str(data.get('time_mode', 'seeding_time')).strip()
    if time_mode not in VALID_TIME_MODES:
        time_mode = 'seeding_time'

    try:
        min_ratio = max(0.0, float(data.get('min_ratio') or 0))
        min_days = max(0.0, float(data.get('min_seed_days') or 0))
        min_up_bytes = max(0, int(data.get('min_uploaded_bytes') or 0))
        speed_op = str(data.get('speed_op', '') or '').strip()
        if speed_op not in VALID_SPEED_OPS:
            speed_op = ''
        speed_bytes = max(0.0, float(data.get('speed_bytes') or 0))
        speed_win = max(1, min(10080, int(data.get('speed_window_min') or 60)))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return None, 'Некорректные числовые параметры правила'

    use_speed = (speed_op in ('>=', '<') and speed_bytes > 0)
    if min_ratio <= 0 and min_days <= 0 and min_up_bytes <= 0 and not use_speed:
        return None, 'Укажите хотя бы одно условие (Ratio, Время, Отдано или Среднюю скорость)'

    return {
        'name': name, 'time_mode': time_mode, 'min_ratio': min_ratio,
        'min_seed_days': min_days, 'min_uploaded_bytes': min_up_bytes,
        'speed_op': speed_op if use_speed else '', 'speed_bytes': speed_bytes if use_speed else 0.0,
        'speed_window_min': speed_win, 'action_pause': bool(data.get('action_pause', False)),
        'action_tag': str(data.get('action_tag', '')).strip()[:32],
        'action_remove_tag': str(data.get('action_remove_tag', '')).strip()[:32],
        'notify': bool(data.get('notify', True))
    }, None


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation', methods=['GET'])
def api_automation_get():
    return jsonify({
        'settings': {
            'enabled': cfg.getboolean('Auto_Enabled', False),
            'interval': cfg.getint('Auto_Interval_Min', 5),
            'has_tg_token': bool(cfg.get('TG_Bot_Token', '').strip()),
            'tg_chat_id': cfg.get('TG_Chat_ID', ''),
            'has_discord': bool(cfg.get('Discord_Webhook', '').strip()),
            'alert_disk_gib': cfg.getint('Alert_Disk_Min_GiB', 0),
            'alert_qb_down': cfg.getboolean('Alert_QB_Down', False),
            'auto_recheck_enabled': cfg.getboolean('AutoRecheck_Enabled', False)
        },
        'rules': automation_engine.get_rules()
    })


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/settings', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_settings():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    enabled = bool(data.get('enabled', False))
    try:
        interval = max(1, min(1440, int(data.get('interval', 5))))
        alert_disk = max(0, min(100000, int(data.get('alert_disk_gib', 0) or 0)))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные числовые параметры'}), 400

    tg_token = str(data.get('tg_token', '')).strip()
    tg_chat = str(data.get('tg_chat_id', '')).strip()
    discord = str(data.get('discord_webhook', '')).strip()

    if discord and not (discord.startswith('https://discord.com/api/webhooks/') or discord.startswith('https://discordapp.com/api/webhooks/')):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный URL Discord Webhook!'}), 400

    cfg.set('Auto_Enabled', str(enabled))
    cfg.set('Auto_Interval_Min', str(interval))
    cfg.set('Alert_Disk_Min_GiB', str(alert_disk))
    cfg.set('Alert_QB_Down', str(bool(data.get('alert_qb_down', False))))
    cfg.set('AutoRecheck_Enabled', str(bool(data.get('auto_recheck_enabled', False))))
    if tg_token:
        cfg.set('TG_Bot_Token', tg_token)
    cfg.set('TG_Chat_ID', tg_chat)
    if discord:
        cfg.set('Discord_Webhook', discord)
    cfg.save()

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', 'Настройки автопилота обновлены', f'enabled={enabled}, interval={interval}m', actor='admin')
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Настройки автопилота сохранены!'})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/test', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_test():
    ok, results = automation_engine.test_notifications()
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': ' | '.join(results)})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/run_now', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_run_now():
    cnt = automation_engine.run_cycle()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'triggered': cnt, 'message': f'Проверка завершена. Сработало раздач: {cnt}'})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/dry_run', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_dry_run():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    rule_id = data.get('rule_id')
    try:
        rid = int(rule_id) if rule_id is not None else None
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный rule_id'}), 400
    return jsonify(automation_engine.dry_run(rule_id=rid))


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_add_rule():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    parsed, err = _parse_rule_payload(data)
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    rid = automation_engine.add_rule(**parsed)
    return jsonify({'success': bool(rid), 'id': rid})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules/update', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_update_rule():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    rid = data.get('id')
    if not rid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID правила'}), 400
    try:
        rid_int = int(rid)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный ID правила'}), 400
    parsed, err = _parse_rule_payload(data)
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    ok = automation_engine.update_rule(rid_int, **parsed)
    return jsonify({'success': ok}), (200 if ok else 404)


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules/toggle', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_toggle_rule():
    rid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    if not rid:
        return jsonify({'success': False}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': automation_engine.toggle_rule(rid)})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_delete_rule():
    rid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    if not rid:
        return jsonify({'success': False}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': automation_engine.delete_rule(rid)})

# web/routes/deep_analytics_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, render_template, Response, g
from utils.config import cfg
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info
from core.torrent_parser import get_global_torrent_summaries
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.private_vault import get_hidden_hashes
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.deep_analytics import get_rhythm_7x24, get_day_hourly_breakdown, get_storage_runway, compare_periods_ab, get_monthly_quota_status
from core.swarm_analytics import get_tracker_survivability, get_protocol_efficiency, get_qb_slot_recommendations, find_normalized_title_groups
from core.wrapped_service import build_wrapped_summary, export_full_inventory

deep_analytics_bp = Blueprint('deep_analytics_bp', __name__)


def _live_and_hidden():
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    return ip, port, live, get_hidden_hashes(live)


@deep_analytics_bp.route('/kiosk')
def kiosk_view():
    """#35 Read-Only режим Киоск / Второй монитор."""
    return render_template('kiosk.html')


@deep_analytics_bp.route('/api/analytics/rhythm')
def api_rhythm():
    return jsonify(get_rhythm_7x24(summary_tracker.db))


@deep_analytics_bp.route('/api/analytics/day_hourly')
def api_day_hourly():
    return jsonify(get_day_hourly_breakdown(summary_tracker.db, request.args.get('date', '')))


@deep_analytics_bp.route('/api/analytics/runway')
def api_runway():
    _, _, live, _ = _live_and_hidden()

        if 'reserve_gib' in d:
            cfg.set('Disk_Reserve_GiB', str(max(0, int(d['reserve_gib'] or 0))))
        cfg.save()

    _, _, live, hidden = _live_and_hidden()
    hid = hidden if g.get('role') != 'admin' else set()
    return jsonify(build_wrapped_summary(summary_tracker.db, get_global_torrent_summaries(), live, period=period, hidden_hashes=hid))


@deep_analytics_bp.route('/api/inventory/export')
def api_inventory_export():
    fmt = request.args.get('fmt', 'json')
    if fmt not in ('json', 'csv', 'md'):
        fmt = 'json'
    include_vault = (g.get('role') == 'admin' and request.args.get('include_vault') == '1')
    _, _, live, hidden = _live_and_hidden()
    hid = set() if include_vault else hidden

    with app_state_db._lock:
        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
            try:
                lineage_map = {r['torrent_hash']: r['torrent_uid'] for r in conn.execute("SELECT torrent_hash, torrent_uid FROM torrent_lineage").fetchall()}
            except Exception:
                lineage_map = {}
            try:
                notes_map = {r['uid']: r['note'] for r in conn.execute("SELECT uid, note FROM torrent_notes").fetchall()}
            except Exception:
                notes_map = {}

    data_bytes, mime, fname = export_full_inventory(get_global_torrent_summaries(), live, lineage_map, notes_map, fmt=fmt, hidden_hashes=hid)
    return Response(data_bytes, mimetype=mime, headers={'Content-Disposition': f'attachment; filename={fname}'})

# web/routes/hardcore_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from utils.config import cfg
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info
from core.private_vault import get_hidden_hashes
from core.hdd_smart_queue import enqueue_recheck, list_recheck_queue, tick_recheck_queue, get_smart_disks_status
from core.rss_dedup import check_rss_duplicates
from core.mediainfo_lite import inspect_torrent_media
from web.security import HASH_RE

hardcore_bp = Blueprint('hardcore_bp', __name__)


def _find_live_ci(live, t_hash):
    hl = str(t_hash or '').lower()
    return next((v for k, v in (live or {}).items() if k.lower() == hl), {})


@hardcore_bp.route('/api/hdd/smart')
def api_hdd_smart():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify(get_smart_disks_status())


@hardcore_bp.route('/api/recheck_queue', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_recheck_queue():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    if request.method == 'POST':
        h = str((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('hash', '')).strip()
        if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
        info = _find_live_ci(live, h)
        enqueue_recheck(h, save_path=info.get('save_path', ''), name=info.get('name', h))
        tick_recheck_queue(ip, port, live)
    return jsonify({'queue': list_recheck_queue()})


@hardcore_bp.route('/api/rss/dedup')
def api_rss_dedup():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify(check_rss_duplicates())


@hardcore_bp.route('/api/torrents/mediainfo')
def api_torrents_mediainfo():
    h = str(request.args.get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    if g.get('role') != 'admin' and h.lower() in get_hidden_hashes(live):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Доступ запрещён'}), 403
    info = _find_live_ci(live, h)
    return jsonify(inspect_torrent_media(info.get('content_path') or info.get('save_path') or ''))

# web/routes/hygiene_bp.py
import os
import json
import urllib.parse
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, Response, g
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import BT_BACKUP_PATH
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, delete_torrents
from core.hygiene_trackers import scan_quarantine, scan_stalled_incompletes, scan_dead_weight
from core.hygiene_disk import scan_bt_backup_garbage, scan_inner_content_junk, trash_paths_list
from core.torrent_archiver import sync_torrent_archive, list_archived_torrents, get_archived_torrent_bytes, export_archive_zip_by_tag
from core.fastresume_merger import paths_overlap
from core.soft_trash import move_to_trash
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from web.security import HASH_RE

hygiene_bp = Blueprint('hygiene_bp', __name__)


def _not_admin():
    return g.get('role') != 'admin'


def _read_bt_torrent_bytes(t_hash):
    if not t_hash or not os.path.isdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        return None
    target = t_hash.lower() + '.torrent'
    for fname in os.listdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        if fname.lower() == target:
            try:
                with open(os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, fname), 'rb') as f:
                    return f.read()
            except OSError:
                return None
    return None


@hygiene_bp.route('/api/hygiene/quarantine')
def api_hygiene_quarantine():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    hrs = request.args.get('hours', 72, type=int)
    apply_tags = request.args.get('tag', '0') == '1'
    return jsonify(scan_quarantine(hours_threshold=hrs, apply_tags=apply_tags))


@hygiene_bp.route('/api/hygiene/incompletes')
def api_hygiene_incompletes():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    days = request.args.get('days', 0, type=int)
    return jsonify(scan_stalled_incompletes(stalled_days=days))


@hygiene_bp.route('/api/hygiene/dead_weight')
def api_hygiene_dead_weight():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    days = request.args.get('days', 30, type=int)
    seeds = request.args.get('min_seeds', 10, type=int)
    return jsonify(scan_dead_weight(idle_days=days, min_seeds=seeds))


@hygiene_bp.route('/api/hygiene/delete_torrents_soft', methods=['POST'])
def api_hygiene_delete_torrents_soft():
    """Удаляет торренты из qB и переносит файлы в _tbr_trash с защитой общих папок и сохранением .torrent."""
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    items = data.get('items') or []
    if not isinstance(items, list) or not items:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Пустой список'}), 400

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен'}), 400

    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port)
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in live.items()}
    del_hashes = {str(it.get('hash', '')).strip().lower() for it in items if HASH_RE.fullmatch(str(it.get('hash', '')).strip())}
    other_paths = [
        str(v.get('content_path') or v.get('save_path') or '').strip()
        for k, v in live_lower.items() if k not in del_hashes and (v.get('content_path') or v.get('save_path'))
    ]

    removed, trashed, skipped_shared = 0, 0, 0
    for it in items:
        h = str(it.get('hash', '')).strip()
        if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
            continue
        lv = live_lower.get(h.lower(), {})
        sp = str(lv.get('save_path') or '').strip()
        cp = str(lv.get('content_path') or it.get('content_path') or '').strip()
        nm = str(lv.get('name') or it.get('name') or h)

        # Копируем .torrent до удаления из qB, иначе клиент сразу сотрёт его из BT_backup
        tor_bytes = _read_bt_torrent_bytes(h)

        if delete_torrents(ip, port, h, delete_files=False):
            removed += 1
            if cp:
                is_root = bool(sp and os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(cp)) == os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(sp)))
                is_shared = any(paths_overlap(cp, op) for op in other_paths)
                if is_root or is_shared:
                    skipped_shared += 1
                else:
                    ok, meta = move_to_trash(cp, name=nm, t_hash=h, actor='admin')
                    if ok:
                        trashed += 1
                        if tor_bytes and isinstance(meta, dict) and not meta.get('has_torrent'):
                            try:
                                item_dir = os.path.join(meta['trash_root'], meta['id'])
                                with open(os.path.join(item_dir, 'backup.torrent'), 'wb') as tf:
                                    tf.write(tor_bytes)
                                meta['has_torrent'] = True
                                with open(os.path.join(item_dir, 'meta.json'), 'w', encoding='utf-8') as mf:
                                    json.dump(meta, mf, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
                            except OSError:
                                pass

    if removed:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Гигиена -> _tbr_trash', f'Удалено: {removed}, в корзину: {trashed}, защищено общих: {skipped_shared}', actor='admin')
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'removed': removed, 'trashed': trashed, 'skipped_shared': skipped_shared})

    if not raw:
        return "Файл не найден или скрыт", 404

# web/routes/inbox_ops_bp.py
import json
import datetime
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from utils.config import cfg
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, set_global_speed_limits
from core.preflight_inbox import preflight_analyze, add_torrent_to_qb, get_update_radar, prepare_qb_update_safe
from core.autopilot_extras import toggle_sniper, list_snipers, get_conductor_config
from core.saved_views import list_saved_views, add_saved_view, delete_saved_view
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from web.security import HASH_RE

inbox_ops_bp = Blueprint('inbox_ops_bp', __name__)
_VALID_ROUTER_FIELDS = {'tracker', 'ext', 'min_gib'}


def _not_admin():
    return g.get('role') != 'admin'


@inbox_ops_bp.route('/api/inbox/preflight', methods=['POST'])
def api_inbox_preflight():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    f = request.files.get('file')
    if f:
        return jsonify(preflight_analyze(torrent_bytes=f.read(), save_path=request.form.get('save_path', '')))
    d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    return jsonify(preflight_analyze(magnet_or_url=d.get('url', ''), save_path=d.get('save_path', '')))


@inbox_ops_bp.route('/api/inbox/add', methods=['POST'])
def api_inbox_add():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    f = request.files.get('file')
    if f:
        ok, msg = add_torrent_to_qb(
            torrent_bytes=f.read(), filename=f.filename or 'upload.torrent',
            savepath=request.form.get('save_path', '').strip(),
            tags=request.form.get('tags', '').strip(),
            paused=request.form.get('paused', '0') == '1'
        )
    else:
        d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
        ok, msg = add_torrent_to_qb(
            magnet_or_url=d.get('url', ''), savepath=str(d.get('save_path', '')).strip(),
            tags=str(d.get('tags', '')).strip(), paused=bool(d.get('paused', False))
        )
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@inbox_ops_bp.route('/api/snipers', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_snipers():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify({'hashes': list_snipers()})
    d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    h = str(d.get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    try:
        idle_h = max(0.5, float(d.get('idle_hours') or 4.0))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        idle_h = 4.0
    ok, active = toggle_sniper(h, idle_hours=idle_h)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'active': active})

    d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    vid = add_saved_view(d.get('name', ''), d.get('filters') or {})
    return jsonify({'success': bool(vid), 'id': vid}), (200 if vid else 400)

@inbox_ops_bp.route('/api/conductor', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_conductor():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    if request.method == 'GET':
        c = get_conductor_config()
        c['smart_queue'] = cfg.getboolean('Smart_Queue_Enabled', False)
        c['smart_reannounce'] = cfg.getboolean('Smart_Reannounce_Enabled', False)
        return jsonify(c)
    d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    enabled = bool(d.get('enabled', False))
    cfg.set('Conductor_Enabled', str(enabled))
    if isinstance(d.get('blocks'), list):
        cfg.set('Conductor_Blocks_JSON', json.dumps(d['blocks'], ensure_ascii=False))
    if 'smart_queue' in d:
        cfg.set('Smart_Queue_Enabled', str(bool(d.get('smart_queue'))))
    if 'smart_reannounce' in d:
        cfg.set('Smart_Reannounce_Enabled', str(bool(d.get('smart_reannounce'))))
    if not enabled and cfg.getint('Conductor_Last_Applied_Up', 0) > 0:
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        if port:
            set_global_speed_limits(ip, port, 0, 0)
        cfg.set('Conductor_Last_Applied_Up', '0')
    cfg.save()
    return jsonify({'success': True})


@inbox_ops_bp.route('/api/conductor/override', methods=['POST'])
def api_conductor_override():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'success': False}), 403
    now_dt = datetime.datetime.now()
    next_hr = (now_dt.replace(minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0) + datetime.timedelta(hours=1)).timestamp()
    cfg.set('Conductor_Override_Until', str(int(next_hr)))
    if cfg.getint('Conductor_Last_Applied_Up', 0) > 0:
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        if port:
            set_global_speed_limits(ip, port, 0, 0)
        cfg.set('Conductor_Last_Applied_Up', '0')
    cfg.save()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'until': int(next_hr)})


@inbox_ops_bp.route('/api/router/rules', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_router_rules():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    if request.method == 'GET':
        with app_state_db._lock:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                return jsonify({'rules': [dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT * FROM auto_router_rules ORDER BY priority ASC, id ASC").fetchall()]})
    d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    fld = str(d.get('match_field', 'tracker')).strip()
    if fld not in _VALID_ROUTER_FIELDS:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректное поле условия'}), 400
    pat = str(d.get('pattern', '')).strip()
    if not pat:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Укажите непустой паттерн'}), 400
    if fld == 'min_gib':
        try:
            if float(pat) <= 0:
                raise ValueError
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Для min_gib укажите число > 0'}), 400
    cat = str(d.get('category', '')).strip()
    path = str(d.get('path', '')).strip()
    if not cat and not path:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Укажите категорию или путь назначения'}), 400
    try:
        prio = int(d.get('priority', 10) or 10)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        prio = 10

    with app_state_db._lock:
        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
            cur = conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO auto_router_rules (priority, match_field, pattern, target_category, target_path) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (prio, fld, pat, cat, path)
            )
            conn.commit()
            return jsonify({'success': True, 'id': cur.lastrowid})


@inbox_ops_bp.route('/api/router/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_router_delete():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'success': False}), 403
    rid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    try:
        rid_i = int(rid)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный ID'}), 400
    with app_state_db._lock:
        with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM auto_router_rules WHERE id = ?", (rid_i,))
            conn.commit()
    return jsonify({'success': True})

def api_radar_updates():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify(get_update_radar(force=request.args.get('force', '0') == '1'))

# web/routes/insights_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from utils.config import cfg
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info
from core.torrent_parser import get_global_torrent_summaries
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.private_vault import get_hidden_hashes
from core.analytics_insights import (
    fetch_torrent_peers_safe, get_peer_clients_breakdown,
    get_tracker_analytics, get_heatmap_365,
    get_records_and_comparisons, detect_isp_throttling,
    get_cached_insights, set_cached_insights, invalidate_insights_cache
)
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from web.security import HASH_RE

insights_bp = Blueprint('insights_bp', __name__)


@insights_bp.route('/api/torrents/peers')
def api_torrents_peers():
    t_hash = str(request.args.get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(t_hash):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if g.get('role') != 'admin' and port:
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port)
        if t_hash.lower() in get_hidden_hashes(live):
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Доступ запрещён'}), 403
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'peers': fetch_torrent_peers_safe(ip, port, t_hash)})


@insights_bp.route('/api/torrents/chart')
def api_torrents_chart():
    t_hash = str(request.args.get('hash', '')).strip()
    period = str(request.args.get('period', '24h')).strip()
    if period not in ('24h', '7d', '30d'):
        period = '24h'
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(t_hash):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
        if t_hash.lower() in get_hidden_hashes(live):
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Доступ запрещён'}), 403
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'period': period, 'points': summary_tracker.get_torrent_chart_series(t_hash, period=period)})


@insights_bp.route('/api/analytics/recalculate_records', methods=['POST'])
def api_recalculate_records():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    invalidate_insights_cache()
    heatmap = get_heatmap_365(summary_tracker.db)
    records = get_records_and_comparisons(summary_tracker.db, heatmap_cells=heatmap, force_recalc=True)
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'records': records})


@insights_bp.route('/api/analytics/ignore_peak', methods=['POST'])
def api_ignore_peak_record():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    snap_id = data.get('snapshot_id')
    if not snap_id:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Нет ID снимка для удаления'}), 400
    ok = summary_tracker.db.set_snapshot_ignored(int(snap_id), ignored=True)
    if not ok:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Снимок не найден'}), 404
    app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Удалена аномалия пика', f'snapshot_id={snap_id}', actor='admin')
    invalidate_insights_cache()
    heatmap = get_heatmap_365(summary_tracker.db)
    records = get_records_and_comparisons(summary_tracker.db, heatmap_cells=heatmap, force_recalc=True)
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'records': records})


@insights_bp.route('/api/analytics/insights')
def api_analytics_insights():
    tracker_period = str(request.args.get('tracker_period', '7d')).strip()
    force_clients = request.args.get('force_clients', '0') == '1'
    role = g.get('role') or 'viewer'
    cache_key = (role, tracker_period)

    if not force_clients:
        cached = get_cached_insights(cache_key)
        if cached is not None:
            return jsonify(cached)

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()

    allowed_hashes = None
    if role != 'admin':
        hidden = get_hidden_hashes(live)
        if hidden:
            allowed_hashes = [
                (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower()
                for s in summaries
                if (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower() not in hidden
            ]

    heatmap = get_heatmap_365(summary_tracker.db)
    records = get_records_and_comparisons(summary_tracker.db, heatmap_cells=heatmap)
    clients = get_peer_clients_breakdown(ip, port, live, force=force_clients)
    trackers = get_tracker_analytics(summary_tracker.db, summaries, live=live, period=tracker_period, allowed_hashes=allowed_hashes)
    throttle = detect_isp_throttling(summary_tracker.db, ip, port)

    payload = {'heatmap': heatmap, 'records': records, 'clients': clients, 'trackers': trackers, 'throttle': throttle}
    set_cached_insights(cache_key, payload)
    return jsonify(payload)

# web/routes/net_ops_bp.py
import json
import datetime
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, Response, g
from utils.config import cfg
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.network_tools import (
    diagnose_bottleneck, ping_trackers_dns_tcp,
    check_network_interface_guard, read_sanitized_qb_log, reanimate_qb
)
from core.webhooks_bundler import get_webhook_deliveries, emit_webhook
from core.undo_presets import list_undo_stack, execute_undo, export_presets_json, import_presets_json

net_ops_bp = Blueprint('net_ops_bp', __name__)


def _not_admin():
    return g.get('role') != 'admin'


@net_ops_bp.route('/api/net/bottleneck')
def api_net_bottleneck():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify(diagnose_bottleneck(summary_tracker.db))


@net_ops_bp.route('/api/net/ping_trackers')
def api_net_ping_trackers():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify(ping_trackers_dns_tcp())


@net_ops_bp.route('/api/net/iface_guard', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_net_iface_guard():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    if request.method == 'GET':
        res = check_network_interface_guard()
        res['expected_cfg'] = cfg.get('Expected_Net_Interface', '')
        res['panic_on_drop'] = cfg.getboolean('Interface_Guard_Panic', False)
        return jsonify(res)
    d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    cfg.set('Interface_Guard_Enabled', str(bool(d.get('enabled', False))))
    cfg.set('Expected_Net_Interface', str(d.get('expected', '')).strip())
    cfg.set('Interface_Guard_Panic', str(bool(d.get('panic_on_drop', False))))
    cfg.save()
    return jsonify({'success': True})


@net_ops_bp.route('/api/qb/log')
def api_qb_log():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ok, txt = read_sanitized_qb_log(lines_count=80)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'log': txt if ok else '', 'error': None if ok else txt})


@net_ops_bp.route('/api/qb/reanimate', methods=['POST'])
def api_qb_reanimate():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    force = bool((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('force', False))
    ok, msg = reanimate_qb(force_kill=force)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@net_ops_bp.route('/api/webhooks', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_webhooks():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify({
            'urls': cfg.get('Outbound_Webhooks', ''),
            'bundler_enabled': cfg.getboolean('Alert_Bundler_Enabled', True),
            'deliveries': get_webhook_deliveries()
        })
    d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    cfg.set('Outbound_Webhooks', str(d.get('urls', '')).strip())
    cfg.set('Alert_Bundler_Enabled', str(bool(d.get('bundler_enabled', True))))
    cfg.save()
    if d.get('test'):
        emit_webhook('test_ping', {'message': 'tbr PRO webhook test'})
    return jsonify({'success': True})


@net_ops_bp.route('/api/undo/list')
def api_undo_list():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'items': []}), 403
    items = list_undo_stack()
    for it in items:
        it['date'] = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(it['created_at']).strftime('%H:%M:%S')
    return jsonify({'items': items})


@net_ops_bp.route('/api/undo/execute', methods=['POST'])
def api_undo_execute():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    uid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    if not uid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Нет ID'}), 400
    ok, msg = execute_undo(uid)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@net_ops_bp.route('/api/presets/export')
def api_presets_export():
    if _not_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return Response(
        json.dumps(export_presets_json(), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
        mimetype='application/json',
        headers={'Content-Disposition': 'attachment; filename=tbr_presets.json'}
    )


@net_ops_bp.route('/api/presets/import', methods=['POST'])
def api_presets_import():

    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)

# web/routes/profiles_bp.py
import datetime
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g, session
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from core.qb_api import get_local_ips
from core.sessions import sessions_mgr
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from web.security import get_client_ip

profiles_bp = Blueprint('profiles_bp', __name__)


@profiles_bp.route('/api/me')
def api_me():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    if not prof:
        return jsonify({'authenticated': False, 'role': 'viewer', 'ui_settings': profiles_db.get_ui_settings(0)})
    return jsonify({
        'authenticated': True, 'id': prof['id'], 'name': prof['name'],
        'login': prof.get('login', ''), 'role': prof['role'],
        'ui_settings': profiles_db.get_ui_settings(prof['id'])
    })


@profiles_bp.route('/api/auth/exchange', methods=['POST'])
def api_auth_exchange():
    ip = get_client_ip()
    if not sessions_mgr.check_rate_limit(ip, bucket='login'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Слишком много попыток. Подождите 10 минут.'}), 429

    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    login = str(data.get('login', '')).strip()
    password = str(data.get('password', '')).strip()
    prof = profiles_db.authenticate_login(login, password)
    if not prof:
        sessions_mgr.record_failure(ip, bucket='login')
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неверный логин или пароль'}), 401

    sessions_mgr.clear_failures(ip, bucket='login')
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'api_token': prof.get('api_token', ''), 'role': prof['role'], 'name': prof['name']})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/sessions')
def api_sessions_list():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    if not prof:
        return jsonify({'sessions': []}), 401
    cur_sid = session.get('sid', '')
    pid_filter = None if g.get('role') == 'admin' else prof['id']
    rows = sessions_mgr.list_active_sessions(current_sid=cur_sid, profile_id=pid_filter)
    for r in rows:
        r['last_seen_fmt'] = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(r['last_seen']).strftime('%d.%m %H:%M')
        r['created_fmt'] = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(r['created_at']).strftime('%d.%m %H:%M')
    return jsonify({'sessions': rows})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/sessions/revoke_others', methods=['POST'])
def api_sessions_revoke_others():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    if not prof:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не авторизован'}), 401
    cur_sid = session.get('sid', '')
    pid_filter = None if g.get('role') == 'admin' else prof['id']
    revoked_cnt = sessions_mgr.revoke_other_sessions(cur_sid, profile_id=pid_filter)
    app_state_db.add_audit_event('auth', 'Завершены прочие сессии', f'count={revoked_cnt}', actor=prof.get('login') or 'user')
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'revoked': revoked_cnt, 'message': f'Завершено сессий: {revoked_cnt}'})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/sessions/revoke', methods=['POST'])
def api_sessions_revoke_one():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    sid = str((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('sid', '')).strip()
    if not sid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан SID'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': sessions_mgr.revoke_session(sid)})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profile/ui', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_profile_ui():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    pid = prof['id'] if prof else 0
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify({'ui_settings': profiles_db.get_ui_settings(pid)})
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'ui_settings': profiles_db.save_ui_settings(pid, data)})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profile/password', methods=['POST'])
def api_self_password():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    if not prof:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не авторизован'}), 401
    new_pwd = str((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('password', '')).strip()
    _, err = profiles_db.change_password(prof['id'], new_password=new_pwd)
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/network/hosts')
def api_network_hosts():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify({'ips': get_local_ips()})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles')
def api_profiles_list():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify({'profiles': profiles_db.list_profiles()})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/create', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_create():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid, raw_pwd, api_tok, err = profiles_db.create_profile(
        data.get('name', ''), data.get('role', 'viewer'),
        login=data.get('login', ''), password=data.get('password', '')
    )
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err or 'Не удалось создать профиль'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'id': pid, 'password': raw_pwd, 'api_token': api_tok})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/change_password', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_change_password():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid = data.get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    new_pwd, err = profiles_db.change_password(pid, new_password=data.get('password', ''), new_login=data.get('login'))
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'password': new_pwd})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/invite', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_invite():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    pid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    inv = profiles_db.create_invite(pid)
    if not inv:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не удалось создать инвайт'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'invite_token': inv})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/reissue_api', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_reissue_api():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    pid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    tok = profiles_db.reissue_api_token(pid)
    if not tok:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Профиль не найден'}), 404
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'api_token': tok})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    pid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    ok, msg = profiles_db.delete_profile(pid)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg, 'error': None if ok else msg}), (200 if ok else 400)

# web/routes/safety_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from utils.config import cfg
from core.soft_trash import list_trash_items, restore_trash_item, purge_trash_item, cleanup_expired_trash

from core.env_doctor import run_environment_doctor, generate_github_issue_report, probe_onboarding_defaults
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db

safety_bp = Blueprint('safety_bp', __name__)


def _require_admin():
    return g.get('role') != 'admin'


@safety_bp.route('/api/trash')
def api_trash_list():
    if _require_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    cleanup_expired_trash()
    return jsonify({'items': list_trash_items(), 'ttl_days': cfg.getint('Trash_TTL_Days', 7)})


@safety_bp.route('/api/trash/restore', methods=['POST'])
def api_trash_restore():
    if _require_admin():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    item_id = str((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id', '')).strip()
    ok, msg = restore_trash_item(item_id)
    if ok:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Восстановление из корзины', msg, actor='admin')
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@safety_bp.route('/api/trash/purge', methods=['POST'])
def api_trash_purge():

        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    item_id = str((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id', '')).strip()
    ok, msg = purge_trash_item(item_id)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)

@safety_bp.route('/api/qb_vault/backup', methods=['POST'])

        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ok, res = create_config_backup(reason='manual')
    if ok:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Бэкап конфига qB', res.get('name', ''), actor='admin')
        return jsonify({'success': True, **res})
    return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': res}), 400


@safety_bp.route('/api/qb_vault/diff', methods=['POST'])
def api_qb_vault_diff():

        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    ok, out = diff_config_backups(d.get('snap_a', ''), d.get('snap_b', ''), d.get('filename', ''))
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'diff': out if ok else '', 'error': None if ok else out}), (200 if ok else 400)


@safety_bp.route('/api/qb_vault/restore', methods=['POST'])
def api_qb_vault_restore():
    if _require_admin():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    name = str((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('name', '')).strip()
    ok, msg = restore_config_backup(name)
    if ok:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Восстановление конфига qB', name, actor='admin')
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@safety_bp.route('/api/doctor/check')
def api_doctor_check():
    if _require_admin():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    diag = run_environment_doctor()
    return jsonify({'diag': diag, 'report_md': generate_github_issue_report(diag)})


@safety_bp.route('/api/onboarding/status')
def api_onboarding_status():
    if _require_admin():
        return jsonify({'completed': True})
    return jsonify(probe_onboarding_defaults())


@safety_bp.route('/api/onboarding/complete', methods=['POST'])
def api_onboarding_complete():
    if _require_admin():
        return jsonify({'success': False}), 403
    d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    if not d.get('skipped'):
        if d.get('qb_ip') is not None:
            cfg.set('WebUI_Address', str(d.get('qb_ip')).strip())
        if d.get('qb_port') is not None:
            cfg.set('WebUI_Port', str(d.get('qb_port')).strip())
        if d.get('qb_user'):
            cfg.set('WebUI_User', str(d.get('qb_user')).strip())
        if d.get('qb_password'):
            cfg.set('WebUI_Password', str(d.get('qb_password')))
        if d.get('max_channel_mbps'):
            cfg.set('Max_Channel_Mbps', str(max(10, min(100000, int(d.get('max_channel_mbps'))))))
        if d.get('monitored_disk') is not None:
            cfg.set('Monitored_Disk_Path', str(d.get('monitored_disk')).strip())
    cfg.set('Onboarding_Completed', 'True')
    cfg.save()
    return jsonify({'success': True})
