# web/routes/settings_bp.py
import os
import hmac
import datetime
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, render_template, redirect, session, current_app, Response, g

from utils.config import cfg
from core.version import APP_VERSION, GITHUB_RELEASES_URL
from core.qb_api import reset_qb_session, get_qb_executable_path, detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info
from core.autostart import is_autostart_enabled
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.scheduler import ALLOWED_INTERVALS, normalize_interval
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from core.sessions import sessions_mgr
from core.backup_manager import create_backup_zip, stage_restore_zip
from core.keep_awake import keep_awake
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.disk_monitor import list_active_disks
from core.analytics_insights import invalidate_insights_cache, get_records_and_comparisons
from web.security import get_csrf_token, is_allowed_qb_ip, get_client_ip

settings_bp = Blueprint('settings_bp', __name__)


def _start_user_session(prof, ip):
    session['authenticated'] = True
    session['profile_id'] = prof['id']
    session['sid'] = sessions_mgr.create_session(prof['id'], ip, request.headers.get('User-Agent', ''))
    sessions_mgr.clear_failures(ip, bucket='login')


@settings_bp.route('/invite/<token>', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def invite_accept(token):
    inv = profiles_db.get_valid_invite(token)
    if not inv:
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='Инвайт-ссылка недействительна, просрочена или уже использована.', invite=None), 410
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error=None, invite=inv)
    expected, sent = session.get('csrf_token'), request.form.get('csrf_token')
    if not (expected and sent and hmac.compare_digest(str(expected), str(sent))):
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='CSRF ошибка, обновите страницу.', invite=inv), 403
    prof, err = profiles_db.consume_invite(token, request.form.get('login', '').strip(), request.form.get('password', '').strip())
    if err or not prof:
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error=err or 'Ошибка активации', invite=inv), 400
    _start_user_session(prof, get_client_ip())
    app_state_db.add_audit_event('auth', 'Активация инвайта', f"Профиль {prof['login']}", actor=prof['login'])
    return redirect('/')


@settings_bp.route('/login', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def login():
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return render_template('login.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error=None)
    ip = get_client_ip()
    if not sessions_mgr.check_rate_limit(ip, bucket='login'):
        return render_template('login.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='Слишком много попыток входа. Подождите 10 минут.'), 429

    login_val, pwd = request.form.get('login', '').strip(), request.form.get('password', '').strip()
    real_pwd = cfg.get('Web_Password', '')
    if real_pwd and hmac.compare_digest(pwd, real_pwd) and (not login_val or login_val.lower() == 'admin'):
        adm = profiles_db.get_default_admin()
        if adm:
            _start_user_session(adm, ip)
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('auth', 'Успешный вход', f'IP: {ip}', actor='admin')
        return redirect('/')

    prof = profiles_db.authenticate_login(login_val, pwd) if login_val else profiles_db.authenticate_api_token(pwd)
    if prof:
        _start_user_session(prof, ip)
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('auth', 'Успешный вход', f'IP: {ip}', actor=prof.get('login') or prof['name'])
        return redirect('/')

    sessions_mgr.record_failure(ip, bucket='login')
    app_state_db.add_audit_event('auth', 'Неудачный вход', f"IP: {ip}, login={login_val or '-'}", actor=login_val or 'guest')
    return render_template('login.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='Неверный логин или пароль!'), 401


@settings_bp.route('/logout', methods=['POST', 'GET'])
def logout():
    sid = session.get('sid')
    if sid:
        sessions_mgr.revoke_session(sid)
    session.pop('authenticated', None)
    session.pop('profile_id', None)
    session.pop('sid', None)
    return redirect('/login')


@settings_bp.route('/')
def index():
    return render_template(
        'index.html',
        csrf_token=get_csrf_token(),
        auth_enabled=current_app.config.get('IS_EXPOSED', False),
        v=current_app.config.get('BUILD_VER', '1'),
        app_version=APP_VERSION,
        releases_url=GITHUB_RELEASES_URL
    )


@settings_bp.route('/api/backup/export')
def api_backup_export():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ex_hist = request.args.get('exclude_history', '0') == '1'
    strip_sec = request.args.get('strip_secrets', '0') == '1'
    zip_bytes = create_backup_zip(exclude_history=ex_hist, strip_secrets=strip_sec)
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
    return Response(zip_bytes, mimetype='application/zip', headers={'Content-Disposition': f'attachment; filename=tbr_backup_{ts}.zip'})


@settings_bp.route('/api/backup/restore', methods=['POST'])
def api_backup_restore():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    f = request.files.get('file')
    if not f:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Файл не передан'}), 400
    ok, msg = stage_restore_zip(f.read())
    if ok:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Восстановление бэкапа', msg, actor='admin')
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@settings_bp.route('/api/settings', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_settings():
    if request.method == 'GET':
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
        return jsonify({
            'web_port': cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000), 'web_host': cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1'),
            'has_password': bool(cfg.get('Web_Password', '').strip()),
            'qb_ip': cfg.get('WebUI_Address', ''), 'qb_port': cfg.get('WebUI_Port', ''),
            'qb_user': cfg.get('WebUI_User', 'admin'), 'has_qb_password': bool(cfg.get('WebUI_Password', '').strip()),
            'w1': cfg.getint('Weight_W1', 70), 'w2': cfg.getint('Weight_W2', 30), 'w3': cfg.getint('Weight_W3', 0),
            'snapshot_interval': normalize_interval(cfg.getint('Snapshot_Interval_Min', 60)),
            'autostart': is_autostart_enabled(), 'is_windows': (os.name == 'nt'),
            'keep_awake_enabled': cfg.getboolean('KeepAwake_Enabled', False),
            'keep_awake_min_kb': cfg.getint('KeepAwake_Min_KB', 500),
            'keep_awake_display': cfg.getboolean('KeepAwake_Display', False),
            'auto_recheck_enabled': cfg.getboolean('AutoRecheck_Enabled', False),
            'watchdog_enabled': cfg.getboolean('Watchdog_Enabled', False),
            'watchdog_timeout_min': cfg.getint('Watchdog_Timeout_Min', 3),
            'watchdog_exe_path': cfg.get('Watchdog_Exe_Path', ''),
            'watchdog_exe_detected': get_qb_executable_path() if os.name == 'nt' else '',
            'manifest_weekly': cfg.getboolean('Manifest_Weekly', False),
            'max_channel_mbps': cfg.getint('Max_Channel_Mbps', 1000),
            'monitored_disk': cfg.get('Monitored_Disk_Path', ''),
            'disks': list_active_disks(live)
        })

    data = request.get_json(silent=True)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные данные'}), 400

    old_p, old_h = cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000), cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1').strip() or '127.0.0.1'
    try:
        new_p = int(data.get('web_port', old_p))
        if not (1 <= new_p <= 65535):
            raise ValueError
        qb_ip = str(data.get('qb_ip', '')).strip()
        if not is_allowed_qb_ip(qb_ip):
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Недопустимый IP qBittorrent'}), 400
        qb_port_raw = str(data.get('qb_port', '')).strip()
        if qb_port_raw and not (1 <= int(qb_port_raw) <= 65535):
            raise ValueError
        w1, w2, w3 = int(data.get('w1', 70)), int(data.get('w2', 30)), int(data.get('w3', 0))
        snap_iv = int(data.get('snapshot_interval', 60))
        if w1 < 0 or w2 < 0 or w3 < 0 or (w1 + w2 + w3) <= 0 or snap_iv not in ALLOWED_INTERVALS:
            raise ValueError
        ka_min_kb = max(0, min(1000000, int(data.get('keep_awake_min_kb', 500))))
        wd_timeout = max(2, min(10, int(data.get('watchdog_timeout_min', 3))))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные числовые параметры'}), 400

    new_host = str(data.get('web_host', '127.0.0.1')).strip() or '127.0.0.1'
    new_pass = str(data.get('web_password', '')).strip()
    if new_host not in ('127.0.0.1', 'localhost') and not (new_pass or cfg.get('Web_Password', '').strip()):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Для внешнего хоста обязательно задайте пароль!'}), 400

    wd_exe = str(data.get('watchdog_exe_path', '')).strip()
    if wd_exe and ('..' in wd_exe or (os.name == 'nt' and not wd_exe.lower().endswith('.exe'))):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный путь к исполняемому файлу'}), 400

    old_snap_iv = normalize_interval(cfg.getint('Snapshot_Interval_Min', 60))
    old_mbps = cfg.getint('Max_Channel_Mbps', 1000)

    cfg.set('Web_Port', new_p); cfg.set('Web_Host', new_host)
    if new_pass:
        cfg.set('Web_Password', new_pass)
    cfg.set('WebUI_Address', qb_ip); cfg.set('WebUI_Port', qb_port_raw)
    cfg.set('WebUI_User', str(data.get('qb_user', 'admin')).strip() or 'admin')
    if str(data.get('qb_password', '')):
        cfg.set('WebUI_Password', str(data.get('qb_password', '')))
    cfg.set('Weight_W1', w1); cfg.set('Weight_W2', w2); cfg.set('Weight_W3', w3)
    cfg.set('Snapshot_Interval_Min', snap_iv)
    cfg.set('KeepAwake_Enabled', str(bool(data.get('keep_awake_enabled', False))))
    cfg.set('KeepAwake_Min_KB', str(ka_min_kb))
    cfg.set('KeepAwake_Display', str(bool(data.get('keep_awake_display', False))))
    cfg.set('Watchdog_Enabled', str(bool(data.get('watchdog_enabled', False))))
    cfg.set('Watchdog_Timeout_Min', str(wd_timeout))
    cfg.set('Watchdog_Exe_Path', wd_exe)
    if 'auto_recheck_enabled' in data:
        cfg.set('AutoRecheck_Enabled', str(bool(data.get('auto_recheck_enabled'))))
    if 'manifest_weekly' in data:
        cfg.set('Manifest_Weekly', str(bool(data.get('manifest_weekly'))))
    if 'max_channel_mbps' in data:
        cfg.set('Max_Channel_Mbps', str(max(10, min(100000, int(data.get('max_channel_mbps', 1000))))))
    if 'monitored_disk' in data:
        cfg.set('Monitored_Disk_Path', str(data.get('monitored_disk', '')).strip())
    cfg.save()

    if not cfg.getboolean('KeepAwake_Enabled', False):
        keep_awake.release()
    reset_qb_session()
    if old_snap_iv != snap_iv:
        summary_tracker.notify_interval_changed()
    if cfg.getint('Max_Channel_Mbps', 1000) != old_mbps:
        invalidate_insights_cache()
        get_records_and_comparisons(summary_tracker.db, force_recalc=True)

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Изменение настроек', f'host={new_host}:{new_p}', actor='admin')
    net_changed = (old_p != new_p) or (old_h != new_host)
    if net_changed:
        ws = current_app.config.get('WEB_SERVER')
        if ws:
            ws.restart(new_host, new_p)

    return jsonify({'success': True, 'port_changed': net_changed, 'new_port': new_p, 'message': 'Настройки сохранены! Веб-сервер перезапущен.' if net_changed else 'Настройки сохранены!'})

# web/routes/social_bp.py
import datetime
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.achievements import get_achievements_view

social_bp = Blueprint('social_bp', __name__)
_VALID_AUDIT_CATS = {'', 'auth', 'automation', 'merger', 'system'}


@social_bp.route('/api/tools/audit')
def api_tools_audit():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    cat = str(request.args.get('category', '')).strip().lower()
    if cat not in _VALID_AUDIT_CATS:
        cat = ''
    limit = max(10, min(200, request.args.get('limit', 100, type=int)))
    rows = app_state_db.get_audit_events(category=cat, limit=limit)
    for r in rows:
        ts = float(r.get('created_at') or 0)
        r['datetime'] = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(ts).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S') if ts else '—'
    return jsonify({'items': rows})


@social_bp.route('/api/achievements')
def api_achievements():
    return jsonify(get_achievements_view())

# web/routes/system_bp.py
import urllib.parse
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, Response, g

from utils.config import cfg
from core.version import APP_VERSION, GITHUB_RELEASES_URL
from core.updater import check_for_updates, download_and_apply_update, is_frozen_windows
from core.disaster_recovery import (
    execute_panic_killswitch, create_disaster_manifest,
    list_manifests, get_manifest_path
)

system_bp = Blueprint('system_bp', __name__)


def _actor_name():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    return (prof.get('login') or prof.get('name') or 'admin') if prof else 'admin'


@system_bp.route('/api/version')
@system_bp.route('/api/status')
def api_version():
    return jsonify({
        'name': 'tbr PRO',
        'version': APP_VERSION,
        'releases_url': GITHUB_RELEASES_URL,
        'can_auto_update': is_frozen_windows(),
        'autopilot_frozen_until': cfg.getint('Autopilot_Frozen_Until', 0),
        'role': g.get('role') or 'viewer'
    })


@system_bp.route('/api/update/check')
def api_update_check():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    force = request.args.get('force', '0') == '1'
    return jsonify(check_for_updates(force=force))


@system_bp.route('/api/update/apply', methods=['POST'])
def api_update_apply():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ok, msg = download_and_apply_update(actor=_actor_name())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@system_bp.route('/api/panic', methods=['POST'])
def api_panic_killswitch():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    kill_qb = bool(data.get('kill_qb', False))
    res = execute_panic_killswitch(kill_qb=kill_qb, actor=_actor_name())
    return jsonify(res)


@system_bp.route('/api/manifest/list')
def api_manifest_list():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify({
        'manifests': list_manifests(),
        'weekly': cfg.getboolean('Manifest_Weekly', False)
    })


@system_bp.route('/api/manifest/weekly', methods=['POST'])
def api_manifest_weekly():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    weekly = bool(data.get('weekly', False))
    cfg.set('Manifest_Weekly', str(weekly))
    cfg.save()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'weekly': weekly})


@system_bp.route('/api/manifest/create', methods=['POST'])
def api_manifest_create():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    res = create_disaster_manifest(actor=_actor_name())
    return jsonify({'success': True, **res})


@system_bp.route('/api/manifest/download')
def api_manifest_download():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return "Только для Admin", 403
    fname = request.args.get('file', '')
    fpath = get_manifest_path(fname)
    if not fpath:
        return "Файл не найден", 404
    with open(fpath, 'rb') as f:
        data = f.read()
    clean_name = urllib.parse.quote(fname)
    return Response(
        data,
        mimetype='application/json',
        headers={'Content-Disposition': f"attachment; filename*=UTF-8''{clean_name}"}
    )

# web/routes/timers_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from utils.config import cfg
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info
from core.private_vault import get_hidden_hashes
from core.smart_timers import (
    get_freeze_status, freeze_active_torrents, unfreeze_torrents,
    get_snooze_status, start_snooze, cancel_snooze,
    list_expirations, set_expiration, remove_expiration, _INACTIVE_STATES
)
from core.smart_tagging import (
    list_tagging_rules, add_tagging_rule, toggle_tagging_rule, delete_tagging_rule
)
from core.torrent_targets import list_targets, set_target, delete_target
from core.dnd_digest import get_dnd_settings, get_digest_settings
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from web.security import HASH_RE

timers_bp = Blueprint('timers_bp', __name__)


def _actor():
    p = g.get('profile')
    return (p.get('login') or p.get('name') or 'admin') if p else 'admin'


@timers_bp.route('/api/freeze/status')
def api_freeze_status():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'frozen': False, 'count': 0, 'active_count': 0}), 403
    st = get_freeze_status()
    active_cnt = 0
    if not st.get('frozen'):
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
        active_cnt = sum(1 for info in live.values() if str(info.get('state', '')).lower() not in _INACTIVE_STATES)
    st['active_count'] = active_cnt
    return jsonify(st)


@timers_bp.route('/api/freeze/toggle', methods=['POST'])
def api_freeze_toggle():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    st = get_freeze_status()
    if st.get('frozen'):
        ok, msg, cnt = unfreeze_torrents(actor=_actor())
        return jsonify({'success': ok, 'frozen': False, 'count': cnt, 'message': msg})
    ok, msg, cnt = freeze_active_torrents(actor=_actor())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'frozen': ok, 'count': cnt, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@timers_bp.route('/api/snooze/status')
def api_snooze_status():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'active': False}), 403
    return jsonify(get_snooze_status())


@timers_bp.route('/api/snooze/start', methods=['POST'])
def api_snooze_start():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    try:
        mins = int(data.get('minutes') or 30)
        if not (1 <= mins <= 1440):
            raise ValueError
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Укажите от 1 до 1440 минут'}), 400
    mode = str(data.get('mode', 'pause')).strip()
    ok, msg = start_snooze(mins, mode=mode, actor=_actor())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@timers_bp.route('/api/snooze/cancel', methods=['POST'])
def api_snooze_cancel():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ok, msg = cancel_snooze(actor=_actor(), auto_triggered=False)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg})


@timers_bp.route('/api/expirations')
def api_expirations_list():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'items': []}), 403
    return jsonify({'items': list_expirations()})


@timers_bp.route('/api/expirations/set', methods=['POST'])
def api_expirations_set():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    h = str(data.get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    try:
        hours = float(data.get('hours') or 0)
        target_bytes = int(data.get('target_uploaded_bytes') or 0)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные числовые значения'}), 400
    delete_files = bool(data.get('delete_files', False))
    ok, msg = set_expiration(h, hours=hours, target_up_bytes=target_bytes, delete_files=delete_files, actor=_actor())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@timers_bp.route('/api/expirations/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_expirations_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    h = str((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': remove_expiration(h)})


@timers_bp.route('/api/targets')
def api_targets_list():
    targets = list_targets()
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
        hidden = get_hidden_hashes(live)
        if hidden:
            targets = {k: v for k, v in targets.items() if k.lower() not in hidden}
    return jsonify({'targets': targets})


@timers_bp.route('/api/targets/set', methods=['POST'])
def api_targets_set():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    h = str(data.get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    try:
        tr = float(data.get('target_ratio') or 0)
        tb = int(data.get('target_bytes') or 0)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные числовые значения'}), 400
    ok, msg = set_target(h, target_ratio=tr, target_bytes=tb, actor=_actor())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@timers_bp.route('/api/targets/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_targets_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    h = str((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': delete_target(h)})

    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    rid = add_tagging_rule(
        data.get('name', ''), data.get('match_field', 'tracker'),
        data.get('pattern', ''), data.get('target_tag', '')
    )
    if not rid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Укажите паттерн и целевой тег'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'id': rid})

@timers_bp.route('/api/notifications/schedule', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_notifications_schedule():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify({'dnd': get_dnd_settings(), 'digest': get_digest_settings()})

    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    dnd, dig = data.get('dnd') or {}, data.get('digest') or {}

    cfg.set('DND_Enabled', str(bool(dnd.get('enabled', False))))
    cfg.set('DND_Start', str(dnd.get('start', '23:00'))[:5] or '23:00')
    cfg.set('DND_End', str(dnd.get('end', '08:00'))[:5] or '08:00')
    days = dnd.get('days') if isinstance(dnd.get('days'), list) else [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]
    cfg.set('DND_Days', ','.join(str(int(d)) for d in days if str(d).isdigit() and 0 <= int(d) <= 6))

    d_mode = str(dig.get('mode', 'off'))
    if d_mode not in ('off', 'daily', 'weekly'):
        d_mode = 'off'
    cfg.set('Digest_Mode', d_mode)
    cfg.set('Digest_Hour', str(max(0, min(23, int(dig.get('hour', 21) or 21)))))
    cfg.set('Digest_Weekday', str(max(0, min(6, int(dig.get('weekday', 6) or 6)))))
    cfg.save()

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', 'Расписание DND/Дайджеста', f'dnd={dnd.get("enabled")}, digest={d_mode}', actor=_actor())
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Расписание DND и дайджеста сохранено!'})

# web/routes/tools_bp.py
import os
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from core.orphan_scanner import get_download_roots, scan_orphan_entries, delete_orphan_entries
from core.private_vault import get_vault_settings, save_vault_settings
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db

tools_bp = Blueprint('tools_bp', __name__)


def _actor_name():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    return (prof.get('login') or prof.get('name') or 'admin') if prof else 'admin'


def _sanitize_custom_roots(raw_roots):
    if raw_roots is None:
        return None, None
    if not isinstance(raw_roots, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in raw_roots):
        return None, 'Ожидался список строковых путей'
    clean = []
    for r in raw_roots:
        ap = os.path.abspath(r.strip())
        if not ap or os.path.dirname(ap) == ap:
            return None, f'Запрещено указывать корень диска/ФС: {r}'
        clean.append(ap)
    return clean, None


@tools_bp.route('/api/tools/orphans/roots')
def api_orphans_roots():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify({'roots': get_download_roots()})


@tools_bp.route('/api/tools/orphans/scan', methods=['POST'])
def api_orphans_scan():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    custom_roots, err = _sanitize_custom_roots(data.get('roots'))
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    res = scan_orphan_entries(custom_roots=custom_roots)
    return jsonify({'success': True, **res})


@tools_bp.route('/api/tools/orphans/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_orphans_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    paths = data.get('paths')
    confirmed = bool(data.get('confirmed', False))
    use_trash = bool(data.get('use_trash', True))
    if not confirmed or not isinstance(paths, list) or not paths:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Требуется явное подтверждение и непустой список путей'}), 400
    custom_roots, err = _sanitize_custom_roots(data.get('custom_roots'))
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    ok, deleted, errors = delete_orphan_entries(paths, actor=_actor_name(), use_trash=use_trash, custom_roots=custom_roots)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'deleted': deleted, 'errors': errors, 'use_trash': use_trash}), (200 if deleted or ok else 400)


@tools_bp.route('/api/vault/settings', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_vault_settings():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify(get_vault_settings())
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    saved = save_vault_settings(data.get('tags', ''), data.get('aggregate_mode', 'exclude'))
    app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Private Vault обновлён', f"tags={','.join(saved['tags'])}", actor=_actor_name())
    return jsonify({'success': True, **saved})

# web/routes/torrents_bp.py
import time
import urllib.parse
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g

from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents, force_clear_cache
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, send_qb_command,
    add_torrent_tags, remove_torrent_tags, delete_torrents,
    is_qb_running, close_qbittorrent, launch_qbittorrent, scan_dead_torrents
)
from core.autostart import set_autostart, is_autostart_enabled
from core.fastresume_merger import (
    run_fastresume_merge, execute_source_deletion,
    VALID_DELETE_MODES, VALID_MERGE_MODES
)
from core.torrent_diff import compute_torrent_diff, find_smart_merge_candidates
from core.private_vault import get_hidden_hashes, filter_torrents_for_role
from core.torrent_targets import list_targets
from core.health_score import enrich_live_metrics
from core.tracker_inspector import inspect_tracker_errors
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.tracker_replacer import push_undo_record
from core.lineage_notes_events import record_torrent_event, push_activity
from web.security import HASH_RE

torrents_bp = Blueprint('torrents_bp', __name__)


def _build_torrents_list(ip, port):
    data_dict = get_all_torrents(use_cache=True)
    live_info = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    targets = list_targets()

    result = []
    for t_id, static in data_dict.items():
        live = live_info.get(t_id) or live_info.get(t_id.lower()) or live_info.get(t_id.upper()) or {}
        size = live.get('total_size', static.get('size', 1))
        uploaded = live.get('uploaded', static.get('uploaded', 0))
        ratio = round(uploaded / size, 4) if size > 0 else 0.0
        state = live.get('state', 'seeding' if static.get('active_seconds', 0) > 0 else 'unknown')
        save_path = live.get('save_path', static.get('save_path', ''))
        content_path = live.get('content_path', '')
        tags = str(live.get('tags', '') or '')
        added_on = int(live.get('added_on') or static.get('created_ts') or 0)

        tracker_host = ''
        tr_url = str(live.get('tracker', '') or '')
        if not tr_url and static.get('trackers'):
            tr_url = str(static['trackers'][0] or '')
        if tr_url:
            tracker_host = urllib.parse.urlparse(tr_url).netloc.lower()

        name = static.get('name', '')
        if not name or name.lower() == t_id.lower() or len(name) in (40, 64):
            if live.get('name'):
                name = live['name']
        if not name:
            name = t_id

        item = {
            'hash': t_id, 'name': name, 'size': size, 'size_fmt': format_file_size(size),
            'uploaded': uploaded, 'uploaded_fmt': format_file_size(uploaded),
            'ratio': ratio, 'state': state, 'save_path': save_path, 'content_path': content_path,
            'tags': tags, 'tracker': tracker_host, 'added_on': added_on,
            'active_seconds': static.get('active_seconds', 0)
        }
        enrich_live_metrics(item, live, targets.get(t_id.lower()))
        result.append(item)
    result.sort(key=lambda x: str(x['name']).lower())
    return result, live_info

    if g.get('role') != 'admin':

    res = scan_dead_torrents(ip, port)

@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/action', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrent_action():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    action = data.get('action')
    t_hash = data.get('hash', '')
    tag = str(data.get('tag', '')).strip()
    if action not in ('stop', 'start', 'setForceStart', 'addTags', 'removeTags'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неизвестная команда'}), 400

    hashes = [h.strip() for h in str(t_hash).split('|') if h.strip()]
    if not hashes or not all(HASH_RE.fullmatch(h) for h in hashes):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные хэши'}), 400

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен'}), 400

    joined = '|'.join(hashes)
    if action in ('addTags', 'removeTags'):
        ok = add_torrent_tags(ip, port, joined, tag) if action == 'addTags' else remove_torrent_tags(ip, port, joined, tag)
        if ok:
            lbl = f"{'+Тег' if action == 'addTags' else '-Тег'} '{tag}' ({len(hashes)} шт.)"
            push_undo_record('tags_bulk', lbl, {'action': action, 'hashes': joined, 'tag': tag})
            if len(hashes) == 1:
                record_torrent_event(hashes[0], 'tag', lbl)
            else:
                push_activity('tag', lbl)
        return jsonify({'success': ok})

    ok = send_qb_command(ip, port, action, joined)
    if ok:
        if action in ('stop', 'start'):
            lbl = f"{'Пауза' if action == 'stop' else 'Пуск'} ({len(hashes)} шт.)"
            if len(hashes) > 1:
                push_undo_record('pause_start_bulk', lbl, {'action': action, 'hashes': joined})
                push_activity('control', lbl)
            else:
                record_torrent_event(hashes[0], 'control', 'Поставлено на паузу' if action == 'stop' else 'Возобновлено')
        elif action == 'setForceStart' and len(hashes) == 1:
            record_torrent_event(hashes[0], 'control', 'Включён форсированный старт')
    return jsonify({'success': ok})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrents_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    t_hash = str(data.get('hash', '')).strip()
    delete_files = bool(data.get('delete_files', False))
    hashes = [h.strip() for h in t_hash.split('|') if h.strip()]
    if not hashes or not all(HASH_RE.fullmatch(h) for h in hashes):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные хэши'}), 400
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен'}), 400
    ok = delete_torrents(ip, port, '|'.join(hashes), delete_files=delete_files)
    if ok:
        force_clear_cache()
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Удаление торрента', f'count={len(hashes)}, files={delete_files}', actor='admin')
        push_activity('delete', f"Удалено раздач: {len(hashes)} (файлы={delete_files})", t_hash=hashes[0] if len(hashes) == 1 else '')
    return jsonify({'success': ok})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/action_all', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrents_action_all():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    action = data.get('action', 'stop')
    if action not in ('stop', 'start'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неизвестная команда'}), 400
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent не запущен'}), 400
    ok = send_qb_command(ip, port, action, 'all')
    msg = "Все раздачи поставлены на паузу" if action == 'stop' else "Все раздачи возобновлены"
    if ok:
        push_activity('control', msg)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/merger/diff')
def api_merger_diff():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    old_hash = str(request.args.get('old_hash', '')).strip()
    new_hash = str(request.args.get('new_hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(old_hash) or not HASH_RE.fullmatch(new_hash) or old_hash.lower() == new_hash.lower():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Укажите два разных корректных хэша'}), 400
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    return jsonify(compute_torrent_diff(ip, port, old_hash, new_hash))

@torrents_bp.route('/api/merger/check_pair')
def api_merger_check_pair():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    old_h = str(request.args.get('old_hash', '')).strip().lower()
    new_h = str(request.args.get('new_hash', '')).strip().lower()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(old_h) or not HASH_RE.fullmatch(new_h) or old_h == new_h:
        return jsonify({'has_history': False})
    hist = app_state_db.get_merger_history(old_h, new_h)
    return jsonify({
        'has_history': len(hist) > 0,
        'count': len(hist),
        'last_merged_at': hist[0]['created_at'] if hist else None
    })


@torrents_bp.route('/api/merger/run', methods=['POST'])
def api_merger_run():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    old_hash = str(data.get('old_hash', '')).strip()
    new_hash = str(data.get('new_hash', '')).strip()
    delete_mode = str(data.get('delete_mode', 'none')).strip()
    merge_mode = str(data.get('merge_mode', 'add')).strip()
    force_dup = bool(data.get('force_duplicate', False))

    if delete_mode not in VALID_DELETE_MODES:
        delete_mode = 'none'
    if merge_mode not in VALID_MERGE_MODES:
        merge_mode = 'add'

    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(old_hash) or not HASH_RE.fullmatch(new_hash) or old_hash.lower() == new_hash.lower():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'log': ['❌ Некорректные или одинаковые хэши торрентов!']}), 400

    ok, log, confirm_info, code, extra = run_fastresume_merge(
        old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode=delete_mode, merge_mode=merge_mode, force_duplicate=force_dup
    )

    if code == 409 and extra and extra.get('is_duplicate'):
        return jsonify({
            'success': False,
            'needs_duplicate_confirm': True,
            'warning': extra.get('warning'),
            'log': log
        }), 409

    if ok:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event(
            'merger', f'Перенос .fastresume ({merge_mode})',
            f'{old_hash[:8]} -> {new_hash[:8]} (del={delete_mode})', actor='admin'
        )

    return jsonify({
        'success': ok, 'log': log, 'needs_delete_confirm': bool(ok and confirm_info),
        'confirm_info': confirm_info, 'calc_info': extra
    }), code


@torrents_bp.route('/api/merger/delete_source', methods=['POST'])
def api_merger_delete_source():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    old_hash, new_hash = str(data.get('old_hash', '')).strip(), str(data.get('new_hash', '')).strip()
    delete_mode, confirmed = str(data.get('delete_mode', '')).strip(), bool(data.get('confirmed', False))

    if not confirmed or delete_mode not in ('safe', 'force'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'log': ['❌ Удаление не подтверждено!']}), 400
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(old_hash) or not HASH_RE.fullmatch(new_hash) or old_hash.lower() == new_hash.lower():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'log': ['❌ Некорректные или одинаковые хэши!']}), 400

    ok, log = execute_source_deletion(old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode)
    if ok:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('merger', 'Удалён исходник', f'{old_hash[:8]} (mode={delete_mode})', actor='admin')
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'log': log}), (200 if ok else 400)

FILE: web/routes/ux_bp.py

# web/routes/ux_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from utils.config import cfg
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, set_torrent_file_prio
from core.torrent_parser import get_global_torrent_summaries
from core.private_vault import get_hidden_hashes
from core.lineage_notes_events import (
    get_torrent_timeline, get_torrent_note, set_torrent_note,
    list_all_notes, get_activity_feed, resolve_uid
)
from core.file_fts_index import reindex_torrent_files, search_files_fts, build_treemap_data, get_torrent_files_paginated
from web.security import HASH_RE

ux_bp = Blueprint('ux_bp', __name__)
_VALID_FILE_PRIOS = {0, 1, 6, 7}


def _hidden_for_role():
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    hidden = get_hidden_hashes(live)
    return ip, port, live, (hidden if g.get('role') != 'admin' else set())


def _find_live_ci(live, t_hash):
    hl = str(t_hash or '').lower()
    return next((v for k, v in (live or {}).items() if k.lower() == hl), {})


@ux_bp.route('/api/torrent/timeline')

    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'events': []}), 400
    _, _, _, hidden = _hidden_for_role()
    if h.lower() in hidden:
        return jsonify({'events': []}), 403
    return jsonify({'events': get_torrent_timeline(h)})


@ux_bp.route('/api/torrent/notes', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_torrent_notes():
    h = str(request.args.get('hash', '') or (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    _, _, live, hidden = _hidden_for_role()
    if h.lower() in hidden:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Доступ запрещён'}), 403
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify({'note': get_torrent_note(h)})
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    nm = _find_live_ci(live, h).get('name', '')
    ok = set_torrent_note(h, d.get('note', ''), last_name=nm)
    return jsonify({'success': ok})

        return jsonify({'files': []}), 400
    ip, port, _, hidden = _hidden_for_role()
    if h.lower() in hidden:
        return jsonify({'files': []}), 403
    page = request.args.get('page', 1, type=int)
    return jsonify(get_torrent_files_paginated(ip, port, h, page=page, per_page=100))


@ux_bp.route('/api/torrents/file_prio', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrents_file_prio():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    d = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    h = str(d.get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    try:
        fid = int(d.get('id', 0))
        prio = int(d.get('priority', 1))
        if fid < 0 or prio not in _VALID_FILE_PRIOS:
            raise ValueError
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные id или priority'}), 400
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    ok = set_torrent_file_prio(ip, port, h, fid, prio)
    return jsonify({'success': ok})

# web/routes/widget_bp.py
import io
import json
from PIL import Image
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, Response, g
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from core.widget_service import get_widget_summary, render_widget_chart_png, get_pwa_icon_png

widget_bp = Blueprint('widget_bp', __name__)
_FAVICON_CACHE = None


@widget_bp.route('/favicon.ico')
def favicon_ico():
    global _FAVICON_CACHE
    if _FAVICON_CACHE is None:
        img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(get_pwa_icon_png(64)))
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        img.save(buf, format='ICO', sizes=[(32, 32), (64, 64)])
        _FAVICON_CACHE = buf.getvalue()
    return Response(_FAVICON_CACHE, mimetype='image/x-icon', headers={'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=86400'})


@widget_bp.route('/manifest.json')
def pwa_manifest():
    manifest = {
        "name": "tbr PRO — qBittorrent",
        "short_name": "tbr PRO",
        "start_url": "/",
        "display": "standalone",
        "background_color": "#121214",
        "theme_color": "#1a1a20",
        "icons": [
            {"src": "/icon-192.png", "sizes": "192x192", "type": "image/png", "purpose": "any maskable"},
            {"src": "/icon-512.png", "sizes": "512x512", "type": "image/png", "purpose": "any maskable"}
        ]
    }
    return Response(json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False), mimetype='application/manifest+json', headers={'Cache-Control': 'no-store'})

# web/security.py
import os
import re
import hmac
import secrets
import ipaddress
import urllib.parse
from flask import request, jsonify, redirect, session, g
from utils.helpers import LOG_FOLDER
from core.summary_tracker import AUTO_FOLDER, MANUAL_FOLDER
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from core.sessions import sessions_mgr

HASH_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-fA-F]{40}(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{24})?$')

_VIEWER_BLOCKED_GET_PREFIXES = ('/api/settings', '/api/automation', '/api/logs', '/api/profiles', '/api/network', '/api/doctor', '/api/vault')
_PUBLIC_ASSET_PATHS = ('/favicon.ico', '/manifest.json', '/icon-192.png', '/icon-512.png', '/apple-touch-icon.png')


def check_rate_limit(ip, bucket='login'):
    return sessions_mgr.check_rate_limit(ip, bucket=bucket)


def record_failed_login(ip, bucket='login'):
    sessions_mgr.record_failure(ip, bucket=bucket)


def _is_trusted_proxy_ip(ip_str):
    s = str(ip_str or '').strip()
    if s in ('127.0.0.1', '::1'):
        return True
    try:
        obj = ipaddress.ip_address(s)
        return obj.is_loopback or obj.is_private
    except ValueError:
        return False


def get_client_ip():
    """Защита от спуфинга X-Forwarded-For: доверяем прокси-заголовку только если remote_addr — локальный/доверенный прокси."""
    remote = (request.remote_addr or '127.0.0.1').strip()
    if _is_trusted_proxy_ip(remote):
        fwd = (request.headers.get('X-Forwarded-For') or request.headers.get('X-Real-IP') or '').split(',')[0].strip()
        if fwd:
            return fwd
    return remote


def get_csrf_token():
    if 'csrf_token' not in session:
        session['csrf_token'] = secrets.token_hex(32)
    return session['csrf_token']


def is_allowed_qb_ip(ip_str):
    s = (ip_str or '').strip().lower()
    if s in ('', 'localhost'):
        return True
    try:
        ip_obj = ipaddress.ip_address(s)
        return ip_obj.is_loopback or ip_obj.is_private or (ip_obj in ipaddress.ip_network('100.64.0.0/10'))
    except ValueError:
        return False


def resolve_log_path(filename):
    if not filename or not isinstance(filename, str):
        return None
    clean = os.path.basename(filename.replace('\\', '/'))
    for directory in (LOG_FOLDER, AUTO_FOLDER, MANUAL_FOLDER):
        try:
            norm_dir = os.path.abspath(directory)
            full_path = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(norm_dir, clean))
            if os.path.commonpath([norm_dir, full_path]) == norm_dir and os.path.isfile(full_path):
                return full_path
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return None


def _resolve_request_profile(is_exposed):
    ip = get_client_ip()
    auth_hdr = request.headers.get('Authorization', '').strip()
    token = auth_hdr[7:].strip() if auth_hdr.lower().startswith('bearer ') else request.headers.get('X-Auth-Token', '').strip()
    if token:
        if not sessions_mgr.check_rate_limit(ip, bucket='api_token'):
            return None, True
        prof = profiles_db.authenticate_api_token(token)
        if prof:
            sessions_mgr.clear_failures(ip, bucket='api_token')
            return prof, True
        sessions_mgr.record_failure(ip, bucket='api_token')
        return None, True

    pid = session.get('profile_id')
    if pid and session.get('authenticated'):
        sid = session.get('sid')
        if sid:
            if not sessions_mgr.validate_and_touch(sid):
                session.clear()
                return None, False
        else:
            session['sid'] = sessions_mgr.create_session(pid, ip, request.headers.get('User-Agent', ''))
        prof = profiles_db.get_by_id(pid)
        if prof:
            return prof, False

    if not is_exposed:
        return profiles_db.get_default_admin(), False

    return None, False


def register_security(app):
    @app.before_request
    def security_middleware():
        if request.path.startswith('/static/') or request.path in _PUBLIC_ASSET_PATHS:
            return None

        if request.path.startswith('/invite/') or request.path == '/api/auth/exchange':
            return None

        is_exposed = app.config.get('IS_EXPOSED', False)
        prof, used_bearer = _resolve_request_profile(is_exposed)
        g.profile = prof
        g.role = prof['role'] if prof else None

        if request.path in ('/login', '/logout'):
            if request.method == 'POST' and request.path == '/login':
                expected_token = session.get('csrf_token')
                sent_token = request.form.get('csrf_token')
                if not (expected_token and sent_token and hmac.compare_digest(str(expected_token), str(sent_token))):
                    return jsonify({'error': 'CSRF ошибка'}), 403
            return None

        if is_exposed and not prof:
            if request.path.startswith('/api/'):
                return jsonify({'error': 'Требуется авторизация'}), 401
            return redirect('/login')

        if g.role == 'viewer':
            if request.path == '/api/profile/password' and request.method == 'POST':
                pass
            elif request.method in ('POST', 'PUT', 'DELETE', 'PATCH'):
                return jsonify({'error': 'Недостаточно прав (режим Viewer — только чтение)'}), 403
            elif any(request.path.startswith(p) for p in _VIEWER_BLOCKED_GET_PREFIXES):
                return jsonify({'error': 'Доступ закрыт для роли Viewer'}), 403

        if request.method == 'POST' and not used_bearer:
            expected_token = session.get('csrf_token')
            sent_token = request.headers.get('X-CSRF-Token') or request.form.get('csrf_token')
            origin = request.headers.get('Origin') or request.headers.get('Referer')
            allowed_hosts = {request.host}
            for hdr in ('X-Forwarded-Host', 'X-Original-Host'):
                if request.headers.get(hdr):
                    allowed_hosts.add(request.headers.get(hdr).split(',')[0].strip())

            origin_ok = True
            if origin:
                parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(origin)
                if parsed.netloc and parsed.netloc not in allowed_hosts:
                    origin_ok = False

            token_ok = bool(expected_token and sent_token and hmac.compare_digest(str(expected_token), str(sent_token)))
            if not token_ok or not origin_ok:
                return jsonify({'error': 'CSRF проверка не пройдена. Обновите страницу.'}), 403

// web/static/analytics.js
let chartDaily = null;
let chartTop5 = null;
let chartHourly = null;
let _lastTopData = null;
let _lastHourlyMeta = [];
const _chartAborts = { daily: null, hourly: null, top5: null };
const _debounceTimers = {};

function _debounce(key, fn, ms = 500) {
    clearTimeout(_debounceTimers[key]);
    _debounceTimers[key] = setTimeout(fn, ms);
}

function _persistUiBg(ui) {
    if (typeof CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS !== 'undefined') CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS = ui;
    localStorage.setItem('tbr_ui_settings', JSON.stringify(ui));
    _debounce('ui_save', async () => {
        if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE === 'undefined' || CURRENT_ROLE === 'admin') {
            try { await api('/api/profile/ui', 'POST', ui); } catch (e) {}
        }
    }, 500);
}

async function loadAnalyticsTab() {
    syncAnalyticsControlsFromUi();
    loadAnalyticsCharts();
    if (typeof loadAnalyticsInsights === 'function') loadAnalyticsInsights(false);
    initDayLogs();
    if (!rawSummaryItems.length) await loadSummary();
    else if (typeof populateAnalyticsSelectors === 'function') populateAnalyticsSelectors();
}

function syncAnalyticsControlsFromUi() {
    const ch = (typeof getUiSettings === 'function' ? getUiSettings().charts : {}) || {};
    const hSel = document.getElementById('chart-hourly-hours');
    if (hSel) hSel.value = String(ch.hourly_hours || 24);

    const yAuto = document.getElementById('chart-hourly-ymin-auto');
    const yInp = document.getElementById('chart-hourly-ymin');
    const hasYMin = ch.hourly_y_min !== null && ch.hourly_y_min !== undefined && ch.hourly_y_min !== '';
    if (yAuto && yInp) {
        yAuto.checked = !hasYMin;
        yInp.disabled = !hasYMin;
        yInp.value = hasYMin ? ch.hourly_y_min : '';
    }
    const topInp = document.getElementById('chart-topn-val');
    if (topInp) topInp.value = Math.max(1, Math.min(25, parseInt(ch.top_n) || 5));
    const perSel = document.getElementById('chart-top-period');
    if (perSel) perSel.value = ch.top_period || '1d';
    const scSel = document.getElementById('chart-top-scale');
    if (scSel) scSel.value = ch.top_scale || 'lin';
}

function onHourlyWindowChange() {
    const ui = getUiSettings(); ui.charts = ui.charts || {};
    ui.charts.hourly_hours = parseInt(document.getElementById('chart-hourly-hours').value) || 24;
    _persistUiBg(ui);
    _debounce('fetch_hourly', () => loadSingleChart('hourly'), 300);
}

function _applyHourlyYMinLocally(yMinVal) {
    if (!chartHourly) return;
    const yScale = chartHourly.options.scales.y;
    if (yMinVal !== null && Number.isFinite(yMinVal)) { delete yScale.beginAtZero; yScale.min = yMinVal; }
    else { delete yScale.min; yScale.beginAtZero = true; }
    chartHourly.update('none');
}

function onHourlyYMinToggle() {
    const isAuto = document.getElementById('chart-hourly-ymin-auto').checked;
    const inp = document.getElementById('chart-hourly-ymin');
    inp.disabled = isAuto;
    const ui = getUiSettings(); ui.charts = ui.charts || {};
    const val = isAuto ? null : Math.max(0, parseFloat(inp.value) || 0);
    ui.charts.hourly_y_min = val;
    _applyHourlyYMinLocally(val);
    _persistUiBg(ui);
}

function onHourlyYMinInput() {
    const raw = parseFloat(document.getElementById('chart-hourly-ymin').value);
    const val = Number.isFinite(raw) && raw >= 0 ? raw : 0;
    const ui = getUiSettings(); ui.charts = ui.charts || {};
    ui.charts.hourly_y_min = val;
    _applyHourlyYMinLocally(val);
    _persistUiBg(ui);
}

function setTopN(n) {
    const clamped = Math.max(1, Math.min(25, parseInt(n) || 5));
    const inp = document.getElementById('chart-topn-val');
    if (inp && document.activeElement !== inp) inp.value = clamped;
    const ui = getUiSettings(); ui.charts = ui.charts || {};
    ui.charts.top_n = clamped;
    _persistUiBg(ui);
    _debounce('fetch_top5', () => loadSingleChart('top5'), 500);
}

function onTopPeriodChange() {
    const ui = getUiSettings(); ui.charts = ui.charts || {};
    ui.charts.top_period = document.getElementById('chart-top-period').value || '1d';
    _persistUiBg(ui);
    _debounce('fetch_top5', () => loadSingleChart('top5'), 300);
}

function onTopScaleChange() {
    const scale = document.getElementById('chart-top-scale').value || 'lin';
    const ui = getUiSettings(); ui.charts = ui.charts || {};
    ui.charts.top_scale = scale;
    _persistUiBg(ui);
    if (chartTop5 && _lastTopData) _renderTop5Chart(_lastTopData, scale, true);
}

function _computeHourMarkerFlags(meta) {
    if (!Array.isArray(meta) || !meta.length) return [];
    const bestByHour = new Map();
    meta.forEach((m, idx) => {
        if (!m || !m.ts) return;
        const dt = new Date(m.ts * 1000);
        const hKey = `${dt.getFullYear()}-${dt.getMonth()}-${dt.getDate()}-${dt.getHours()}`;
        const dist = dt.getMinutes() * 60 + dt.getSeconds();
        const prev = bestByHour.get(hKey);
        if (!prev || dist < prev.dist) bestByHour.set(hKey, { idx, dist });
    });
    const flags = new Array(meta.length).fill(false);
    bestByHour.forEach(v => { flags[v.idx] = true; });
    return flags;
}

function _buildTwoColorGradient(canvasId, c1Hex, c2Hex, opacity, isHorizontal = false) {
    const canvas = document.getElementById(canvasId);
    if (!canvas) return c1Hex;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const rgb1 = typeof hexToRgb === 'function' ? hexToRgb(c1Hex) : { r: 46, g: 204, b: 113 };
    const rgb2 = typeof hexToRgb === 'function' ? hexToRgb(c2Hex || c1Hex) : rgb1;
    const op = opacity ?? 0.75;
    const grad = isHorizontal ? ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, canvas.clientWidth || 600, 0) : ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, canvas.clientHeight || 220);
    grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${rgb1.r},${rgb1.g},${rgb1.b},${op})`);
    grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${rgb2.r},${rgb2.g},${rgb2.b},${Math.max(0.08, op * 0.45)})`);
    return grad;
}

function applyAnalyticsVisualSettingsLocally(ui) {
    const chCfg = (ui && ui.charts) || {};
    if (chartDaily) {
        const cD = chCfg.daily || { color: '#2ecc71', color2: '#10b981', opacity: 0.75 };
        chartDaily.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor = cD.gradient !== false ? _buildTwoColorGradient('chart-daily', cD.color, cD.color2, cD.opacity, false) : cD.color;
        chartDaily.data.datasets[0].borderColor = cD.color;
        chartDaily.update('none');
    }
    if (chartHourly) {
        const cH = chCfg.hourly || { color: '#00f5d4', color2: '#9b5de5', opacity: 0.35 };
        chartHourly.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor = cH.gradient !== false ? _buildTwoColorGradient('chart-hourly', cH.color, cH.color2, cH.opacity, false) : cH.color;
        chartHourly.data.datasets[0].borderColor = cH.color;
        chartHourly.update('none');
    }
    if (chartTop5 && _lastTopData) _renderTop5Chart(_lastTopData, chCfg.top_scale || 'lin', true);
}

function _chartThemeColors() {
    const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    return {
        tick: cs.getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#95a5a6',
        grid: cs.getPropertyValue('--border').trim() || 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)'
    };
}

function _renderDailyChart(dailyData) {
    const cD = ((typeof getUiSettings === 'function' ? getUiSettings().charts : {}) || {}).daily || { color: '#2ecc71', color2: '#10b981', opacity: 0.75 };
    const bg = cD.gradient !== false ? _buildTwoColorGradient('chart-daily', cD.color, cD.color2, cD.opacity, false) : cD.color;
    const { tick, grid } = _chartThemeColors();
    if (chartDaily) {
        chartDaily.data.labels = dailyData.labels || [];
        chartDaily.data.datasets[0].data = dailyData.values || [];
        chartDaily.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor = bg;
        chartDaily.data.datasets[0].borderColor = cD.color;
        chartDaily.update('none');
        return;
    }
    chartDaily = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-daily'), {
        type: 'bar',
        data: { labels: dailyData.labels || [], datasets: [{ data: dailyData.values || [], backgroundColor: bg, borderColor: cD.color, borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 4, minBarLength: 2, barPercentage: 0.65 }] },
        options: {
            responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
            scales: { x: { ticks: { color: tick, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: grid } }, y: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { color: tick, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: grid } } },
            plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip: { callbacks: { label: ctx => `${currentLang === 'en' ? 'Daily Upload' : 'Отдано за день'}: ${ctx.parsed.y} GiB` } } }
        }
    });
}

function _renderTop5Chart(topData, topScale = 'lin', updateOnly = false) {
    _lastTopData = topData;
    const chCfg = (typeof getUiSettings === 'function' ? getUiSettings().charts : {}) || {};
    const rawVals = [...(topData?.values || [])], labels = [...(topData?.labels || [])], fullLabels = [...(topData?.full_labels || topData?.labels || [])];
    const others = topData?.others, hasOthers = Boolean(others && (others.gb > 0 || others.count > 0));
    const isEn = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'en');
    if (hasOthers) {
        labels.push(isEn ? `Others (${others.count})` : `Все остальные (${others.count})`);
        fullLabels.push(isEn ? `All other torrents (${others.count} pcs)` : `Все остальные раздачи (${others.count} шт.)`);
        rawVals.push(others.gb);
    }
    const topWrap = document.getElementById('chart-top5-wrap');
    if (topWrap) topWrap.style.height = `${Math.max(210, (labels.length || 5) * 32 + 48)}px`;

    const isLog = (topScale === 'log10'), plotVals = isLog ? rawVals.map(v => Math.log10(1 + Math.max(0, v))) : rawVals;
    const { tick, grid } = _chartThemeColors();
    const xScale = { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { color: tick, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: grid } };
    if (isLog) {
        const maxR = Math.max(1, ...rawVals), pwr = [0, 1, 10, 100, 1000, 10000, 100000].filter(p => p === 0 || p <= maxR * 2.5);
        if (pwr.length < 2) pwr.push(1, 10);
        xScale.afterBuildTicks = ax => { ax.ticks = pwr.map(p => ({ value: Math.log10(1 + p) })); };
        xScale.ticks.callback = v => { const o = Math.round(Math.pow(10, v) - 1); return o <= 0 ? '0' : String(o); };
    }
    const cTop = chCfg.top5 || { color: '#ff9f1c', color2: '#ff4d6d', opacity: 0.8 };
    const grad = cTop.gradient !== false ? _buildTwoColorGradient('chart-top5', cTop.color, cTop.color2, cTop.opacity, true) : cTop.color;
    const bg = plotVals.map((_, i) => (hasOthers && i === plotVals.length - 1) ? 'rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.55)' : grad);
    const tooltips = {
        title: items => (items.length ? (fullLabels[items[0].dataIndex] || items[0].label) : ''),
        label: ctx => (hasOthers && ctx.dataIndex === rawVals.length - 1)
            ? `${isEn ? 'All other torrents' : 'Все остальные раздачи'} (${others.count} шт.): ${rawVals[ctx.dataIndex] ?? 0} GiB`
            : `${isEn ? 'Uploaded' : 'Отдано'}: ${rawVals[ctx.dataIndex] ?? 0} GiB`
    };
    if (chartTop5 && updateOnly) {
        chartTop5.data.labels = labels;
        chartTop5.data.datasets[0].data = plotVals;
        chartTop5.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor = bg;
        chartTop5.data.datasets[0].borderColor = cTop.color;
        chartTop5.options.scales.x = xScale;
        chartTop5.options.plugins.tooltip.callbacks = tooltips;
        chartTop5.resize();
        chartTop5.update('none');
        return;
    }
    if (chartTop5) chartTop5.destroy();
    chartTop5 = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-top5'), {
        type: 'bar',
        data: { labels, datasets: [{ data: plotVals, backgroundColor: bg, borderColor: cTop.color, borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 4, minBarLength: 2, barPercentage: 0.72 }] },
        options: { responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, indexAxis: 'y', scales: { x: xScale, y: { ticks: { color: tick, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: grid } } }, plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip: { callbacks: tooltips } } }
    });
}

function _renderHourlyChart(hourlyData) {
    const chCfg = (typeof getUiSettings === 'function' ? getUiSettings().charts : {}) || {};
    const cH = chCfg.hourly || { color: '#00f5d4', color2: '#9b5de5', opacity: 0.35 };
    const bg = cH.gradient !== false ? _buildTwoColorGradient('chart-hourly', cH.color, cH.color2, cH.opacity, false) : cH.color;
    const winHours = parseInt(hourlyData?.hours || chCfg.hourly_hours || 24);
    _lastHourlyMeta = hourlyData?.meta || [];
    const flags = _computeHourMarkerFlags(_lastHourlyMeta);
    const yMin = (chCfg.hourly_y_min !== null && chCfg.hourly_y_min !== undefined && chCfg.hourly_y_min !== '') ? parseFloat(chCfg.hourly_y_min) : null;
    const { tick, grid } = _chartThemeColors();
    const yOpts = { ticks: { color: tick, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: grid } };
    if (yMin !== null && Number.isFinite(yMin)) yOpts.min = yMin; else yOpts.beginAtZero = true;

    if (chartHourly) {
        chartHourly.data.labels = hourlyData?.labels || [];
        chartHourly.data.datasets[0].data = hourlyData?.values || [];
        chartHourly.data.datasets[0].borderColor = cH.color;
        chartHourly.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor = bg;
        chartHourly.data.datasets[0].pointRadius = ctx => (flags[ctx.dataIndex] ? (winHours <= 24 ? 3.5 : 1.5) : 0);
        chartHourly.options.scales.y = yOpts;
        chartHourly.update('none');
        return;
    }
    chartHourly = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-hourly'), {
        type: 'line',
        data: { labels: hourlyData?.labels || [], datasets: [{ data: hourlyData?.values || [], borderColor: cH.color, backgroundColor: bg, fill: true, tension: 0.35, cubicInterpolationMode: 'monotone', clip: true, pointRadius: ctx => (flags[ctx.dataIndex] ? (winHours <= 24 ? 3.5 : 1.5) : 0), pointHoverRadius: 5, pointHitRadius: 10 }] },
        options: {
            responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
            scales: { x: { ticks: { color: tick, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: grid } }, y: yOpts },
            plugins: {
                legend: { display: false },
                tooltip: { callbacks: { label: ctx => { const m = _lastHourlyMeta[ctx.dataIndex], l = `⚡ ${currentLang === 'en' ? 'Speed' : 'Скорость'}: ${(ctx.parsed.y ?? m?.speed_mbs ?? 0).toFixed(2)} MiB/s`; return (!m || !m.delta_fmt) ? l : [l, `📦 ${currentLang === 'en' ? 'Uploaded' : 'Отдано'}: +${m.delta_fmt}${m.interval_min > 0 ? ` (${m.interval_min}м)` : ''}`]; } } }
            }
        }
    });
}

async function loadSingleChart(chartType) {
    if (typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    if (_chartAborts[chartType]) _chartAborts[chartType].abort();
    const ctrl = new AbortController();
    _chartAborts[chartType] = ctrl;
    const chCfg = (typeof getUiSettings === 'function' ? getUiSettings().charts : {}) || {};
    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/analytics/charts?chart=${encodeURIComponent(chartType)}&hours=${parseInt(chCfg.hourly_hours) || 24}&top_n=${Math.max(1, Math.min(25, parseInt(chCfg.top_n) || 5))}&top_period=${encodeURIComponent(chCfg.top_period || '1d')}`, { signal: ctrl.signal });
        if (res.status === 401) { window.location.href = '/login'; return; }
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        if (chartType === 'daily' && d.daily) _renderDailyChart(d.daily);
        if (chartType === 'hourly' && d.hourly) _renderHourlyChart(d.hourly);
        if (chartType === 'top5' && d.top5) _renderTop5Chart(d.top5, chCfg.top_scale || 'lin', Boolean(chartTop5));
    } catch (e) { if (e.name !== 'AbortError') console.error(e); }
}

async function loadAnalyticsCharts() {
    if (typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    try {
        const chCfg = (typeof getUiSettings === 'function' ? getUiSettings().charts : {}) || {};
        const res = await api(`/api/analytics/charts?chart=all&hours=${parseInt(chCfg.hourly_hours) || 24}&top_n=${Math.max(1, Math.min(25, parseInt(chCfg.top_n) || 5))}&top_period=${encodeURIComponent(chCfg.top_period || '1d')}`);
        const d = await res.json();
        if (d.daily) _renderDailyChart(d.daily);
        if (d.hourly) _renderHourlyChart(d.hourly);
        if (d.top5) _renderTop5Chart(d.top5, chCfg.top_scale || 'lin', Boolean(chartTop5));
    } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
}

// web/static/analytics_insights.js
let chartPeerClients = null;
let chartTrackers = null;
let chartTorrentPersonal = null;
let _inspHash = null;
let _currentPeakSnapId = null;

async function loadAnalyticsInsights(forceClients = false) {
    try {
        const trPer = document.getElementById('chart-trackers-period')?.value || '7d';
        const res = await api(`/api/analytics/insights?tracker_period=${encodeURIComponent(trPer)}&force_clients=${forceClients ? '1' : '0'}`);
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        _renderRecords(d.records || {});
        _renderThrottleBadge(d.throttle || {});
        _renderHeatmap365(d.heatmap || []);
        _renderPeerClientsChart(d.clients || {});
        _renderTrackersChart(d.trackers || {});
    } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
    }
}

function _renderRecords(r) {
    const pctFmt = p => (p === null || p === undefined) ? 'мало данных' : `${p >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${p}% к пред.`;
    document.getElementById('rec-today-val').textContent = r.today_fmt || '—';
    document.getElementById('rec-today-cmp').textContent = `Вчера: ${r.yesterday_fmt || '—'} (${pctFmt(r.day_pct)})`;
    document.getElementById('rec-week-val').textContent = r.this_week_fmt || '—';
    document.getElementById('rec-week-cmp').textContent = `Прошлая: ${r.prev_week_fmt || '—'} (${pctFmt(r.week_pct)})`;
    document.getElementById('rec-peak-val').textContent = r.peak_speed_fmt || '—';
    const cleanDate = String(r.peak_speed_date || '').replace('|', '').trim();
    document.getElementById('rec-peak-date').textContent = cleanDate || '—';
    document.getElementById('rec-best-val').textContent = r.best_day_fmt || '—';
    document.getElementById('rec-best-date').textContent = r.best_day_date || '—';

    _currentPeakSnapId = r.peak_snapshot_id || null;
    const delBtn = document.getElementById('btn-ignore-peak');
    if (delBtn) delBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !_currentPeakSnapId);
}

async function recalculateRecords() {
    if (!confirm('Пересчитать рекорды? Любые аномальные скачки скорости будут отброшены по порогу канала и перезаписаны в БД.')) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/analytics/recalculate_records', 'POST');
        if (!res.ok) return alert('Ошибка пересчёта');
        const d = await res.json();
        if (d.records) _renderRecords(d.records);
        alert('✅ Рекорды успешно пересчитаны!');
    } catch (e) {
        alert(e.message);
    }
}

async function ignorePeakRecord() {
    if (!_currentPeakSnapId) return;
    if (!confirm('Пометить снимок с текущим пиком как аномальный (ignore_in_stats) и пересчитать рекорд?')) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/analytics/ignore_peak', 'POST', { snapshot_id: _currentPeakSnapId });
        const d = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка удаления');
        if (d.records) _renderRecords(d.records);
        if (typeof loadAnalyticsCharts === 'function') loadAnalyticsCharts();
        if (typeof loadDayLogs === 'function' && document.getElementById('logs-day-picker')?.value) loadDayLogs(dayLogsPage);
    } catch (e) {
        alert(e.message);
    }
}

function _renderThrottleBadge(th) {
    const b = document.getElementById('isp-throttle-badge');
    if (!b) return;
    b.classList.toggle('hidden', !th.throttled);
    if (th.throttled) b.textContent = `🚧 Полка скорости ~${th.tier_mbps} Мбит/с (${th.plateau_mbs} MiB/s)`;
}

function _renderHeatmap365(cells) {
    const grid = document.getElementById('heatmap-grid');
    const wrap = document.getElementById('heatmap-scroll-wrap');
    if (!grid || !cells.length) return;
    grid.innerHTML = '';
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    const firstWd = cells[0].weekday || 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < firstWd; i++) {
        const emp = document.createElement('span');
        emp.className = 'hm-cell hm-empty';
        frag.appendChild(emp);
    }
    cells.forEach(c => {
        const sp = document.createElement('span');
        sp.className = `hm-cell hm-l${c.level}`;
        sp.title = `${c.date}: ${c.fmt}`;
        frag.appendChild(sp);
    });
    grid.appendChild(frag);
    if (wrap) wrap.scrollLeft = wrap.scrollWidth;
}

function _renderPeerClientsChart(c) {
    const tot = document.getElementById('peer-clients-total');
    const totalCount = c.total_peers || 0;
    if (tot) tot.textContent = `${totalCount} пиров`;
    if (typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    const ctx = document.getElementById('chart-peer-clients');
    if (!ctx) return;
    if (chartPeerClients) chartPeerClients.destroy();

    const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    const txtColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#95a5a6';
    const ui = typeof getUiSettings === 'function' ? getUiSettings() : {};
    const colMap = ui.peer_client_colors || {};
    const defColMap = {
        "qBittorrent": "#2ecc71", "Transmission": "#e74c3c", "uTorrent": "#27ae60",
        "Deluge": "#3498db", "BiglyBT": "#9b59b6", "Aria2": "#1abc9c",
        "Tixati": "#f39c12", "Vuze": "#00bcd4", "libtorrent": "#95a5a6", "Прочие": "#64748b"
    };

    const labels = c.labels?.length ? c.labels : ['Нет пиров'];
    const values = c.values?.length ? c.values : [1];
    const bgColors = labels.map(name => colMap[name] || defColMap[name] || '#64748b');

    chartPeerClients = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'doughnut',
        data: { labels, datasets: [{ data: values, backgroundColor: c.values?.length ? bgColors : ['rgba(149,165,166,0.2)'], borderWidth: 0 }] },
        options: {
            responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
            plugins: {
                legend: {
                    position: 'right',
                    labels: {
                        color: txtColor, boxWidth: 10, font: { size: 10 },
                        generateLabels: chart => {
                            const data = chart.data;
                            if (!data.labels.length) return [];
                            return data.labels.map((label, i) => {
                                const val = data.datasets[0].data[i] || 0;
                                const pct = totalCount > 0 ? ((val / totalCount) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0';
                                return { text: `${label}: ${val} (${pct}%)`, fillStyle: data.datasets[0].backgroundColor[i], hidden: false, index: i };
                            });
                        }
                    }
                },
                tooltip: {
                    callbacks: {
                        label: ctxItem => {
                            const val = ctxItem.parsed || 0;
                            const pct = totalCount > 0 ? ((val / totalCount) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0';
                            return ` ${ctxItem.label}: ${val} (${pct}%)`;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    });
}

function _renderTrackersChart(tr) {
    if (typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    const ctx = document.getElementById('chart-trackers');
    if (!ctx) return;
    if (chartTrackers) chartTrackers.destroy();
    const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    const tickColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#95a5a6';
    const gridColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--border').trim() || 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';

    chartTrackers = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'bar',
        data: { labels: tr.labels || [], datasets: [{ data: tr.values || [], backgroundColor: 'rgba(155, 89, 182, 0.72)', borderColor: '#9b59b6', borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 4 }] },
        options: {
            responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, indexAxis: 'y',
            scales: { x: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } }, y: { ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } } },
            plugins: { legend: { display: false } }
        }
    });
}

function openTorrentInspectorModal(hashOverride = null) {
    if (hashOverride) selectedHash = hashOverride;
    if (typeof selectedHash === 'undefined' || !selectedHash) return alert(t('err_select_torrent'));
    _inspHash = selectedHash;
    const item = (typeof torrentsData !== 'undefined' ? torrentsData : []).find(x => x.hash === _inspHash);
    document.getElementById('insp-title').textContent = item ? item.name : _inspHash;
    document.getElementById('inspector-modal').style.display = 'flex';
    loadInspectorChart('24h');
    loadInspectorPeers();
    if (typeof loadInspectorPhase4 === 'function') loadInspectorPhase4(_inspHash, 1);
    if (typeof loadInspectorMediaInfo === 'function') loadInspectorMediaInfo(_inspHash);
}

function closeInspectorModal() { document.getElementById('inspector-modal').style.display = 'none'; }

async function loadInspectorChart(period = '24h') {
    if (!_inspHash || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    const res = await api(`/api/torrents/chart?hash=${encodeURIComponent(_inspHash)}&period=${encodeURIComponent(period)}`);
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const d = await res.json();
    const pts = d.points || [];
    const ctx = document.getElementById('chart-torrent-personal');
    if (!ctx) return;
    if (chartTorrentPersonal) chartTorrentPersonal.destroy();

    const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    const tickColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#95a5a6';
    const gridColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--border').trim() || 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';

    chartTorrentPersonal = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'line',
        data: {
            labels: pts.map(p => p.label),
            datasets: [{
                data: pts.map(p => p.speed_mbs), borderColor: '#2ecc71', backgroundColor: 'rgba(46, 204, 113, 0.2)',
                fill: true, tension: 0.3,
                pointRadius: ctxItem => (pts[ctxItem.dataIndex]?.is_merge ? 5 : (pts.length <= 40 ? 2.5 : 0)),
                pointBackgroundColor: ctxItem => (pts[ctxItem.dataIndex]?.is_merge ? '#f39c12' : '#2ecc71')
            }]
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
            scales: { x: { ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } }, y: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } } },
            plugins: {
                legend: { display: false },
                tooltip: { callbacks: { label: ctxItem => `⚡ ${ctxItem.parsed.y} MiB/s (+${pts[ctxItem.dataIndex]?.delta_fmt || '0 B'})${pts[ctxItem.dataIndex]?.is_merge ? ' [🔀 Мердж]' : ''}` } }
            }
        }
    });
}

async function loadInspectorPeers() {
    if (!_inspHash) return;
    const tbody = document.getElementById('insp-peers-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg">⏳ Загрузка пиров...</td></tr>';
    const res = await api(`/api/torrents/peers?hash=${encodeURIComponent(_inspHash)}`);
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(d.error || 'Ошибка')}</td></tr>`;
        return;
    }
    const peers = d.peers || [];
    if (!peers.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg">Активных пиров сейчас нет</td></tr>'; return; }
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    peers.forEach(p => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        const ctry = p.country_code ? `[${escapeHtml(p.country_code)}] ` : '';
        tr.innerHTML = `<td>${ctry}<b>${escapeHtml(p.client)}</b></td><td>${p.progress}%</td><td class="accent-txt">${escapeHtml(p.up_speed_fmt)}</td><td class="blue-txt">${escapeHtml(p.dl_speed_fmt)}</td><td class="muted-text">${escapeHtml(p.flags || '—')}</td>`;
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);
}
