// web/static/analytics_logs.js
let dayLogsPage = 1;
let dayLogsTotalPages = 1;

async function loadTorrentHourlyHistory() {
    const hash = document.getElementById('history-torrent-select').value || 'all';
    const res = await api(`/api/analytics/history?hash=${encodeURIComponent(hash)}&limit=36`);
    const list = await res.json();
    const tbody = document.getElementById('torrent-history-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    if (!list.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">${t('no_hourly_snaps')}</td></tr>`;
        return;
    }
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    list.forEach(r => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        const intLbl = r.interval_min > 0 ? ` (${r.interval_min}м)` : '';
        tr.innerHTML = `
            <td>${escapeHtml(r.datetime)}</td>
            <td>${escapeHtml(r.uploaded_fmt)}</td>
            <td class="purple-txt" style="font-weight:700;">+${escapeHtml(trBackend(r.delta_mb))}<span class="muted-text" style="font-size:10px;">${intLbl}</span></td>
            <td class="blue-txt" style="font-weight:600;">${escapeHtml(r.speed_fmt)}</td>
        `;
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);
}

async function initDayLogs() {
    const card = document.getElementById('admin-day-logs-card');
    if (!card) return;
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE !== 'undefined' && CURRENT_ROLE === 'viewer') {
        card.classList.add('hidden');
        return;
    }
    try {
        const [canRes, daysRes] = await Promise.all([api('/api/logs/can_open_folder'), api('/api/logs/days')]);
        if (canRes.ok) {
            const d = await canRes.json();
            document.getElementById('btn-open-logs-folder')?.classList.toggle('hidden', !d.can_open);
        }
        if (daysRes.ok) {
            const dd = await daysRes.json();
            const sel = document.getElementById('logs-day-picker');
            if (sel) {
                sel.innerHTML = '';
                const ph = document.createElement('option');
                ph.value = ''; ph.textContent = `-- ${t('pick_day_hint')} --`;
                sel.appendChild(ph);
                (dd.days || []).forEach(day => {
                    const opt = document.createElement('option');
                    opt.value = day; opt.textContent = day;
                    sel.appendChild(opt);
                });
            }
        }
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function loadDayLogs(page = 1) {
    const picker = document.getElementById('logs-day-picker');
    const dateVal = picker ? picker.value : '';
    const dlBtn = document.getElementById('btn-dl-day-logs');
    if (!dateVal) {
        if (dlBtn) dlBtn.classList.add('hidden');
        return;
    }
    if (dlBtn) {
        dlBtn.href = `/api/logs/day/download?date=${encodeURIComponent(dateVal)}`;
        dlBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    const targetPage = Math.max(1, Math.min(dayLogsTotalPages || 1, page));
    const res = await api(`/api/logs/day?date=${encodeURIComponent(dateVal)}&page=${targetPage}&per_page=15`);
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const d = await res.json();
    dayLogsPage = d.page || 1;
    dayLogsTotalPages = d.total_pages || 1;
    document.getElementById('day-logs-page-info').textContent = `${dayLogsPage} / ${dayLogsTotalPages} (${d.total || 0})`;

    const tbody = document.getElementById('day-logs-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    if (!d.items || !d.items.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg">${t('no_snaps_day')}</td></tr>`;
        return;
    }
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    d.items.forEach(r => {
        const isIgn = Boolean(r.ignore_in_stats);
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        if (isIgn) tr.style.cssText = 'opacity:0.45; text-decoration:line-through;';
        tr.innerHTML = `
            <td>${escapeHtml(r.datetime)}</td>
            <td class="accent-txt"><b>${escapeHtml(r.uploaded_fmt)}</b></td>
            <td>${escapeHtml(r.size_fmt)}</td>
            <td>${r.count}</td>
            <td>
                <button class="btn ${isIgn ? 'btn-secondary' : 'btn-danger'} btn-xs"
                        title="${isIgn ? 'Вернуть снимок в расчёты' : 'Исключить аномальный снимок из статистики'}"
                        onclick="toggleSnapshotIgnore(${r.id}, ${!isIgn})">${isIgn ? '♻️' : '🗑️'}</button>
            </td>
        `;
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);
}

async function toggleSnapshotIgnore(snapId, ignored) {
    const msg = ignored
        ? 'Исключить этот снимок из статистики (графиков, рекордов и тепловой карты)?'
        : 'Вернуть этот снимок обратно в статистику?';
    if (!confirm(msg)) return;
    const res = await api('/api/logs/snapshot/ignore', 'POST', { id: snapId, ignored });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка');
    if (d.records && typeof _renderRecords === 'function') _renderRecords(d.records);
    loadDayLogs(dayLogsPage);
    if (typeof loadAnalyticsCharts === 'function') loadAnalyticsCharts();
}

async function openLogsFolderHost() {
    const res = await api('/api/logs/open_folder', 'POST');
    if (!res.ok) {
        const d = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        alert(d.error || 'Недоступно');
    }
}

async function loadAutomation() {
    const res = await api('/api/automation');
    const d = await res.json();
    const s = d.settings || {};

    document.getElementById('auto-enabled').checked = Boolean(s.enabled);
    document.getElementById('auto-interval').value = s.interval || 5;
    document.getElementById('auto-tg-token').value = '';
    document.getElementById('auto-tg-token').placeholder = s.has_tg_token ? t('token_set_ph') : '123456:ABC-DEF...';
    document.getElementById('auto-tg-chat').value = s.tg_chat_id || '';
    document.getElementById('auto-discord').value = '';
    document.getElementById('auto-discord').placeholder = s.has_discord ? t('webhook_set_ph') : 'https://discord.com/api/webhooks/...';

    const diskInp = document.getElementById('auto-alert-disk');
    if (diskInp) diskInp.value = s.alert_disk_gib || '';
    const qbChk = document.getElementById('auto-alert-qb');
    if (qbChk) qbChk.checked = Boolean(s.alert_qb_down);
    const recheckChk = document.getElementById('auto-recheck-io');
    if (recheckChk) recheckChk.checked = Boolean(s.auto_recheck_enabled);

    currentRules = d.rules || [];
    renderRules(currentRules);
    if (typeof loadDndDigestAndTagging === 'function') loadDndDigestAndTagging();
}

function _fmtBytesBinary(b) {
    if (!b || b <= 0) return '';
    if (b >= 1024 ** 4) return `${(b / (1024 ** 4)).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '')} TiB`;
    if (b >= 1024 ** 3) return `${(b / (1024 ** 3)).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '')} GiB`;
    return `${(b / (1024 ** 2)).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '')} MiB`;
}

function _fmtSpeedBinary(bps) {
    if (!bps || bps <= 0) return '';
    if (bps >= 1024 ** 2) return `${(bps / (1024 ** 2)).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '')} MiB/s`;
    return `${(bps / 1024).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '')} KiB/s`;
}

function renderRules(rules) {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('rules-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    if (!rules.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg">${t('no_rules')}</td></tr>`;
        return;
    }
    const isEn = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'en');
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    rules.forEach(r => {
        const conds = [];
        if (r.min_ratio > 0) conds.push(`Ratio >= ${r.min_ratio}x`);
        if (r.min_seed_days > 0) {
            const tmLbl = r.time_mode === 'time_active' ? (isEn ? 'Active' : 'Актив') : (isEn ? 'Seed' : 'Сид');
            conds.push(`${tmLbl} >= ${r.min_seed_days} ${t('rule_days_unit')}`);
        }
        if (r.min_uploaded_bytes > 0) conds.push(`${isEn ? 'Up' : 'Отдано'} >= ${_fmtBytesBinary(r.min_uploaded_bytes)}`);
        if (r.speed_op && r.speed_bytes > 0) {
            conds.push(`${isEn ? 'AvgSpd' : 'СрСкор'} ${r.speed_op} ${_fmtSpeedBinary(r.speed_bytes)} (${r.speed_window_min || 60}m)`);
        }

        const acts = [];
        if (r.action_pause) acts.push(t('chk_pause'));
        if (r.action_tag) acts.push(`🏷️ +${r.action_tag}`);
        if (r.action_remove_tag) acts.push(`✂️ -${r.action_remove_tag}`);
        if (r.notify) acts.push(t('rule_bot'));

        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.innerHTML = `
            <td><strong>${escapeHtml(r.name)}</strong></td>
            <td style="white-space:normal;">${escapeHtml(conds.join(t('rule_and')))}</td>
            <td style="white-space:normal;">${escapeHtml(acts.join(', ') || '—')}</td>
            <td><span class="badge ${r.enabled ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-pause'}">${r.enabled ? t('rule_on') : t('rule_off')}</span></td>
            <td class="row-gap">
                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" onclick="editRule(${r.id})">✏️</button>
                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" onclick="toggleRule(${r.id})">${r.enabled ? t('rule_off') : t('rule_on')}</button>
                <button class="btn btn-danger btn-xs" onclick="deleteRule(${r.id})">🗑️</button>
            </td>
        `;
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);
}

function editRule(id) {
    const r = currentRules.find(x => x.id === id);
    if (!r) return;

    editingRuleId = id;
    document.getElementById('rule-name').value = r.name || '';
    document.getElementById('rule-time-mode').value = r.time_mode || 'seeding_time';
    document.getElementById('rule-ratio').value = r.min_ratio > 0 ? r.min_ratio : '';
    document.getElementById('rule-days').value = r.min_seed_days > 0 ? r.min_seed_days : '';

    const upBytes = r.min_uploaded_bytes || 0;
    if (upBytes >= 1024 ** 4) {
        document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value = +(upBytes / (1024 ** 4)).toFixed(3);
        document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value = 'TiB';
    } else if (upBytes >= 1024 ** 3) {
        document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value = +(upBytes / (1024 ** 3)).toFixed(3);
        document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value = 'GiB';
    } else if (upBytes > 0) {
        document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value = +(upBytes / (1024 ** 2)).toFixed(2);
        document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value = 'MiB';
    } else {
        document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value = '';
        document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value = 'GiB';
    }

    document.getElementById('rule-speed-op').value = r.speed_op || '';
    const spBytes = r.speed_bytes || 0;
    if (spBytes >= 1024 ** 2) {
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-val').value = +(spBytes / (1024 ** 2)).toFixed(3);
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-unit').value = 'MiB';
    } else if (spBytes > 0) {
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-val').value = +(spBytes / 1024).toFixed(2);
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-unit').value = 'KiB';
    } else {
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-val').value = '';
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-unit').value = 'KiB';
    }
    document.getElementById('rule-speed-win').value = r.speed_window_min || 60;

    document.getElementById('rule-tag').value = r.action_tag || '';
    document.getElementById('rule-remove-tag').value = r.action_remove_tag || '';
    document.getElementById('rule-pause').checked = Boolean(r.action_pause);
    document.getElementById('rule-notify').checked = Boolean(r.notify);

    document.getElementById('rule-form-title').textContent = t('card_edit_rule', { id });
    document.getElementById('btn-rule-submit').textContent = t('btn_save');
    document.getElementById('btn-rule-cancel').classList.remove('hidden');
    document.getElementById('rule-form-card').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
}

function cancelEditRule() {
    editingRuleId = null;
    document.getElementById('rule-name').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-time-mode').value = 'seeding_time';
    document.getElementById('rule-ratio').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-days').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value = 'GiB';
    document.getElementById('rule-speed-op').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-speed-val').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-speed-unit').value = 'KiB';
    document.getElementById('rule-speed-win').value = 60;
    document.getElementById('rule-tag').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-remove-tag').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-pause').checked = false;
    document.getElementById('rule-notify').checked = true;

    document.getElementById('rule-form-title').textContent = t('card_add_rule');
    document.getElementById('btn-rule-submit').textContent = t('btn_add');
    document.getElementById('btn-rule-cancel').classList.add('hidden');
}

async function saveAutomationSettings() {
    const payload = {
        enabled: document.getElementById('auto-enabled').checked,
        interval: parseInt(document.getElementById('auto-interval').value) || 5,
        alert_disk_gib: parseInt(document.getElementById('auto-alert-disk')?.value || 0) || 0,
        alert_qb_down: document.getElementById('auto-alert-qb')?.checked || false,
        auto_recheck_enabled: document.getElementById('auto-recheck-io')?.checked || false,
        tg_token: document.getElementById('auto-tg-token').value.trim(),
        tg_chat_id: document.getElementById('auto-tg-chat').value.trim(),
        discord_webhook: document.getElementById('auto-discord').value.trim()
    };
    const res = await api('/api/automation/settings', 'POST', payload);
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) return alert(d.error || t('save_err'));
    document.getElementById('auto-status-msg').textContent = d.message;
    loadAutomation();
}

    msgEl.textContent = t('auto_testing');
    const res = await api('/api/automation/test', 'POST');

async function submitAutomationRule() {
    const upVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value) || 0;
    const upUnit = document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value;
    const upMult = upUnit === 'TiB' ? (1024 ** 4) : (upUnit === 'GiB' ? (1024 ** 3) : (1024 ** 2));

    const spVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rule-speed-val').value) || 0;
    const spUnit = document.getElementById('rule-speed-unit').value;
    const spMult = spUnit === 'MiB' ? (1024 ** 2) : 1024;

    const payload = {
        name: document.getElementById('rule-name').value.trim(),
        time_mode: document.getElementById('rule-time-mode').value,
        min_ratio: parseFloat(document.getElementById('rule-ratio').value) || 0,
        min_seed_days: parseFloat(document.getElementById('rule-days').value) || 0,
        min_uploaded_bytes: upVal > 0 ? Math.round(upVal * upMult) : 0,
        speed_op: document.getElementById('rule-speed-op').value,
        speed_bytes: spVal > 0 ? (spVal * spMult) : 0,
        speed_window_min: parseInt(document.getElementById('rule-speed-win').value) || 60,
        action_tag: document.getElementById('rule-tag').value.trim(),
        action_remove_tag: document.getElementById('rule-remove-tag').value.trim(),
        action_pause: document.getElementById('rule-pause').checked,
        notify: document.getElementById('rule-notify').checked
    };

    let url = '/api/automation/rules';
    if (editingRuleId !== null) {
        url = '/api/automation/rules/update';
        payload.id = editingRuleId;
    }

    try {
        const res = await api(url, 'POST', payload);
        const d = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || d.success === false) {
            return alert(d.error || `HTTP ${res.status}`);
        }
        cancelEditRule();
        loadAutomation();
    } catch (e) {
        alert(e.message);
    }
}

function addAutomationRule() {
    return submitAutomationRule();
}

async function toggleRule(id) {
    await api('/api/automation/rules/toggle', 'POST', { id });
    loadAutomation();
}

async function deleteRule(id) {
    if (!confirm(t('rule_del_confirm'))) return;
    if (editingRuleId === id) cancelEditRule();
    await api('/api/automation/rules/delete', 'POST', { id });
    loadAutomation();
}

// web/static/common.js
const VALID_TABS = ['torrents', 'merger', 'summary', 'analytics', 'automation', 'tools'];
const CSRF_TOKEN = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.content || '';

function escapeHtml(str) {
    if (str === null || str === undefined) return '';
    return String(str)
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
        .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
}

async function api(url, method = 'GET', body = null) {
    const opts = { method, headers: {} };
    if (method === 'POST') {
        opts.headers['X-CSRF-Token'] = CSRF_TOKEN;
        if (body !== null) {
            opts.headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
            opts.body = JSON.stringify(body);
        }
    }
    const res = await fetch(url, opts);
    if (res.status === 401) {
        window.location.href = '/login';
        throw new Error('Auth required');
    }
    return res;
}

function formatBytesJS(bytes) {
    if (!bytes || bytes <= 0) return '0 B';
    const units = ['B', 'KiB', 'MiB', 'GiB', 'TiB', 'PiB'];
    let val = parseFloat(bytes);
    let u = 0;
    while (val >= 1024 && u < units.length - 1) { val /= 1024; u++; }
    return `${val.toFixed(2)} ${units[u]}`;
}

function parseDateToTs(str) {
    if (!str || typeof str !== 'string' || str.includes('неизвестна')) return 0;
    const parts = str.trim().split(' ');
    if (parts.length < 2) return 0;
    const dmy = parts[0].split('.');
    const hm = parts[1].split(':');
    if (dmy.length !== 3 || hm.length !== 2) return 0;
    return new Date(parseInt(dmy[2]), parseInt(dmy[1]) - 1, parseInt(dmy[0]), parseInt(hm[0]), parseInt(hm[1])).getTime();
}

function switchTab(name, updateHash = true) {
    if (!VALID_TABS.includes(name)) name = 'torrents';
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.tab === name));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('active', c.id === 'tab-' + name));
    if (updateHash && window.location.hash !== '#' + name) window.location.hash = name;

    if (name === 'torrents') loadTorrents();
    if (name === 'merger') loadMergerLists();
    if (name === 'summary') loadSummary();
    if (name === 'analytics') {
        loadAnalyticsTab();
        if (typeof loadDeepAnalyticsWidgets === 'function') loadDeepAnalyticsWidgets();
    }
    if (name === 'automation') {
        loadAutomation();
        if (typeof loadAutoExtrasTab === 'function') loadAutoExtrasTab();
    }
    if (name === 'tools') {
        if (typeof loadToolsTab === 'function') loadToolsTab();
        if (typeof loadSafetyToolsTab === 'function') loadSafetyToolsTab();
        if (typeof loadNetOpsTab === 'function') loadNetOpsTab();
    }
}

function refreshActiveTab() {
    const active = document.querySelector('.tab-btn.active')?.dataset.tab || 'torrents';
    if (active === 'torrents') loadTorrents(true);
    else if (active === 'summary') loadSummary(true);
    else if (active === 'analytics') {
        loadAnalyticsTab();
        if (typeof loadDeepAnalyticsWidgets === 'function') loadDeepAnalyticsWidgets();
    } else if (active === 'automation') {
        loadAutomation();
        if (typeof loadAutoExtrasTab === 'function') loadAutoExtrasTab();
    } else if (active === 'merger') loadTorrents(true);
    else if (active === 'tools') {
        if (typeof loadToolsTab === 'function') loadToolsTab();
        if (typeof loadSafetyToolsTab === 'function') loadSafetyToolsTab();
        if (typeof loadNetOpsTab === 'function') loadNetOpsTab();
    }
    checkQbStatus();
}

function syncTabWithHash() {
    const rawHash = (window.location.hash || '').replace('#', '').trim();
    switchTab(VALID_TABS.includes(rawHash) ? rawHash : 'torrents', false);
}
window.addEventListener('hashchange', syncTabWithHash);

document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (typeof openCommandPalette === 'function') openCommandPalette();
    }
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
    applyI18n();
    await loadMeAndApplyRole();
    syncTabWithHash();
    if (typeof initSystemStatusAndUpdates === 'function') initSystemStatusAndUpdates();
    if (typeof initTimersUi === 'function') initTimersUi();
    setInterval(checkQbStatus, 5000);
});

    if (typeof CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS !== 'undefined' && CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS) return CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS;

        theme: 'dark',

        }
    };
}

function hexToRgb(hex) {
    const m = String(hex || '#2ecc71').replace('#', '').match(/^([0-9a-f]{2})([0-9a-f]{2})([0-9a-f]{2})$/i);
    if (!m) return { r: 46, g: 204, b: 113 };
    return { r: parseInt(m[1], 16), g: parseInt(m[2], 16), b: parseInt(m[3], 16) };
}

function _maybeLoadGoogleFont(fontObj, allowGf) {
    if (!fontObj || !fontObj.web || !allowGf) return;
    const linkId = 'tbr-google-font-link';
    const fam = encodeURIComponent(fontObj.name).replace(/%20/g, '+') + ':wght@400;600;700;800';
    const href = `https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=${fam}&display=swap`;
    let link = document.getElementById(linkId);
    if (!link) {
        link = document.createElement('link');
        link.id = linkId;
        link.rel = 'stylesheet';
        document.head.appendChild(link);
    }
    if (link.href !== href) link.href = href;
}

function updateFontNote(fontObj) {
    const el = document.getElementById('cust-font-note');
    if (!el) return;
    const allow = document.getElementById('cust-allow-gf')?.checked;
    if (fontObj && fontObj.web && !allow) {
        el.textContent = `ℹ️ Шрифт «${fontObj.name}» будет использован, если установлен локально (или включите Google Fonts).`;
    } else {
        el.textContent = '';
    }
}

function applyUiSettings(ui) {
    if (!ui) return;
    const root = document.documentElement;
    const theme = ui.theme || 'dark';
    let palette = THEME_PRESETS[theme] || THEME_PRESETS.dark;

    if (theme === 'custom' && ui.custom_colors) {
        const c = ui.custom_colors;
        const isDark = hexToRgb(c.bg_main).r * 0.299 + hexToRgb(c.bg_main).g * 0.587 + hexToRgb(c.bg_main).b * 0.114 < 128;
        palette = {
            bg_main: c.bg_main || '#121214', bg_panel: c.bg_panel || '#1a1a20',
            bg_input: isDark ? '#262630' : '#e8eaef', bg_hover: isDark ? '#22222a' : '#f4f6f8',
            text: c.text || '#ffffff', text_muted: isDark ? '#95a5a6' : '#6b7280',
            accent: c.accent || '#2ecc71', border: isDark ? '#2a2a34' : '#d1d5db',
            th_bg: isDark ? '#1e1e26' : '#f3f4f6'
        };
    }

    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(palette)) root.style.setProperty(`--${k.replace('_', '-')}`, v);
    document.body.dataset.theme = theme;
    document.body.classList.toggle('mobile-cards-on', ui.mobile_mode !== false);
    if (typeof updateMobileCardsBtn === 'function') updateMobileCardsBtn();

    const fontObj = AVAILABLE_FONTS.find(f => f.name === ui.font) || AVAILABLE_FONTS[0];
    _maybeLoadGoogleFont(fontObj, Boolean(ui.allow_google_fonts));
    root.style.setProperty('--app-font', fontObj.css);

    if (typeof syncAnalyticsControlsFromUi === 'function') syncAnalyticsControlsFromUi();
    if (typeof applySummarySortAndRender === 'function' && typeof rawSummaryItems !== 'undefined' && rawSummaryItems.length) {
        applySummarySortAndRender();
    }
    if (typeof applyAnalyticsVisualSettingsLocally === 'function' && document.getElementById('tab-analytics')?.classList.contains('active')) {
        applyAnalyticsVisualSettingsLocally(ui);
    }
}

async function persistUiSettings(ui) {
    if (typeof CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS !== 'undefined') CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS = ui;
    localStorage.setItem('tbr_ui_settings', JSON.stringify(ui));
    applyUiSettings(ui);
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE === 'undefined' || CURRENT_ROLE === 'admin') {
        try { await api('/api/profile/ui', 'POST', ui); } catch (e) {}
    }
}

function openCustomizeModal() {
    const ui = getUiSettings();
    document.getElementById('cust-theme').value = ui.theme || 'dark';
    const cc = ui.custom_colors || {};
    document.getElementById('cust-bg-main').value = cc.bg_main || '#121214';
    document.getElementById('cust-bg-panel').value = cc.bg_panel || '#1a1a20';
    document.getElementById('cust-text').value = cc.text || '#ffffff';
    document.getElementById('cust-accent').value = cc.accent || '#2ecc71';
    document.getElementById('cust-custom-colors').classList.toggle('hidden', (ui.theme || 'dark') !== 'custom');

    const fontSel = document.getElementById('cust-font');
    fontSel.innerHTML = '';
    AVAILABLE_FONTS.forEach(f => {
        const opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = f.name; opt.textContent = f.label;
        fontSel.appendChild(opt);
    });
    fontSel.value = ui.font || 'Segoe UI';
    document.getElementById('cust-allow-gf').checked = Boolean(ui.allow_google_fonts);
    document.getElementById('cust-mobile-mode').checked = (ui.mobile_mode !== false);
    updateFontNote(AVAILABLE_FONTS.find(f => f.name === fontSel.value));

    renderChartEditors(ui.charts || {});
    renderClientColorEditors(ui.peer_client_colors || {});
    document.getElementById('customize-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}

function closeCustomizeModal() { document.getElementById('customize-modal').style.display = 'none'; }
function onCustThemeChange() { document.getElementById('cust-custom-colors').classList.toggle('hidden', document.getElementById('cust-theme').value !== 'custom'); }

function renderChartEditors(chartsCfg) {
    const box = document.getElementById('cust-charts-editors');
    box.innerHTML = '';
    const defs = [
        { key: 'daily', title: '1. По дням (GiB)', def1: '#2ecc71', def2: '#10b981' },
        { key: 'top5', title: '2. Топ-N раздач (горизонтальный)', def1: '#ff9f1c', def2: '#ff4d6d' },
        { key: 'hourly', title: '3. Скорость нагрузки', def1: '#00f5d4', def2: '#9b5de5' }
    ];
    defs.forEach(d => {
        const cfg = chartsCfg[d.key] || { color: d.def1, color2: d.def2, gradient: true, opacity: 0.7 };
        const c1 = cfg.color || d.def1, c2 = cfg.color2 || cfg.color || d.def2;
        const div = document.createElement('div');
        div.style.cssText = 'background:var(--bg-input); padding:8px; border-radius:6px; margin-bottom:8px;';
        div.innerHTML = `
            <div class="row-between mb-8">
                <b style="font-size:12px;">${d.title}</b>
                <label class="row-gap lbl-xs pointer"><input type="checkbox" id="cc-${d.key}-grad" ${cfg.gradient !== false ? 'checked' : ''}> Градиент</label>
            </div>
            <div class="row-wrap" style="gap:8px;">
                <div class="row-gap"><span class="lbl-xs">Цвет 1:</span><input type="color" id="cc-${d.key}-c1" value="${c1}"></div>
                <div class="row-gap"><span class="lbl-xs">Цвет 2:</span><input type="color" id="cc-${d.key}-c2" value="${c2}"></div>
                <div class="row-gap"><span class="lbl-xs">Прозрачность:</span><input type="range" id="cc-${d.key}-op" min="0.1" max="1" step="0.05" value="${cfg.opacity ?? 0.7}" style="width:75px;"></div>
            </div>
            <div class="row-wrap mt-10" style="gap:4px;">
                <span class="lbl-xs muted-text" style="width:100%;">Неоновые пары:</span>
                ${NEON_PRESETS.map(p => `<button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" style="font-size:10px; padding:2px 6px;" onclick="applyNeonPreset('${d.key}','${p.c1}','${p.c2}')"><span style="display:inline-block;width:8px;height:8px;background:${p.c1};border-radius:2px;"></span><span style="display:inline-block;width:8px;height:8px;background:${p.c2};border-radius:2px;"></span> ${p.label}</button>`).join('')}
            </div>
        `;
        box.appendChild(div);
    });
}

function applyNeonPreset(key, c1, c2) {
    const el1 = document.getElementById(`cc-${key}-c1`), el2 = document.getElementById(`cc-${key}-c2`);
    if (el1) el1.value = c1; if (el2) el2.value = c2;
}

function renderClientColorEditors(clientColors) {
    let box = document.getElementById('cust-clients-editors');
    if (!box) {
        box = document.createElement('div');
        box.id = 'cust-clients-editors';
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="card-title mt-10">👥 ЦВЕТА КЛИЕНТОВ ПИРОВ</div><div id="cust-clients-grid" class="grid-2" style="gap:6px;"></div>';
        document.getElementById('cust-charts-editors').after(box);
    }
    const grid = document.getElementById('cust-clients-grid');
    grid.innerHTML = '';
    const defs = { "qBittorrent": "#2ecc71", "Transmission": "#e74c3c", "uTorrent": "#27ae60", "Deluge": "#3498db", "BiglyBT": "#9b59b6", "Aria2": "#1abc9c", "Tixati": "#f39c12", "Vuze": "#00bcd4", "libtorrent": "#95a5a6", "Прочие": "#64748b" };
    for (const [name, defCol] of Object.entries(defs)) {
        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'row-between';
        row.style.cssText = 'background:var(--bg-input); padding:4px 8px; border-radius:5px; font-size:11px;';
        row.innerHTML = `<span>${name}</span><input type="color" class="client-col-inp" data-client="${name}" value="${clientColors[name] || defCol}">`;
        grid.appendChild(row);
    }
}

async function saveCustomization() {
    const ui = getUiSettings();
    ui.theme = document.getElementById('cust-theme').value;
    ui.custom_colors = {
        bg_main: document.getElementById('cust-bg-main').value, bg_panel: document.getElementById('cust-bg-panel').value,
        text: document.getElementById('cust-text').value, accent: document.getElementById('cust-accent').value
    };
    ui.font = document.getElementById('cust-font').value;
    ui.allow_google_fonts = document.getElementById('cust-allow-gf').checked;
    ui.mobile_mode = document.getElementById('cust-mobile-mode').checked;
    ui.charts = ui.charts || {};
    ['daily', 'top5', 'hourly'].forEach(k => {
        ui.charts[k] = {
            color: document.getElementById(`cc-${k}-c1`)?.value || '#2ecc71', color2: document.getElementById(`cc-${k}-c2`)?.value || '#10b981',
            gradient: document.getElementById(`cc-${k}-grad`)?.checked !== false, opacity: parseFloat(document.getElementById(`cc-${k}-op`)?.value) || 0.7
        };
    });
    ui.peer_client_colors = ui.peer_client_colors || {};
    document.querySelectorAll('.client-col-inp').forEach(inp => { ui.peer_client_colors[inp.dataset.client] = inp.value; });
    await persistUiSettings(ui);
    closeCustomizeModal();
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => applyUiSettings(getUiSettings()));

// web/static/hardcore_ui.js
async function loadSmartStatus() {
    const box = document.getElementById('smart-hdd-box');
    if (!box) return;
    box.innerHTML = '⏳ Опрос smartctl...';
    const [sRes, qRes] = await Promise.all([api('/api/hdd/smart'), api('/api/recheck_queue')]);
    const s = await sRes.json();
    const q = qRes.ok ? ((await qRes.json()).queue || []) : [];

    let html = `<div class="lbl-xs mb-8">Очередь речеков (1 на диск): <b>${q.length}</b> шт.</div>`;
    if (!s.available) {
        html += `<div class="lbl-xs muted-text">ℹ️ SMART: ${escapeHtml(s.reason)}</div>`;
    } else {
        html += (s.disks || []).map(d => `
            <div class="row-between" style="background:var(--bg-input); padding:4px 8px; border-radius:5px; margin-bottom:4px;">
                <span><b>${escapeHtml(d.model)}</b> (${escapeHtml(d.device)})</span>
                <span class="badge ${d.passed ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-err'}">${d.passed ? 'OK' : 'FAIL'} ${d.temp_c ? `• ${d.temp_c}°C` : ''}</span>
            </div>
        `).join('');
    }
    box.innerHTML = html;
}

async function enqueueSelectedRecheck() {
    if (typeof selectedHash === 'undefined' || !selectedHash) return alert(t('err_select_torrent'));
    const res = await api('/api/recheck_queue', 'POST', { hash: selectedHash });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка');
    alert(`💽 Поставлено в безопасную очередь речека (в очереди: ${(d.queue || []).length})`);
}

async function checkRssDuplicates() {
    const box = document.getElementById('rss-dedup-box');
    if (!box) return;
    box.innerHTML = '⏳ Проверка RSS-лент через API qB...';
    const res = await api('/api/rss/dedup');
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!d.success) { box.innerHTML = `<span class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(d.error)}</span>`; return; }
    const dups = (d.items || []).filter(x => x.is_duplicate);
    if (!dups.length) {
        box.innerHTML = `<span class="lbl-xs accent-txt">✅ Проверено релизов: ${d.total}, дубликатов не найдено.</span>`;
        return;
    }
    box.innerHTML = `<div class="lbl-xs orange-txt mb-8">Найдено дублей: ${dups.length} из ${d.total}</div>` + dups.slice(0, 15).map(x => `
        <div class="lbl-xs mb-8" style="background:var(--bg-input); padding:4px 8px; border-radius:5px;">
            <b>${escapeHtml(x.title)}</b> (${x.similarity}%)<div class="muted-text">Уже есть: ${escapeHtml(x.matched_name)}</div>
        </div>
    `).join('');
}

async function loadInspectorMediaInfo(tHash) {
    const box = document.getElementById('insp-mediainfo-box');
    if (!box) return;
    box.innerHTML = '<span class="lbl-xs muted-text">⏳ Чтение метаданных видео...</span>';
    const res = await api(`/api/torrents/mediainfo?hash=${encodeURIComponent(tHash)}`);
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!d.success) { box.innerHTML = `<span class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(d.error || 'Нет видео')}</span>`; return; }
    box.innerHTML = (d.items || []).map(m => `
        <div class="lbl-xs" style="background:var(--bg-input); padding:5px 8px; border-radius:5px; margin-bottom:4px;">
            <b>🎬 ${escapeHtml(m.file)}</b> (${escapeHtml(m.size_fmt)}, ${escapeHtml(m.duration_fmt)})<br>
            <span class="muted-text">Видео: ${escapeHtml(m.video)} • Битрейт: ${escapeHtml(m.bitrate_fmt)} • Аудио: ${escapeHtml(m.audio)} • Субтитры: ${escapeHtml(m.subtitles)}</span>
        </div>
    `).join('');
}

// web/static/hygiene_ui.js
let _hygieneTorItems = [];
let _hygienePathItems = [];
let _hygieneMode = 'quarantine';
let _pathMode = 'bt_backup';
let _relocatePollTimer = null;

function toggleAllHygTor(master) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.hyg-tor-chk').forEach(c => { c.checked = master.checked; });
}

function toggleAllHygPath(master) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.hyg-path-chk').forEach(c => { c.checked = master.checked; });
}

async function runHygieneTorrentScan(mode) {
    _hygieneMode = mode;
    const tbody = document.getElementById('hyg-tor-tbody');
    const summary = document.getElementById('hyg-tor-summary');
    const delBtn = document.getElementById('btn-hyg-tor-trash');
    const masterChk = document.getElementById('hyg-tor-chk-all');
    if (masterChk) masterChk.checked = false;
    if (!tbody) return;
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">⏳ Сканирование клиента...</td></tr>';
    if (delBtn) delBtn.classList.add('hidden');

    let url = '/api/hygiene/quarantine?hours=72&tag=1';
    if (mode === 'incompletes') url = '/api/hygiene/incompletes?days=0';
    if (mode === 'dead_weight') url = '/api/hygiene/dead_weight?days=30&min_seeds=10';

    const res = await api(url);
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) {
        summary.textContent = d.error || 'Ошибка';
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(d.error || 'Ошибка')}</td></tr>`;
        return;
    }
    _hygieneTorItems = d.items || [];
    summary.textContent = `Найдено: ${_hygieneTorItems.length}`;
    if (!_hygieneTorItems.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg accent-txt">🎉 Чисто! Кандидатов не найдено.</td></tr>';
        return;
    }
    if (delBtn) delBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
    tbody.innerHTML = _hygieneTorItems.map((it, idx) => {
        let meta = '';
        if (mode === 'quarantine') {
            meta = `Ошибка ${it.elapsed_hours}ч: ${escapeHtml(it.error_msg)}`;
        } else if (mode === 'incompletes') {
            const stBadge = it.state.includes('pause') ? '⏸️ Пауза' : (it.state.includes('stall') ? '⏳ Зависла' : it.state);
            meta = `${stBadge} • Скачано: ${escapeHtml(it.downloaded_fmt || '—')} (${it.progress_pct}%) • Сиды/Avail: ${it.availability} • Простой: ${it.idle_days}д`;
        } else {
            meta = `Сиды: ${it.seeds} • Простой: ${it.idle_days}д • Ratio: ${it.ratio}x`;
        }
        return `
            <tr>
                <td><input type="checkbox" class="hyg-tor-chk" data-idx="${idx}"></td>
                <td style="white-space:normal;"><b>${escapeHtml(it.name)}</b><div class="lbl-xs muted-text">${meta}</div></td>
                <td><b>${escapeHtml(it.size_fmt)}</b></td>
                <td><span class="badge ${it.ready ? 'badge-err' : 'badge-stall'}">${escapeHtml(it.state || mode)}</span></td>
            </tr>
        `;
    }).join('');
}

async function deleteSelectedHygieneTorrents() {
    const chosen = [...document.querySelectorAll('.hyg-tor-chk:checked')].map(c => _hygieneTorItems[parseInt(c.dataset.idx)]).filter(Boolean);
    if (!chosen.length) return alert('Отметьте хотя бы одну раздачу');
    if (!confirm(`Удалить из qBittorrent (${chosen.length} шт.) и переместить файлы в корзину _tbr_trash?`)) return;
    if (!confirm('Подтвердите повторно перенос выбранных раздач в _tbr_trash:')) return;
    const res = await api('/api/hygiene/delete_torrents_soft', 'POST', { items: chosen });
    const d = await res.json();
    alert(`Удалено из qBittorrent: ${d.removed || 0}\nПеренесено в _tbr_trash: ${d.trashed || 0}\nЗащищено общих папок: ${d.skipped_shared || 0}`);
    runHygieneTorrentScan(_hygieneMode);
    if (typeof loadTrashItems === 'function') loadTrashItems();
}

async function runHygienePathScan(mode) {
    _pathMode = mode;
    const tbody = document.getElementById('hyg-path-tbody');
    const summary = document.getElementById('hyg-path-summary');
    const delBtn = document.getElementById('btn-hyg-path-trash');
    const masterChk = document.getElementById('hyg-path-chk-all');
    if (masterChk) masterChk.checked = false;
    if (!tbody) return;
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">⏳ Сканирование...</td></tr>';
    if (delBtn) delBtn.classList.add('hidden');

    const res = await api(mode === 'bt_backup' ? '/api/hygiene/bt_backup' : '/api/hygiene/inner_junk');
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) {
        summary.textContent = d.error || 'Ошибка';
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(d.error || 'Ошибка')}</td></tr>`;
        return;
    }
    _hygienePathItems = d.items || [];
    summary.textContent = `Найдено: ${_hygienePathItems.length}`;
    if (!_hygienePathItems.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg accent-txt">🎉 Лишних файлов не найдено!</td></tr>';
        return;
    }
    if (delBtn) delBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
    tbody.innerHTML = _hygienePathItems.map((it, idx) => `
        <tr>
            <td><input type="checkbox" class="hyg-path-chk" data-idx="${idx}"></td>
            <td style="white-space:normal;"><b>${escapeHtml(it.name)}</b><div class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(it.torrent_name || it.path)}</div></td>
            <td><span class="badge badge-stall">${escapeHtml(it.reason || it.category)}</span></td>
            <td><b>${escapeHtml(it.size_fmt)}</b></td>
        </tr>
    `).join('');
}

async function trashSelectedHygienePaths() {
    const paths = [...document.querySelectorAll('.hyg-path-chk:checked')].map(c => _hygienePathItems[parseInt(c.dataset.idx)]?.path).filter(Boolean);
    if (!paths.length) return alert('Отметьте файлы');
    if (!confirm(`Переместить выбранные файлы (${paths.length} шт.) в _tbr_trash?`)) return;
    const res = await api('/api/hygiene/trash_paths', 'POST', { paths, reason: _pathMode });
    const d = await res.json();
    alert(`Перенесено в корзину: ${d.moved || 0}`);
    runHygienePathScan(_pathMode);
    if (typeof loadTrashItems === 'function') loadTrashItems();
}

async function syncTorrentArchive() {
    const res = await api('/api/archive/sync', 'POST');
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка');
    alert(`✅ Архив синхронизирован! Новых: ${d.added}, всего: ${d.total}`);
    loadArchivedTorrents();
}

async function loadArchivedTorrents() {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('archive-tbody');
    if (!tbody) return;
    const q = document.getElementById('archive-search')?.value || '';
    const tag = document.getElementById('archive-tag')?.value || '';
    const res = await api(`/api/archive/list?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&tag=${encodeURIComponent(tag)}`);
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const items = (await res.json()).items || [];
    if (!items.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="3" class="center-msg">Архив пуст</td></tr>'; return; }
    tbody.innerHTML = items.slice(0, 100).map(it => `
        <tr>
            <td style="white-space:normal;"><b>${escapeHtml(it.name)}</b><div class="lbl-xs muted-text">UID: ${escapeHtml(it.uid.slice(0, 10))} • ${escapeHtml(it.tags || 'без тегов')}</div></td>
            <td>${escapeHtml(it.gz_fmt)}</td>
            <td><a class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" href="/api/archive/download?hash=${encodeURIComponent(it.hash_id)}">⬇️ .torrent</a></td>
        </tr>
    `).join('');
}

function downloadArchiveZip() {
    const tag = document.getElementById('archive-tag')?.value.trim() || '';
    window.location.href = `/api/archive/export_zip?tag=${encodeURIComponent(tag)}`;
}

async function previewBatchTrackers() {
    const find = document.getElementById('batch-tr-find').value;
    const replace = document.getElementById('batch-tr-rep').value;
    const out = document.getElementById('batch-op-out');
    out.textContent = '⏳ Поиск совпадений...';
    const res = await api('/api/batch/trackers/preview', 'POST', { find, replace });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!d.success) { out.textContent = `❌ ${d.error}`; return; }
    out.textContent = `Найдено URL: ${d.count}\n` + (d.matches || []).slice(0, 20).map(m => `${m.name}: ${m.old_url} → ${m.new_url}`).join('\n');
}

async function applyBatchTrackers() {
    const find = document.getElementById('batch-tr-find').value;
    const replace = document.getElementById('batch-tr-rep').value;
    if (!confirm('Применить замену URL трекеров в qBittorrent?')) return;
    const res = await api('/api/batch/trackers/apply', 'POST', { find, replace });
    const d = await res.json();
    document.getElementById('batch-op-out').textContent = d.success ? `✅ Обновлено URL: ${d.updated} из ${d.total}` : `❌ ${d.error}`;
}

async function previewBatchRelocate() {
    const old_prefix = document.getElementById('batch-rel-old').value;
    const new_prefix = document.getElementById('batch-rel-new').value;
    const out = document.getElementById('batch-op-out');
    const res = await api('/api/batch/relocate/preview', 'POST', { old_prefix, new_prefix });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!d.success) { out.textContent = `❌ ${d.error}`; return; }
    out.textContent = `Раздач: ${d.count} | Нужно места: ${d.need_fmt} | Свободно: ${d.free_fmt} (${d.space_ok ? 'ОК' : 'МАЛО МЕСТА!'})\n` +
        (d.items || []).slice(0, 20).map(i => `${i.old_path} → ${i.new_path}`).join('\n');
}

function pollBatchRelocateStatus() {
    if (_relocatePollTimer) clearInterval(_relocatePollTimer);
    _relocatePollTimer = setInterval(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api('/api/batch/relocate/status');
            if (!res.ok) return;
            const st = await res.json();
            const out = document.getElementById('batch-op-out');
            if (out) {
                out.textContent = `🚚 Переезд: всего ${st.total} | Готово: ${st.done} | В процессе: ${st.moving} | В очереди: ${st.pending}`;
            }
            if (!st.running && st.pending === 0 && st.moving === 0) {
                clearInterval(_relocatePollTimer);
                _relocatePollTimer = null;
            }
        } catch (e) {}
    }, 3000);
}

async function startBatchRelocate() {
    const old_prefix = document.getElementById('batch-rel-old').value;
    const new_prefix = document.getElementById('batch-rel-new').value;
    if (!confirm('Запустить фоновый пакетный переезд раздач в qBittorrent?')) return;
    const res = await api('/api/batch/relocate/start', 'POST', { old_prefix, new_prefix });
    const d = await res.json();
    document.getElementById('batch-op-out').textContent = d.success ? `🚀 В очереди переезда: ${d.queued} раздач` : `❌ ${d.error}`;
    if (d.success) pollBatchRelocateStatus();
}

async function previewBatchRemap() {
    const old_prefix = document.getElementById('batch-rem-old').value;
    const new_prefix = document.getElementById('batch-rem-new').value;
    const res = await api('/api/batch/remap/preview', 'POST', { old_prefix, new_prefix });
    const d = await res.json();
    document.getElementById('batch-op-out').textContent = d.success
        ? `Найдено .fastresume: ${d.count} (qB запущен: ${d.qb_running ? 'ДА — остановите перед записью!' : 'НЕТ'})\n` + (d.items || []).slice(0, 20).map(i => `${i.old_path} → ${i.new_path}`).join('\n')
        : `❌ ${d.error}`;
}

async function applyBatchRemap() {
    const old_prefix = document.getElementById('batch-rem-old').value;
    const new_prefix = document.getElementById('batch-rem-new').value;
    if (!confirm('Переписать save_path в локальных .fastresume файлах? (Снапшот будет создан автоматически)')) return;
    const res = await api('/api/batch/remap/apply', 'POST', { old_prefix, new_prefix });
    const d = await res.json();
    document.getElementById('batch-op-out').textContent = d.success ? `✅ Обновлено .fastresume: ${d.updated}` : `❌ ${d.error || (d.errors || []).join(', ')}`;
}

================================================================================
FILE: web/static/i18n.js
================================================================================
// web/static/i18n.js
const I18N = { ru: I18N_RU, en: I18N_EN };
let currentLang = localStorage.getItem('tbr_lang') || 'ru';

function t(key, vars = {}) {
    let str = (I18N[currentLang] && I18N[currentLang][key]) || I18N.ru[key] || key;
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(vars)) {
        str = str.replace(new RegExp(`\\{${k}\\}`, 'g'), v);
    }
    return str;
}

function trBackend(str) {
    if (!str || currentLang === 'ru') return str || '';
    const s = String(str);
    if (s === 'ожидание замера') return 'waiting for snapshot';
    if (s === 'прошлый полный час (<5м нового)') return 'previous full hour (<5m elapsed)';
    if (s === 'по снимку ~1ч назад') return 'from snapshot ~1h ago';
    if (s === 'расчётное от суток') return 'estimated from 24h';
    const mMin = s.match(/^прошло\s+(\d+)\s+мин\s+с\s+техлога$/);
    if (mMin) return `${mMin[1]} min since last snapshot`;
    if (s === 'Дата неизвестна') return 'Unknown date';

    return s
        .replace(/МБ\/день/g, 'MB/day')
        .replace(/МБ\/ч/g, 'MB/h')
        .replace(/\/\s*час/g, '/ h')
        .replace(/\/\s*день/g, '/ day')
        .replace(/\/ч/g, '/h')
        .replace(/МБ/g, 'MB');
}

function applyI18n() {
    document.title = t('title');
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el => {
        el.textContent = t(el.dataset.i18n);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-ph]').forEach(el => {
        el.placeholder = t(el.dataset.i18nPh);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-title]').forEach(el => {
        el.title = t(el.dataset.i18nTitle);
    });
    const btn = document.getElementById('lang-toggle-btn');
    if (btn) btn.textContent = currentLang === 'ru' ? '🌐 RU' : '🌐 EN';
}

function toggleLang() {
    currentLang = currentLang === 'ru' ? 'en' : 'ru';
    localStorage.setItem('tbr_lang', currentLang);
    applyI18n();
    if (typeof checkQbStatus === 'function') checkQbStatus();
    if (typeof filterTorrents === 'function' && torrentsData.length) filterTorrents();
    if (typeof renderSummaryMeta === 'function') renderSummaryMeta();
    if (typeof applySummarySortAndRender === 'function' && rawSummaryItems.length) applySummarySortAndRender();
    if (typeof populateAnalyticsSelectors === 'function' && rawSummaryItems.length) populateAnalyticsSelectors();
    if (typeof onSmartCalcTargetChange === 'function') onSmartCalcTargetChange();
    if (typeof renderRules === 'function' && currentRules.length) renderRules(currentRules);
}

// web/static/merger_tools.js
function onMergerSelectionChange() {
    updateMergerDiffBtn();
    updateMergerCalcPreview();
}

function updateMergerDiffBtn() {
    const oldH = document.getElementById('merger-old')?.value || '';
    const newH = document.getElementById('merger-new')?.value || '';
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-merger-diff');
    if (btn) btn.disabled = (!oldH || !newH || oldH.toLowerCase() === newH.toLowerCase());
}

async function updateMergerCalcPreview() {
    const oldH = (document.getElementById('merger-old')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
    const newH = (document.getElementById('merger-new')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
    const mode = document.getElementById('merger-mode')?.value || 'add';
    const txtEl = document.getElementById('merger-calc-txt');
    const dupWarn = document.getElementById('merger-dup-warn');

    if (!txtEl) return;
    if (!oldH || !newH || oldH === newH) {
        txtEl.textContent = '—';
        if (dupWarn) dupWarn.classList.add('hidden');
        return;
    }

    const items = typeof torrentsData !== 'undefined' ? torrentsData : [];
    const src = items.find(x => x.hash.toLowerCase() === oldH);
    const dst = items.find(x => x.hash.toLowerCase() === newH);

    const srcUp = parseInt(src?.uploaded || 0);
    const dstUp = parseInt(dst?.uploaded || 0);

    if (mode === 'add') {
        const sumUp = srcUp + dstUp;
        txtEl.textContent = `${formatBytesJS(srcUp)} + ${formatBytesJS(dstUp)} = ${formatBytesJS(sumUp)}`;
    } else {
        txtEl.textContent = `${formatBytesJS(srcUp)} (перезапишет текущие ${formatBytesJS(dstUp)})`;
    }

    if (dupWarn) {
        try {
            const res = await api(`/api/merger/check_pair?old_hash=${encodeURIComponent(oldH)}&new_hash=${encodeURIComponent(newH)}`);
            const d = await res.json();
            dupWarn.classList.toggle('hidden', !(d && d.has_history));
        } catch (e) {
            dupWarn.classList.add('hidden');
        }
    }
}

async function loadSmartMatchCandidates() {
    const box = document.getElementById('smart-match-list');
    if (!box) return;
    box.innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">⏳ Поиск кандидатов на объединение...</span>';
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/merger/candidates');
        if (!res.ok) {
            box.innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">Ошибка подбора кандидатов</span>';
            return;
        }
        const d = await res.json();
        const list = d.candidates || [];
        if (!list.length) {
            box.innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">Совпадающих пар для объединения не найдено.</span>';
            return;
        }
        box.innerHTML = '';
        const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        list.forEach(c => {
            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.className = 'row-between';
            row.style.cssText = 'background:var(--bg-input); padding:6px 8px; border-radius:6px; gap:8px;';
            const sumBytes = (c.old_up_bytes || 0) + (c.new_up_bytes || 0);
            row.innerHTML = `
                <div style="min-width:0; flex:1;">
                    <div style="overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap;">
                        <b>${escapeHtml(c.old_name)}</b> <span class="muted-text">(${escapeHtml(c.old_up_fmt)})</span>
                        → <b>${escapeHtml(c.new_name)}</b> <span class="muted-text">(${escapeHtml(c.new_up_fmt)})</span>
                        <span class="accent-txt" style="font-weight:700;">[Σ ${formatBytesJS(sumBytes)}]</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(c.reason)}</div>
                </div>
                <button class="btn btn-xs" onclick="applySmartPair('${escapeHtml(c.old_hash)}', '${escapeHtml(c.new_hash)}')">Подставить</button>
            `;
            frag.appendChild(row);
        });
        box.appendChild(frag);
    } catch (e) {
        box.innerHTML = `<span class="muted-text">Ошибка: ${escapeHtml(e.message)}</span>`;
    }
}

function applySmartPair(oldHash, newHash) {
    const o = document.getElementById('merger-old');
    const n = document.getElementById('merger-new');
    if (o) o.value = oldHash;
    if (n) n.value = newHash;
    onMergerSelectionChange();
}

async function openTorrentDiffModal() {
    const oldH = document.getElementById('merger-old')?.value || '';
    const newH = document.getElementById('merger-new')?.value || '';
    if (!oldH || !newH || oldH.toLowerCase() === newH.toLowerCase()) return alert(t('err_same_torrent'));

    const modal = document.getElementById('diff-modal');
    const tbody = document.getElementById('diff-tbody');
    const badges = document.getElementById('diff-summary-badges');
    const matchBadge = document.getElementById('diff-match-badge');

    modal.style.display = 'flex';
    matchBadge.textContent = '⏳ Анализ...';
    badges.innerHTML = '';
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">⏳ Читаем списки файлов...</td></tr>';

    try {
        const res = await api(`/api/merger/diff?old_hash=${encodeURIComponent(oldH)}&new_hash=${encodeURIComponent(newH)}`);
        const d = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || !d.success) {
            matchBadge.textContent = 'Ошибка';
            tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(d.error || 'Ошибка анализа')}</td></tr>`;
            return;
        }
        matchBadge.textContent = `Совпадение: ${d.match_percent}%`;
        const st = d.stats || {};
        badges.innerHTML = `
            <span class="badge badge-up">✅ Совпадают: ${st.exact || 0}</span>
            <span class="badge badge-stall">⚠️ Размер изменён: ${st.changed || 0}</span>
            <span class="badge badge-stop">➖ Удалены: ${st.removed || 0}</span>
            <span class="badge badge-dl">➕ Новые: ${st.added || 0}</span>
            <span class="badge badge-pause">${escapeHtml(d.old_total_fmt)} → ${escapeHtml(d.new_total_fmt)}</span>
        `;
        const files = d.files || [];
        if (!files.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">Файлы не найдены</td></tr>'; return; }
        tbody.innerHTML = '';
        const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        const statusMap = { exact: { cls: 'diff-row-exact', badge: 'badge-up', txt: '100% ОК' }, changed: { cls: 'diff-row-changed', badge: 'badge-stall', txt: 'Размер ≠' }, removed: { cls: 'diff-row-removed', badge: 'badge-stop', txt: 'Удалён' }, added: { cls: 'diff-row-added', badge: 'badge-dl', txt: 'Новый' } };
        files.forEach(f => {
            const s = statusMap[f.status] || statusMap.exact;
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.className = s.cls;
            tr.innerHTML = `<td style="white-space:normal; word-break:break-all;">${escapeHtml(f.path)}</td><td><span class="badge ${s.badge}">${s.txt}</span></td><td>${escapeHtml(f.old_size_fmt)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(f.new_size_fmt)}</td>`;
            frag.appendChild(tr);
        });
        tbody.appendChild(frag);
    } catch (e) {
        matchBadge.textContent = 'Ошибка';
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(e.message)}</td></tr>`;
    }
}

function closeTorrentDiffModal() { document.getElementById('diff-modal').style.display = 'none'; }

async function scanOrphans() {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('orphan-tbody');
    const summary = document.getElementById('orphan-summary');
    const rootsInfo = document.getElementById('orphan-roots-info');
    const delBtn = document.getElementById('btn-delete-orphans');
    const custom = document.getElementById('orphan-custom-root')?.value.trim();

    summary.textContent = '⏳ Сканирование...';
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg">⏳ Сверка файловой системы с торрентами...</td></tr>';
    delBtn.classList.add('hidden');

    try {
        const body = custom ? { roots: [custom] } : {};
        const res = await api('/api/tools/orphans/scan', 'POST', body);
        const d = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || !d.success) {
            summary.textContent = 'Ошибка';
            tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(d.error || 'Ошибка')}</td></tr>`;
            return;
        }
        rootsInfo.textContent = 'Проверенные каталоги: ' + ((d.roots || []).join(', ') || 'не найдены');
        summary.textContent = `Найдено: ${d.count} (${d.total_size_fmt})`;

        const list = d.orphans || [];
        if (!list.length) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--accent);">🎉 Лишних файлов и папок не найдено!</td></tr>';
            return;
        }
        tbody.innerHTML = '';
        const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        list.forEach(item => {
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.innerHTML = `
                <td><input type="checkbox" class="orphan-chk" value="${escapeHtml(item.path)}" onchange="updateOrphanDelBtn()"></td>
                <td style="white-space:normal; word-break:break-all;" title="${escapeHtml(item.path)}"><b>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</b><div class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(item.root)}</div></td>
                <td>${item.is_dir ? '📁 Папка' : '📄 Файл'}</td>
                <td><b>${escapeHtml(item.size_fmt)}</b></td>
                <td class="muted-text">${escapeHtml(item.date)}</td>
            `;
            frag.appendChild(tr);
        });
        tbody.appendChild(frag);
    } catch (e) {
        summary.textContent = 'Ошибка';
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(e.message)}</td></tr>`;
    }
}

function toggleAllOrphans(master) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.orphan-chk').forEach(c => { c.checked = master.checked; });
    updateOrphanDelBtn();
}

function updateOrphanDelBtn() {
    const cnt = document.querySelectorAll('.orphan-chk:checked').length;
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-delete-orphans');
    if (btn) {
        btn.classList.toggle('hidden', cnt === 0);
        btn.textContent = `🗑️ В корзину _tbr_trash (${cnt})`;
    }
}

async function deleteSelectedOrphans() {
    const paths = [...document.querySelectorAll('.orphan-chk:checked')].map(c => c.value);
    if (!paths.length) return;
    if (!confirm(`Переместить выбранные объекты (${paths.length} шт.) в корзину _tbr_trash?\n(Можно восстановить в течение 7 дней)`)) return;
    if (!confirm(`Подтвердите перенос ${paths.length} объектов в корзину _tbr_trash:`)) return;

    const custom = document.getElementById('orphan-custom-root')?.value.trim();
    const res = await api('/api/tools/orphans/delete', 'POST', {
        paths, confirmed: true, use_trash: true, custom_roots: custom ? [custom] : undefined
    });
    const d = await res.json();
    const msg = `Перенесено в _tbr_trash: ${(d.deleted || []).length}` + ((d.errors && d.errors.length) ? `\nОшибки:\n${d.errors.join('\n')}` : '');
    alert(msg);
    scanOrphans();
    if (typeof loadTrashItems === 'function') loadTrashItems();
}

async function loadVaultSettings() {
    const inp = document.getElementById('vault-tags-input');
    const sel = document.getElementById('vault-aggregate-mode');
    if (!inp || !sel) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/vault/settings');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        inp.value = (d.tags || []).join(', ');
        sel.value = d.aggregate_mode || 'exclude';
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function saveVaultSettings() {
    const tags = document.getElementById('vault-tags-input')?.value || '';
    const aggregate_mode = document.getElementById('vault-aggregate-mode')?.value || 'exclude';
    const res = await api('/api/vault/settings', 'POST', { tags, aggregate_mode });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка сохранения Private Vault');
    alert(`✅ Private Vault сохранён (${(d.tags || []).length} тегов).`);
}

// web/static/palette_targets.js
let _cmdItems = [];
let _cmdIdx = 0;
let _ftsDebounce = null;

function renderTargetBarHtml(target) {
    if (!target) return '';
    const pct = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, parseFloat(target.percent || 0)));
    const color = target.achieved ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--info)';
    const goalTxt = target.target_ratio > 0 ? `${target.target_ratio}x` : target.target_bytes_fmt;
    return `
        <div style="margin-top:4px; width:100%;">
            <div class="row-between lbl-xs muted-text" style="font-size:10px; margin-bottom:2px;">
                <span>🎯 Цель: ${escapeHtml(goalTxt)}</span>
                <span style="color:${color}; font-weight:700;">${pct}%</span>
            </div>
            <div style="height:4px; background:var(--bg-main); border-radius:2px; overflow:hidden;">
                <div style="height:100%; width:${pct}%; background:${color}; transition:width 0.25s;"></div>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
}

function openCommandPalette() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('cmd-palette-modal');
    const inp = document.getElementById('cmd-palette-input');
    if (!modal || !inp) return;
    modal.style.display = 'flex';
    inp.value = '';
    _cmdIdx = 0;
    renderCommandPalette();
    inp.focus();
}

function closeCommandPalette() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('cmd-palette-modal');
    if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
}

function _buildCommandActions() {
    const actions = [
        { label: '⚡ Вкладка: Раздачи', run: () => switchTab('torrents') },
        { label: '📊 Вкладка: Сводка', run: () => switchTab('summary') },
        { label: '📈 Вкладка: Аналитика', run: () => switchTab('analytics') },
        { label: '🛠️ Вкладка: Инструменты', run: () => switchTab('tools') },
        { label: '🖥️ Режим Киоск (/kiosk)', run: () => window.open('/kiosk', '_blank') },
        { label: '🎨 Тема: Dark (Тёмная)', run: () => _quickSetTheme('dark') },
        { label: '☀️ Тема: Light (Светлая)', run: () => _quickSetTheme('light') },
        { label: '🖤 Тема: OLED (Чёрная)', run: () => _quickSetTheme('oled') },
    ];
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE === 'undefined' || CURRENT_ROLE === 'admin') {
        actions.push(
            { label: '🔀 Вкладка: Подменщик', run: () => switchTab('merger') },
            { label: '🤖 Вкладка: Автопилот', run: () => switchTab('automation') },
            { label: '❄️ Умная заморозка / Разморозка', run: () => toggleSmartFreeze() },
            { label: '😴 Таймер сна (Snooze)', run: () => openSnoozeModal() },
            { label: '🔄 Переиндексировать файлы FTS5', run: () => api('/api/search/reindex', 'POST', { force: false }).then(() => alert('✅ Индекс файлов обновлён')) },
            { label: '⚙️ Настройки системы', run: () => openSettingsModal() }
        );
    }
    return actions;
}

function _quickSetTheme(theme) {
    if (typeof getUiSettings === 'function' && typeof persistUiSettings === 'function') {
        const ui = getUiSettings();
        ui.theme = theme;
        persistUiSettings(ui);
    }
}

function renderCommandPalette(extraFiles = []) {
    const rawQ = (document.getElementById('cmd-palette-input')?.value || '').trim();
    const q = rawQ.toLowerCase();
    const listEl = document.getElementById('cmd-palette-list');
    if (!listEl) return;

    const actions = _buildCommandActions().filter(a => !q || a.label.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    const source = (typeof torrentsData !== 'undefined' && torrentsData.length)
        ? torrentsData : (typeof rawSummaryItems !== 'undefined' ? rawSummaryItems : []);

    const matchedTorrents = [];
    for (const item of source) {
        const nm = String(item.name || ''), hs = String(item.hash || item.hash_id || '');
        if (!q || nm.toLowerCase().includes(q) || hs.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
            matchedTorrents.push({
                label: `📦 ${nm}`,
                sub: `${hs.slice(0, 10)} • ${item.uploaded_fmt || formatBytesJS(item.uploaded)}`,
                run: () => {
                    switchTab('torrents'); selectedHash = hs;
                    const t = torrentsData.find(x => x.hash === hs);
                    if (t) { document.getElementById('selected-title').textContent = `${t.name} (${t.size_fmt})`; renderTorrents(visibleTorrents); }
                }
            });
        }
        if (matchedTorrents.length >= 15) break;
    }

    const fileItems = extraFiles.map(f => ({
        label: `📄 ${f.file_path}`,
        sub: `${f.torrent_name} (${f.size_fmt})`,
        run: () => { switchTab('torrents'); selectedHash = f.hash; }
    }));

    _cmdItems = [...actions, ...matchedTorrents, ...fileItems].slice(0, 28);
    if (_cmdIdx >= _cmdItems.length) _cmdIdx = 0;

    listEl.innerHTML = '';
    if (!_cmdItems.length) { listEl.innerHTML = '<div class="center-msg muted-text">Ничего не найдено</div>'; return; }
    _cmdItems.forEach((it, idx) => {
        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'row-between pointer';
        row.style.cssText = `padding:8px 10px; border-radius:6px; background:${idx === _cmdIdx ? 'rgba(46,204,113,0.18)' : 'var(--bg-input)'}; border:1px solid ${idx === _cmdIdx ? 'var(--accent)' : 'transparent'};`;
        row.innerHTML = `<span style="font-size:12.5px; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap;">${escapeHtml(it.label)}</span>` +
            (it.sub ? `<span class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(it.sub)}</span>` : '');
        row.onclick = () => { closeCommandPalette(); it.run(); };
        listEl.appendChild(row);
    });

    if (!extraFiles.length && rawQ.length >= 3) {
        clearTimeout(_ftsDebounce);
        _ftsDebounce = setTimeout(async () => {
            try {
                const clean = rawQ.replace(/^file:/i, '').trim();
                if (clean.length < 2) return;
                const res = await api(`/api/search/files?q=${encodeURIComponent(clean)}`);
                if (res.ok && document.getElementById('cmd-palette-modal').style.display === 'flex') {
                    const files = (await res.json()).items || [];
                    if (files.length) renderCommandPalette(files);
                }
            } catch (e) {}
        }, 300);
    }
}

function onCmdPaletteKey(e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { closeCommandPalette(); return; }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') { e.preventDefault(); if (_cmdItems.length) _cmdIdx = (_cmdIdx + 1) % _cmdItems.length; renderCommandPalette(); }
    else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') { e.preventDefault(); if (_cmdItems.length) _cmdIdx = (_cmdIdx - 1 + _cmdItems.length) % _cmdItems.length; renderCommandPalette(); }
    else if (e.key === 'Enter' && _cmdItems[_cmdIdx]) { e.preventDefault(); const c = _cmdItems[_cmdIdx]; closeCommandPalette(); c.run(); }
}

function openTargetModal() {
    if (typeof selectedHash === 'undefined' || !selectedHash) return alert(t('err_select_torrent'));
    const item = (typeof torrentsData !== 'undefined' ? torrentsData : []).find(x => x.hash === selectedHash);
    document.getElementById('tgt-torrent-name').textContent = item ? item.name : selectedHash;
    const tgt = item?.target || null;
    document.getElementById('tgt-ratio').value = tgt && tgt.target_ratio > 0 ? tgt.target_ratio : '';
    document.getElementById('tgt-gib').value = tgt && tgt.target_bytes > 0 ? Math.round(tgt.target_bytes / (1024 ** 3)) : '';
    document.getElementById('btn-tgt-remove').classList.toggle('hidden', !tgt);
    document.getElementById('target-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}
function closeTargetModal() { document.getElementById('target-modal').style.display = 'none'; }

async function saveTargetForSelected() {
    const tr = parseFloat(document.getElementById('tgt-ratio').value) || 0, gib = parseFloat(document.getElementById('tgt-gib').value) || 0;
    const res = await api('/api/targets/set', 'POST', { hash: selectedHash, target_ratio: tr, target_bytes: gib > 0 ? Math.round(gib * (1024 ** 3)) : 0 });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка');
    closeTargetModal(); loadTorrents(false);
}

async function removeTargetForSelected() {
    const res = await api('/api/targets/delete', 'POST', { hash: selectedHash });
    if (res.ok) { closeTargetModal(); loadTorrents(false); }
}

// web/static/phase3_ui.js
function openPreflightModal() {
    document.getElementById('pf-url').value = '';
    document.getElementById('pf-file').value = '';
    document.getElementById('pf-save-path').value = '';
    document.getElementById('pf-tags').value = '';
    document.getElementById('pf-paused').checked = false;
    document.getElementById('pf-result-box').innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">Вставьте magnet/ссылку или выберите .torrent и нажмите «Проверить».</span>';
    document.getElementById('preflight-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}

async function runPreflightCheck() {
    const box = document.getElementById('pf-result-box');
    const fileInp = document.getElementById('pf-file');
    const url = document.getElementById('pf-url').value.trim();
    const savePath = document.getElementById('pf-save-path').value.trim();
    box.innerHTML = '⏳ Проверка места и поиск дублей...';

    let res;
    if (fileInp.files.length) {
        const fd = new FormData();
        fd.append('file', fileInp.files[0]);
        fd.append('save_path', savePath);
        res = await fetch('/api/inbox/preflight', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-CSRF-Token': CSRF_TOKEN }, body: fd });
    } else {
        res = await api('/api/inbox/preflight', 'POST', { url, save_path: savePath });
    }
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!d.success) { box.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--danger);">❌ ${escapeHtml(d.error || 'Ошибка')}</span>`; return; }

    const simHtml = (d.similar || []).length
        ? `<div class="mt-10" style="color:var(--warning);"><b>⚠️ Похожие раздачи в клиенте:</b><br>${d.similar.map(s => `• ${escapeHtml(s.name)} (${s.similarity}%)`).join('<br>')}</div>`
        : '<div class="lbl-xs accent-txt mt-10">✅ Дубликатов в библиотеке не найдено</div>';
    const warnHtml = d.warning ? `<div class="lbl-xs orange-txt mt-10">ℹ️ ${escapeHtml(d.warning)}</div>` : '';
    box.innerHTML = `
        <div><b>${escapeHtml(d.name)}</b> (${escapeHtml(d.size_fmt)})</div>
        <div class="lbl-xs muted-text mt-10">Свободно на диске: <b>${escapeHtml(d.disk_free_fmt)}</b> • В очереди: <b>${escapeHtml(d.queue_left_fmt)}</b> • Реально свободно: <b class="${d.space_ok ? 'accent-txt' : 'orange-txt'}">${escapeHtml(d.real_free_fmt)}</b></div>
        ${warnHtml}${simHtml}
    `;
}

async function submitPreflightAdd() {
    const fileInp = document.getElementById('pf-file');
    const url = document.getElementById('pf-url').value.trim();
    const savePath = document.getElementById('pf-save-path').value.trim();
    const tags = document.getElementById('pf-tags').value.trim();
    const paused = document.getElementById('pf-paused').checked;

    let res;
    if (fileInp.files.length) {
        const fd = new FormData();
        fd.append('file', fileInp.files[0]);
        fd.append('save_path', savePath);
        fd.append('tags', tags);
        fd.append('paused', paused ? '1' : '0');
        res = await fetch('/api/inbox/add', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-CSRF-Token': CSRF_TOKEN }, body: fd });
    } else {
        res = await api('/api/inbox/add', 'POST', { url, save_path: savePath, tags, paused });
    }
    const d = await res.json();
    alert(d.message || d.error);
    if (d.success) {
        document.getElementById('preflight-modal').style.display = 'none';
        if (typeof loadTorrents === 'function') loadTorrents(true);
    }
}

async function toggleSelectedSniper() {
    if (typeof selectedHash === 'undefined' || !selectedHash) return alert(t('err_select_torrent'));
    const res = await api('/api/snipers', 'POST', { hash: selectedHash, idle_hours: 4 });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.success) alert(d.active ? '🎯 Маячок включён! Пришлёт пуш по завершении, зависании (>4ч) или нехватке места.' : '🔕 Маячок снят.');
}

async function loadSavedViews() {
    const bar = document.getElementById('saved-views-bar');
    if (!bar) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/views');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const views = (await res.json()).views || [];
        bar.innerHTML = views.map(v => `
            <span class="badge badge-dl pointer" style="gap:5px;" onclick='applySavedView(${JSON.stringify(v.filters)})'>
                🔖 ${escapeHtml(v.name)}
                ${CURRENT_ROLE === 'admin' ? `<b onclick="event.stopPropagation(); deleteSavedView(${v.id})">✕</b>` : ''}
            </span>
        `).join('') + (CURRENT_ROLE === 'admin' ? `<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" onclick="saveCurrentView()">+ Вид</button>` : '');
    } catch (e) {}
}

function applySavedView(flt) {
    if (!flt) return;
    if (document.getElementById('search-box')) document.getElementById('search-box').value = flt.q || '';
    if (document.getElementById('filter-tag-select')) document.getElementById('filter-tag-select').value = flt.tag || '';
    if (document.getElementById('filter-tracker-select')) document.getElementById('filter-tracker-select').value = flt.tracker || '';
    if (document.getElementById('filter-tracker-err-select')) document.getElementById('filter-tracker-err-select').value = flt.err || '';
    if (typeof filterTorrents === 'function') filterTorrents();
}

async function saveCurrentView() {
    const name = prompt('Название быстрого вида (например: Ошибки или Игры):');
    if (!name || !name.trim()) return;
    const filters = {
        q: document.getElementById('search-box')?.value || '',
        tag: document.getElementById('filter-tag-select')?.value || '',
        tracker: document.getElementById('filter-tracker-select')?.value || '',
        err: document.getElementById('filter-tracker-err-select')?.value || ''
    };
    await api('/api/views', 'POST', { name: name.trim(), filters });
    loadSavedViews();
}

async function deleteSavedView(id) {
    await api('/api/views/delete', 'POST', { id });
    loadSavedViews();
}

            document.getElementById('iface-guard-en').checked = Boolean(d.enabled);
            const ifInp = document.getElementById('iface-guard-name');

            ifInp.placeholder = `Эталонный интерфейс (сейчас в qB: ${curLbl})`;
            document.getElementById('iface-guard-panic').checked = Boolean(d.panic_on_drop);

        if (whRes.ok) {
            const w = await whRes.json();
            document.getElementById('webhooks-urls').value = w.urls || '';
            document.getElementById('webhooks-bundler').checked = Boolean(w.bundler_enabled);
        }
    } catch (e) {}
}

// web/static/phase4_ui.js
let _hmMode = '365';
let _inspFilesPage = 1;
let _inspTotalPages = 1;

async function toggleHeatmapMode(mode) {
    _hmMode = mode;
    document.getElementById('heatmap-grid')?.classList.toggle('hidden', mode !== '365');
    document.getElementById('rhythm-7x24-box')?.classList.toggle('hidden', mode !== '7x24');
    if (mode === '7x24') {
        const box = document.getElementById('rhythm-7x24-box');
        box.innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">⏳ Расчёт медианы 7×24...</span>';
        const res = await api('/api/analytics/rhythm');
        const d = await res.json();
        const days = ['Пн', 'Вт', 'Ср', 'Чт', 'Пт', 'Сб', 'Вс'];
        const maxM = Math.max(0.1, d.max_mbs || 0.1);
        let html = '<table style="font-size:10px; text-align:center;"><thead><tr><th></th>' + Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, h) => `<th>${h}</th>`).join('') + '</tr></thead><tbody>';
        (d.matrix || []).forEach((row, wd) => {
            html += `<tr><td><b>${days[wd]}</b></td>` + row.map((v, hr) => {
                const op = v > 0 ? Math.min(1, 0.15 + (v / maxM) * 0.85) : 0.05;
                return `<td title="${days[wd]} ${hr}:00 — ${v} MiB/s" style="background:rgba(46,204,113,${op.toFixed(2)}); padding:3px 2px;">${v >= 1 ? Math.round(v) : ''}</td>`;
            }).join('') + '</tr>';
        });
        box.innerHTML = html + '</tbody></table>';
    }
}

async function loadDayHourlyBreakdown(dateStr) {
    const out = document.getElementById('heatmap-day-detail');
    if (!out) return;
    out.classList.remove('hidden');
    out.innerHTML = `⏳ Загрузка часов за ${escapeHtml(dateStr)}...`;
    const res = await api(`/api/analytics/day_hourly?date=${encodeURIComponent(dateStr)}`);
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!d.success) { out.textContent = d.error || 'Ошибка'; return; }
    if (!d.has_detail) { out.innerHTML = `📅 <b>${escapeHtml(dateStr)}</b>: детальные почасовые точки за этот архивный день отсутствуют.`; return; }
    out.innerHTML = `<b>📅 ${escapeHtml(dateStr)} по часам:</b> ` + ((d.hours || []).filter(h => h.bytes > 0).map(h => `<span class="badge badge-up" style="margin:2px;">${h.hour}: ${escapeHtml(h.fmt)}</span>`).join('') || 'Нулевая активность');
}
