    loadRunwayAndQuota();
    loadTreemap('tags');
    loadSwarmAnalytics();
    refreshActivityTicker();
}

async function loadRunwayAndQuota() {
    try {
        const [rRes, qRes] = await Promise.all([api('/api/analytics/runway'), api('/api/analytics/quota')]);
        if (rRes.ok) {
            const r = await rRes.json();
            const resInp = document.getElementById('inp-reserve-gib');
            if (resInp && document.activeElement !== resInp) resInp.value = r.reserve_gib ?? 20;
            document.getElementById('runway-box').innerHTML = `
                <div>Диск <b>${escapeHtml(r.disk_path)}</b>: свободно <b>${escapeHtml(r.free_fmt)}</b> (после очереди: <b class="${r.guard_triggered ? 'orange-txt' : 'accent-txt'}">${escapeHtml(r.post_queue_fmt)}</b>)</div>
                <div class="lbl-xs muted-text mt-10">Темп роста (30д): ${escapeHtml(r.daily_growth_fmt)} • Запас до резерва (${r.reserve_gib} GiB): <b>${r.days_left !== null ? r.days_left + ' дн.' : '∞'}</b></div>
            `;
        }
        if (qRes.ok) {
            const q = await qRes.json();
            const qInp = document.getElementById('inp-quota-tib');
            if (qInp && document.activeElement !== qInp) qInp.value = q.quota_tib > 0 ? q.quota_tib : '';
            document.getElementById('quota-box').innerHTML = `
                <div>За месяц: <b class="accent-txt">${escapeHtml(q.used_fmt)}</b> / <b>${escapeHtml(q.quota_fmt)}</b> (${q.percent}%)</div>
                <div style="height:6px; background:var(--bg-main); border-radius:3px; overflow:hidden; margin:6px 0;"><div style="height:100%; width:${q.percent}%; background:var(--accent);"></div></div>
                <div class="lbl-xs muted-text">Прогноз к концу месяца: ~${escapeHtml(q.projected_fmt)}</div>
            `;
        }
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function saveQuotaAndReserve() {
    const quota_tib = parseFloat(document.getElementById('inp-quota-tib')?.value || 0);
    const reserve_gib = parseInt(document.getElementById('inp-reserve-gib')?.value || 20);
    await api('/api/analytics/quota', 'POST', { quota_tib, reserve_gib });
    loadRunwayAndQuota();
}

async function runABCompare() {
    const payload = {
        start_a: document.getElementById('ab-a1').value, end_a: document.getElementById('ab-a2').value,
        start_b: document.getElementById('ab-b1').value, end_b: document.getElementById('ab-b2').value,
        same_n_days: document.getElementById('ab-same-n').checked
    };
    const res = await api('/api/analytics/ab_compare', 'POST', payload);
    const d = await res.json();
    const out = document.getElementById('ab-out');
    if (!d.success) { out.textContent = `❌ ${d.error}`; return; }
    out.innerHTML = `Период A: <b>${escapeHtml(d.period_a.fmt)}</b> (${d.period_a.avg_mbs} MiB/s, пик ${d.period_a.peak_hour}) vs Период B: <b>${escapeHtml(d.period_b.fmt)}</b> (${d.period_b.avg_mbs} MiB/s, пик ${d.period_b.peak_hour}) • Дельта: <b class="accent-txt">${d.diff_pct !== null ? d.diff_pct + '%' : '—'}</b>`;
}

async function loadTreemap(groupBy = 'tags') {
    const box = document.getElementById('treemap-box');
    if (!box) return;
    const res = await api(`/api/analytics/treemap?group_by=${encodeURIComponent(groupBy)}`);
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const items = (await res.json()).items || [];
    const maxB = Math.max(1, ...items.map(x => x.bytes));
    const cols = ['#2ecc71', '#3498db', '#9b59b6', '#e67e22', '#1abc9c', '#f39c12'];
    box.innerHTML = items.map((it, idx) => {
        const flex = Math.max(12, Math.round((it.bytes / maxB) * 100));
        return `<div style="flex:${flex} 1 110px; background:${cols[idx % cols.length]}22; border:1px solid ${cols[idx % cols.length]}; border-radius:6px; padding:6px 8px; min-width:110px;">
            <div style="font-size:11px; font-weight:700; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap;">${escapeHtml(it.label)}</div>
            <div class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(it.fmt)}</div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('') || '<span class="muted-text">Нет данных</span>';
}

async function loadSwarmAnalytics() {
    const box = document.getElementById('swarm-analytics-box');
    if (!box) return;
    const res = await api('/api/analytics/swarm');
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const d = await res.json();
    const p = d.protocols || {};
    const recs = (d.slot_recs || []).map(r => `<div>${escapeHtml(r)}</div>`).join('');
    const trRows = (d.survivability || []).slice(0, 8).map(t => `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(t.tracker)}</td><td>${t.count}</td><td>${t.uptime_pct}%</td><td><b>${t.gb_roi}x</b></td><td>${t.uploaded_gb} GiB</td></tr>`).join('');
    box.innerHTML = `
        <div class="mb-8 lbl-xs"><b>📡 Протоколы (топ-${p.sampled || 0} активных):</b> uTP: <b>${p.utp || 0}</b> (${p.utp_avg_kbs || 0} KB/s) • TCP: <b>${p.tcp || 0}</b> (${p.tcp_avg_kbs || 0} KB/s) • IPv4: <b>${p.ipv4 || 0}</b> • IPv6: <b>${p.ipv6 || 0}</b></div>
        <div class="mb-8 lbl-xs" style="background:var(--bg-main); padding:6px 8px; border-radius:5px;"><b>🎛️ Авто-тюнер qB:</b>${recs}</div>
        <div class="scroll-box" style="max-height:170px;"><table><thead><tr><th>Трекер</th><th>Раздач</th><th>Аптайм</th><th>GB-ROI</th><th>Отдано</th></tr></thead><tbody>${trRows}</tbody></table></div>
    `;
}

async function loadNormalizedTitleGroups() {
    const box = document.getElementById('title-groups-box');
    box.innerHTML = '⏳ Поиск похожих релизов...';
    const res = await api('/api/analytics/title_groups');
    const groups = (await res.json()).groups || [];
    box.innerHTML = groups.map(g => `<div class="mb-8" style="background:var(--bg-main); padding:6px 8px; border-radius:6px;"><b>${escapeHtml(g.title)}</b> (${g.count} версий) — ${formatBytesJS(g.total_size)}<div class="lbl-xs muted-text">${g.items.map(x => escapeHtml(x.name)).join(' | ')}</div></div>`).join('') || '<span class="muted-text">Дубликатов версий не найдено</span>';
}

async function openWrappedModal(period = 'month') {
    document.getElementById('wrapped-modal').style.display = 'flex';
    const res = await api(`/api/analytics/wrapped?period=${encodeURIComponent(period)}`);
    const d = await res.json();
    const c = document.getElementById('wrapped-canvas'), ctx = c.getContext('2d');
    ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0d12'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    const grd = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    grd.addColorStop(0, '#10b98133'); grd.addColorStop(1, '#9b5de533');
    ctx.fillStyle = grd; ctx.fillRect(20, 20, c.width - 40, c.height - 40);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#2ecc71'; ctx.font = 'bold 34px Segoe UI, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(`⚡ tbr PRO WRAPPED`, 50, 80);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#95a5a6'; ctx.font = 'bold 18px Segoe UI, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(d.period_label, 50, 115);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.font = 'bold 42px Segoe UI, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(d.total_uploaded_fmt, 50, 195);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#95a5a6'; ctx.font = '16px Segoe UI, sans-serif'; ctx.fillText(`Лучший день: ${d.best_day_date} (${d.best_day_fmt})`, 50, 250);
    ctx.fillText(`Спасено (Последний сид): ${d.last_seed_saved} • Раздач: ${d.visible_torrents}`, 50, 285);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#2ecc71'; ctx.fillText(`Топ теги: ${(d.top_tags || []).map(t => `${t.tag} (${t.fmt})`).join(', ') || '—'}`, 50, 330);
}

async function refreshActivityTicker() {
    const bar = document.getElementById('live-activity-ticker');
    if (!bar) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/activity/ticker');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const evs = (await res.json()).events || [];
        bar.innerHTML = evs.length ? `📡 <b>Пульс:</b> ` + evs.slice(0, 4).map(e => `<span class="badge badge-pause">[${e.time}] ${escapeHtml(e.text)}</span>`).join(' ') : '📡 <b>Пульс:</b> ожидание событий...';
    } catch (e) {}
}

function changeInspectorFilesPage(delta) {
    const h = (typeof _inspHash !== 'undefined' && _inspHash) ? _inspHash : selectedHash;
    if (!h) return;
    const next = Math.max(1, Math.min(_inspTotalPages, _inspFilesPage + delta));
    if (next !== _inspFilesPage) loadInspectorPhase4(h, next);
}

async function loadInspectorPhase4(tHash, page = 1) {
    _inspFilesPage = page;
    const [noteRes, timeRes, filesRes] = await Promise.all([
        api(`/api/torrent/notes?hash=${encodeURIComponent(tHash)}`),
        api(`/api/torrent/timeline?hash=${encodeURIComponent(tHash)}`),
        api(`/api/torrents/files?hash=${encodeURIComponent(tHash)}&page=${page}`)
    ]);
    if (noteRes.ok) document.getElementById('insp-note-inp').value = (await noteRes.json()).note || '';
    if (timeRes.ok) {
        const evs = (await timeRes.json()).events || [];
        document.getElementById('insp-timeline-box').innerHTML = evs.map(e => `<div class="lbl-xs"><b>[${escapeHtml(e.date)}]</b> ${escapeHtml(e.title)}</div>`).join('') || '<span class="muted-text lbl-xs">Событий по UID пока нет</span>';
    }
    if (filesRes.ok) {
        const fd = await filesRes.json();
        _inspTotalPages = fd.total_pages || 1;
        const pgInfo = document.getElementById('insp-files-page-info');
        if (pgInfo) pgInfo.textContent = `${_inspFilesPage} / ${_inspTotalPages} (${fd.total || 0})`;
        document.getElementById('insp-files-tbody').innerHTML = (fd.files || []).map(f => `
            <tr>
                <td style="white-space:normal; word-break:break-all;">${escapeHtml(f.name)}</td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(f.size_fmt)}</td>
                <td>${f.progress}%</td>
                <td><select class="form-control" style="padding:2px; font-size:11px;" onchange="setInspectorFilePrio('${escapeHtml(tHash)}', ${f.id}, this.value)"><option value="0" ${f.priority===0?'selected':''}>Пропуск</option><option value="1" ${f.priority===1?'selected':''}>Обычн.</option><option value="6" ${f.priority===6?'selected':''}>Высок.</option><option value="7" ${f.priority===7?'selected':''}>Макс.</option></select></td>
            </tr>
        `).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">Файлы недоступны</td></tr>';
    }
}

async function saveInspectorNote() {
    const h = (typeof _inspHash !== 'undefined' && _inspHash) ? _inspHash : selectedHash;
    if (!h) return;
    await api('/api/torrent/notes', 'POST', { hash: h, note: document.getElementById('insp-note-inp').value });
    alert('📝 Заметка сохранена (привязана к UID)');
}

async function setInspectorFilePrio(hash, id, prio) {
    await api('/api/torrents/file_prio', 'POST', { hash, id, priority: parseInt(prio) });
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    document.getElementById('heatmap-grid')?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const cell = e.target.closest('.hm-cell');
        if (cell?.title?.includes(':')) {
            const dStr = cell.title.split(':')[0].trim();
            if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dStr)) loadDayHourlyBreakdown(dStr);
        }
    });
});

// web/static/profiles.js
let CURRENT_ROLE = 'admin';
let CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS = null;
let _qrActiveProfile = null;

async function loadMeAndApplyRole() {
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/me');
        const d = await res.json();
        CURRENT_ROLE = d.role || 'viewer';
        CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS = d.ui_settings || null;
        if (typeof applyUiSettings === 'function' && d.ui_settings) applyUiSettings(d.ui_settings);
        if (CURRENT_ROLE === 'viewer') {
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-tab="merger"], [data-tab="automation"], .admin-only').forEach(el => el.classList.add('hidden'));
            document.querySelectorAll('button[onclick*="openSettingsModal"], button[onclick*="restartQb"], button[onclick*="scanDeadTorrents"], button[onclick*="torrentAction"], button[onclick*="globalTorrentAction"]').forEach(el => el.classList.add('hidden'));
        }
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function changeSelfPassword() {
    const pwd = prompt('Введите новый пароль для вашего профиля (мин. 3 символа):');
    if (pwd === null) return;
    const res = await api('/api/profile/password', 'POST', { password: pwd.trim() });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка смены пароля');
    alert('✅ Пароль успешно обновлён!');
}

async function loadProfilesList() {
    const box = document.getElementById('profiles-list');
    if (!box || CURRENT_ROLE !== 'admin') return;
    loadQrHosts();
    const res = await api('/api/profiles');
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const d = await res.json();
    box.innerHTML = '';
    (d.profiles || []).forEach(p => {
        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'row-between mb-8';
        row.style.cssText = 'background:var(--bg-input); padding:5px 8px; border-radius:5px;';
        row.innerHTML = `
            <span><b>${escapeHtml(p.name)}</b> <span class="muted-text">(@${escapeHtml(p.login || '')})</span> <span class="badge ${p.role === 'admin' ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-pause'}">${p.role}</span></span>
            <span class="row-gap" style="gap:4px;">
                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" title="Одноразовый инвайт" onclick="createInviteLink(${p.id}, '${escapeHtml(p.name)}')">✉️</button>
                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" title="QR-код подключения" onclick="openQrForProfile('${escapeHtml(p.api_token || '')}', '${escapeHtml(p.name)}', '${p.role}')">📱</button>
                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" title="Скопировать API-токен" onclick="copyTextPrompt('API-токен для ${escapeHtml(p.name)}:', '${escapeHtml(p.api_token || '')}')">📋</button>
                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" title="Перевыпустить API-токен" onclick="reissueApiToken(${p.id})">🔄</button>
                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" title="Сменить пароль" onclick="changeProfilePassword(${p.id}, '${escapeHtml(p.name)}')">🔑</button>
                <button class="btn btn-danger btn-xs" title="Удалить" onclick="deleteProfile(${p.id})">🗑️</button>
            </span>
        `;
        box.appendChild(row);
    });
}

function copyTextPrompt(label, val) {
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(val).then(() => alert(`${label}\n\n${val}`)).catch(() => prompt(label, val));
    } else {
        prompt(label, val);
    }
}

async function createInviteLink(id, name) {
    const res = await api('/api/profiles/invite', 'POST', { id });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка создания инвайта');
    const link = `${window.location.origin}/invite/${encodeURIComponent(d.invite_token)}`;
    copyTextPrompt(`Одноразовый инвайт для "${name}" (сгорит после 1-го входа):`, link);
}

async function reissueApiToken(id) {
    if (!confirm('Перевыпустить API-токен? Старый перестанет работать в виджете и скриптах.')) return;
    const res = await api('/api/profiles/reissue_api', 'POST', { id });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка');
    copyTextPrompt('Новый API-токен:', d.api_token);
    loadProfilesList();
}

async function changeProfilePassword(id, name) {
    const newPwd = prompt(`Новый пароль для "${name}" (или пусто для авто):`);
    if (newPwd === null) return;
    const res = await api('/api/profiles/change_password', 'POST', { id, password: newPwd.trim() });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка смены пароля');
    prompt('Пароль обновлён! Новый пароль:', d.password);
    loadProfilesList();
}

async function createProfile() {
    const name = document.getElementById('new-profile-name').value.trim();
    const login = document.getElementById('new-profile-login').value.trim();
    const password = document.getElementById('new-profile-token').value.trim();
    const role = document.getElementById('new-profile-role').value;
    const res = await api('/api/profiles/create', 'POST', { name, login, password, role });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка');
    document.getElementById('new-profile-name').value = '';
    document.getElementById('new-profile-login').value = '';
    document.getElementById('new-profile-token').value = '';
    prompt(`Профиль создан!\nПароль: ${d.password}\nAPI-токен:`, d.api_token);
    loadProfilesList();
}

async function deleteProfile(id) {
    if (!confirm('Удалить профиль?')) return;
    const res = await api('/api/profiles/delete', 'POST', { id });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка');
    loadProfilesList();
}

async function loadQrHosts() {
    const sel = document.getElementById('qr-host-select');
    if (!sel) return;
    const port = window.location.port || (window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? '' : '5000');
    const opts = [];
    if (window.location.hostname && !['localhost', '127.0.0.1'].includes(window.location.hostname)) {
        opts.push(window.location.origin);
    }
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/network/hosts');
        if (res.ok) {
            const d = await res.json();
            (d.ips || []).forEach(ip => {
                const u = `http://${ip}${port ? ':' + port : ''}`;
                if (!opts.includes(u)) opts.push(u);
            });
        }
    } catch (e) {}
    sel.innerHTML = '';
    opts.forEach(u => {
        const o = document.createElement('option');
        o.value = u; o.textContent = u;
        sel.appendChild(o);
    });
}

function openQrForProfile(apiToken, name, role) {
    _qrActiveProfile = { apiToken, name, role };
    document.getElementById('qr-title').textContent = `📱 QR-код: ${name}`;
    document.getElementById('qr-panel').classList.remove('hidden');
    refreshActiveQr();
}

function refreshActiveQr() {
    if (!_qrActiveProfile || typeof qrcode === 'undefined') return;
    const custom = document.getElementById('qr-custom-url').value.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '');
    const selected = document.getElementById('qr-host-select').value;
    const baseUrl = custom || selected || window.location.origin;
    const mode = document.getElementById('qr-mode-select').value;

    document.getElementById('qr-admin-warn').classList.toggle('hidden', !(mode === 'widget' && _qrActiveProfile.role === 'admin'));
    const payload = mode === 'widget' ? JSON.stringify({ url: baseUrl, token: _qrActiveProfile.apiToken }) : baseUrl;

    const qr = qrcode(0, 'M');
    qr.addData(payload);
    qr.make();
    document.getElementById('qr-preview-box').innerHTML = qr.createSvgTag(4, 2);
}

// web/static/safety_ui.js
let _lastDoctorReportMd = '';
let _qbVaultList = [];

async function loadSafetyToolsTab() {
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE !== 'undefined' && CURRENT_ROLE !== 'admin') return;
    loadTrashItems();
    loadQbVault();
    loadActiveSessions();
}

async function loadTrashItems() {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('trash-tbody');
    if (!tbody) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/trash');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        const items = d.items || [];
        if (!items.length) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg muted-text">Корзина _tbr_trash пуста</td></tr>';
            return;
        }
        tbody.innerHTML = '';
        items.forEach(it => {
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.innerHTML = `
                <td style="white-space:normal;"><b>${escapeHtml(it.name)}</b><div class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(it.original_path)}</div></td>
                <td><b>${escapeHtml(it.size_fmt)}</b></td>
                <td class="muted-text">${escapeHtml(it.expires_fmt)}</td>
                <td class="row-gap">
                    <button class="btn btn-xs" onclick="restoreTrashItem('${escapeHtml(it.id)}')">♻️ Вернуть</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-danger btn-xs" onclick="purgeTrashItem('${escapeHtml(it.id)}')">🗑️</button>
                </td>
            `;
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
        });
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function restoreTrashItem(id) {
    const res = await api('/api/trash/restore', 'POST', { id });
    const d = await res.json();
    alert(d.message || d.error);
    loadTrashItems();
}

async function purgeTrashItem(id) {
    if (!confirm('Удалить объект из корзины НАВСЕГДА без возможности восстановления?')) return;
    const res = await api('/api/trash/purge', 'POST', { id });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) alert(d.message || d.error);
    loadTrashItems();
}

async function loadQbVault() {

    if (!box) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/qb_vault');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        if (dirInp && !dirInp.value) dirInp.placeholder = d.config_dir || 'Путь к конфигам qB';
        _qbVaultList = d.backups || [];
        if (!_qbVaultList.length) {
            box.innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">Снимков конфигов пока нет</span>';
            return;
        }
        box.innerHTML = _qbVaultList.map(b => `
            <div class="row-between" style="background:var(--bg-input); padding:6px 8px; border-radius:6px;">
                <div><b>${escapeHtml(b.name)}</b> <span class="lbl-xs muted-text">(${escapeHtml(b.date)}, ${escapeHtml(b.size_fmt)})</span></div>
                <div class="row-gap">
                    <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" onclick="openQbDiffModal('${escapeHtml(b.name)}')">🔍 Diff</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-danger btn-xs" onclick="restoreQbVault('${escapeHtml(b.name)}')">♻️ Восст.</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `).join('');
    } catch (e) {}
}

    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка бэкапа');

}

async function saveQbVaultDir() {
    const dir = document.getElementById('qb-vault-dir')?.value.trim() || '';
    const res = await api('/api/qb_vault/set_dir', 'POST', { config_dir: dir });
    const d = await res.json();
    alert('Папка конфигов: ' + (d.config_dir || 'не найдена'));
    loadQbVault();
}

async function restoreQbVault(name) {
    if (!confirm(`Восстановить конфиг qBittorrent из снимка "${name}"?\nqBittorrent должен быть предварительно остановлен!`)) return;
    const res = await api('/api/qb_vault/restore', 'POST', { name });
    const d = await res.json();
    alert(d.message || d.error);
}

function openQbDiffModal(snapName) {
    const selA = document.getElementById('qb-diff-a');
    const selB = document.getElementById('qb-diff-b');
    if (!selA || !selB) return;
    selA.innerHTML = ''; selB.innerHTML = '';
    _qbVaultList.forEach(b => {
        selA.add(new Option(b.name, b.name));
        selB.add(new Option(b.name, b.name));
    });
    if (_qbVaultList.length > 1) selA.selectedIndex = 1;
    selB.value = snapName;
    document.getElementById('qb-diff-modal').style.display = 'flex';
    runQbConfigDiff();
}

async function runQbConfigDiff() {
    const snap_a = document.getElementById('qb-diff-a')?.value;
    const snap_b = document.getElementById('qb-diff-b')?.value;
    const filename = document.getElementById('qb-diff-file')?.value || 'qBittorrent.ini';
    const out = document.getElementById('qb-diff-out');
    if (!out) return;
    out.textContent = '⏳ Сравнение...';
    const res = await api('/api/qb_vault/diff', 'POST', { snap_a, snap_b, filename });
    const d = await res.json();
    out.textContent = d.success ? d.diff : (d.error || 'Ошибка');
}

async function runEnvDoctor() {
    const box = document.getElementById('doctor-results-box');
    const copyBtn = document.getElementById('btn-doctor-copy-md');
    if (!box) return;
    box.innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">⏳ Диагностика системы...</span>';
    const res = await api('/api/doctor/check');
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const { diag, report_md } = await res.json();
    _lastDoctorReportMd = report_md || '';
    if (copyBtn) copyBtn.classList.remove('hidden');

    const dbHtml = (diag.databases || []).map(x => `<span class="badge ${x.quick_check === 'ok' ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-err'}">${escapeHtml(x.name)}: ${escapeHtml(x.quick_check)}</span>`).join(' ');
    box.innerHTML = `
        <div class="row-wrap" style="gap:6px;">
            <span class="badge ${diag.qb.api_ok ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-err'}">qB API: ${escapeHtml(diag.qb.version)}</span>
            <span class="badge ${diag.bt_backup.rw ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-err'}">BT_backup r/w: ${diag.bt_backup.rw ? 'OK' : 'FAIL'}</span>
            <span class="badge ${diag.time_skew.ok === false ? 'badge-stall' : 'badge-up'}">Время: ${diag.time_skew.skew_sec !== null ? diag.time_skew.skew_sec + 'с' : '—'}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="row-wrap mt-10" style="gap:6px;">${dbHtml}</div>
    `;
}

function copyDoctorReport() {
    if (!_lastDoctorReportMd) return;
    if (navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(_lastDoctorReportMd).then(() => alert('✅ Санитизированный отчёт скопирован в буфер (без токенов, пасскеев и имён)!'));
    } else prompt('Отчёт для GitHub Issue:', _lastDoctorReportMd);
}

async function loadActiveSessions() {
    const box = document.getElementById('sessions-list-box');
    if (!box) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/sessions');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const list = (await res.json()).sessions || [];
        if (!list.length) { box.innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">Нет активных сессий</span>'; return; }
        box.innerHTML = list.map(s => `
            <div class="row-between" style="background:var(--bg-input); padding:6px 8px; border-radius:6px;">
                <div style="min-width:0; flex:1;">
                    <b>${escapeHtml(s.profile_login)}</b> <span class="badge ${s.is_current ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-pause'}">${s.is_current ? 'Текущая' : escapeHtml(s.ip)}</span>
                    <div class="lbl-xs muted-text" style="overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap;">${escapeHtml(s.last_seen_fmt)} • ${escapeHtml(s.user_agent)}</div>
                </div>
                ${s.is_current ? '' : `<button class="btn btn-danger btn-xs" onclick="revokeSingleSession('${escapeHtml(s.sid)}')">✕</button>`}
            </div>
        `).join('');
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function revokeOtherSessions() {
    if (!confirm('Завершить все остальные активные сессии?')) return;
    const res = await api('/api/sessions/revoke_others', 'POST');
    const d = await res.json();
    alert(d.message || 'Готово');
    loadActiveSessions();
}

async function revokeSingleSession(sid) {
    await api('/api/sessions/revoke', 'POST', { sid });
    loadActiveSessions();
}

async function runAutopilotDryRun(ruleId = null) {
    const modal = document.getElementById('dry-run-modal');
    const tbody = document.getElementById('dry-run-tbody');
    const sum = document.getElementById('dry-run-summary');
    if (!modal || !tbody) return;
    modal.style.display = 'flex';
    sum.textContent = '⏳ Прогон правил без изменений...';
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">Оценка условий...</td></tr>';
    const res = await api('/api/automation/dry_run', 'POST', ruleId ? { rule_id: ruleId } : {});
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!d.success) { sum.textContent = d.error || 'Ошибка'; tbody.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    sum.textContent = `Проверено правил: ${d.rules_checked} • Раздач: ${d.torrents_scanned} • Попало под правила: ${(d.matches || []).length}`;
    if (!d.matches?.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">Ни одна раздача сейчас не попадает под условия</td></tr>';
        return;
    }
    tbody.innerHTML = d.matches.map(m => `
        <tr>
            <td style="white-space:normal;"><b>${escapeHtml(m.name)}</b><div class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(m.rule_name)}${m.already_triggered ? ' (уже срабатывало)' : ''}</div></td>
            <td>${m.ratio.toFixed(2)}x</td>
            <td>${escapeHtml(m.uploaded_fmt)}</td>
            <td>${escapeHtml((m.planned || []).join(', ') || 'Уведомление')}</td>
        </tr>
    `).join('');
}

async function checkOnboardingWizard() {
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE !== 'undefined' && CURRENT_ROLE !== 'admin') return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/onboarding/status');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        if (d.completed) return;
        document.getElementById('onb-qb-ip').value = d.detected_ip || '127.0.0.1';
        document.getElementById('onb-qb-port').value = d.detected_port || '8080';
        document.getElementById('onb-mbps').value = d.max_channel_mbps || 1000;
        const dSel = document.getElementById('onb-disk');
        if (dSel) {
            dSel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Авто (по максимуму раздач)</option>';
            (d.disks || []).forEach(dk => dSel.add(new Option(`${dk.path} (${dk.free_fmt} своб.)`, dk.path)));
        }
        document.getElementById('onb-probe-status').textContent = d.qb_api_ok
            ? `🟢 Найден qBittorrent ${d.qb_version} • BT_backup: ${d.bt_backup_ok ? 'OK' : 'проверьте права'}`
            : '🟡 qBittorrent не ответил на автопорту — укажите вручную или пропустите';
        _setOnbStep(1);
        document.getElementById('onboarding-modal').style.display = 'flex';
    } catch (e) {}
}

function _setOnbStep(n) {
    _onbStep = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, n));
    for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) document.getElementById(`onb-step-${i}`)?.classList.toggle('hidden', i !== _onbStep);
}

async function finishOnboarding(skipped = false) {
    const payload = skipped ? { skipped: true } : {
        qb_ip: document.getElementById('onb-qb-ip').value.trim(),
        qb_port: document.getElementById('onb-qb-port').value.trim(),
        qb_user: document.getElementById('onb-qb-user').value.trim(),
        qb_password: document.getElementById('onb-qb-pass').value,
        max_channel_mbps: parseInt(document.getElementById('onb-mbps').value) || 1000,
        monitored_disk: document.getElementById('onb-disk').value
    };
    await api('/api/onboarding/complete', 'POST', payload);
    document.getElementById('onboarding-modal').style.display = 'none';
    if (!skipped && typeof loadTorrents === 'function') loadTorrents(true);
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    setTimeout(checkOnboardingWizard, 1200);
});

// web/static/settings.js
async function checkQbStatus() {
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/qb/status');
        const d = await res.json();
        const badge = document.getElementById('qb-status-badge');
        badge.className = d.running ? 'badge badge-up pointer' : 'badge badge-stop pointer';
        badge.textContent = d.running ? t('qb_running') : t('qb_stopped');
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function toggleQbPower() {
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE !== 'undefined' && CURRENT_ROLE === 'viewer') return;
    const res = await api('/api/qb/status');
    const d = await res.json();
    if (confirm(d.running ? t('confirm_qb_stop') : t('confirm_qb_start'))) {
        await api(`/api/qb/${d.running ? 'stop' : 'start'}`, 'POST');
        setTimeout(checkQbStatus, 1500);
    }
}

async function restartQb() {
    if (confirm(t('confirm_qb_restart'))) {
        await api('/api/qb/restart', 'POST');
        setTimeout(checkQbStatus, 2000);
    }
}

async function checkAutostart() {
    const res = await api('/api/autostart/status');
    const d = await res.json();
    const btn = document.getElementById('autostart-toggle-btn');
    btn.className = d.enabled ? 'btn btn-danger' : 'btn';
    btn.textContent = d.enabled ? t('disable') : t('enable');
}

async function toggleAutostart() {
    const res = await api('/api/autostart/toggle', 'POST');
    const d = await res.json();
    alert(d.message);
    checkAutostart();
}

async function openSettingsModal() {
    checkAutostart();
    if (typeof loadProfilesList === 'function') loadProfilesList();
    if (typeof loadVaultSettings === 'function') loadVaultSettings();
    const res = await api('/api/settings');
    const d = await res.json();
    document.getElementById('setting-web-host').value = d.web_host;
    document.getElementById('setting-web-port').value = d.web_port;
    document.getElementById('setting-web-password').value = '';
    document.getElementById('setting-web-password').placeholder = d.has_password ? t('pwd_set_ph') : t('pwd_req_ph');
    document.getElementById('setting-qb-ip').value = d.qb_ip;
    document.getElementById('setting-qb-port').value = d.qb_port;
    document.getElementById('setting-qb-user').value = d.qb_user || 'admin';
    document.getElementById('setting-qb-password').value = '';
    document.getElementById('setting-qb-password').placeholder = d.has_qb_password ? t('qb_pwd_set_ph') : t('qb_pass_ph');
    document.getElementById('setting-snapshot-interval').value = d.snapshot_interval || 60;
    document.getElementById('setting-w1').value = d.w1;
    document.getElementById('setting-w2').value = d.w2;
    document.getElementById('setting-w3').value = d.w3;

    const chMbps = document.getElementById('setting-max-channel-mbps');
    if (chMbps) chMbps.value = d.max_channel_mbps ?? 1000;

    const diskSel = document.getElementById('setting-monitored-disk');
    if (diskSel) {
        diskSel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Авто (диск с макс. числом раздач)</option>';
        (d.disks || []).forEach(dk => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = dk.path;
            opt.textContent = `${dk.path} — ${dk.free_fmt} своб. (${dk.torrents_count} раздач)`;
            diskSel.appendChild(opt);
        });
        diskSel.value = d.monitored_disk || '';
    }

    const kaBlock = document.getElementById('settings-keepawake-block');
    if (kaBlock) kaBlock.classList.toggle('hidden', !d.is_windows);
    const kaChk = document.getElementById('setting-keepawake-enabled');
    if (kaChk) kaChk.checked = Boolean(d.keep_awake_enabled);
    const kaKb = document.getElementById('setting-keepawake-kb');
    if (kaKb) kaKb.value = d.keep_awake_min_kb ?? 500;
    const kaDisp = document.getElementById('setting-keepawake-display');
    if (kaDisp) kaDisp.checked = Boolean(d.keep_awake_display);

    const wdChk = document.getElementById('setting-watchdog-enabled');
    if (wdChk) wdChk.checked = Boolean(d.watchdog_enabled);
    const wdTo = document.getElementById('setting-watchdog-timeout');
    if (wdTo) wdTo.value = d.watchdog_timeout_min ?? 3;
    const wdExe = document.getElementById('setting-watchdog-exe');
    if (wdExe) {
        wdExe.value = d.watchdog_exe_path || '';
        wdExe.placeholder = d.watchdog_exe_detected || 'автоопределение';
    }

    document.getElementById('settings-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}

function closeSettingsModal() { document.getElementById('settings-modal').style.display = 'none'; }

async function saveSettings() {
    const payload = {
        web_host: document.getElementById('setting-web-host').value.trim(),
        web_port: parseInt(document.getElementById('setting-web-port').value),
        web_password: document.getElementById('setting-web-password').value,
        qb_ip: document.getElementById('setting-qb-ip').value.trim(),
        qb_port: document.getElementById('setting-qb-port').value.trim(),
        qb_user: document.getElementById('setting-qb-user').value.trim() || 'admin',
        qb_password: document.getElementById('setting-qb-password').value,
        snapshot_interval: parseInt(document.getElementById('setting-snapshot-interval').value) || 60,
        w1: parseInt(document.getElementById('setting-w1').value) || 0,
        w2: parseInt(document.getElementById('setting-w2').value) || 0,
        w3: parseInt(document.getElementById('setting-w3').value) || 0,
        max_channel_mbps: parseInt(document.getElementById('setting-max-channel-mbps')?.value) || 1000,
        monitored_disk: document.getElementById('setting-monitored-disk')?.value || '',
        keep_awake_enabled: document.getElementById('setting-keepawake-enabled')?.checked || false,
        keep_awake_min_kb: parseInt(document.getElementById('setting-keepawake-kb')?.value) || 0,
        keep_awake_display: document.getElementById('setting-keepawake-display')?.checked || false,
        watchdog_enabled: document.getElementById('setting-watchdog-enabled')?.checked || false,
        watchdog_timeout_min: parseInt(document.getElementById('setting-watchdog-timeout')?.value) || 3,
        watchdog_exe_path: document.getElementById('setting-watchdog-exe')?.value.trim() || ''
    };
    const res = await api('/api/settings', 'POST', payload);
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) return alert(d.error || t('save_err'));
    alert(d.message);
    closeSettingsModal();
    if (d.port_changed) setTimeout(() => { window.location.port = payload.web_port; }, 1500);
    else loadTorrents(true);
}

function downloadBackupZip() {
    const noHist = document.getElementById('backup-no-history').checked ? '1' : '0';
    const noSec = document.getElementById('backup-no-secrets').checked ? '1' : '0';
    window.location.href = `/api/backup/export?exclude_history=${noHist}&strip_secrets=${noSec}`;
}

async function uploadRestoreZip(file) {
    if (!file) return;
    if (!confirm(`⚠️ ВНИМАНИЕ: Восстановление из архива "${file.name}" заменит текущие базы данных и настройки!\n\nПродолжить?`)) {
        document.getElementById('restore-file-input').value = '';
        return;
    }
    const fd = new FormData();
    fd.append('file', file);
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/backup/restore', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-CSRF-Token': CSRF_TOKEN }, body: fd });
        const d = await res.json();
        alert(d.message || d.error);
        if (d.success) closeSettingsModal();
    } catch (e) {
        alert('Ошибка загрузки: ' + e.message);
    } finally {
        document.getElementById('restore-file-input').value = '';
    }
}

// web/static/smart_calc.js
let lastForecastData = null;

function populateAnalyticsSelectors() {
    const fill = (selId) => {
        const sel = document.getElementById(selId);
        if (!sel) return;
        const cur = sel.value;
        sel.innerHTML = '';
        const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        const def = document.createElement('option');
        def.value = 'all'; def.textContent = t('whole_seedbox');
        frag.appendChild(def);
        rawSummaryItems.forEach(item => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = item.hash_id; opt.textContent = `📦 ${item.name}`;
            frag.appendChild(opt);
        });
        sel.appendChild(frag);
        if (cur) sel.value = cur;
    };
    fill('smart-calc-target-type');
    fill('history-torrent-select');
}

function onSmartCalcTargetChange() {
    const val = document.getElementById('smart-calc-target-type').value;
    document.getElementById('smart-calc-goal-label').textContent = val === 'all' ? t('goal_lbl_all') : t('goal_lbl_one');
    document.getElementById('smart-calc-goal').placeholder = val === 'all' ? t('goal_ph_all') : t('goal_ph_one');
}

async function runSmartCalc() {
    const term = document.getElementById('smart-calc-terminal');
    const targetVal = document.getElementById('smart-calc-target-type').value;
    const isAll = (targetVal === 'all');
    const goal = document.getElementById('smart-calc-goal').value.trim();
    const speed = document.getElementById('smart-calc-speed').value.trim();
    if (!goal) { term.textContent = t('err_no_goal'); return; }
    term.textContent = t('calc_running');
    let d;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/smart_calc', 'POST', { target_type: isAll ? 'all' : 'torrent', hash: isAll ? '' : targetVal, target: goal, speed });
        if (!res.ok) { term.textContent = t('http_err', { status: res.status }); return; }
        d = await res.json();
    } catch (err) { term.textContent = t('net_err', { msg: err.message }); return; }

    const btnTable = document.getElementById('btn-toggle-forecast-table');
    const btnCsv = document.getElementById('btn-export-forecast-csv');
    const wrapTable = document.getElementById('smart-res-table-wrap');
    if (!d.success) {
        btnTable.classList.add('hidden'); btnCsv.classList.add('hidden'); wrapTable.classList.add('hidden');
        term.textContent = d.need_manual ? `=================================================================\n${t('need_manual_msg', { avail: d.days_available, req: d.req_days })}\n=================================================================` : t('err_prefix', { msg: d.error });
        return;
    }

    lastForecastData = d;
    btnCsv.classList.remove('hidden');
    if (d.achieved) { btnTable.classList.add('hidden'); term.textContent = t('goal_achieved', { name: d.name, cur: d.current_up_fmt, tgt: d.target_fmt }); return; }

    btnTable.classList.remove('hidden');
    const modeTxt = d.is_manual ? t('mode_manual') : t('mode_smart', { w: d.weights_desc });
    let out = `=================================================================\n${t('res_header')}\n${t('obj_lbl')}        ${d.name}\n${t('up_now_lbl')} ${d.current_up_fmt}  -->  ${t('goal_lbl')} ${d.target_fmt}\n${t('rem_lbl')}      ${d.remaining_fmt}\n`;
    if (!d.is_seedbox) out += `${t('ratio_lbl', { cur: d.ratio_current, nxt: d.ratio_after_24h })}\n`;
    out += `-----------------------------------------------------------------\n${t('mode_lbl')} ${modeTxt}\n${t('f24_lbl')} +${d.forecast_24h_vol_fmt}\n${t('eta_lbl')}   ${d.eta_str}\n=================================================================`;
    term.textContent = out;

    const tbody = document.getElementById('smart-res-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    (d.forecast_rows || []).forEach(r => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.innerHTML = `<td>${escapeHtml(r.date)}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(r.hour)}</strong></td><td class="purple-txt" style="font-weight:700;">+${escapeHtml(trBackend(r.volume_mb))}</td><td class="blue-txt" style="font-weight:600;">${escapeHtml(r.speed_fmt)}</td><td class="muted-text" style="font-size:11px;">${escapeHtml(r.basis)}</td>`;
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);
}

function toggleForecastTable() {
    const isHidden = document.getElementById('smart-res-table-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden');
    document.getElementById('btn-toggle-forecast-table').textContent = isHidden ? t('btn_hourly_show') : t('btn_hourly_hide');
}

async function exportForecastCSV() {
    if (!lastForecastData) return;
    const res = await api('/api/smart_calc/export', 'POST', { forecast_data: lastForecastData });
    if (!res.ok) return alert(t('export_err'));
    const blob = await res.blob();
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = `forecast_24h_${new Date().toLocaleString('sv-SE').replace(/[- :]/g, '_')}.csv`;
    a.click();
}

// web/static/social_ui.js
let _lastAchData = null;

function updateMobileCardsBtn() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-toggle-mobile-cards');
    if (!btn) return;
    const isCards = document.body.classList.contains('mobile-cards-on');
    btn.textContent = isCards ? '📱 Карточки' : '📋 Таблица';
}

function toggleMobileCardsMode() {
    if (typeof getUiSettings !== 'function' || typeof persistUiSettings !== 'function') return;
    const ui = getUiSettings();
    ui.mobile_mode = (ui.mobile_mode === false);
    persistUiSettings(ui);
    updateMobileCardsBtn();
}

function renderUploadEquivalents(totalBytes) {
    const box = document.getElementById('summary-equivalents-bar');
    if (!box) return;
    const gib = (Number(totalBytes) || 0) / (1024 ** 3);
    if (gib < 1) {
        box.classList.add('hidden');
        return;
    }
    const games = Math.max(1, Math.round(gib / 100));
    const movies = Math.max(1, Math.round(gib / 40));
    const isos = Math.max(1, Math.round(gib / 4));
    const isEn = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'en');

    box.innerHTML = isEn
        ? `🤯 <b>That's roughly equal to:</b> 🎮 <b>${games}</b> modern games (100 GiB) &nbsp;•&nbsp; 🎬 <b>${movies}</b> 4K UHD movies (40 GiB) &nbsp;•&nbsp; 💿 <b>${isos}</b> OS ISO images (4 GiB)`
        : `🤯 <b>Это примерно как:</b> 🎮 <b>${games}</b> совр. игр (по 100 GiB) &nbsp;•&nbsp; 🎬 <b>${movies}</b> фильмов 4K UHD (по 40 GiB) &nbsp;•&nbsp; 💿 <b>${isos}</b> образов ОС (по 4 GiB)`;
    box.classList.remove('hidden');
}

async function loadAuditLog() {
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE !== 'undefined' && CURRENT_ROLE !== 'admin') return;
    const tbody = document.getElementById('audit-tbody');
    if (!tbody) return;
    const cat = document.getElementById('audit-cat-filter')?.value || '';
    try {
        const res = await api(`/api/tools/audit?category=${encodeURIComponent(cat)}&limit=100`);
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        const items = d.items || [];
        if (!items.length) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg">Событий по фильтру пока нет</td></tr>';
            return;
        }
        const badgeCls = { auth: 'badge-dl', automation: 'badge-up', merger: 'badge-force', system: 'badge-stall' };
        tbody.innerHTML = '';
        const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        items.forEach(r => {
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.innerHTML = `
                <td class="muted-text">${escapeHtml(r.datetime)}</td>
                <td><span class="badge ${badgeCls[r.category] || 'badge-pause'}">${escapeHtml(r.category)}</span></td>
                <td><b>${escapeHtml(r.actor)}</b></td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(r.action)}</td>
                <td class="muted-text" style="white-space:normal;">${escapeHtml(r.details)}</td>
            `;
            frag.appendChild(tr);
        });
        tbody.appendChild(frag);
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function loadAchievements() {
    const grid = document.getElementById('ach-grid');
    const badge = document.getElementById('ach-progress-badge');
    if (!grid) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/achievements');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        _lastAchData = d;
        if (badge) badge.textContent = `${d.unlocked_count || 0} / ${d.total_count || 10}`;

        const isEn = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'en');
        grid.innerHTML = '';
        (d.items || []).forEach(a => {
            const card = document.createElement('div');
            card.style.cssText = `background:var(--bg-input); padding:8px; border-radius:6px; border:1px solid ${a.unlocked ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--border)'}; opacity:${a.unlocked ? '1' : '0.48'};`;
            const title = isEn ? a.title_en : a.title;
            const desc = isEn ? a.desc_en : a.desc;
            const dateHtml = a.unlocked
                ? `<div class="lbl-xs accent-txt" style="margin-top:4px;">✅ ${escapeHtml(a.unlocked_at || '')}</div>`
                : `<div class="lbl-xs muted-text" style="margin-top:4px;">🔒 ${isEn ? 'Locked' : 'Закрыто'}</div>`;
            card.innerHTML = `
                <div style="font-size:13px; font-weight:700;">${escapeHtml(a.icon)} ${escapeHtml(title)}</div>
                <div class="lbl-xs muted-text" style="margin-top:2px;">${escapeHtml(desc)}</div>
                ${dateHtml}
            `;
            grid.appendChild(card);
        });
    } catch (e) {}
}

function _computeSeederRank(totalBytes) {
    const tib = (Number(totalBytes) || 0) / (1024 ** 4);
    if (tib >= 50) return '👑 Seedbox Overlord';
    if (tib >= 10) return '🔥 Torrent Legend';
    if (tib >= 1) return '⚡ Terabyte Knight';
    return '🌱 Rising Seeder';
}

async function openShareCardModal() {
    document.getElementById('share-card-modal').style.display = 'flex';
    if (!_lastAchData) await loadAchievements();
    renderShareCardCanvas();
}

function closeShareCardModal() {
    document.getElementById('share-card-modal').style.display = 'none';
}

function renderShareCardCanvas() {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('share-card-canvas');
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    const bgMain = cs.getPropertyValue('--bg-main').trim() || '#121214';
    const bgPanel = cs.getPropertyValue('--bg-panel').trim() || '#1a1a20';
    const bgInput = cs.getPropertyValue('--bg-input').trim() || '#262630';
    const txtMain = cs.getPropertyValue('--text').trim() || '#ffffff';
    const txtMuted = cs.getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#95a5a6';
    const accent = cs.getPropertyValue('--accent').trim() || '#2ecc71';
    const border = cs.getPropertyValue('--border').trim() || '#2a2a34';

    const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
    ctx.fillStyle = bgMain;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    ctx.fillStyle = bgPanel;
    ctx.strokeStyle = border;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.roundRect(24, 24, W - 48, H - 48, 16);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.stroke();

    // Логотип-молния
    ctx.fillStyle = accent;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(64, 48); ctx.lineTo(48, 72); ctx.lineTo(62, 72);
    ctx.lineTo(57, 92); ctx.lineTo(76, 67); ctx.lineTo(62, 67);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();

    ctx.fillStyle = accent;
    ctx.font = 'bold 28px Segoe UI, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText('tbr PRO', 90, 78);

    const meta = (typeof lastSummaryMeta !== 'undefined' && lastSummaryMeta) ? lastSummaryMeta : {};
    const items = (typeof rawSummaryItems !== 'undefined' && rawSummaryItems) ? rawSummaryItems : [];
    const totalUp = meta.total_uploaded || items.reduce((a, b) => a + (b.uploaded || 0), 0);
    const maxSec = items.reduce((m, x) => Math.max(m, x.active_seconds || 0), 0);
    const activeDays = Math.floor(maxSec / 86400);
    const achCount = _lastAchData ? `${_lastAchData.unlocked_count}/${_lastAchData.total_count}` : '—';

    ctx.fillStyle = txtMuted;
    ctx.font = 'bold 16px Segoe UI, sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'right';
    ctx.fillText(`${_computeSeederRank(totalUp)}  •  🏆 ${achCount}`, W - 52, 76);
    ctx.textAlign = 'left';

    const cards = [
        { lbl: 'ВСЕГО ОТДАНО', val: meta.total_uploaded_fmt || formatBytesJS(totalUp), col: accent },
        { lbl: 'ЗА 24 ЧАСА', val: meta.global_day_volume_fmt || '—', col: '#e67e22' },
        { lbl: 'РАЗДАЧ / СТАЖ', val: `${meta.total_torrents || items.length} шт. / ${activeDays} дн.`, col: '#3498db' },
    ];
    cards.forEach((c, idx) => {
        const x = 48 + idx * 272, y = 114, cw = 256, ch = 88;
        ctx.fillStyle = bgInput;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(x, y, cw, ch, 10); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = txtMuted;
        ctx.font = 'bold 12px Segoe UI, sans-serif';
        ctx.fillText(c.lbl, x + 16, y + 28);
        ctx.fillStyle = c.col;
        ctx.font = 'bold 22px Segoe UI, sans-serif';
        ctx.fillText(String(c.val).slice(0, 22), x + 16, y + 62);
    });

    const hideNames = document.getElementById('share-hide-names')?.checked ?? true;
    ctx.fillStyle = txtMuted;
    ctx.font = 'bold 13px Segoe UI, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(hideNames ? 'ТОП-3 РАЗДАЧИ (НАЗВАНИЯ СКРЫТЫ)' : 'ТОП-3 РАЗДАЧИ ПО ОТДАЧЕ', 48, 240);

    const top3 = [...items].sort((a, b) => (b.uploaded || 0) - (a.uploaded || 0)).slice(0, 3);
    top3.forEach((it, idx) => {
        const y = 258 + idx * 62;
        ctx.fillStyle = bgInput;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(48, y, W - 96, 50, 8); ctx.fill();
        const nm = hideNames ? `*** Скрытая раздача #${idx + 1} ***` : String(it.name || '').slice(0, 56);
        ctx.fillStyle = txtMain;
        ctx.font = 'bold 16px Segoe UI, sans-serif';
        ctx.fillText(`${idx + 1}. ${nm}`, 66, y + 31);

        ctx.fillStyle = accent;
        ctx.textAlign = 'right';
        ctx.fillText(`${formatBytesJS(it.uploaded)} (${Number(it.ratio || 0).toFixed(2)}x)`, W - 66, y + 31);
        ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    });

    ctx.fillStyle = txtMuted;
    ctx.font = '12px Segoe UI, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(`Generated locally by tbr PRO • ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}`, 48, H - 40);
}

function downloadShareCardPng() {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('share-card-canvas');
    if (!canvas) return;
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
    a.download = `tbr_share_card_${Date.now()}.png`;
    a.click();
}

function copyShareCardPng() {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('share-card-canvas');
    if (!canvas) return;
    canvas.toBlob(async blob => {
        if (!blob) return;
        try {
            await navigator.clipboard.write([new ClipboardItem({ 'image/png': blob })]);
            alert('✅ Карточка скопирована в буфер обмена!');
        } catch (e) {
            alert('⚠️ Копирование PNG в буфер требует HTTPS или localhost. Нажмите «Скачать PNG».');
        }
    }, 'image/png');
}

// web/static/summary.js
let rawSummaryItems = [];
let filteredSummaryItems = [];
let summarySortCol = 'uploaded';
let summarySortAsc = false;
let lastSummaryMeta = null;
let selectedSummaryHash = null;
let _summaryLiveOnly = false;
let _summaryLastSeedOnly = false;
const _liveDecayMap = new Map();

function _renderNameWithBadges(i) {
    const lastBadge = i.is_last_seed ? ` <span class="badge badge-stall" title="Вы единственный раздающий в сети">🛡️ Единственный сид</span>` : '';
    const bar = typeof renderTargetBarHtml === 'function' ? renderTargetBarHtml(i.target) : '';
    return `<div><strong>${escapeHtml(i.name)}</strong>${lastBadge}${bar}</div>`;
}

function _renderHealthBadge(i) {
    if (i.health_score === undefined || i.health_score < 0) {
        return `<span class="badge badge-pause" title="Трекер не вернул статистику пиров">нет данных</span>`;
    }
    const cls = i.is_last_seed ? 'badge-stall' : (i.health_score >= 50 ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-dl');
    return `<span class="badge ${cls}" title="Сиды: ${i.num_complete} | Личи: ${i.num_incomplete}">💎 ${i.health_score.toFixed(1)}</span>`;
}

const SUMMARY_COLS = [
    { id: 'name', labelRu: 'Имя раздачи', labelEn: 'Torrent Name', render: _renderNameWithBadges },
    { id: 'size', labelRu: 'Размер', labelEn: 'Size', render: i => escapeHtml(formatBytesJS(i.size)) },
    { id: 'uploaded', labelRu: 'Отдано', labelEn: 'Uploaded', render: i => escapeHtml(formatBytesJS(i.uploaded)) },
    { id: 'ratio', labelRu: 'Ratio', labelEn: 'Ratio', render: i => `<strong class="accent-txt">${parseFloat(i.ratio || 0).toFixed(2)}x</strong>` },
    { id: 'health_score', labelRu: 'Ценность', labelEn: 'Health', render: _renderHealthBadge },
    { id: 'speed_all_time', labelRu: 'Ср. скорость', labelEn: 'Avg Speed', render: i => `<span class="blue-txt" style="font-weight:600;">${escapeHtml(i.speed_all_time_fmt)}</span>` },
    { id: 'speed_1h', labelRu: 'Скор. 1ч', labelEn: '1h Speed', render: i => `<span class="purple-txt" style="font-weight:600;">${escapeHtml(i.speed_1h_fmt)}</span>` },
    { id: 'speed_24h', labelRu: 'Скор. 24ч', labelEn: '24h Speed', render: i => `<span class="orange-txt" style="font-weight:600;">${escapeHtml(i.speed_24h_fmt)}</span>` },
    { id: 'vol_hour', labelRu: 'МБ/ч', labelEn: 'MB/h', render: i => `<span class="purple-txt" style="font-weight:700;">${escapeHtml(trBackend(i.vol_hour_fmt))}</span>` },
    { id: 'vol_day', labelRu: 'МБ/день', labelEn: 'MB/day', render: i => `<span class="orange-txt" style="font-weight:700;">${escapeHtml(trBackend(i.vol_day_fmt))}</span>` },
    { id: 'ratio_h_all', labelRu: 'Ratio/ч', labelEn: 'Ratio/h', render: i => `<span class="muted-text" style="font-size:11px;">${escapeHtml(trBackend(i.ratio_h_all_fmt))}</span>` },
    { id: 'created', labelRu: 'Дата', labelEn: 'Added', render: i => `<span class="muted-text" style="font-size:11px;">${escapeHtml(trBackend(i.created))}</span>` }
];

function getSummaryColPrefs() {
    const ui = typeof getUiSettings === 'function' ? getUiSettings() : {};
    const sc = ui.summary_columns || {};
    const allIds = SUMMARY_COLS.map(c => c.id);
    const order = (sc.order || allIds).filter(id => allIds.includes(id));
    allIds.forEach(id => { if (!order.includes(id)) order.push(id); });
    const visible = (sc.visible || allIds).filter(id => allIds.includes(id));
    if (!localStorage.getItem('tbr_seen_health_col') && !visible.includes('health_score')) {
        visible.push('health_score');
        localStorage.setItem('tbr_seen_health_col', '1');
    }
    return { order, visible: visible.length ? visible : ['name'], widths: sc.widths || {} };
}

function saveSummaryColPrefs(prefs) {
    const ui = getUiSettings();
    ui.summary_columns = prefs;
    if (typeof persistUiSettings === 'function') persistUiSettings(ui);
}

function toggleSummaryColsMenu() {
    const menu = document.getElementById('summary-cols-menu');
    if (!menu) return;
    if (!menu.classList.contains('hidden')) { menu.classList.add('hidden'); return; }
    const prefs = getSummaryColPrefs();
    const isEn = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'en');
    menu.innerHTML = '';
    SUMMARY_COLS.forEach(col => {
        const lbl = document.createElement('label');
        lbl.className = 'row-gap pointer lbl-xs';
        lbl.style.padding = '3px 0';
        const chk = document.createElement('input');
        chk.type = 'checkbox';
        chk.checked = prefs.visible.includes(col.id);
        chk.onchange = () => {
            const p = getSummaryColPrefs();
            if (chk.checked) { if (!p.visible.includes(col.id)) p.visible.push(col.id); }
            else { p.visible = p.visible.filter(x => x !== col.id); if (!p.visible.length) { chk.checked = true; return; } }
            saveSummaryColPrefs(p);
            applySummarySortAndRender();
        };
        lbl.appendChild(chk);
        lbl.appendChild(document.createTextNode(isEn ? col.labelEn : col.labelRu));
        menu.appendChild(lbl);
    });
    menu.classList.remove('hidden');
}

function toggleSummaryLiveFilter() {
    _summaryLiveOnly = !_summaryLiveOnly;
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-live-filter');
    if (btn) btn.className = _summaryLiveOnly ? 'btn' : 'btn btn-secondary';
    applySummaryFilters();
}

function toggleSummaryLastSeedFilter() {
    _summaryLastSeedOnly = !_summaryLastSeedOnly;
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-last-seed-filter');
    if (btn) btn.className = _summaryLastSeedOnly ? 'btn' : 'btn btn-secondary';
    applySummaryFilters();
}

async function loadSummary(force = false) {
    if (force) await api('/api/torrents/refresh', 'POST');
    const res = await api('/api/summary');
    const d = await res.json();
    lastSummaryMeta = d;
    renderSummaryMeta();

    rawSummaryItems = d.items || [];
    const nowMs = Date.now();
    rawSummaryItems.forEach(it => {
        const h = (it.hash_id || it.hash || '').toLowerCase();
        if (h && ((it.upspeed || 0) > 0 || (it.dlspeed || 0) > 0)) _liveDecayMap.set(h, nowMs);
    });

    if (typeof populateAnalyticsSelectors === 'function') populateAnalyticsSelectors();
    applySummaryFilters();
}

function renderSummaryMeta() {
    const d = lastSummaryMeta;
    if (!d) return;
    document.getElementById('sum-count').textContent = d.total_torrents;
    document.getElementById('sum-size').textContent = d.total_size_fmt;
    document.getElementById('sum-uploaded').textContent = d.total_uploaded_fmt;
    document.getElementById('sum-global-hour').textContent = trBackend(d.global_hour_volume_fmt || '-');
    document.getElementById('sum-global-hour-speed').textContent = d.global_hour_speed_fmt || '-';
    document.getElementById('sum-global-hour-note').textContent = trBackend(d.global_hour_note || '');
    document.getElementById('sum-global-day').textContent = trBackend(d.global_day_volume_fmt || '-');
    document.getElementById('sum-global-day-speed').textContent = d.global_day_speed_fmt || '-';
    document.getElementById('sum-ratio-h').textContent = `${trBackend(d.global_ratio_h_all_fmt)} / ${trBackend(d.global_ratio_h_day_fmt)}`;
    if (typeof renderUploadEquivalents === 'function') renderUploadEquivalents(d.total_uploaded);
}

function applySummaryFilters() {
    const q = (document.getElementById('sum-filter-search').value || '').toLowerCase();
    const minRatio = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sum-filter-ratio').value) || 0;
    const minUpGb = (parseFloat(document.getElementById('sum-filter-uploaded').value) || 0) * (1024 ** 3);
    const nowMs = Date.now();

    filteredSummaryItems = rawSummaryItems.filter(i => {
        if (q && !(i.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
        if (minRatio > 0 && (i.ratio || 0) < minRatio) return false;
        if (minUpGb > 0 && (i.uploaded || 0) < minUpGb) return false;
        if (_summaryLastSeedOnly && !i.is_last_seed) return false;
        if (_summaryLiveOnly) {
            const h = (i.hash_id || i.hash || '').toLowerCase();
            const lastSeen = _liveDecayMap.get(h) || 0;
            if ((nowMs - lastSeen) > 45000) return false;
        }
        return true;
    });
    applySummarySortAndRender();
}

function resetSummaryFilters() {
    document.getElementById('sum-filter-search').value = '';
    document.getElementById('sum-filter-ratio').value = '';
    document.getElementById('sum-filter-uploaded').value = '';
    _summaryLiveOnly = false;
    _summaryLastSeedOnly = false;
    const b1 = document.getElementById('btn-live-filter');
    const b2 = document.getElementById('btn-last-seed-filter');
    if (b1) b1.className = 'btn btn-secondary';
    if (b2) b2.className = 'btn btn-secondary';
    applySummaryFilters();
}

function sortSummary(col) {
    if (summarySortCol === col) summarySortAsc = !summarySortAsc;
    else { summarySortCol = col; summarySortAsc = (col === 'name'); }
    applySummarySortAndRender();
}

function applySummarySortAndRender() {
    const scrollWrap = document.getElementById('summary-table-wrap');
    const prevTop = scrollWrap ? scrollWrap.scrollTop : 0;
    const prevLeft = scrollWrap ? scrollWrap.scrollLeft : 0;

    filteredSummaryItems.sort((a, b) => {
        if (summarySortCol === 'health_score') {
            const ha = (a.health_score ?? -1), hb = (b.health_score ?? -1);
            if (ha < 0 && hb >= 0) return 1;
            if (hb < 0 && ha >= 0) return -1;
            return summarySortAsc ? (ha - hb) : (hb - ha);
        }
        let vA = a[summarySortCol], vB = b[summarySortCol];
        if (summarySortCol === 'created') {
            vA = a.created_ts || parseDateToTs(a.created);
            vB = b.created_ts || parseDateToTs(b.created);
        } else {
            if (typeof vA === 'string') vA = vA.toLowerCase();
            if (typeof vB === 'string') vB = vB.toLowerCase();
        }
        return vA < vB ? (summarySortAsc ? -1 : 1) : (vA > vB ? (summarySortAsc ? 1 : -1) : 0);
    });

    const prefs = getSummaryColPrefs();
    const isEn = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'en');
    const isMobileCards = window.innerWidth <= 768 && document.body.classList.contains('mobile-cards-on');
    const activeCols = prefs.order.filter(id => prefs.visible.includes(id)).map(id => SUMMARY_COLS.find(c => c.id === id));

    const theadRow = document.getElementById('summary-thead-row');
    theadRow.innerHTML = '';
    let dragSrcId = null;

    activeCols.forEach(col => {
        const th = document.createElement('th');
        th.style.position = 'sticky';
        if (prefs.widths[col.id]) th.style.width = prefs.widths[col.id] + 'px';
        const arrow = (col.id === summarySortCol) ? (summarySortAsc ? ' ▲' : ' ▼') : '';
        th.textContent = (isEn ? col.labelEn : col.labelRu) + arrow;
        th.onclick = (e) => { if (!e.target.classList.contains('col-resizer')) sortSummary(col.id); };

        if (!isMobileCards) {
            th.draggable = true;
            th.ondragstart = () => { dragSrcId = col.id; };
            th.ondragover = (e) => e.preventDefault();
            th.ondrop = (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                if (!dragSrcId || dragSrcId === col.id) return;
                const p = getSummaryColPrefs();
                const fromIdx = p.order.indexOf(dragSrcId), toIdx = p.order.indexOf(col.id);
                if (fromIdx > -1 && toIdx > -1) {
                    p.order.splice(fromIdx, 1); p.order.splice(toIdx, 0, dragSrcId);
                    saveSummaryColPrefs(p); applySummarySortAndRender();
                }
            };
            const resizer = document.createElement('div');
            resizer.className = 'col-resizer';
            resizer.onmousedown = (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault();
                const startX = e.pageX, startW = th.offsetWidth;
                const onMove = (ev) => { th.style.width = Math.max(60, startW + (ev.pageX - startX)) + 'px'; };
                const onUp = () => {
                    document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove); document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
                    const p = getSummaryColPrefs(); p.widths[col.id] = th.offsetWidth; saveSummaryColPrefs(p);
                };
                document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove); document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
            };
            th.appendChild(resizer);
        }
        theadRow.appendChild(th);
    });

    const tbody = document.getElementById('summary-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    filteredSummaryItems.forEach(i => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        const hId = i.hash_id || i.hash || '';
        if (selectedSummaryHash && hId === selectedSummaryHash) tr.classList.add('selected');
        tr.onclick = () => {
            selectedSummaryHash = hId;
            tbody.querySelectorAll('tr').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('selected'));
            tr.classList.add('selected');
        };
        activeCols.forEach(col => {
            const td = document.createElement('td');
            td.dataset.col = col.id;
            td.setAttribute('data-label', isEn ? col.labelEn : col.labelRu);
            td.innerHTML = col.render(i);
            tr.appendChild(td);
        });
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);

    if (scrollWrap) { scrollWrap.scrollTop = prevTop; scrollWrap.scrollLeft = prevLeft; }
}

async function exportFilteredSummaryCSV() {
    const res = await api('/api/export_csv', 'POST', { items: filteredSummaryItems });
    if (!res.ok) return alert(t('export_err'));
    const blob = await res.blob();
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = `summary_manual_${new Date().toLocaleString('sv-SE').replace(/[- :]/g, '_')}.csv`;
    a.click();
}

setInterval(() => {
    const tab = document.getElementById('tab-summary');
    if (tab && tab.classList.contains('active') && !document.hidden) loadSummary(false);
}, 5000);

// web/static/system_tools.js
let _cachedUpdateInfo = null;
let _panicHoldTimer = null;
let _panicHoldTriggered = false;

async function initSystemStatusAndUpdates() {
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/version');
        if (res.ok) {
            const d = await res.json();
            const freezeBadge = document.getElementById('autopilot-freeze-badge');
            if (freezeBadge) {
                const frozen = (d.autopilot_frozen_until || 0) > Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
                freezeBadge.classList.toggle('hidden', !frozen);
            }
        }
    } catch (e) {}

    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE !== 'undefined' && CURRENT_ROLE === 'admin') {
        checkAppUpdates(false);
    }
    _bindPanicTouchHold();
}

async function checkAppUpdates(force = false) {
    try {
        const res = await api(`/api/update/check?force=${force ? '1' : '0'}`);
        if (!res.ok) return null;
        const d = await res.json();
        _cachedUpdateInfo = d;
        const badge = document.getElementById('update-available-badge');
        if (badge) {
            badge.classList.toggle('hidden', !d.update_available);
            if (d.update_available) {
                badge.textContent = `⬆️ Доступно v${d.latest_version}`;
            }
        }
        return d;
    } catch (e) {
        return null;
    }
}

async function openUpdateModal(force = true) {
    const modal = document.getElementById('update-modal');
    const statusEl = document.getElementById('upd-status-text');
    const logEl = document.getElementById('upd-changelog-box');
    const applyBtn = document.getElementById('btn-apply-update');
    const dockerHint = document.getElementById('upd-docker-hint');
    const verBadge = document.getElementById('upd-ver-badge');
    const ghLink = document.getElementById('upd-github-link');

    modal.style.display = 'flex';
    statusEl.textContent = 'Проверяем GitHub Releases...';
    logEl.textContent = 'Загрузка...';
    applyBtn.classList.add('hidden');
    dockerHint.classList.add('hidden');

    const d = (force || !_cachedUpdateInfo) ? await checkAppUpdates(true) : _cachedUpdateInfo;
    if (!d || !d.success) {
        statusEl.textContent = `⚠️ Не удалось проверить обновления: ${d?.error || 'ошибка сети'}`;
        logEl.textContent = 'Проверьте подключение к интернету.';
        return;
    }

    if (ghLink && d.html_url) ghLink.href = d.html_url;
    if (verBadge) verBadge.textContent = `v${d.current_version} → v${d.latest_version}`;

    if (d.update_available) {
        statusEl.textContent = `🔥 Доступна новая версия v${d.latest_version} (${d.release_name || ''})`;
        if (d.can_auto_update) applyBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
        else dockerHint.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else {
        statusEl.textContent = `✅ У вас установлена актуальная версия v${d.current_version}`;
    }
    logEl.textContent = d.changelog || 'Описание релиза отсутствует.';
}

function closeUpdateModal() {
    document.getElementById('update-modal').style.display = 'none';
}

async function applyAppUpdate() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-apply-update');
    const statusEl = document.getElementById('upd-status-text');
    btn.disabled = true;
    statusEl.textContent = '⏳ Скачивание tbr_new.exe и проверка SHA-256...';
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/update/apply', 'POST');
        const d = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || !d.success) {
            statusEl.textContent = `❌ ${d.message || d.error || 'Ошибка обновления'}`;
            btn.disabled = false;
            return;
        }
        statusEl.textContent = `🚀 ${d.message} Ожидаем перезапуск сервера...`;
        const poll = setInterval(async () => {
            try {
                const r = await fetch('/api/version');
                if (r.ok) {
                    clearInterval(poll);
                    window.location.reload();
                }
            } catch (e) {}
        }, 2000);
    } catch (e) {
        statusEl.textContent = `❌ Ошибка: ${e.message}`;
        btn.disabled = false;
    }
}

function _bindPanicTouchHold() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-panic-killswitch');
    if (!btn) return;
    btn.addEventListener('touchstart', () => {
        _panicHoldTriggered = false;
        btn.classList.add('holding');
        _panicHoldTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            _panicHoldTriggered = true;
            btn.classList.remove('holding');
            openPanicModal();
        }, 2000);
    }, { passive: true });
    const cancel = () => {
        if (_panicHoldTimer) clearTimeout(_panicHoldTimer);
        btn.classList.remove('holding');
    };
    btn.addEventListener('touchend', cancel);
    btn.addEventListener('touchcancel', cancel);
}

function onPanicBtnClick() {
    if (_panicHoldTriggered) {
        _panicHoldTriggered = false;
        return;
    }
    openPanicModal();
}

function openPanicModal() {
    document.getElementById('panic-kill-qb').checked = false;
    document.getElementById('panic-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}

function closePanicModal() {
    document.getElementById('panic-modal').style.display = 'none';
}

async function confirmPanicKillswitch() {
    const killQb = document.getElementById('panic-kill-qb').checked;
    const res = await api('/api/panic', 'POST', { kill_qb: killQb });
    const d = await res.json();
    closePanicModal();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка активации Panic Killswitch');
    alert('🚨 PANIC KILLSWITCH:\n' + (d.details || []).join('\n'));
    initSystemStatusAndUpdates();
    if (typeof loadTorrents === 'function') loadTorrents(true);
}

async function loadToolsTab() {
    if (typeof loadAchievements === 'function') loadAchievements();
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE !== 'undefined' && CURRENT_ROLE !== 'admin') return;
    if (typeof loadAuditLog === 'function') loadAuditLog();

    const listBox = document.getElementById('tools-manifest-list');
    const weeklyChk = document.getElementById('tools-manifest-weekly');
    if (!listBox) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/manifest/list');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        if (weeklyChk) weeklyChk.checked = Boolean(d.weekly);
        const files = d.manifests || [];
        if (!files.length) {
            listBox.innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">Копий пока нет. Нажмите «Выгрузить сейчас».</span>';
            return;
        }
        listBox.innerHTML = files.map(f => `
            <div class="row-between" style="background:var(--bg-input); padding:6px 8px; border-radius:6px;">
                <span>📄 <b>${escapeHtml(f.filename)}</b> <span class="muted-text">(${escapeHtml(f.size_fmt)}, ${escapeHtml(f.date)})</span></span>
                <a class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" href="/api/manifest/download?file=${encodeURIComponent(f.filename)}">⬇️ Скачать</a>
            </div>
        `).join('');
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function createManifestNow() {
    const res = await api('/api/manifest/create', 'POST');
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка создания манифеста');
    loadToolsTab();
}

async function toggleManifestWeeklyFromTools() {
    const chk = document.getElementById('tools-manifest-weekly');
    const res = await api('/api/manifest/weekly', 'POST', { weekly: Boolean(chk?.checked) });
    if (!res.ok) {
        const d = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        alert(d.error || 'Ошибка сохранения');
    }
}
