// web/static/timers_ui.js
let _snoozeRemainSec = 0;
let _snoozeTickInt = null;

async function initTimersUi() {
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE !== 'undefined' && CURRENT_ROLE !== 'admin') return;
    await Promise.all([refreshFreezeBadge(), refreshSnoozeBadge()]);
    setInterval(() => {
        if (!document.hidden && CURRENT_ROLE === 'admin') {
            refreshFreezeBadge();
            refreshSnoozeBadge();
        }
    }, 15000);
}

async function refreshFreezeBadge() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-smart-freeze');
    if (!btn) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/freeze/status');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        btn.textContent = d.frozen ? `▶️ Разморозить (${d.count})` : '❄️ Заморозка';
        btn.className = d.frozen ? 'btn admin-only' : 'btn btn-secondary admin-only';
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function toggleSmartFreeze() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('freeze-confirm-modal');
    const desc = document.getElementById('freeze-confirm-desc');
    const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('btn-freeze-confirm-go');
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/freeze/status');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        if (modal && desc && confirmBtn) {
            if (d.frozen) {
                desc.innerHTML = `Возобновить замороженные раздачи: <b class="accent-txt">${d.count} шт.</b>?<br><span class="lbl-xs muted-text">Старые паузы, которые были до заморозки, останутся на месте.</span>`;
                confirmBtn.textContent = `▶️ Разморозить (${d.count})`;
                confirmBtn.className = 'btn';
            } else {
                const act = d.active_count ?? 0;
                desc.innerHTML = `Поставить на паузу активные раздачи: <b class="orange-txt">${act} шт.</b>?<br><span class="lbl-xs muted-text">Список активных сохранится в БД для точного возврата.</span>`;
                confirmBtn.textContent = `❄️ Заморозить (${act})`;
                confirmBtn.className = 'btn';
            }
            modal.style.display = 'flex';
            return;
        }
    } catch (e) {}
}

function closeFreezeConfirmModal() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('freeze-confirm-modal');
    if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
}

async function executeSmartFreezeToggle() {
    closeFreezeConfirmModal();
    const res = await api('/api/freeze/toggle', 'POST');
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || d.message || 'Ошибка');
    alert(d.message);
    refreshFreezeBadge();
    if (typeof loadTorrents === 'function') loadTorrents(true);
}

function _fmtCountdown(sec) {
    const s = Math.max(0, parseInt(sec) || 0);
    const h = Math.floor(s / 3600);
    const m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60);
    const r = s % 60;
    return h > 0
        ? `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(r).padStart(2, '0')}`
        : `${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(r).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function _renderSnoozeButtons() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-snooze-timer');
    const box = document.getElementById('snooze-active-box');
    const cd = document.getElementById('snooze-modal-countdown');
    if (_snoozeRemainSec > 0) {
        const str = _fmtCountdown(_snoozeRemainSec);
        if (btn) { btn.textContent = `⏰ ${str}`; btn.className = 'btn admin-only'; }
        if (box) box.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (cd) cd.textContent = str;
    } else {
        if (btn) { btn.textContent = '😴 Сон'; btn.className = 'btn btn-secondary admin-only'; }
        if (box) box.classList.add('hidden');
    }
}

async function refreshSnoozeBadge() {
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/snooze/status');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        _snoozeRemainSec = d.active ? (d.remaining_sec || 0) : 0;
        _renderSnoozeButtons();
        if (_snoozeTickInt) clearInterval(_snoozeTickInt);
        if (_snoozeRemainSec > 0) {
            _snoozeTickInt = setInterval(() => {
                _snoozeRemainSec = Math.max(0, _snoozeRemainSec - 1);
                _renderSnoozeButtons();
                if (_snoozeRemainSec <= 0) {
                    clearInterval(_snoozeTickInt);
                    refreshFreezeBadge();
                }
            }, 1000);
        }
    } catch (e) {}
}

function openSnoozeModal() {
    refreshSnoozeBadge();
    document.getElementById('snooze-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}
function closeSnoozeModal() { document.getElementById('snooze-modal').style.display = 'none'; }

async function startSnoozeQuick(minutes) {
    const mode = document.getElementById('snooze-mode')?.value || 'pause';
    const res = await api('/api/snooze/start', 'POST', { minutes, mode });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || d.message || 'Ошибка');
    closeSnoozeModal();
    refreshSnoozeBadge();
    refreshFreezeBadge();
    if (typeof loadTorrents === 'function') loadTorrents(true);
}

function startSnoozeCustom() {
    const mins = parseInt(document.getElementById('snooze-custom-min')?.value) || 0;
    if (mins < 1 || mins > 1440) return alert('Укажите от 1 до 1440 минут');
    startSnoozeQuick(mins);
}

async function cancelSnoozeTimer() {
    const res = await api('/api/snooze/cancel', 'POST');
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.success) {
        _snoozeRemainSec = 0;
        _renderSnoozeButtons();
        refreshFreezeBadge();
        if (typeof loadTorrents === 'function') loadTorrents(true);
    }
}

async function openExpirationModal() {
    if (typeof selectedHash === 'undefined' || !selectedHash) return alert(t('err_select_torrent'));
    const item = (typeof torrentsData !== 'undefined' ? torrentsData : []).find(x => x.hash === selectedHash);
    document.getElementById('exp-torrent-name').textContent = item ? item.name : selectedHash;
    document.getElementById('exp-time-val').value = '';
    document.getElementById('exp-target-gib').value = '';
    document.getElementById('exp-delete-files').checked = false;
    const remBtn = document.getElementById('btn-exp-remove');
    remBtn.classList.add('hidden');

    try {
        const res = await api('/api/expirations');
        if (res.ok) {
            const d = await res.json();
            const ex = (d.items || []).find(r => r.hash_id.toLowerCase() === selectedHash.toLowerCase());
            if (ex) {
                remBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
                if (ex.expires_at) {
                    const leftH = Math.max(0.5, (ex.expires_at - Date.now() / 1000) / 3600).toFixed(1);
                    document.getElementById('exp-time-val').value = leftH;
                    document.getElementById('exp-time-unit').value = 'hours';
                }
                if (ex.target_uploaded) document.getElementById('exp-target-gib').value = Math.round(ex.target_uploaded / (1024 ** 3));
                document.getElementById('exp-delete-files').checked = Boolean(ex.delete_files);
            }
        }
    } catch (e) {}
    document.getElementById('expiration-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}
function closeExpirationModal() { document.getElementById('expiration-modal').style.display = 'none'; }

async function saveExpirationForSelected() {
    const val = parseFloat(document.getElementById('exp-time-val').value) || 0;
    const unit = document.getElementById('exp-time-unit').value;
    const hours = unit === 'days' ? val * 24 : val;
    const gib = parseFloat(document.getElementById('exp-target-gib').value) || 0;
    const delFiles = document.getElementById('exp-delete-files').checked;

    if (delFiles && !confirm('⚠️ Подтвердите: по истечении срока файлы раздачи будут УДАЛЕНЫ С ДИСКА!')) return;

    const res = await api('/api/expirations/set', 'POST', {
        hash: selectedHash, hours, target_uploaded_bytes: gib > 0 ? Math.round(gib * (1024 ** 3)) : 0, delete_files: delFiles
    });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || d.message || 'Ошибка');
    closeExpirationModal();
    alert('✅ ' + d.message);
}

async function removeExpirationForSelected() {
    const res = await api('/api/expirations/delete', 'POST', { hash: selectedHash });
    if (res.ok) { closeExpirationModal(); alert('Таймер снят.'); }
}

async function loadDndDigestAndTagging() {
    try {
        const [schRes, tagRes] = await Promise.all([api('/api/notifications/schedule'), api('/api/tagging/rules')]);
        if (schRes.ok) {
            const s = await schRes.json();
            const dnd = s.dnd || {}, dig = s.digest || {};
            const en = document.getElementById('dnd-enabled');
            if (en) en.checked = Boolean(dnd.enabled);
            if (document.getElementById('dnd-start')) document.getElementById('dnd-start').value = dnd.start || '23:00';
            if (document.getElementById('dnd-end')) document.getElementById('dnd-end').value = dnd.end || '08:00';
            document.querySelectorAll('.dnd-day-chk').forEach(chk => { chk.checked = (dnd.days || []).includes(parseInt(chk.value)); });
            if (document.getElementById('digest-mode')) document.getElementById('digest-mode').value = dig.mode || 'off';
            if (document.getElementById('digest-hour')) document.getElementById('digest-hour').value = dig.hour ?? 21;
            if (document.getElementById('digest-weekday')) document.getElementById('digest-weekday').value = dig.weekday ?? 6;
        }
        if (tagRes.ok) renderTaggingRules((await tagRes.json()).rules || []);
    } catch (e) {}
}

function renderTaggingRules(rules) {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('tagging-rules-tbody');
    if (!tbody) return;
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    if (!rules.length) { tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg">Правил тегирования нет</td></tr>'; return; }
    rules.forEach(r => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.innerHTML = `
            <td><b>${escapeHtml(r.name)}</b></td>
            <td>${r.match_field === 'tracker' ? '🌐 Трекер' : '📁 Путь'} содержит <code>${escapeHtml(r.pattern)}</code></td>
            <td><span class="badge badge-up">+${escapeHtml(r.target_tag)}</span></td>
            <td><span class="badge ${r.enabled ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-pause'}">${r.enabled ? 'Вкл' : 'Выкл'}</span></td>
            <td class="row-gap">
                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" onclick="toggleTaggingRule(${r.id})">${r.enabled ? 'Выкл' : 'Вкл'}</button>
                <button class="btn btn-danger btn-xs" onclick="deleteTaggingRule(${r.id})">🗑️</button>
            </td>
        `;
        tbody.appendChild(tr);
    });
}

async function saveDndDigestSchedule() {
    const days = [...document.querySelectorAll('.dnd-day-chk:checked')].map(c => parseInt(c.value));
    const payload = {
        dnd: { enabled: document.getElementById('dnd-enabled')?.checked || false, start: document.getElementById('dnd-start')?.value || '23:00', end: document.getElementById('dnd-end')?.value || '08:00', days },
        digest: { mode: document.getElementById('digest-mode')?.value || 'off', hour: parseInt(document.getElementById('digest-hour')?.value) || 21, weekday: parseInt(document.getElementById('digest-weekday')?.value) ?? 6 }
    };
    const res = await api('/api/notifications/schedule', 'POST', payload);
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка');
    document.getElementById('auto-status-msg').textContent = d.message;
}

async function addTaggingRule() {
    const payload = {
        name: document.getElementById('tag-rule-name').value.trim(),
        match_field: document.getElementById('tag-rule-field').value,
        pattern: document.getElementById('tag-rule-pattern').value.trim(),
        target_tag: document.getElementById('tag-rule-target').value.trim()
    };
    const res = await api('/api/tagging/rules/add', 'POST', payload);
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка');
    document.getElementById('tag-rule-name').value = '';
    document.getElementById('tag-rule-pattern').value = '';
    document.getElementById('tag-rule-target').value = '';
    loadDndDigestAndTagging();
}

async function toggleTaggingRule(id) { await api('/api/tagging/rules/toggle', 'POST', { id }); loadDndDigestAndTagging(); }
async function deleteTaggingRule(id) { if (confirm('Удалить правило тегирования?')) { await api('/api/tagging/rules/delete', 'POST', { id }); loadDndDigestAndTagging(); } }

================================================================================
FILE: web/static/torrents.js
================================================================================
// web/static/torrents.js
let torrentsData = [];
let visibleTorrents = [];
let currentSortCol = 'name';
let currentSortAsc = true;
let selectedHash = null;
const selectedHashes = new Set();
let lastDeadHashes = [];
let trackerErrorsMap = {};

const STATUS_MAP = {
    'downloading': { key: 'st_downloading', badge: 'badge-dl' },
    'seeding': { key: 'st_seeding', badge: 'badge-up' },
    'uploading': { key: 'st_seeding', badge: 'badge-up' },
    'stalledup': { key: 'st_stalledup', badge: 'badge-stall' },
    'stalleddl': { key: 'st_stalleddl', badge: 'badge-stall' },
    'pausedup': { key: 'st_pausedup', badge: 'badge-pause' },
    'pauseddl': { key: 'st_pauseddl', badge: 'badge-pause' },
    'stoppedup': { key: 'st_stoppedup', badge: 'badge-stop' },
    'stoppeddl': { key: 'st_stoppeddl', badge: 'badge-stop' },
    'forcedup': { key: 'st_forcedup', badge: 'badge-force' },
    'forceddl': { key: 'st_forceddl', badge: 'badge-force' },
    'checkingup': { key: 'st_checking', badge: 'badge-pause' },
    'checkingdl': { key: 'st_checking', badge: 'badge-pause' },
    'queuedup': { key: 'st_queued', badge: 'badge-pause' },
    'queueddl': { key: 'st_queued', badge: 'badge-pause' },
    'error': { key: 'st_error', badge: 'badge-err' },
    'missingfiles': { key: 'st_missing', badge: 'badge-err' },
    'moving': { key: 'st_moving', badge: 'badge-dl' },
    'unknown': { key: 'st_unknown', badge: 'badge-up' }
};

async function loadTorrents(force = false) {
    if (force) await api('/api/torrents/refresh', 'POST');
    const res = await api('/api/torrents');
    const json = await res.json();
    torrentsData = json.torrents || [];
    populateTorrentFilters();
    checkQbStatus();
    applySortAndRender();
    if (document.getElementById('tab-merger').classList.contains('active')) loadMergerLists();
    fetchTrackerErrorsAsync(force);
}

async function fetchTrackerErrorsAsync(force = false) {
    try {
        const res = await api(`/api/torrents/tracker_errors?force=${force ? '1' : '0'}`);
        if (res.ok) {
            trackerErrorsMap = (await res.json()).errors || {};
            filterTorrents();
        }
    } catch (e) {}
}

function populateTorrentFilters() {
    const tagSel = document.getElementById('filter-tag-select');
    const trSel = document.getElementById('filter-tracker-select');
    if (!tagSel || !trSel) return;
    const curTag = tagSel.value, curTr = trSel.value;
    const tags = new Set(), trackers = new Set();
    torrentsData.forEach(t => {
        if (t.tags) t.tags.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean).forEach(s => tags.add(s));
        if (t.tracker) trackers.add(t.tracker);
    });
    tagSel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Все теги</option>';
    [...tags].sort().forEach(tg => { const o = document.createElement('option'); o.value = tg; o.textContent = `🏷️ ${tg}`; tagSel.appendChild(o); });
    tagSel.value = curTag;

    trSel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Все трекеры</option>';
    [...trackers].sort().forEach(tr => { const o = document.createElement('option'); o.value = tr; o.textContent = tr; trSel.appendChild(o); });
    trSel.value = curTr;
}

function sortTable(col) {
    if (currentSortCol === col) currentSortAsc = !currentSortAsc;
    else { currentSortCol = col; currentSortAsc = (col === 'name' || col === 'save_path'); }
    applySortAndRender();
}

function applySortAndRender() {
    ['name', 'size', 'uploaded', 'ratio', 'health_score', 'state', 'save_path'].forEach(c => {
        const el = document.getElementById('sort-icon-' + c);
        if (el) el.textContent = (c === currentSortCol) ? (currentSortAsc ? ' ▲' : ' ▼') : '';
    });
    torrentsData.sort((a, b) => {
        if (currentSortCol === 'health_score') {
            const ha = (a.health_score ?? -1), hb = (b.health_score ?? -1);
            if (ha < 0 && hb >= 0) return 1;
            if (hb < 0 && ha >= 0) return -1;
            return currentSortAsc ? (ha - hb) : (hb - ha);
        }
        let vA = a[currentSortCol], vB = b[currentSortCol];
        if (typeof vA === 'string') vA = vA.toLowerCase();
        if (typeof vB === 'string') vB = vB.toLowerCase();
        return vA < vB ? (currentSortAsc ? -1 : 1) : (vA > vB ? (currentSortAsc ? 1 : -1) : 0);
    });
    filterTorrents();
}

function filterTorrents() {
    const q = (document.getElementById('search-box').value || '').toLowerCase();
    const tagF = (document.getElementById('filter-tag-select')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
    const trF = (document.getElementById('filter-tracker-select')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
    const errF = (document.getElementById('filter-tracker-err-select')?.value || '').toLowerCase();

    visibleTorrents = torrentsData.filter(t => {
        if (q && !(t.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) && !(t.save_path || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
        if (tagF && !(t.tags || '').toLowerCase().split(',').map(s => s.trim()).includes(tagF)) return false;
        if (trF && (t.tracker || '').toLowerCase() !== trF) return false;
        if (errF) {
            const errObj = trackerErrorsMap[t.hash.toLowerCase()];
            if (!errObj) return false;
            if (errF !== 'any' && errObj.category !== errF) return false;
        }
        return true;
    });
    renderTorrents(visibleTorrents);
}

function renderTorrents(list) {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('torrents-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    list.forEach(item => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        if (item.hash === selectedHash) tr.classList.add('selected');
        tr.addEventListener('click', e => {
            if (e.target.type === 'checkbox') return;
            selectTorrent(item, tr);
        });
        const st = STATUS_MAP[(item.state || '').toLowerCase()] || STATUS_MAP['unknown'];
        const chk = document.createElement('input');
        chk.type = 'checkbox';
        chk.checked = selectedHashes.has(item.hash);
        chk.onchange = () => {
            if (chk.checked) selectedHashes.add(item.hash);
            else selectedHashes.delete(item.hash);
            updateBulkSelectionUI();
        };
        const tdChk = document.createElement('td');
        tdChk.appendChild(chk);
        tr.appendChild(tdChk);

        const lastSeedHtml = item.is_last_seed ? ` <span class="badge badge-stall">🛡️ Единственный сид</span>` : '';
        const errInfo = trackerErrorsMap[item.hash.toLowerCase()];
        const errHtml = errInfo ? ` <span class="badge badge-err" title="${escapeHtml(errInfo.message)}">⚠️ ${escapeHtml(errInfo.category_label)}</span>` : '';
        const tgtBar = typeof renderTargetBarHtml === 'function' ? renderTargetBarHtml(item.target) : '';
        const healthHtml = (item.health_score === undefined || item.health_score < 0)
            ? `<span class="badge badge-pause">нет данных</span>`
            : `<span class="badge ${item.is_last_seed ? 'badge-stall' : (item.health_score >= 50 ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-dl')}" title="Сиды: ${item.num_complete} | Личи: ${item.num_incomplete}">💎 ${Number(item.health_score).toFixed(1)}</span>`;

        tr.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `
            <td style="white-space:normal;"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong>${lastSeedHtml}${errHtml}${item.tags ? ` <span class="muted-text" style="font-size:10px;">[${escapeHtml(item.tags)}]</span>` : ''}${tgtBar}</td>
            <td>${escapeHtml(item.size_fmt)}</td>
            <td>${escapeHtml(item.uploaded_fmt)}</td>
            <td><strong class="accent-txt">${Number(item.ratio).toFixed(2)}x</strong></td>
            <td>${healthHtml}</td>
            <td><span class="badge ${st.badge}">${t(st.key)}</span></td>
            <td class="muted-text" style="font-size:11px;">${escapeHtml(item.save_path)}</td>
        `);
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);
    updateBulkSelectionUI();
}

function toggleSelectAllVisible(masterChk) {
    visibleTorrents.forEach(item => {
        if (masterChk.checked) selectedHashes.add(item.hash);
        else selectedHashes.delete(item.hash);
    });
    renderTorrents(visibleTorrents);
}

function updateBulkSelectionUI() {
    const bar = document.getElementById('bulk-actions-bar');
    const cnt = document.getElementById('bulk-selected-count');
    if (cnt) cnt.textContent = selectedHashes.size;
    if (bar) bar.classList.toggle('hidden', selectedHashes.size === 0);
}

async function bulkTorrentAction(action) {
    if (!selectedHashes.size) return;
    let tag = '';
    if (action === 'addTags' || action === 'removeTags') {
        tag = prompt(action === 'addTags' ? 'Введите тег для добавления:' : 'Введите тег для удаления:');
        if (!tag || !tag.trim()) return;
    }
    const res = await api('/api/torrents/action', 'POST', { hash: [...selectedHashes].join('|'), action, tag: tag.trim() });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!d.success && d.error) alert(d.error);
    loadTorrents(true);
}

function selectTorrent(item, tr) {
    selectedHash = item.hash;
    document.querySelectorAll('#torrents-tbody tr').forEach(r => r.classList.remove('selected'));
    if (tr) tr.classList.add('selected');
    document.getElementById('selected-title').textContent = `${item.name} (${item.size_fmt})`;
}

async function deleteSelectedTorrent() {
    if (!selectedHash) return alert(t('err_select_torrent'));
    const item = torrentsData.find(x => x.hash === selectedHash);
    if (item && item.is_last_seed) {
        if (!confirm(`🛡️ ПРЕДУПРЕЖДЕНИЕ!\nВы единственный раздающий этот файл в сети:\n«${item.name}»\n\nТочно перейти к удалению?`)) return;
    }
    if (!confirm(`Удалить раздачу «${item ? item.name : selectedHash}» из qBittorrent (файлы на диске останутся)?`)) return;
    const delFiles = confirm('Нажмите OK, чтобы также УДАЛИТЬ ФАЙЛЫ С ДИСКА, или Отмена — удалить только торрент из клиента.');
    const res = await api('/api/torrents/delete', 'POST', { hash: selectedHash, delete_files: delFiles });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.success) { selectedHash = null; loadTorrents(true); }
    else alert(d.error || 'Ошибка удаления');
}

async function torrentAction(action) {
    if (!selectedHash) return alert(t('err_select_torrent'));
    const res = await api('/api/torrents/action', 'POST', { hash: selectedHash, action });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!d.success && d.error) alert(d.error);
    loadTorrents();
}

async function globalTorrentAction(action) {
    const msg = action === 'stop' ? t('confirm_pause_all') : t('confirm_start_all');
    if (!confirm(msg)) return;
    const res = await api('/api/torrents/action_all', 'POST', { action });
    const d = await res.json();
    alert(d.message || d.error);
    loadTorrents();
}

function goToAnalyticsWithTorrent() {
    if (!selectedHash) return alert(t('err_select_torrent_tbl'));
    switchTab('analytics');
    setTimeout(() => {
        const sel = document.getElementById('smart-calc-target-type');
        sel.value = selectedHash;
        onSmartCalcTargetChange();
        document.getElementById('smart-calc-goal').focus();
    }, 150);
}

async function scanDeadTorrents() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('dead-torrents-modal');
    const tbody = document.getElementById('dead-torrents-tbody');
    const badges = document.getElementById('dead-summary-badges');
    const pauseAllBtn = document.getElementById('btn-pause-all-dead');
    modal.style.display = 'flex';
    badges.innerHTML = `<span class="badge badge-pause">${t('dead_scanning_badge')}</span>`;
    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">${t('scanning')}</td></tr>`;
    pauseAllBtn.classList.add('hidden');
    lastDeadHashes = [];
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/torrents/scan_dead', 'POST');
        const d = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || !d.success) { badges.innerHTML = ''; tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(d.error || 'Error')}</td></tr>`; return; }
        badges.innerHTML = `<span class="badge badge-dl">${t('dead_scanned', { n: d.total_scanned })}</span><span class="badge ${d.dead_count > 0 ? 'badge-err' : 'badge-up'}">${t('dead_found', { n: d.dead_count })}</span>`;
        (d.groups || []).forEach(g => { const sp = document.createElement('span'); sp.className = 'badge badge-stall'; sp.textContent = `${g.error_msg}: ${g.count}`; badges.appendChild(sp); });
        if (!d.items || !d.items.length) { tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--accent);">${t('dead_none')}</td></tr>`; return; }
        lastDeadHashes = d.items.map(i => i.hash);
        pauseAllBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
        tbody.innerHTML = '';
        const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        d.items.forEach(item => {
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.innerHTML = `<td style="max-width:260px; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis;" title="${escapeHtml(item.name)}"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong></td><td>${escapeHtml(item.size_fmt)}</td><td><span class="badge badge-err">${escapeHtml(item.error_msg)}</span></td><td><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" onclick="pauseSingleDead('${escapeHtml(item.hash)}', this)">${t('btn_pause')}</button></td>`;
            frag.appendChild(tr);
        });
        tbody.appendChild(frag);
    } catch (e) { badges.innerHTML = ''; tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(e.message)}</td></tr>`; }
}

async function pauseSingleDead(hash, btn) {
    const res = await api('/api/torrents/action', 'POST', { hash, action: 'stop' });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.success) { btn.textContent = t('dead_paused'); btn.disabled = true; loadTorrents(); } else alert(d.error || 'Error');
}

async function pauseAllDeadTorrents() {
    if (!lastDeadHashes.length || !confirm(t('dead_pause_confirm', { n: lastDeadHashes.length }))) return;
    const res = await api('/api/torrents/action', 'POST', { hash: lastDeadHashes.join('|'), action: 'stop' });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.success) { alert(t('dead_paused_msg', { n: lastDeadHashes.length })); closeDeadModal(); loadTorrents(); } else alert(d.error || 'Error');
}

function closeDeadModal() { document.getElementById('dead-torrents-modal').style.display = 'none'; }

function loadMergerLists() {
    const o = document.getElementById('merger-old'), n = document.getElementById('merger-new');
    if (!torrentsData.length) { loadTorrents().then(() => loadMergerLists()); return; }
    o.innerHTML = ''; n.innerHTML = '';
    torrentsData.forEach(item => {
        const opt1 = document.createElement('option');
        opt1.value = item.hash; opt1.textContent = `${item.name} [${item.hash.substring(0, 8)}]`;
        o.appendChild(opt1); n.appendChild(opt1.cloneNode(true));
    });
    if (torrentsData.length > 1) n.selectedIndex = 1;
    if (typeof onMergerSelectionChange === 'function') onMergerSelectionChange();
    if (typeof loadSmartMatchCandidates === 'function') loadSmartMatchCandidates();
}

function onMergerDeleteModeChange() {
    document.getElementById('merger-force-warn').classList.toggle('hidden', document.getElementById('merger-delete-mode').value !== 'force');
}

async function runMerger(forceDup = false) {
    const logBox = document.getElementById('merger-log');
    const old_hash = document.getElementById('merger-old').value;
    const new_hash = document.getElementById('merger-new').value;
    const delete_mode = document.getElementById('merger-delete-mode').value || 'none';
    const merge_mode = document.getElementById('merger-mode')?.value || 'add';

    if (!old_hash || !new_hash) return alert(t('err_select_both'));
    if (old_hash.toLowerCase() === new_hash.toLowerCase()) return alert(t('err_same_torrent'));

    logBox.textContent = t('merger_running');
    const res = await api('/api/merger/run', 'POST', {
        old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode, merge_mode, force_duplicate: forceDup
    });
    const d = await res.json();

    if (res.status === 409 && d.needs_duplicate_confirm) {
        if (confirm(d.warning || 'Эти раздачи уже объединялись! Сложить ещё раз?')) {
            return runMerger(true);
        }
        logBox.textContent = '❌ Операция отменена пользователем (защита от повторного сложения).';
        return;
    }

    logBox.textContent = (d.log || []).join('\n');
    if (d.success && d.needs_delete_confirm && d.confirm_info) {
        const ci = d.confirm_info;
        const srcTor = torrentsData.find(x => x.hash.toLowerCase() === old_hash.toLowerCase());
        if (srcTor && srcTor.is_last_seed && !confirm('🛡️ ПРЕДУПРЕЖДЕНИЕ: Вы единственный раздающий этот файл в сети! Точно удалить исходник?')) {
            logBox.textContent += '\n' + t('del_cancelled');
            return;
        }
        const msg = ci.method === 'disk_only' ? t('confirm_del_disk_only', { name: ci.old_name })
            : (ci.delete_files ? t('confirm_del_with_files', { name: ci.old_name })
                : (ci.downgraded ? t('confirm_del_downgraded', { name: ci.old_name }) : t('confirm_del_torrent_only', { name: ci.old_name })));
        if (confirm(msg)) {
            const delRes = await api('/api/merger/delete_source', 'POST', { old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode, confirmed: true });
            const delData = await delRes.json();
            logBox.textContent += '\n' + (delData.log || []).join('\n');
            loadTorrents(true);
        } else logBox.textContent += '\n' + t('del_cancelled');
    }
}

FILE: web/templates/kiosk.html

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ru">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>tbr PRO — Kiosk</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="/static/style.css">
    <style>
        body { background: #000; color: #f5f5f5; justify-content: center; align-items: center; padding: 24px; overflow: hidden; }
        #kiosk-wrap { width: 100%; max-width: 1100px; transition: transform 1.2s ease; }
        .k-val { font-size: 2.2rem; font-weight: 800; }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
<div id="kiosk-wrap">
    <div class="row-between mb-12">
        <span class="logo-text" style="font-size:1.5rem;">⚡ tbr PRO KIOSK</span>
        <span id="k-qb-badge" class="badge badge-pause">...</span>
    </div>
    <div class="grid-cards mb-12">
        <div class="card"><div class="lbl-sm">Скорость отдачи</div><div id="k-speed" class="k-val blue-txt">—</div></div>
        <div class="card"><div class="lbl-sm">Отдано за 24ч</div><div id="k-24h" class="k-val accent-txt">—</div></div>
        <div class="card"><div class="lbl-sm">Свободно на диске</div><div id="k-disk" class="k-val orange-txt">—</div></div>
        <div class="card"><div class="lbl-sm">Активные раздачи</div><div id="k-cnt" class="k-val purple-txt">—</div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
        <div class="card-title">🏆 ТОП-5 РАЗДАЧ (24Ч)</div>
        <div id="k-top" class="col-gap" style="font-size:15px;"></div>
    </div>
</div>
<script>
let _shift = 0;
async function refreshKiosk() {
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/widget?kiosk=1');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        document.getElementById('k-speed').textContent = d.current_speed_fmt || '—';
        document.getElementById('k-24h').textContent = d.uploaded_24h_fmt || '—';
        document.getElementById('k-disk').textContent = d.disk_free_fmt || '—';
        document.getElementById('k-cnt').textContent = `${d.active_torrents} / ${d.total_torrents}`;
        const qb = document.getElementById('k-qb-badge');
        qb.className = d.qb_running ? 'badge badge-up' : 'badge badge-stop';
        qb.textContent = d.qb_running ? '🟢 qBittorrent OK' : '🔴 qBittorrent OFF';
        const top = d.charts?.top_5 || [];
        document.getElementById('k-top').innerHTML = top.map((x, i) => `<div class="row-between"><span>${i + 1}. ${x.name}</span><b class="accent-txt">${x.gb} GiB</b></div>`).join('') || 'Нет данных';
        _shift = (_shift + 1) % 4;
        const offsets = ['translate(0,0)', 'translate(3px,2px)', 'translate(-2px,3px)', 'translate(2px,-2px)'];
        document.getElementById('kiosk-wrap').style.transform = offsets[_shift];
    } catch (e) {}
}
refreshKiosk();
setInterval(refreshKiosk, 5000);
</script>
</body>
</html>
